ic cōtaṉiyil tēṟuvataṟkuk kurumār pala vaḻikaḷi aṟintirun tārkaḷ. camāti aṟiyil paci tākattukku vēṇṭiya uṇavu, nīr mutaliyavi kaḷiyum utavikku vēṇṭiya vēliyāḷkaḷiyum viṭṭuvippatu oṉṟu. maṟṟoṉṟu camāti aṟiyil mīṉ, paruntu, pāmpu, vaṇṭu mutaliya vikaḷip pōl ceyta uruvaṅkaḷi vittal. kurumārāl naṉku mantirikkappaṭṭāl ivi ellāp pakikaḷiyum, tīmikaḷiyum ōṭṭum. iṉṉorumuṟi maraṇap puttakattip putippatu. ip puttakattil pala mantiraṅkaḷum tutikaḷum eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi ociris teyvatti makiḻvikkattakkaṉa.
kurumār nītikaḷi makkaḷukkup pōtippataṟkup patilākat tāyattukaḷi viṟṟārkaḷ; mantira vittikaḷ campantamāṉa kiriyikaḷ purintārkaḷ. maraṇamaṭintavarukku vaḻiyil nērum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa iṭiyūṟukaḷi maraṇapputtakattāl vellalām eṉa an nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. nērmiyāka vāḻtalilum pārkka mantiraṅkaḷi uccarippatāl atika naṉmi uṇṭākum eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.
tēvarkaḷil oruvar iṉṉoruvarukku etirāka mantiraṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ekiptiyariṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mantira vittikkārarippaṟṟi atikam kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ mantirattiṉāl kuḷaṅkaḷi vaṟṟac ceytārkaḷ; tuṇiyuṇṭa uṟuppukaḷi uriya iṭaṅkaḷil poruntumpaṭic ceytārkaḷ. ovvoru vīṭṭiṉ vāyililum tīya āvikaḷi ōṭṭumpaṭi oru kaṭavuḷ iruntatu. ovvoru mātamum ovvoru nāḷum yātum oru teyvattukku urittāy iruntaṉa. oruvaṉ piṟanta nāḷiyum nērattiyum koṇṭu vāḻnāḷil avaṉukku eṉṉa nērum eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭatu. iṟantavarkaḷi avarkaḷ cellum ulakil naṉmippaṭuttum mantiraṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa.
ikkantaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ
ki.mu. 1,380il ikkantaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ paṭṭattukku vantāṉ. ivaṉ nālām amkotep eṉavum aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ. paṭṭa meytiyatum ivaṉ kōyiṟ kurumārukku māṟākap puraṭci ceytāṉ. kōyilkaḷil āṭukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭuvatiyum, viyapicāraṅkaḷ naṭappati yum veṟuttāṉ. kurumār mantiravitti, tāyattukaḷ viṟṟal mutaliyavi kaḷik koṇṭu vāṇipam purital, veḷippāṭu kūṟutal mutaliyavi aracāṅka ūḻalkaḷ eṉṟum, kurumāriṉ vārttikaḷ makkaḷukkut tīmi viḷivippaṉa eṉṟum tuṇintāṉ; pala kaṭavuḷar vaṇakkam tīyatu eṉṟu aṟintāṉ; ataṉ eṉṉum orē kaṭavuḷ māttiram vaḻipaṭappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṭṭaḷi ceytāṉ. ataṉ kaṭavuḷ ellā nalla kuṇaṅ kaḷum, ellā āṟṟalum, eṅkum niṟintirukkum iyalpum uṭiyar eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭār. ataṉ vaḻipāṭṭuk kuriyaṉavallāta ellāk kōyil kaḷiyum mūṭumpaṭi aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ; tīps nakaram puṉitamuṭiya taṉṟu eṉakkoṇṭu akērāṉ eṉṉum nakari amittu atit talinaka rākak koṇṭāṉ. kuṭikaḷ kurumāriṉ kaṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭuc cēmamāka vāḻntārkaḷ. celvamum atikāramum mikunta kurumāri aracaṉ orē aṭiyil vīḻttiviṭṭāṉ. kurumār tiri maṟiviliruntu aracaṉukku māṟākac cati ālōcaṉikaḷ ceytaṉar.
tūtaṉ kāpaṉ
ikkantaṉ maraṇamāṉa iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷiṉpiṉ avaṉ makaḷiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa tūtaṉ kāpaṉ āṭciyi ēṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ kurumāriṉ naṇpaṉāyiruntāṉ. ippoḻutu kurumār muntiya aracaṉ ceytataṟku māṟākat tamatu koḷkikaḷi nāṭṭiṉar. ivaṉuṭiya camātiyil aḷavillāta viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ac camātiyil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷiṉ matippu 30,000 taṅka nāṇayam variyilākum. ivaṉuṭiya āṭcipaṟṟi yātum teriyavilli. ivaṉukkuppiṉ harma hāl āṭci purintāṉ; mutalām cēti eṉpavaṉ ivaṉukkuppiṉ aracaṉā ṉāṉ. karnāk eṉṉum iṭattil apicipel maliyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa iv varacaṉiṉ camāti camīpakālattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṉukkuppiṉ iraṇṭām irāmcē āṭci purintāṉ. avaṉ pālattīṉi veṟṟikoṇṭu aṅkuniṉṟum pala yūtari aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭuvantāṉ. pipiḷiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭilē yāttiri ākamattil collappaṭum ekiptiya aracaṉ ivaṉ eṉṟu karutappaṭupavaṉ. iṟakkumpōtu ivaṉukku nūṟu putalvarum aimpatu putalviyarum iruntaṉar. ivaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ mikap palarāyiruntamiyiṉ aṭutta nūṟu āṇṭukaḷil avarkaḷ taṉik kūṭṭattiṉarākap perukiyiruntārkaḷ.
aracaṉuṭiya atikārattilum pārkka iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉariṉ atikāram mēlāka iruntatu. ak kūṭṭattiṉar kurumārāvar. ellā nāṭu kaḷilum aracarukkum kurumārukkumiṭiyil atikārattiṉ poruṭṭup pōṭṭi yiruntu vantatu. ovvoru pōrilum kiṭikkum koḷḷip poruḷiṉ perum pakuti kurumārukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. mūṉṟām irāmcē kālattil avarkaḷiṭam 107,000 aṭimikaḷ iruntārkaḷ. itu ekiptiya caṉattokiyil muppatilorupakuti; 750,000 ēkkar nilam avarkaḷvacam iruntatu. itu ekiptil payiriṭum nilam muḻumiyilum ēḻil oru paṅku. avarkaḷiṭam 500,000 āṭumāṭukaḷiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ ekiptilum ciriyāvilumuḷḷa 169 nakaraṅkaḷiṉ varuvāyip peṟṟaṉar. ovvoru āṇṭum kurumārukku 32,000 kilōkirām poṉṉum, pattu ilaṭcam kilōkirām veḷḷiyum, 18,50,000 mūṭṭi tāṉiyaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. karuvūlattip pārttapōtu vēliyāḷkaḷukkuk kūli koṭukka yātum eñciyirukkavilli.
inniliyil aracar kurumāriṉ aṭimikaḷātal iyalpēyākum. irāmciṭ kālattil mutaṉmik kuru avaṉuṭiya ceṅkōlip piṭuṅkit tāṉē āṭcipurintāṉ. ak kālattil periya kōyilkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. mūṭak koḷkikaḷ ceḻippaṭintaṉa. ellā vakiyāṉa muṭivu ceyvataṟkum kurumār nimittam pārppatic ciṟappākak koṇṭaṉar. ayalnāṭṭavar ekiptiṉ mītu paṭi eṭukka āyattam ceyyum kālattil nāṭṭiṉ celvam muḻumiyum kurumārāl uṟiñcappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
ekiptiṉ vīḻcci
pāpilōṉ, acīriyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷ palamaṭintaṉa. ekiptōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭa nāṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟum ekipti veṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 954il mēṟku ellip pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ilipiyar ekiptiṉ mītu paṭi eṭuttārkaḷ. ki.mu. 722il ettiyōppiyar teṟkiṉiṉṟum vantu tammi muṉ aṭimip paṭuttiya ekiptiyarip paḻivāṅkiṉar. ki.mu. 674il acīriyar ekipti veṉṟu atit tamatu tiṟi nāṭākkiṉar. ki.pi. 525il pāracīkar ekipti veṟṟi koṇṭaṉar. ki.mu. 322il alakcāntar ekipti māciṭōṉ irācciyattiṉ pakutiyākkiṉār. ki.mu. 30il ekiptu, urōma irācciyattiṉ pakutiyāka māṟic carittirattiliruntu maṟintatu. ki.pi. 650il mukammatiyar ekipti veṉṟu keyrō nakari amittārkaḷ. ippoḻutu ekiptiyar arāpiyarōṭu kalantārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyum arapi moḻiyōṭu kalan tuḷḷatu. ekiptu, ippoḻutu mucilimkaḷuṭiya atikārattil irukkiṉṟatu.
pāpilōṉ
cumēriyāvum akkēṭiyāvum cērnta nāṭu pāpilōṉiyāvākum. yūpirātas tikiras āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil kiṭakkum aḻipāṭukaḷi iṉṟu kāṇpōrkaḷ avviṭam celvamum atikāramum niṟinta makkaḷiṉ talinakarāka iruntateṉa orupōtum niṉikkamāṭṭārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyā cālatiyā1 eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. pāpilōṉiṉ mutalaracaṉ hamurapi (ki.mu. 2,123 - ki.mu. 2,081). ivaṉ cemittiya kulattavaṉ. arāpiyāvil vāḻnta paḻiya makkaḷ cemittiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivaṉukkuc camāṣ eṉṉum ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ caṭṭaṅkaḷi aruḷicceyyum kāṭciyum, ac caṭṭaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa kaṟṟūṇ oṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ tuṇi koṇṭu akkālattil vaḻaṅkiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ, makkaḷ vāḻkki mutaliya pala ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ2 cemittiyar pōṉṟa tōṟṟamum, iruṇṭa mayirum, tāṭiyum uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālārum mayiri nīḷamāka vaḷaraviṭṭaṉar. iru pālārum pātam variyum maṟikkakkūṭiya veṇcaṇalāṭi uṭuttārkaḷ; uṭalukku naṟumaṇam pūciṉārkaḷ; peṇkaḷ iṭatu tōḷ veḷiyē teriyumpaṭi viṭṭārkaḷ. āṭavarkaḷ iru tōḷkaḷiyum maṟittuc caṭṭiyiṭṭārkaḷ. celvam vaḷarntapōtu āṭikaḷukkup palavaki niṟaṅkaḷ ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa; kōṭu, vaṭṭam, puḷḷipōṉṟa palavaki vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; makkaḷ kālukku aḻakiya cerupput tarittaṉar; āṭavar taliyil pāki vittārkaḷ; peṇkaḷ, kaḻuttu māli, kivaḷi mutaliya aṇikaḷ aṇintaṉar. āṭavar kiyil aḻakiya kittaṭikaḷik koṇṭu tirintaṉar. avikaḷiṉ talikaḷil aḻakiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avar kaḷiṉ arippaṭṭiyil aḻakāka veṭṭappaṭṭa muttirikaḷ toṅkiṉa. ivi avarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. kurumār tamatu tōṟṟatti maṟippataṟku ucciyil nīṇṭa muṉiyuṭiya toppiyi aṇintaṉar. nākarikatti uṇṭākkum atika celvam aḻivukku ētu vākum. celvattiṉāl kalikaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa; makkaḷ ullācamākavum āṭamparamākavum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl makkaḷiṉ pōrkkuṇam maḻuṅki viṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu valiyuṭiyavarkaḷum paciyuṭiyavarkaḷumākiya veḷiyē yuḷḷavarkaḷ paṭi eṭukkiṟārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiṉ kiḻakku elliyil kācirīcar (Kassites) eṉṉum makkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāpilōṉiyariṉ celvattik kaṇṭu poṟāmiyaṭintārkaḷ. hamurapi iṟantu eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷuḷ ivarkaḷ pala taṭavi pāpilōṉmītu paṭi eṭuttu nāṭṭic cūṟiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. iṟutiyil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷarāy aṅku amarntārkaḷ. aṟunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉ kācarīcarkaḷ veḷiyē turattappaṭṭārkaḷ. aṭutta nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷil āṭci kuḻappam aṭintiruntatu. appoḻutu acīriyar pāpilōṉi veṉṟu ataṉi niṉēvāviṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭuttiṉar. pāpilōṉiyar kalakam ceytapōtu ceṉṉāccerip eṉṉum acīriya aracaṉ pāpilōṉi muṟṟāka aḻittāṉ. mītiyar taliyeṭuttapōtu acīriyariṉ palam kuṟin tiruntatu. napōpolacār eṉṉum pāpilēṉiyaṉ mītiyariṉ tuṇiyip peṟṟup pāpilōṉi acīriyariṭamiruntu viṭuttāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ ivaṉ kumāraṉākiya nepucaṭṉēcar(Nebuchadnezar) āṭcipurintāṉ. ivaṉē paḻiya ēḻu viyappukaḷil oṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa toṅku tōṭ ṭatti(Hanging gardens) amittāṉ.
toḻilāḷar
pāpilōṉiṉ orupakuti kāṭāka iruntatu. neruṅki vaḷarnta puṟkaḷiṉiṭiyē pāmpukaḷ tirintaṉa. pāpilōṉiya aracar kāṭukaḷil ceṉṟu ciṅkaṅkaḷōṭu nēriṟ pōrceytu avikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiṉar. itu aracariṉ ciṟanta poḻutupōkkākak karutappaṭṭatu.
nilattiṉ perumpakuti vārakkārarālum aṭimikaḷālum payiriṭap paṭṭatu. cila pakuti nilattukkuriya kuṭikaḷāl payiriṭappaṭṭatu. putiya kaṟkālattiṟpōla nilam pāriyiṉālum maṇveṭṭiyiṉālum kiḷaṟap paṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kalappi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṟṟuveḷḷam vayaluṭ pāyātapaṭi ovvoru vayaliyum cuṟṟip periya varampukaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āṟu perukumpōtu veḷiyē vaḻintuōṭum veḷḷam kālvāykaḷ mūlam kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vikkappaṭṭatu; vēṇṭiya kālaṅkaḷil maṭikaḷit tiṟantu vayalkaḷukku nīr pāyccappaṭṭatu. ēṟṟaṅkaḷāl iṟittu nīr pāyccuvatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. nīr tēkki vikkappaṭṭa kuḷam oṉṟiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 140 mil.
vayalkaḷil palavakit tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷintaṉa; palavakip paḻa maraṅkaḷ ceḻittu ōṅkiṉa. muntiriki, ‘aliv’ mutaliyaṉa pāpilōṉi liruntu kirīs, urōm mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṉa. nāṭṭil cempu, īyam, irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷ aritteṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; maṇṇeṇ ṇiyum eṭukkappaṭṭatu. hamurapi kālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kal āyutaṅkaḷukkup patil veṇkala āyutaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ulōkaṅ kaḷi urukki vārkkum toḻil aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu. ivi veḷināṭu kaḷukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷ, vikkōl kalanta kaḷimaṇṇi ṉālum, kaḷimaṇṇil arintu kāyaviṭappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷiṉālum, ceṅkaṟkaḷālum eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
uḷnāṭṭup pōkkuvarattukkuk kaḻuti pūṭṭiya vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu. 2,100il eḻutiya paṭṭiyamoṉṟil kutiriyip paṟṟi mutaṉmutal collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kiḻakkuttēcak kaḻuti eṉappaṭṭatu. mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiṉ vaṇikar pāpilōṉ nakaril kūṭi ṉārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyar nāṇayaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttavilli; kōtumi, tāṉiyam, poṉtuṇṭu, veḷḷi mutaliyavi nāṇayaṅkaḷukkup patil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭaṉ vāṅkiyavaṉ kaṭaṉit tiruppik koṭukka muṭiyāviṭiṉ avaṉuṭiya aṭimikaḷi allatu piḷḷikaḷik kaṭaṉkāraṉ piṭittukkoḷḷap pāpilōṉiyac caṭṭam aṉumatittatu. āṉāl avaṉ avarkaḷi mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumēl vittiruttal kūṭātu. pōrkaḷil akappaṭṭa pakivar aṭimiyākkappaṭṭaṉar. piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kirāmaṅkaḷit tākki aṭimikaḷip piṭittu vāṇikam purivōrum iruntaṉar. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa vēlikaḷ aṭimikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. peṇ aṭimikaḷ tammi aṭimikaḷāka vittirukkum talivaṉiṉ tayavil taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ; talivaṉ mūlam piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟārkaḷ. aṭimikaḷ kaṭaṉukkāka īṭu vikkavum viṟkavum paṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ iruppatilum iṟantupōtal ilāpam eṉat talivaṉ niṉit tāl avaṉ avarkaḷik koṉṟuviṭalām. tappi ōṭiya aṭimiyi oruvarum vīṭṭil maṟittu vittiruttal kūṭātu. av vaṭimiyip piṭittuk koṭuppavarkaḷukku vekumati aḷikkappaṭṭatu. aṭimikaḷ allāta potumakkaḷippōla ivarkaḷum pōrukku allatu potu vēlikaḷukkut tiraṭṭappaṭṭārkaḷ. aṭimi oruvaṉ aṭimi yallāta peṇṇi maṇantāl avaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ aṭimikaḷāka māṭṭārkaḷ. talivaṉ virumpiṉāl aṭimiyi viṭutali ceyyalām.
caṭṭam
aracaṉāl pāpilōṉ āḷappaṭṭatu. kāṇiyāḷarākiya pirapukkaḷ aracaṉuṭiya tuṇivarkaḷākaviruntaṉar. ap pirapukkaḷiṉ atikāram ciṟituciṟitāka maṟitalum vaṇikarākiya celvariṉ atikāram atikarittatu. ivarkaḷ aracaṉukkum potumakkaḷum iṭippaṭṭa niliyiluḷḷavar kaḷāvār. aracaṉukkup piṉ nāṭu avaṉ virumpiya avaṉ putalvarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉic cērntatu. āṭci aracaṉāl amikkappaṭṭa pirapukkaḷ capiyāl naṭattappaṭṭatu.
hamurapi aracaṉiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. teyvattiṉ peyarāl naṭattappaṭum cila cōtaṉikaḷāl cila kuṟṟaṅkaḷ muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ mantira vittikkāraṉeṉṟō, orutti paratti eṉṟō kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭāl avarkaḷ yūpirātas āṟṟil eṟiyap paṭṭārkaḷ. naṉṟāka nīntak kūṭiyavar cārpil kaṭavuḷ iruntār. mantira vittikkāraṉ nīril mūḻki iṟavāviṭiṉ kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiyavaṉiṉ cotti avaṉ peṟṟāṉ. parattik kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭavaḷ iṟantuviṭṭāl, avaḷ parattiyāvāḷ! piḻittāl kuṟṟamaṟṟavaḷāvāḷ. kurumār nītipati kaḷāvar. kōyilkaḷil nītimaṉṟaṅkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ iṉṉoru vaṉiṉ kaṇṇiyō palliyō ūṉap paṭuttiṉāl avaṉukku atē taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. oru vīṭu viḻuntu ataṉi vāṅkiyavaṉik koṉṟuviṭṭāl, ataṉik kaṭṭiṉavaṉ allatu kaṭṭuvittavaṉ maraṇa taṇṭaṉi peṟṟāṉ. ciṟumi oruttiyi oruvaṉ aṭittuk koṉṟuviṭṭāl avaṉuṭiya ciṟumi avvāṟu kollappaṭṭāḷ. kālattil taṇṭaṉi kaḷukkup patil paṇam naṭṭa īṭākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. tantiyi oruvaṉ aṭittāl avaṉuṭiya kikaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cattira vittiyaṅ kāraṇa māka nōyāḷi iṟantāl allatu avaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ paḻutu aṭin tāl maruttuvaṉiṉ kiviralkaḷ naṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟpaḻittal, peṇ kaḷik kaṭattic cellutal, koḷḷi, tiruṭu, vēṟu kaṇavaṉi maṇat taṟkuk kaṇavaṉip peṇ koli ceytal, peṇkuru matukkaṭikaḷil nuḻital, ōṭippōṉa aṭimiyi vīṭṭil vittiruttal, pōril payantu ōṭutal, vīṭṭup peṇ kavaṉakkuṟivāyiruttal allatu cikkaṉamillātu celavu ceytal pōṉṟa kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. hamurapiyiṉ caṭṭattil kottar, ceṅkal cūḷiyiṭuvōr, tiyaṟkārar, kaṟkottar, taccar ōṭakkāraṉ, iṭiyaṉ, kūliyāḷ mutaliyōriṉ kūlikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tantiyiṉ cottu piḷḷikaḷic camapaṅkāka aṭintatu. kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ peṇ taṉatu maraṇam variyil kuṭumpat taliviyāka iruntāḷ. kurumār eḻuttāḷarāka vēlikaḷ purintaṉar. oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉik kolikkuṟṟañ cāṭṭi ak kuṟṟatti uṟutip paṭuttāviṭṭāl kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiyavaṉ kolittaṇṭaṉi aṭintāṉ. aracaṉuṭiya nītipatikaḷ aṭaṅkiya vicāraṇi capi oṉṟu pāpilōṉ paṭṭiṉattil iruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉē mēl vicāraṇikaḷi naṭattiṉāṉ.
teyvaṅkaḷ
aracaṉuṭiya atikāram caṭṭaṅkaḷālum, atikārikaḷālum, kurumārālum kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu. aracaṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ cārpāḷaṉāka (Agent) iruntāṉ. kaṭavuḷ peyarāl varikaḷ vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. kurumārāl araca atikāram vaḻaṅkappaṭum variyil aracaṉi uṇmiyāṉa aracaṉāka makkaḷ karutavilli. ivvaki atikāram vaḻaṅkum potu kurumār mārṭuk eṉṉum kaṭavuḷi alaṅkarittu ataṉi ūrvalam ceytārkaḷ. appoḻutu aracaṉ kuruvippōl uṭi uṭuttappaṭṭāṉ. cumēriya paṟṟēci eṉṉum kōyiṟ kurumārākiya ciṟṟaracar mutal nepuccaṭṉēcar variyil nāṭu kurumār atikārattuk kuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. talimuṟitōṟum kōyilkaḷiṉ celvam vaḷarntatu. teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tayavip peṟum poruṭṭu aracar kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ; avikaḷukku vēṇṭiya aṭimikaḷiyum, taḷavāṭaṅḷiyum koṭuttārkaḷ; periya nilaṅkaḷi avikaḷukku māṉiyaṅkaḷāka viṭṭārkaḷ. pōrkaḷil ciṟipiṭitta aṭimikaḷiṉ mutaṟ pakuti kōyilukkuk koṭukkap paṭṭatu; koḷḷip poruḷkaḷum avvāṟu koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. pērīntiṉ paḻam, tāṉiyam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ, cila nilaṅkaḷiliruntu kōyilkaḷukkuk kiṭittaṉa. ivvāṟu kiṭikkat tavaṟiyapōtu nilaṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷāl mēṟpārkkap paṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu nilaṅkaḷ kurumāriṉ kikku māṟiṉa. celvarum vaṟiyarum ivvulaka vāḻvil tamatu cemmiyi virumpik kōvil kaḷukku atikap poruḷik koṭuttārkaḷ. kōyiṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷil poṉ, veḷḷi, vayiram, maṇikaḷ pōṉṟa viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷ kuvintaṉa. kurumār ic celvattit tām nērākap payaṉpaṭutta muṭiyā tiruntatu. ākavē avarkaḷ payirttoḻil, kittoḻil naṭattuvō rākavum, vaṭṭikkup paṇam koṭuttu vāṅkuvōrākavu miruntaṉar. avar kaḷiṭattil periya nilaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avikaḷil vēli ceyvataṟku aṭimikaḷum iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṭimikaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷiṭattil vēli ceyyumpaṭi viṭṭuk kūli peṟṟaṉar. kurumār periya vaṇikarāyu miruntaṉar; kōyiliṟ kiṭikkum kāṇikkip poruḷkaḷ kōyililuḷḷa kaṭikaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. palar tamatu uṭamikaḷi avarkaḷiṭam nampikki yiruntatāl koṭuttu vittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutuvōrākavum, vaḻakkukaḷi vicāraṇi ceytu tīrppaḷippōrākavum iruntaṉar.
cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉ kōyiṟ paṇattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ. āṉāl itu āpattāṉa ceyalāka viruntatu. kōyiṟ cottil oru turumputāṉum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟavarkaḷ mītu kurumār koṭiya cāpaṅkaḷip poḻintārkaḷ aracaṉukkup potumakkaḷiṭamuḷḷa atikārattilum mēlākak kurumārukku iruntatu. aracaṉ iṟantupōkiṉṟavaṉ. kaṭavuḷar eppoḻutum iruppavar. kurumār tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟavarkaḷallar. avarkaḷukku varikaḷumilli; pōril kalappatāl nērum tuṉpamum illi. vaṇikar nāṭṭic celvattāl ceḻippaṭiyac ceytārkaḷ. kurumār ataṉi anupavittārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil pala teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiṟittuvukkumuṉ oṉpa tām nūṟṟāṇṭil eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi aṅku 65,000 kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭiṉattilum, kirāmattilum pala teyvaṅkaḷ irun taṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ camāṣ eṉavum, tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ naṉṉar eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru vīṭṭilum vīṭṭut teyvam iruntatu. avi kaḷukkuk kāliyilum māliyilum paliyiṭappaṭṭatu. muṉpu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa mārṭuk ippoḻutu tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ āṉāṉ.
cuvarkkattil tēvarkaḷ māttiram vāḻntārkaḷ. iṟantavar aralu eṉṉum narakattil, kiyum kālum kaṭṭuṇṭu kuḷirāl naṭuṅkikkoṇṭum, paci tākaṅkaḷāl varuntikkoṇṭum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ putalvar camātiyil kālantōṟum uṇavi vittālaṉṟi ivarkaḷukku uṇavu kiṭippatilli. ivvulakil keṭṭa vāḻkki naṭattiyavarkaḷukku atika taṇṭaṉikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ uṭalil kuṭṭa nōyum piṟa nōykaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa.
piṇaṅkaḷ nila aṟikaḷil putikkappaṭṭaṉa; cila koḷuttap paṭṭaṉa. piṇaṅkaḷ tilamiṭṭup pakkuvañ ceyyappaṭavilli. kūlikku aḻuvōr avikaḷik kaḻuvi, vācaṉi pūci, uṭutti, kaṇṇukku mi eḻuti viralkaḷil mōtiramiṭṭu alaṅkarittārkaḷ. peṇ piṇaṅkaḷiṉ pakkattē vācaṉip puṭṭikaḷ, cīppu, mi tīṭṭuṅ kucci, kaṇṇukkut tīṭṭum mi mutaliyaṉa vikkappaṭṭaṉa. nalla muṟiyākap piṇaṅkaḷ putikkap paṭāviṭṭāl iṟantavar iruppavarkaḷukkut tuṉpam viḷivippar; uṇavukkāka alintu tirivar; nāṭṭil koḷḷi nōyi uṇṭākkuvar eṉa eṇṇiṉar. iv vaccaṅ kāraṇamākavē pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ kurumārukkum kaṭavuḷarukkum nalla uṇavukaḷi aḷittu vantārkaḷ.
kaṭavuḷukku uṇavum nīrum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; āṭṭukkuṭṭi pali yiṭappaṭṭatu. āṭṭukkuṭṭi maṉitaṉukkup patil iṭappaṭum pali. kurumār puriyum palavakik kiriyikaḷōṭu paliyum iṭappaṭṭatu. oḻuṅkupaṭṭa vāḻkkiyilum pārkka muṟiyāṉa kiriyiyē mēlāṉatu eṉak koḷ ḷappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉuṭiya kaṭami kōvilkaḷukku muṟiyākap palika ḷiṭṭuc cariyāṉa tutikaḷ pāṭuvatākum. oruvaṉ viḻuntu kiṭakkum etiriyiṉ kaṇkaḷip piṭuṅkiviṭalām; kikaḷi veṭṭi viṭalām; avaṉuṭiya iṟicciyi aṟuttu vaṟukkalām; avikaḷik kuṟittuk kaṭavuḷ veṟuppaṭiya māṭṭār. kurumār kaṭavuḷukku etiril cāmpirāṇip puki iṭṭārkaḷ; kaṭavuḷari aḻakiya uṭikaḷālum aṇikalaṉkaḷālum alaṅkarittārkaḷ; kurumār avarkaḷmītu pala tutikaḷip pāṭiṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil palar oṉṟucērntu niṉṟu pāṭiṉar.
pāpilōṉiya camaya nūlkaḷum tutikaḷum cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kattōlikkak kiṟittuvak kōyilkaḷil ilattiṉ moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatupōla, cumēriya moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tutikaḷ pūtaṅkaḷi ōṭṭiṉa. ivi paḻiya mantira vitti toṭarpāka eḻuntavi. pūtaṅkaḷiliruntu tammip pātukāttuk koḷvataṟkut tāyattukaḷ, mantiraṅkaḷ eḻutiya ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ kaṭavuḷariṉ vaṭivaṅ kaḷ mutaliyaṉa uṭampil aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa; ciṟiya kaṟkaḷ kayiṟṟil allatu caṅkiliyil kōttuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟuppu, veḷḷi, civappu mutaliya kayiṟukaḷ nōkkattukku ēṟṟavāṟu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paricutta māṉa tikiras, yūpirātas āṟukaḷiṉ nīrit teḷippatālum pūtaṅkaḷ vilakiṉa. acur pāṉippāliṉ nūlniliyattil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ ēṭu kaḷil, cōtiṭam, kaṉaviṉ palaṉ, cakuṉaṅkaḷiṉ palaṉ pōṉṟavikaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paliyiṭappaṭṭa vilaṅkiṉ īralip pārttup pāpilōṉiyak kurumār varuṅ kāriyam kūṟiṉārkaḷ. iv vaḻakkam kirēkkak kurumārālum kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. nimittam pārppavaṉāvatu kōviṟ kuruvāvatu cakuṉam pārttup palaṉ kūṟuvataṉ muṉ aracaṉ pōrukkuc cellamāṭṭāṉ.
ilakkiyam
pāpilōṉiya vāṇikam caṭṭa campantamāṉa oḻuṅku peṟṟiruntatu. mempis, tīps nakaraṅkaḷiliruntatu pōlap pāpilōṉil eḻuttāḷar palar iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ īramāṉa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil nuṉi mukkōṇamāka veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻutukōlāl eḻutiṉārkaḷ. eḻutap paṭṭatu tirumukamāyiṉ atu kaḷimaṇ uṟiyil iṭṭu eḻutiyavaṉiṉ muttiri mēlē poṟikkappaṭṭatu. kōyilkaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nūlniliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ cāṭikaḷil aṭukki oḻuṅkupaṭuttit taṭṭukkaḷil niriyāka vikkap paṭṭaṉa. pōrcippā nūl niliyattiliruntu nūlkaḷ paṭiyeṭuttu acur pāṉippāliṉ nūl niliyattil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. acīriyāvil kāṇap paṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷiṉ mūlam pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāḻkkiyip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.
nīṇṭa kālam pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vācikkappaṭāmal iruntaṉa. heṉṟi iraviliṉcaṉ eṉṉum āṅkilar paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷāka muyaṉṟu 1847il av veḻuttukaḷi vācittār. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ cumēriya akkēṭiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ cemittiya muṟiyāṉa vaḷarcciyāl tōṉṟiyavi. toṭakkattil nūlkaḷ cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kālattil am moḻi māṟutalaṭintatu. paḻiya puṉita moḻiyākiya cumēriyattiliruntu nūlkaḷip pāpilōṉiya moḻippaṭuttuvataṟku akarātikaḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. niṉēvā nūl niliyattil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ perumpālum cumēriya, pāpilōṉiya acīriya moḻi akarātikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vakarātikaḷ akkāṭ aracaṉākiya cārkaṉ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭavi.
cumēriyattilum pāpilōṉiyattilum eḻuttukaḷ colliṉ pakuti kaḷāka iruntaṉa. pāpilōṉiyattukkuc contamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷ illi; colliṉ pakutikaḷi uṇartta 300 aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ variyil iruntaṉa. ivvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ mūlam kaṇakku, camayam, vāṇikam pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷ kōyiṟ kurumārāl māṇavarukkup payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aḻipāṭu oṉṟil paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavar pārttu eḻutiya nītivākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷip paṟṟiya kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ, camayak kiriyikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi eḻutappaṭṭa ēṭukaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. aracāṅka varalāṟukaḷ, aracariṉ camayappaṟṟu, avarkaḷiṉ veṟṟikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. perōcas (ki.mu.280) eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya varalāṟṟāciriyar paṭippuk kālam mutal maṉitaṉuṭiya varalāṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāpilōṉiṉ mutal aracaṉ kaṭavuḷāl teriyap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉ periya veḷḷapperukku variyil 36,000 āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉeṉṟum ulakam toṭaṅki 691,200 āṇṭukaḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
oḻukkam
kōyilkaḷil tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar. maṇaṅkaḷ peṟṟōrāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. maṇamakaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantikku vekumati aḷit tāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tanti peṇṇukku av vekumatiyilum pārkka atika peṟumatiyuḷḷa cītaṉam koṭuttāṉ. pāpilōṉiyaṉ oruvaṉ oru peṇṇiyē maṇantāṉ. viyapicārak kuṟṟattōṭu campantappaṭṭa iru pālārum āṟṟil eṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oruvaṉ peṇṇiṉ cītaṉap paṇattit tiruppik koṭuttut tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytukoḷḷalām. kaṇavaṉ koṭumiyāka naṭattukiṉṟāṉ, vicuvāca millātirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉap peṇ attāṭcip paṭuttiṉāl avaḷ kaṇavaṉik tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām.
peṇkaḷ āṭavarippōla veḷiyē ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṭam cottu iruntatu. avarkaḷ ataṉit tām virumpiyavāṟu ceyya urimi peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ cilar vāṇikam purintaṉar; cilar eḻuttāḷarāka iruntaṉar. uyar kuṭumpaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil taṉiyiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟapōtu aṇṇakarum vēliyāḷkaḷum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. tāḻnta vakuppiṉar aṭimikaḷ pōl vāḻntaṉar. ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiya makkaḷi atika nākarikam aṭiyātavarkaḷ eṉak karutiṉar.
kalikaḷ
pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāṇikam, poḻutupōkku, kaṭavuḷ patti mutaliya vikaḷi veḷiyiṭum ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. miṉukkañ ceyyap paṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ, veṇkalam, irumpu, veḷḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nuṭpamāṉa tiyal vēlikaḷ, mirutuvāṉa tuṇikaḷ, aḻakiya cāyam ūṭṭiya uṭikaḷ, kampaḷaṅkaḷ, mējikaḷ, paṭukkikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟavi pāpilōṉiyariṉ nākarikatti viḷakku kiṉṟaṉa. palavaki aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teyva uruvaṅkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. icik karuvikaḷ pala payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kuḻal, yāḻ, vīṇi, mēḷam, kompu, nāṇaṟkuḻal, ekkāḷam, kittāḷam, mattaḷam eṉpaṉa avikaḷuṭ cila. kōyilkaḷilum, araṇmaṉikaḷilum, celvariṉ viruntukaḷilum pāṭakar taṉittum, palar oṉṟu kūṭiyum pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil ōviyam eḻututal taṉik kaliyāka vaḷaravilli. cuvarkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi ekiptiya camātic cuvar kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟavipōla niṟaṅkaḷāl eḻutappaṭavilli. ciṟpak kaliyum naṉṟāka vaḷarcciyaṭiyavilli. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ muka irēkikaḷ perumpālum orē vakiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōriṟ piṭikkap paṭṭavarkaḷik kāṭṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷ orē vakiyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāpilōṉiyak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ tarikku mēlē cila aṭikaḷē uyarn tirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōyilkaḷiyō araṇmaṉikaḷiyō kāṭṭum ōviyaṅ kaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum kiṭikkavilli. vīṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. celvar ceṅkallip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vīṭukaḷukkuc caṉṉalkaḷ irukkavilli. vīṭukaḷiṉ katavukaḷ vītiyi nōkki irukkavilli. uḷ muṟṟatti nōkkiyiruntaṉa. mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku māṭikaḷuṭiya vīṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. kōyil vīṭukaḷippōlavē caturamāka viruntatu. kōyiliṉ mēlē ēṟic celvataṟku caṅkup puripōl paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ amikkappaṭṭa vaḻi veḷiyē iruntatu. itu ik kaṭavuḷiṉ uyaramāṉa vīṭākum. vāṉa ārāyccit toṭarpākavum itu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉ ucciyiliruntu kurumār viṇmīṉkaḷi nōkkiṉārkaḷ. pōrcippā vilirunta ivvakik kōpuram ēḻu maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ovvoru māṭiyum ovvoru kirakattukku uriyatu. ovvoru māṭiyum vevvēṟu niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. kīḻē uḷḷa māṭi caṉikku uriyatu. itu kaṟuppu niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṟku mēluḷḷatu veḷḷikkuriyatu. atu veḷḷiyā yiruntatu. mūṉṟāvatu māṭi viyāḻaṉuk kuriyatu. im māṭi karuñcivappu niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭatu. putaṉukkuriya nālāvatu māṭi nīla niṟamuṭiyatu. aintāvatu pacci kalanta civappu niṟamuṭiyatu. itu cevvāykkuriyatu. āṟāvatu māṭi veḷḷi niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. itu tiṅkaḷukkuriyatu. ēḻāvatu māṭi poṉṉiṟamāṉatu. itu ñāyiṟṟukkuriyatu. ivvāṟu ovvoru māṭiyum vārattiṉ ovvōr nāḷik kuṟippatā yiruntatu.
ceṅkaṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ veḷippuṟaṅkaḷ miṉukkañceytu niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avikaḷil vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, marañceṭikaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷippakkam aḻuttañ ceyyappaṭṭatu aḻakukkāka māttiramaṉṟu, veyyil maḻikaḷāl avi paḻutaṭiyāmal iruppataṟkumākum.
viññāṉam
vāṇikattāl kaṇakku vaḷarcci yaṭintatu. kaṇakku camayattōṭum, vāṉa ārāycciyōṭum toṭarpu peṟṟiruntatu. nītipatikaḷ, aracāṅka nirvākikaḷ, payirttoḻilukkut talivar, varuṅkālam colvōr, vāṉa ārāycciyāḷar ellōrum kurumārākavē yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷē viññāṉam tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇarāyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṟintirunta viññāṉa muṟikaḷik kirēkkar camayat toṭarpākak kiyāṇṭaṉar.
pāpilōṉiyak kaṇitam vaṭṭatti 360 pākikaḷākavum, āṇṭi 360 nāṭkaḷākavum pirittatu. itiliruntē aṟupatākak kaṇakkiṭum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aṟupatākak kaṇakkiṭuvatiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭākak kaṇakkiṭum vaḻakkup piṟkālattil terintatu. nila aḷavippaṟṟiya kaṇakkum pāpilōṉil vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntatu. vāṉa ārāycciyil pāpilōṉiyar pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyar vaṉāntarattuk kūṭākac cellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattukkum kaṭal vaḻiyākac cellum kappal kaḷukkum vaḻikāṭṭuvataṟku vāṉa ārāycci ceyyavilli; makkaḷukku nērum vāyppu, vāyppiṉmikaḷi aṟivataṟku ārāyntārkaḷ. ovvoru kirakamum ovvoru teyvamākavum, ovvoru kirakattiṉ celavum varuṅkāriyatti aṟivippatākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cōtiṭa maṟinta kurumār aracariṭamum, potu makkaḷiṭamu miruntu atika ūtiyam peṟṟaṉar; cārkaṉ kālattil cōtiṭa nūlkaḷ eḻutap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu paḻiya varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. niṟinta cōtiṭap paḻakkamiṉṟi ūtiyattiṟkākac cōtiṭam kūṟukiṉṟavarkaḷip paṟṟik kurumār kuṟi kūṟiṉārkaḷ. nepucaṭṉēcar kālattil kirakaṅkaḷukkum naṭcattiraṅkaḷukku muḷḷa vēṟu pāṭu aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu. pūmi cūriyaṉic cuṟṟivarum pāti paṉṉiraṇṭu vīṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. pāki ‘miṉiṭ’kaḷākavum, ‘miṉiṭ’ noṭi kaḷākavum pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṟupatu miṉiṭ oru pāki eṉavum 60 noṭi allatu ‘cekaṇṭ’ oru miṉiṭ eṉavum kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. nēram nāḻiki vaṭṭam (Water-Clock) cūriya niḻal vaṭṭam (Sun-dial) ākiya ivikaḷāl aḷakkappaṭṭatu.
āṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu cantira mātaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭatu. mutal āṟu mātaṅkaḷ muppatu nāḷum, piṉ āṟu mātaṅkaḷ 29 nāṭkaḷum koṇṭavi. ākavē āṇṭil 354 nāḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. iṭi iṭiyē oru mātam cērttuk kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu. mātattil nāṉku vāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu. cantiraṉ eḻum nērattiliruntu nāḷ kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
maruntum, cōtiṭamum camayat toṭarpu peṟṟiruntaṉa. hamurapi aracaṉ kālattil maruttuvam camayattiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭṭatu. ak kālattil maruttuvar palar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷukkuk koṭukkavēṇṭiya kūliyum, tamatu kaṭamiyic carivarac ceyyāviṭṭāl avarkaḷ aṭiya vēṇṭiya taṇṭaṉi kaḷum caṭṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maruttuvaṉ oruvaṉi vīṭṭukku aḻikkumoruvaṉ avaṉukkuk koṭukka vēṇṭiya kūliyi aṟintiruntāṉ. nōyāḷi vaṟiyavaṉāṉāl kūli kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. maruttuvaṉ muṟiyāka maruttuvam ceyyāviṭṭāl avaṉ nōyāḷikku naṭṭa īṭu ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil maruttuvaṉiṉ kiviralkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. maruttuvarilum pārkka mantira vittikkārarukku atika matippu uṇṭāyiruntatu. nōy pāvattiṉāl uṇṭāvatu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. ākavē atu tuti, mantira vittikaḷāl pōkkappaṭṭatu. maruntukaḷ uṭalic cuttañ ceyvaṉa eṉṟu karutap paṭavilli. uḷḷē irukkum pūtattip payamuṟuttukiṉṟatu eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. mika aruvarukkattakka poruḷkaḷ cērttu aritta maruntukaḷ oḷaṭatamākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇa maruntuc carakkukaḷ pacci iṟicci, pāmpiṉ iṟicci, marattūḷ mutaliyaṉa. ivi cārāyamum eṇṇeyum ūṟṟi arikkappaṭṭaṉa. aḻukiya uṇavu, elumputtūḷ, koḻuppu, aḻukku mutaliyaṉavum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ allatu maṉitaṉiṉ mala mūttiraṅkaḷum cērttu arikkappaṭṭa kūṭṭukaḷ maruntākap payaṉpaṭut tappaṭṭaṉa. pūtattit tiruptippaṭuttum poruṭṭup pāl, tēṉ, veṇṇey, naṟumaṇamuḷḷa mūlikikaḷ uṭkoḷḷumpaṭi koṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. maruntukaḷāl nōy kuṇamaṭiyāviṭiṉ nōyāḷi cantikkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭāṉ. aṅku avvaki nōy aṭintu kuṇap paṭṭavarkaḷ tāmaṟinta maruntukaḷi avaṉukkuk kūṟiṉārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉiṉ viḻuki
pāpilōṉiṉ kaṭici aracaṉ nepucaṭṉēcar. ivaṉ 54 kōyil kaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉ taṉatu vāḻnāḷiṉ iṟuti nāṉku āṇṭukaḷil pittiyam koṇṭiruntāṉ. ki.mu. 562il ivaṉ maraṇamaṭintāṉ. ivaṉ maraṇa maṭinta muppatu āṇṭukaḷuḷ pāpilōṉ irācciyam tuṇṭu tuṇṭākac citaṟuṇṭatu. ivaṉukkup piṉ āṭci naṭattiya pōṉiṭas eṉpavaṉ irācciyattil karuttuc celuttātu cumēriyāviluḷḷa aḻipāṭu kaḷi veṭṭi ārāycci ceyvatil kālattip pōkkiṉāṉ. irāṇuvat til kuḻappa muṇṭāyiṟṟu. vāṇikattilum kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷilum paḻakiya makkaḷ pōri maṟantārkaḷ. kurumār aracāṅka atikārattik kippaṟṟit tamatu pikaḷi nirappiṉar. ippoḻutu ciras eṉṉum pāracīka aracaṉ pāpilōṉi veṟṟikoṇṭāṉ. iraṇṭu talimuṟikaḷākap pāpilōṉ pāracīkar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. piṉpu alakcāntar veṟṟiyāḷaṉākip pāpilōṉi āṇṭāṉ. pāpilōṉiyariṭamiruntē kirēkkarkaḷ, kaṇakku, vāṉacāttiram, maruntu, ilakkaṇam,nikaṇṭu, carittiram, tattuva cāttiram mutaliyavikaḷip peṟṟaṉar. urōmar kirēkkariṭamiruntu ivikaḷip peṟṟaṉar. kirēkka moḻiyil ulōkaṅkaḷ, irācikaḷ, niṟikaḷ, aḷavi kaḷ, icikkaruvikaḷ, maruntuc carakkukaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ perumpālum pāpilōṉiya moḻikkuriyaṉavē. kirēkkak kaṭṭakkali ekiptiya, kirīttiya, pāpilōṉiyak kaṭṭaṭak kalikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi eḻuntatu. cuṟṟi varap paṭikkaṭṭukkaḷuṭiya pāpilōṉiya kōpuraṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi mukamatiya kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amikkap paṭṭaṉa. hamirapiyiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷiliruntu urōmariṉ niyāyac caṭṭaṅkaḷ utittaṉa.
cintuveḷi
cintu eṉpatu intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkiluḷḷa ōr āṟu. ivvāṟṟiṉ peyariyē nāvalantīvu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirunta ematu nāṭṭukku mēṟkut tēca makkaḷ peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṉar. cintu eṉpatu intu eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. cintu āṟṟiṉ camaveḷikaḷil aḻipāṭukaḷ 1 pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷuḷ arappā, mokañcatarō eṉṉum iru iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa aḻipāṭukaḷ paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarāl akaḻap paṭṭaṉa. ivi aḻintu maṇmēṭāka māṟiya iraṇṭu periya nakaraṅkaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. aṅku vāḻnta paḻiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷi aṟintu koḷvataṟkēṟṟa pala paḻam poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cintuveḷip putiporuḷ ārāyccikku muṉpu airōppiya amerikka ārāycciyāḷar, yūpirātas tikiras āṟukaḷum nīla natiyum nīr pāyccukiṉṟa mecapatōmiyā, acīriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷē nākarikattiṉ toṭṭil eṉak karuti vantārkaḷ. an nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ paḻamiyi oppiṭumpōtu intiya nākarikam pacciḷaṅkuḻavi eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. ākavē, intiya varalāṟṟi alakcāntariṉ paṭi eṭuppu allatu muriya araca parampari ārampattiliruntu toṭaṅkuvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntu vantatu. cintuveḷiyilē arappā, mokañcatarō eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷ kaḷ, intiya nākarikattiṉ paḻamiyi mēṟku āciyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ toṉmiyōṭu ottatāka māṟṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷip putiporuḷ ārāycci yiṟ kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷ pala cumēriyāviṟ kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷi ottavi. ākavē cintu nākarikam eṉpatu intu-cumēriya nākarikam eṉak koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. mēlum kiṭitta paḻamporuḷkaḷ cintu nākarikam intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta paḻiya nākarikam eṉpatik kāṭṭiṉa. cintu veḷi nākarikattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3,500 variyil eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.
arappā eṉṉum paḻiya aḻipāṭu pañcāp mākāṇattil iravi āṟṟukku aṇmiyiluḷḷatu. iravi āṟu cintu natiyiṉ oru kiḷi. arappā, mokañcatarōvukku vaṭakkē nāṉūṟu mil tūrattiluḷḷatu. arappā aḻipāṭu 1920kkum 1921kku miṭiyil mutaṉmutal akaḻntu pārkkappaṭṭatu.
mokañcatarō ilārkāṉāvukku vaṭakkē irupattintu mil tūrattil uḷḷatu. mokañcatarō eṉpataṟku iṟantavarkaḷiṉ mēṭu eṉṟu poruḷ. ivaḻipāṭu 240 ēkkar nilap parappik koṇṭirukkiṟatu. itil tōṇṭi ārāyappaṭṭa pakuti 13 ēkkarkaḷē. cintu āṟu mokañcatarō vukkuk kiḻakkē mūṉṟari mil tūram taḷḷi ōṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil atu mokañcatarōviṉ pakkattil ceṉṟirukkalām. in nakar muṉpu āṟṟu maṭṭattiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. cintu āṟu kālantōṟum uyarntu vantamiyāl makkaḷ nakaratti uyarttip putiya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷi amit tārkaḷ. ivvāṟu oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa ēḻu nakaraṅkaḷ mokañcatarōviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēḻāvatu paṭi kaṟkāla iṟutiyilum ulōka kāla ārampattilum amikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷi uṭiyatu.
vītikaḷ: mokañcatarōvil akaṉṟa periya vītikaḷ vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkē celkiṉṟaṉa. avi oṉṟukkoṉṟu cama tūrattiluḷḷaṉa. periya vītikaḷiliruntu ciṟiya vītikaḷ kiḷiviṭṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa. kiḷi vītikaḷ ivvāṟu celvatāl nakar pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru pirivilum oṭuṅkiya vītikaḷ pala celkiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷiṉ ōraṅkaḷil vīṭukaḷ neruṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru ciṟiya vītiyilum potukkiṇaṟu uṇṭu. perumpālum ovvoru vīṭṭukkum kiṇaṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
vīṭukaḷ: vīṭukaḷ pala perumiyuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu aṟikaḷukkuk kuṟivāyuḷḷa vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. cila vīṭukaḷ araṇmaṉikaḷpōl periyaṉa. vīṭukaḷ vicālamākavum kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭa kuḷikkum aṟiyuṭiyaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāpilōṉilum mokañcatarōvilum naṭuvil muṟṟamum muṟṟattic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟamum aṟikaḷumāka vīṭukaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṟṟattukkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēṉmāṭi puḻaṅkavum, nittiri koḷḷavum, kuḷikkavum, kīḻmāṭi camikkavum, paṇṭaṅkaḷic cēmittu vikkavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vītiyiliruntu vīṭṭukkuc cella oru vāyil māttiram iruntatu. cuvarukku veḷiyē yātum irukkavilli.
kiṇaṟukaḷ: kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ akalam palavāṟu iruntatu. cila kiṇaṟu kaḷiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ēḻu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. mikac ciṟiya kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ōr aṭi ōr aṅkulam. cātāraṇa kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu iraṇṭu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. periya nīrāṭum kēṇiyiṉ pakkattē kāṇappaṭum kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu āṟu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. perum pālum ovvoru vīṭṭukkum ovvoru kiṇaṟu iruntatu. potuk kiṇaṟukaḷ iraṇṭu vīṭukaḷiṉ mattiyil iruntaṉa. kiṇaṟṟic cuṟṟic ceṅkaṟkaḷ patikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ceṅkaṟkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērttup piṭikkac ceyvataṟkuk kaḷimaṇ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kiṇaṟṟuk kaṭṭu ōraṅkaḷil kayiṟu urāynta aṭiyāḷam kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ēṉaṅ kaḷik kayiṟṟil kaṭṭik kiyiṉāl iḻuttut taṇṇīr eṭukkap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
kuḷikkum aṟi: ovvoru vīṭṭukkum taṉiyākak kuḷikkum aṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. avikaḷic cuṟṟip patikkappaṭṭirunta ceṅkaṟkaḷ mītu taṇṇīr cuvaṟic cellamuṭiyāmal mēlē oruvakik kaṟuppup poruḷ pūcap paṭṭiruntatu. kuḷikkumaṟi vīṭṭiṉ vāyiṟ pakkamāka viruntatu. ataṉic cuṟṟic ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭa tari oru mūliyi nōkkic carivāka iruntatu. mēl vīṭṭil kuḷikkum aṟi iruntateṉpati mēlē yiruntu kīḻē ceṅkuttākac cellum kuḻāykaḷik koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭṭatu.
tari: vīṭṭiṉ tari ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭiruntatu; allatu maṇ iṭṭu aṭittu maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. maṇ iṭṭu aṭittut tariyi maṭṭañ ceyvatu potu nikaḻcci. cila vīṭukaḷukkuc cūḷiyiṭāta kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
katavukaḷ: katavukaḷ marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavi. cuvar muṭivu eytu miṭattuk katavu vikkappaṭṭiruntatu; atu cuvariṉ mattiyil vikkappaṭa villi. katavu niliyiṉ akalam mūṉṟu aṭi mūṉṟu aṅkulam mutal mūṉṟu aṭi pattu aṅkulam variyil iruntatu.
caṉṉalkaḷ: cātāraṇa vīṭukaḷukkup perumpālum veḷiccuvar kaḷil caṉṉalkaḷ irukkavilli. caṉṉalkaḷ cuvaril viṭappaṭṭa veḷikaḷpōla iruntaṉa. periya nīrāṭum kēṇiyic cuṟṟi amikkap paṭṭa cuvaril caṉṉalkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ: perumpālum ellā vīṭukaḷukkum mēlē ēṟic cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uṇṭu. avi cuvariṉ etirē allatu iraṇṭu vīṭukaḷukku iṭiyē uḷḷa iṭiveḷiyil ceṅkuttāka amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ kūrikku māttiramaṉṟu; mēlmāṭikkum cel kiṉṟaṉa. vītiyiliruntu mēl māṭikkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvakip paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uḷḷa vīṭukaḷil vevvēṟu kuṭumpattiṉar vāḻntārkaḷ ākalām. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ oṭuṅkiyaviyāka vum, paṭi ovvoṉṟum patiṉoru aṅkula uyaramum āṟu mutal eṭṭu aṅkula akalamu muṭiyatāka uḷḷatu.
kūri: kūrikaḷ taṭṭiyāṉavi. avi marattiṉāl amikkap paṭṭavi. cila vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil maraṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭa tuvāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maraṅkaḷmītu palakikaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. vaṟiya makkaḷ maraṅkaḷ mītu nāṇaṟpāyi virittu ataṉmēl marattūḷum umiyum kalantu kuḻitta kaḷimaṇṇi iṭṭu meḻuki maṭṭañ ceytārkaḷ.
cuvarkaḷ: vīṭṭuc cuvar ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akalamāṉa cuvarkaḷiṉ veḷippuṟattukkum uṭpuṟattukkum iṭiyē kaḷimaṇ iṭṭu nirappappaṭṭatu. veḷiccuvarkaḷ uḷḷē carintu cellumpaṭi amikkap paṭṭaṉa.
katavillāta aṟikaḷ, malakkūṭaṅkaḷ: katavillāta cila aṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi nilattukkuk kīḻ uḷḷa aṟikaḷāka allatu malak kūṭaṅkaḷāka irukkalām. iraṇṭu malak kūṭaṅkaḷit tavira vēṟu malak kūṭaṅkaḷ ituvari kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavilli.
kaḻivu nīr cellum kālvāy: cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷil kaḻivunīr veḷiyē celvataṟkuk kaṟpatitta vāykkālkaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. maṟunāṭukaḷil viḷaṅkiya ak kāla nakaraṅkaḷil ivi kāṇappaṭavilli. ellā vīṭukaḷukkum vītip pakkamuḷḷa cuvaril oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu tuvāraṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivi vaḻiyāka aḻukku nīr veḷiyē yuḷḷa kālvāyiṟ ceṉṟu viḻuntatu. aḻukku nīr viḻumiṭattil pāramāṉa poruḷkaḷ taṅki niṟkumpaṭi kaṟpatitta kuḻikaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ovvoru vītiyilum 18 aṅkulam mutal 2 aṅkulam vari āḻamuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mēlē ceṅkaṟkaḷ iṭṭu ivi mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic ceṅkaṟkaḷ vēṇṭiyapōtu eṭukkakkūṭiyaṉa. ciṟiya kālvāykaḷil cellum kaḻivu nīr periya vāykkāliṟ ceṉṟu viḻuntatu. iv vāykkālkaḷum aḻukkut taṅki niṟkum kuḻikaḷum iṭi yiṭi cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vāykkālkaḷ cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭa iṭattil maṇal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mokañcatarōk kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum tūṇkaḷ caturamāṉavi. cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaṭa amippup pāpilōṉiya allatu ekiptiya kaṭṭaṭa amippiviṭa atikam vaḷarcci yaṭintiruntatu eṉa ṭākṭar pirāṅpōrṭ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
periya tāṉiyak kaḷañciyamum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum: arappāvil 169 aṭi nīḷamum, 135 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa tāṉiyak kaḷañciyamoṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu varicikaḷil taṉittaṉi ovvoṉṟilum ēḻu vīṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya patiṉāṉku kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv viraṇṭu nirikaḷiyum oru pāti pirikkiṉṟatu. ip pātiyi āṟu pātikaḷ kuṟukkē pirikkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ovvoru kaṭṭaṭattiyum cuṟṟi veḷi irukkiṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭuk kum oru muṟṟamum iraṇṭu aṟi kaḷum uṇṭu. ciṟiya aṟi vāyiṟ pakkattilum periya aṟi muṟṟattukkup piṉṉālum uḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkut tūṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl vītikaḷukku veḷiccam iṭap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. vītikaḷukku appuṟattil kāṇappaṭum aḻukkuk kuviyalkaḷāl nakar naṉku cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil aḻukkukkup patil caṭṭi pāṉi uṭivukaḷum aḻukiya ili taḻikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟpam: uruvaṅkaḷ mirutuvāṉa kaṟkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa maṉita uruvaṅkaḷil oṉṟu, alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pōrviyi iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl vantu valatu puyattukkuk kīḻē cellumpaṭi aṇintirukkiṉṟatu. ataṟkuk kuṟukiya tāṭiyum kaṉṉa mīci yum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tali mayir naṭuvē vakirntu viṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. taliyic cuṟṟi nāṭā oṉṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cintuveḷiyiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭavikaḷuḷ muttirikaḷ, uṭampil aṇiyum poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavi mutaṉmiyuṭiyaṉa. ciṟiya uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kālkaḷum kikaḷum uṭintuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ pala peṇ uruvaṅkaḷākum. ariyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa oṭukkamāṉa tuṇiyillāviṭil avi nirvāṇat tōṟṟam aḷippaṉavākum. perumpālum ellā vaṭivaṅkaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintuḷḷaṉa. aṇil, kuraṅku mutaliyavi niṟak kaṟkaḷilum āmi ōṭṭilum veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. niṟakkallil veṭṭappaṭṭa aṇil iraṇṭu aṅkula uyaramuḷḷatu. aṇiliṉ vāl nimirntu niṟkiṉṟatu. atu muṉṉaṅ kālkaḷ iraṇṭiṉālum etiyō piṭittirukkiṉṟatu.
veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭṭiyap peṇṇiṉvaṭivam oṉṟu mikavum kavarccikkuriyatā yuḷḷatu. avaḷ ki mūṭṭuvariyum vaḷiyaṅkaḷi aṇintirukkiṟāḷ. nīlaniṟak kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṉita vaṭivam aṭṭaṇik kāliṭṭukkoṇṭu kaṭṭiliṉ mītu iruk kiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷilum ovvoruvar muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. piṉpuṟattil oru pāmpu uḷḷatu. iṉṟu cintuviṟ kāṇappaṭum makkaḷ mokañcatarō makkaḷiviṭa vēṟāṉavarkaḷallar eṉac car ārtar kīt kūṟiyuḷḷār.
āṭavar iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl vantu iṭatu puyattukkuk kīḻē cellumpaṭi pōrvi aṇintiruntārkaḷ. ivvaḻakkē nāḷaṭivil pūṇūl aṇiyum vaḻakkāka māṟiṟṟu. iṉṟu uṭukkum ‘vēṭṭi’ pōṉṟa uṭiyi āṭavar ariyil aṇintaṉar. pañciṉālum kampaḷiyiṉālum uṭikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ūcikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ tikkap paṭṭa uṭikaḷiyum aṇintārkaḷ ākalām.
āṭavar taliyil mayiri vaḷaraviṭṭuk kuṟuka veṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. cilar mayirip piṉpuṟattil kuṭumiyāka muṭintu nāṭāviṉāṟ kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. iṉṟu maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ muṭivatupōl mayir ucciyil muṭiyap paṭṭatu. cila uruvaṅkaḷiṉ mayir piṉpuṟamākat toṅkukiṉṟatu. āṭavar mēlutaṭukaḷiṉ mayiri maḻittārkaḷ allatu mayiri oṭṭa veṭṭi ṉārkaḷ.
āparaṇaṅkaḷ: āṭavarum makaḷirum āparaṇaṅkaḷ aṇin tārkaḷ. kaḻuttaṇi, talikkuk kaṭṭum nāṭā, mōtiram, puyavaṇi mutaliyavi āṇka ḷālum peṇkaḷālum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kālaṇi, kātu vaḷiyaṅkaḷ, tōṭu, alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa ūcikaḷ, mūkku vaḷiyam mutaliyavikaḷip peṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. celvar poṉ, veḷḷi, niṟakkaṟkaḷ, tantam, viliyuyarnta kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ceyta āparaṇaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ōṭu, elumpu, cempu, cūḷiyiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nakikaḷ vaṟiya makkaḷāl payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ ari yil aṇiyum paṭṭikikaḷ maṇi kōtta vaṭaṅkaḷāl ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. avikaḷiṉ mukappuk kaḷ poṉ, cempu mutaliyavi kaḷālāṉavi. maṇikaḷik kōttu mālikaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. vayirak kaṟkaḷiyum maṇikaḷiyum tuḷiyiṭuvataṟkup pōtiya tiṟami vēṇṭum. cempil ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭṭiyap peṇ kimuṭṭa vaḷikaḷ aṇintirukkiṉṟāḷ. kivaḷikaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, niṟak kaṟkaḷ, cūḷi yiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aḻakiya kivēli ceyyappaṭṭa niṟakkaṟkaḷum talimuṭikkuc cerukum palavaki ūcikaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi veṇkalam, cempu, elumpu, niṟakkaṟkaḷ, cuṇṇāmpukkal mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi. talikkuc cerukum ūciyil mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatākiya kāṭci kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷ: cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil kiṭitta mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷ pala kiṣ, ūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭittavi pōṉṟavi. mēṉimiṉukkup poruḷkaḷ vikkum peṭṭikaḷ marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. niṟakkaṟkaḷiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa vum nāṉku aṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉavumākiya peṭṭi kaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakip pēḻikaḷ kirēttāvil māttiram kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. atu nīṇṭa vaṭṭa vaṭivuṭiyatu. kaṇṇukku mitīṭṭum kuccikaḷ veṇkalattiṉālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉit tantattil ceyyappaṭṭa cīppukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mayir maḻikkum kattikaḷ cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ: kal, ōṭu, niṟakkal, tantam, ulōkam mutaliyavikaḷāl vīṭṭupporuḷkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kal āyutaṅ kaḷukkup patil ulōka āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. niṟakkal, vēlippāṭuṭiya ēṉaṅkaḷ ceytaṟku māttiram payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭu ēṉaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṭukkaḷi yil payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉaṅkaḷ, kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, taṭṭukkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ mutaliya palavakiyiṉa. nīr aruntum ēṉaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippākam muṉiyuṭiyatu. nīr uṇṇum ēṉaṅkaḷ pala, kiṇaṟṟic cuṟṟi uṭintu kiṭappatāl avi oru muṟi māttiram payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ūciyum, tuḷiyiṭum karuvikaḷum cempiṉālum tantattiṉālum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. kōṭari, arivāḷ, katti, mīṉ piṭikkum tūṇṭil, kuṭiyum karuvikaḷ pōlvaṉa veṇkalattiṉālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. valikaḷi nīril āḻttuvataṟku īyat tuṇṭukaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṭukkikaḷum cāyvu nāṟkālikaḷum muṉ aṟiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. cila nāṟkālikaḷiṉ pātaṅkaḷ nāḻiki vaṭṭam pōlavum, iṭapattiṉ kāl pōlavum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. iṭapakkāl uḷḷa nāṟkālikaḷ ekiptiya mutal aracaparamparik kālam mutalākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viḷakkukaḷ, ōṭu, maṇ, cempu mutaliyavikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vītikaḷilum viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa. meḻukut tiri vittu erikkun tāḷ oṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu.
vēḷāṇmi: kōtumiyum, vāṟkōtumiyum, nellum viḷi vikkappaṭṭaṉa. maṇveṭṭikkup patil kalappi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatō eṉpatu aṟiya muṭiyavilli. pērīntiṉpaḻa vitikaḷ mokañcatarōvilum arappāvilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl aṅkup pērīntu vaḷarntateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
uṇavu: kōtumi, vāṟkōtumi, arici, kāykaṉikaḷ, pāl mutaliyaṉa uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. arappāvil payaṟum eḷḷum payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa māṭu, āṭu, paṉṟi kōḻi, āmi mutaliyavi kaḷiṉ iṟicciyum, mīṉ, mīṉ-karuvāṭu, kaṭal uyirkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum uṇavākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ: māṭu, erumi, āṭu, yāṉi, paṉṟi, oṭṭakam, nāy mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. maṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa erumi, kāṇṭāmirukam, puli, kuraṅku, karaṭi, muyal, aṇil mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl akkāla makkaḷ ivikaḷip paṟṟi aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaḻuti, pūṉi, kīri, mayil, kōḻi mutaliyaṉavum avarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ: caturaṅkam, kōli mutaliyavi mukkiya viḷiyāṭṭukaḷākum. caturaṅkam āṭum kāykaḷ pala kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi kaḷimaṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi.
viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ: maṇṇiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa maṉitar, vilaṅkukaḷ, taliyāṭṭum vilaṅkukaḷ, kōḻi, puṟā vaṭivāṉa ūtu kuḻalkaḷ, kilukiluppi, ciṟuvar viḷiyāṭum pāṉi caṭṭi, iraṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷil vikkappaṭṭuk kayiṟu kōttu iḻuppataṟkuk kaḻuttil tuḷi iṭappaṭṭa āṭṭuk kaṭā, viḷiyāṭṭu vaṇṭi mutaliyavi viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷiṟ cila. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭa viḷiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ ki.mu. 3,200il ūr eṉṉum mēṟku āciya nakarilē kalliṟ poṟikkap paṭṭa tēr pōṉṟaṉa. ivi uḷ nāṭṭilēyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu caṅku tarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyap paṭukiṉṟatu.
pōkkuvarattu: paḻiya cintuveḷi makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya vaṇṭikaḷ iṉṟu pañcāppilum cintuvilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṇṭikaḷi ottavi. arappāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa cempiṉāṟ ceyta vaṇṭiyiṉāl akkāla vaṇṭi, veyyiliyum maḻiyiyum tāṅka mēlē mēṟkaṭṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṉṟiya ‘ekkā’ vaṇṭi pōṉṟateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
niṟiyum aḷavum: cintuveḷi makkaḷ pala vakiyāṉa niṟikaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉar. periya niṟikaḷ kūriya tali uṭiyaṉavāyum kayiṟu kōkkak kūṭiya tuḷiyuṭiyaṉavāyum iruntaṉa. ciṟiya niṟikaḷ iruṇṭa nari niṟak kaṟkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi. avi ‘pīppā’1 vaṭivāṉavi. ellam, mecapetōmiyā niṟikaḷum ivvakiyiṉavē.
necavu: mokañcatarō vīṭukaḷil nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. pañcu, kampaḷikaḷil nūl nūṟṟal potu nikaḻcciyāka iruntatu. vili ēṟappeṟṟa niṟakkaṟkaḷilum ceyyappaṭṭa nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl celvarum vaṟiyarum nūl nūṟṟārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. āṭikaḷukkup palavakic cāyaṅkaḷ ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
pōr āyutaṅkaḷ: kōṭari, īṭṭi, vēl, vil, ampu, taṇṭu, kavaṇ, vāḷ, kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa pōril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa: kōṭarikaḷ cūcāviṟ kiṭittavi pōṉṟaṉa. vāḷkaḷ cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi. avikaḷukku iru pakkaṅkaḷilum alaku uṇṭu. ivi ak kālattil ekiptil kāṇappaṭṭa avvakip poruḷkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkat takuntavi. meyuṟi allatu kavacam tōliṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu.
maruntu: cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil cilācattu eṉṉum maruntu kāṇappaṭṭatu. itu nīriḻivu, paciyiṉmi, vātam, īraṟ kōḷāṟu mutaliyavi kaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum maruntu. maruntiṉ poruṭṭu māṉ kompum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. kāṇṭāmirukattiṉ kompum maruntiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mantiravātam, tāyattup pōṉṟavi nōyi āṟṟattakkaṉa eṉṟu makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.
vāṇikam: vaṭa intiyāvukkum teṉṉintiyāvukkum mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷukkum toṭarpu uṇṭāyiruntatu. cumēriyāvōṭu cintu nāṭu toṭarpu peṟṟiruntatik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ mikap pala uḷḷaṉa. ekiptōṭum kirēttā (Crete)vōṭum vāṇikam naṭipeṟṟatu. muttiri oṉṟil marakkalam kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. paḻiya mīṉōva muttiri, cumēriya uruḷi vaṭivāṉa muttiri, ekiptiya araca paramparikku muṟpaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉpavikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatupōl im marakkalam mēlē uyarntu vaḷinta muṉpiṉ puṟaṅkaḷi yuṭiyatu. itaṟkup pāymara milli. naṭuvē ōr aṟi irukkiṟatu. marakkalam celuttuvōṉ cukkāṉukkup pakkattē irukkiṉṟāṉ. mokañcatarō, ūr, kiṣ, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkiṭiyil vāṇika uṟavu iruntatu.
kittoḻil: cintuveḷi makkaḷ ellā vaki maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷiyum ceytārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟukku niṟam pūcappaṭṭaṉa; cilavaṟṟil miṉukkam koṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. mēlē kumiḻiyuḷḷa muttirikaḷ, pāvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa aḻakākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, vili uyarnta kaṟkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa āparaṇa vakikaḷ akkālap poṟkollaṉiṉ kivēlit tiṟamiyik kāṭṭuvaṉa. muttirikaḷiṟ cetukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ, puli, yāṉi, māṭu, maram, ceṭi mutaliyavi akkālac ciṟpaṉiṉ kiviṉi māṭciyi veḷiyiṭuvaṉa. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu kotta ruṭiyavum, āṭiyik koṇṭu necavāḷaruṭiyavum tiṟamikaḷi aṟital amiyum.
makkaḷ: āriyar varukikkumuṉ vaṭanāṭu muḻumiyum tirāviṭa makkaḷē vāḻntārkaḷ eṉap peṟappaṭukiṉṟamiyālum, iṉṟum palucis tāṉattil vaḻaṅkum pirākūy moḻi tirāviṭattukku iṉamuṭiyatāyk kāṇappaṭutalālum, piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarēyāvar eṉat tuṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. heras pātiriyār oli muṟiyāka vācitta paḻiya cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ oli muṟiyāṉa uccarippukaḷil perumpālāṉavi tamiḻc coṟkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.
moḻi: cintuveḷi makkaḷ oru collik kuṟikka oru kuṟiyīṭṭi vaḻaṅkiṉar. cīṉamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukaḷ ivvakiyiṉavē. mēṟku āciya, ekiptiya, ciṉṉa āciya, cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ orē aṭippaṭiyi ṉiṉṟum ciṟcila vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaḷarcci yaṭintuḷḷavikaḷē eṉac cēr yāṉ mārcal, ṭākṭar pirāṅpōrṭ, heras pātiriyār mutali yōrum vēṟu palarum ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. teṉṉamerikkāvukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa īsṭar tīvukaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷil 150 eḻuttukaḷ variyil mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟu vaṭa intiya teṉṉintiya eḻuttukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. piṉīciya eḻuttukkum pirāmi eḻuttukkum uṟavu uṇṭu. piṉīciya eḻuttiliruntu kirēkka eḻuttukaḷum, kirēkka eḻuttukaḷi liruntu urōmaṉ eḻuttukaḷum, urōmaṉ eḻuttukaḷiliruntu airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷi ottavi hitarāpāttilum tirunelvēliyilum kiṭitta carittira kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa pāṉi caṭṭikaḷilum, ilaṅkiyilum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
“civappuk kallil veṭṭappaṭṭatum tiṟamiyāṉa vēlippāṭu uṭiyatumāṉa ciṟuvaṉiṉ uruvam oṉṟu arappāviṟ kiṭittatu. ataṉi nōkkumiṭattu atu kirēkkar ki.mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ceyta ki vēlippāṭu pōlat tōṉṟum; āṉāl atu ki.mu. 3,000 variyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. iv vuruvattik koṇṭu aṟiyavaruvatu, ciṟpak kaliyiṉ vaḷarcciyic carittira mūlam aṟiya muṭiyāteṉpatēyākum. ivvulakam muḻumiyilum intiyarē mikap paḻiya ciṟpikaḷāvar eṉat teriyavarukiṉṟatu.”1
* * *
“mokañcatarōvil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa oru muttiriyil veṭṭappaṭṭa iṭapattiṉ vaṭivam mikavum uyirppu uṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉik kāṇum oruvar ciṟpakkali vaḷarvatilli; āṉāl atu taṉatu pōkki māṟṟik koḷkiṉṟatu eṉat taṉakkuḷḷē muṭivu ceytu koḷvar.”2
* * *
mokañcatarōp putiporuḷ ārāycci, puttar piṟappataṟku muṟpaṭṭa kāla nilimikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. intiya aracāṅkat tāriṉ cācaṉap paricōtakarāyirunta hiranta cāttiri kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: mokañcatarō nakaril vāḻnta makkaḷ naṉku amitta vicāla māṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭattakkavi, varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa ekiptilō mecapetōmiyāvilō vēṟu evviṭattilō irukka villi. aṅkuḷḷa kuḷikkum aṟikaḷum, kaḻivu nīr ōṭum vāykkāl kaḷum aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ ulaka nākarika makkaḷāl māttiram aṟiyap paṭum vāyppukaḷiyum, ullāca vāḻkkiyiyum uṭiyarāyiruntār kaḷeṉpati aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. itu. ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa nikaḻcci.”3

tirāviṭa intiyā
muṉṉuri
tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi nūṟu vakiyil virittu eḻutalām. avvāṟu nūlkaḷ veḷivariṉ potumakkaḷum māṇavarum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ uṇmi varalāṟukaḷi naṉkukaṟṟu uṇmi aṟivar. iṉṟu paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷukku eṉa eḻutappaṭum varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭuva tirāviṭa makkaḷip paṟṟiya uṇmic ceytikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭuvatilli. tirāviṭa intiyā eṉṉum innūl paṟpala ārāycci aṟiñar ārāyntu kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷi ātāramākak koṇṭu kaṟpōr eḷitiṟ paṭittup poruḷ viḷaṅkum muṟiyil amintuḷḷatu. tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālam iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ uḷḷatu. caṅkakālam tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālattiṉ kaṭikkālamāka virunta teṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. muṉpiṉ mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷ variyil tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālam nilaviṟṟu. intiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikameṉpatu tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ nākarikamē. ivvuṇmiyi iṉṟu varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷ eṭuttukkūṟa muṟpaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi, paṇṭitar cavarirāya piḷḷi kālam mutal aṟiyappaṭṭirunta paṟpala uṇmikaḷ potumakkaḷiṭiyē paravātum parappuvāraṟṟum kiṭantu iṉṟu paravuvatu ōr naṟkālattiṉ aṟikuṟiyē yākum.
ceṉṉi
1-4-1949 na.ci. kantiyā.
tirāviṭa intiyā
tōṟṟuvāy
intiya makkaḷ varalāṟṟil tirāviṭa makkaḷ varalāṟu mika mutaṉmi yuṭiyatu. iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉap peyar peṟuvatu āriya tirāviṭa nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ kalappiṉāl tōṉṟiyatu. āriya nākarikattukku muṟpaṭṭatu tirāviṭa nākarikam. itiṟ ciṟitum aiyappāṭu illi. nīṇṭa kālam tirāviṭa nākarikattippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭātu kiṭantaṉa; ataṉāl tirāviṭariṉ paṇpāṭṭik kuṟitta ceytikaḷ varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷāl paṟpalavāṟu tirittuk kūṟappaṭalāyiṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻariṉ nākarikattip pulappaṭuttum cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. icciṟiya nūl tirāviṭariṉ nākarikac ciṟappukkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
tirāviṭar piṟappiṭam
“tirāviṭa makkaḷ ayalnāṭukaḷiliruntu intiyāviṉ vaṭapakuti kaḷi aṭintārkaḷ. piṉpu āriyar eṉṉum putiya cātiyiṉar aṅku vantaṉar. avarkaḷ tirāviṭari veṉṟu teṟkē turattiṉar. avarkaḷ teṟkē vantu vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷē tamiḻar eṉappaṭuvōr.” eṉa mēlnāṭṭāciriyar cilar, pala āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ uṇmi aṟiyātu mayaṅki eḻutivittaṉar. akkoḷki iṉṟu ciṟitum ēṟṟuk koḷḷat takkatāyirukkavilli. paḷḷic ciṟuvarkaḷukku varalāṟu eḻutum āciriyar palar uṇmiyallāta paḻiya koḷkiyip piṉpaṟṟi eḻutivaruvatu varuntattakkatē yākum.
tirāviṭa makkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷiliruntu vantu intiya nāṭṭi aṭintārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkēṟṟa ātāram oṉṟēṉum kāṇappaṭa villi. paḻiya kaṟkālam mutal irumpukkālam variyil makkaḷ toṭarpākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷum piṟaporuḷkaḷum intiya nāṭṭiṉ palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
tirāviṭa makkaḷ ayalnāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrkkut tuṇiyāyiruppatu āpkāṉistāṉattiṉ oru pakutiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭiya pirākūy moḻi vaḻaṅkuvatu. iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ cintu āṟṟu veḷikaḷil tirāviṭar nākarikam paraviyiruntatu. aṅku tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. immakkaḷiṉ toṭarpuṭiya varkaḷē āpkāṉistāṉattil taṅki vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉarē tirāviṭattiṉ campantamuṭiya pirākūy moḻiyi iṉṟum pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar.1
tirāviṭa makkaḷ paḻamitoṭṭē intiya nāṭṭil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ paḻiya nāṭu kumari muṉikkut teṟkilum parantu kiṭantatu.
nākarikattiṉ paḻami
mēṟku āciyā ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil paḻiya iṭipāṭṭu mēṭukaḷ tōṇṭi ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅku kiṭitta paḻam poruḷ kaḷikkoṇṭu an nāṭukaḷiṉ nākarikam ēḻāyiram allatu eṇṇā yiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭateṉa ārāycciyāḷar tuṇintuḷḷaṉar. micūrilē ciṟṟālrak eṉṉum iṭattilum cintuveḷiyilum ceyyap paṭṭa akaḻ ārāyccikaḷāl intiya nāṭṭiṉ nākarikamum annāṭṭu nākarikaṅkaḷi otta paḻamiyuṭiyateṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ekiptu, mēṟku āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil nākarikattit tōṟṟuvittōr ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟavarkaḷāvar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ intiya nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟum, ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉak koḷvataṟkuppala ētukkaḷ uḷḷaveṉṟum ciṟanta mēṟtici ārāycci aṟiñar palar pukaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ.2
tamiḻum tirāviṭamum
iṉṟu tamiḻarikkuṟikkat tirāviṭar eṉṉum peyarum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. tirāviṭam eṉṉum collip paṟṟippalar palavāṟu ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tirāviṭameṉpatu tamiḻ eṉṉum colliṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu eṉpatē ārāycci aṟiñar iṟutiyākak koṇṭa muṭivu. iṉṟu tirāviṭam eṉṉum col tamiḻ, teluṅku, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu mutaliya moḻik kūṭṭattik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tirāviṭar eṉṉum peyar im moḻik kūṭṭattukkuriya makkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.1
cūḻalnilikkēṟpa makkaḷ paṇpāṭu aṭital
ulakil makkaḷ vāḻakkūṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ aintu vakiyiṉa eṉṟu paḻantamiḻar kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ avvaki nilaṅkaḷit tiṇikaḷ eṉṟaṉar. tiṇi eṉpatu tiṭ allatu tiṇ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. nilapparappu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. tiṭ allatu tiṇ eṉpatiliruntē tiṭṭu, tiṭṭi, tiṭar, tiṇṇi mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantaṉa. tiṇ eṉpatu valimiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum. iṭaṅkaḷiṉ cūḻnilikkēṟpavē makkaḷiṉ tiruttamum vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. taṇṇīrillāta vaṟaṇṭa maṇaṟ pakuti pāli eṉavum, malināṭu kuṟiñci eṉavum, malināṭṭukkum kīḻnāṭṭuk kum iṭippaṭṭa kuṟuṅkāṭṭu nilam mulliyeṉavum, āṟṟōraṅkaḷ marutameṉavum, kaṭaṟkari neytal eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivvaki nilaṅ kaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil ōraḷavu uṇṭu. makkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālam mutal tamiḻar teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntuvarukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ oru nilattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru nilattukkuc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāṉa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷi aṭintaṉar.
maliyum malicārnta nilamum
makkaḷ mutalmutalkuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta iṭam mali. mikamika nīṇṭa kālam maḻiyiṉālum paruvakkāṟṟiṉālum tākkuṇṭatāl tēyntu pōṉa ciṟiya kuṉṟukaḷ pala teṉṉintiya pūmikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. im mali iṭaṅkaḷukkuk kīḻē taṇṭakam eṉṉum iruṇṭa kāṭu iruntatu. aṅku ciṅkam, puli, yāṉi, kāṭṭu erumi, ciṟutti, maṉitaṉukku aḻivic ceyyum pūcci vakikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ātikāla maṉitaṉ, veyyil, maḻi, vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiṉiṉṟu taṉṉik kāppāṟṟikkoḷḷak kūṭiya kukikaḷ maliyiṭaṅkaḷiliruntaṉa. avaṉ taṇṇīric cēmittu vippataṟku maṇ pāttiraṅkaḷic ceyya aṟintirukkavilli. nīrūṟ ṟukaḷ vaṟṟiya kālattil pāṟikaḷiluḷḷa kuḻikaḷil nīr niṟintiruntatu. avaṉ ataṉik kikaḷāl aḷḷip parukiṉāṉ; kālaṭiyiṟ kiṭanta kaṟ kaḷi eṭuttu avikaḷiliruntu kōṭari, īṭṭimuṉi, ariyum karuvikaḷ, curaṇṭum karuvi mutaliyavi kaḷic ceyyap paḻakikkoṇṭāṉ. in niliyiliruntu maṉitaṉiṉ cīrtiruttam vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. mika muṟpaṭṭa tirutta kālam paḻiya kaṟkālam eṉappaṭum. paḻiya kaṟkāla nākarikattik kāṭṭum kiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ kaṭappā, nellūr, vaṭa ārkkāṭu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ paḻaṅkaḷ vitikaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷi uṇṭu vāḻntaṉar. paruvakāla māṟutalkaḷāl ivvuṇavup poruḷkaḷ, ciṟcila kālaṅkaḷil aritiṟkiṭippavāyiṉa. ākavē avarkaḷ ūṉ uṇaviyum koḷḷattoṭaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ taṟkāppiṉ poruṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷōṭu atikam pōrāṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ vēṭṭi āṭuvatil tiṟami peṟṟaṉar. ākavē toṭakkattil maṉita ṉiṉ toḻil vēṭṭiyāṭuvatākaviruntatu; appoḻutu avarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkātu alintu tirintārkaḷ. ulakam muḻumiyilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa paḻiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ ellām orē vaṭiviṉa. itaṉāl ātikāla maṉitaṉ ulakil eṅkum alintu tiripava ṉāyiruntāṉeṉat terikiṉṟatu.
kuṟiñci nilaccūḻal, maṉita cīrtiruttattiṟku aṭippaṭiyākiya iraṇṭu putiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpaṭi ceytatu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu villum ampum, maṟṟatu neruppu uṇṭākkutal. malikaḷil mūṅkil atikam vaḷarkiṉṟatu. maliyil vāḻum makkaḷ mūṅkiliṉ vaḷiyun taṉmiyi nōkki aṟintārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ataṉip piḷantu iru muṉikaḷilum koṭiyik kaṭṭi nīṇṭa muṭkaḷi atil vittu eyyap paḻakiṉārkaḷ. iṉṟum malikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ orē ampiṉāl oru puliyik kollum tiṟami peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ.
ātikāla makkaḷ maliyil tī uṇṭāki erivatik kaṇṭārkaḷ. mūṅkil oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu urōñcuvatāl tīyuṇṭākiṉṟateṉpati avarkaḷ aṟintārkaḷ; ākavē tāmum iraṇṭu taṭikaḷi oṉṟōṭu oṉṟi urōñcit tī uṇṭākkalāmeṉa eṇṇiṉārkaḷ. tām vēṭṭi yāṭik koṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyi vēkac ceyyum poruṭṭu avarkaḷ mutalil neruppip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
āṭavar vēṭṭiyāṭac ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu paḻaṅkaḷip poṟukkiṉārkaḷ; kiḻaṅkukaḷi akaḻntārkaḷ; tām vāḻiṭaṅkaḷic cuṟṟit tāṉē viḷinta mali nel, mūṅkilarici mutali yavikaḷiyum cēkarittārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṉ aṭutta kaṭami kuḻanti kaḷi vaḷarppatu. ivvaki niliyilirukkumpōtu avarkaḷ vīṭu amikka aṟiyavilli. teṉṉintiya veppa nilikku vīṭu vēṇṭiya tilli; mara niḻalkaḷum malikkukikaḷumē otukkiṭaṅkaḷākap payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ toṭakkattil vīṭukaḷi uṟiviṭattiṉ poruṭṭu amikkavilli; tamatu celvamākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷip pattirap paṭuttivikkum poruṭṭu amittaṉar. paḻiya kaṟkāla maṉitaṉukku uṇavic cēmittu vikkum tēvi uṇṭāyirukkavilli. vīṭu illāmiyālum, alintu tiriyavēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmiyālum vīṭṭil taṅki vāḻvatākiya uṇarcci āṭavariṭiyē eḻavilli. ākavē tāyāṭci muṟiyāṉa vāḻkki ātiyil vaḷarcciyaṭiva tāyiṟṟu.
ivvakiyāṉa vāḻkkiyi iṉṉoṉṟum ūkkiyatu. muṟkāla maṉitaṉ āṭamparamāṉa maṇakkiriyikaḷāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukkavilli. āṭavarum makaḷirum kātalittu maṇantaṉar. cila nāḷkaḷiṉ piṉ uṟavi ṉari aḻittu viruntu iṭṭaṉar. ituvē muṟkālat tirumaṇak kiriyi yāka viruntatu. tirumaṇam eṉṟum niliyāṉa taṉmiyuṭiyatāka virukka villi. taṉippaṭṭa cottu iṉmiyum niliyāṉa vīṭu iṉmiyum tāyāṭci muṟiyāṉa vāḻkkikku nīṇṭa kālam vāyppu aḷittaṉa.
tamatu mēṉiyi alaṅkarittuk koḷvatil āṭavarukkum makaḷi rukkum viruppu uṇṭu; ivviruppup peṇkaḷukku atikam. kuṟap peṇkaḷ ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil ōṭukaḷip poṟukkiṉārkaḷ; avikaḷi māliyākak kōttu aṇintārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kātalar vēṭṭi yāṭuvatiṟ kiṭitta pulinakam, pulippal pōṉṟavikaḷik koṭut tārkaḷ. avikaḷiyum avarkaḷ kaḻuttil aṇintārkaḷ. piṟkālattil iviyē tāliyiṉ vaṭivākamāṟiṉa. teṉṉintiyappeṇ tālitarittal tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭamikku aṭiyāḷamākum. peṇkaḷiṉ iṉṉoru alaṅkāram taḻiuṭi. taḻiuṭi eṉpatu koṭiyiṟ kaṭṭiya taḻikaḷālāṉa uṭi. ivvuṭi uṭukkum vaḻakkam iṉṟum cila kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉariṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
maṇalum maṇalcārnta iṭaṅkaḷum
maṇalum maṇalcārnta iṭamum pāli eṉappaṭum. itu makkaḷ vāḻakkūṭiya iṭattiṉ ōr uṭpirivil aṭaṅkum. vēṭaṉ kāṭṭu vilaṅku kaḷit turattic cellumpōtu pālinilattil niliyillātu cilakālam taṅkuvāṉ. pālinilattil cilakālam taṅkuvōr allatu aṅku vāḻvōr maṟavar eṉappaṭuvar. maṟam vīratti uṇarttum. ivar 1kaḷḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. pāli nilam ceḻumiyaṟṟatātalālum pālinila makkaḷ pōrt toḻilil ciṟantavarkaḷ ātaliṉālum maṟavarum kaḷḷarum celvariyum maṟṟanilaṅkaḷil vāḻum valiyaṟṟavarkaḷiyum koḷḷi yiṭṭu vāḻvārāyiṉar. itaṉāl maṟam eṉpatu koṭumiyiyum kaḷḷar eṉpatu tiruṭariyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa.
kāṭum kāṭucārnta nilamum
kuṟiñci nilattil makkaḷ perukiyapōtu uṇavu curuṅkiṟṟu. appoḻutu makkaḷ ayaliluḷḷa mulli nilattiṟkuc celvārāyiṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷ cīrtiruttattil iṉṉoru paṭiyi aṭintaṉar. erumi, pacu, āṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷi ivarkaḷ paḻakki vaḷarttārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kuṟavarāyiruntapōtu nāy paḻakki vaḷarkkap paṭṭatu. mulli nilattil āṭumāṭukaḷ viriviṟ perukiṉa. ākavē cottu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl kuṭumpaṅkaḷum, kuṭikaḷum tōṉṟi vaḷarntaṉa.
āṭavarum makaḷirum oruvari oruvar kātalittalum, kātaliṉ aṭiyāḷamākak kātalaṉ pulinakam, iliyuṭi mutaliyavaṟṟik kātalikkuk koṭuttuk kiriyi iṉṟi maṇantukoḷvatumākiya maṇam kaḷavu eṉappaṭṭatu. kuṟiñci nilattiṟṟōṉṟiya ivvaki maṇaṅkaḷ niṉṟupōyiṉa. ippoḻutu kaṟpu oḻukkam tōṉṟiṟṟu. kātalar oruva rōṭu oruvar paḻakuvataṉmuṉ maṇac caṭaṅku iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ilika ḷālum malarkaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pantariṉkīḻ cuṟṟattiṉar tiraṇ ṭiruntu maṇakkiriyi naṭattappaṭṭatu.
cottuc cērattoṭaṅkiyatum, kaṟpu oḻukkam tōṉṟiyatum camūkam tanti āṭcimuṟiyāka vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. tantiyē āṭumāṭu kaḷukkuc contamuṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. nilaṅkaḷip pirittāl āṭumāṭukaḷ mēyvataṟku muṭiyāmal pōkum. ākavē kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkki tōṉṟiṟṟu. tanti āṭciyil periya kuṭumpattalivaṉ aracaṉpōl āyiṉāṉ. im muṟiyil aracaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. āṭci muṟi mutalil mulli nilattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. kōṉ eṉpatu paḻiya tamiḻccol. itu aracaṉik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āycci eṉṉum col iṭicciyiyum araciyiyum kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
maṟṟa nāṭukaḷil iṭiyariṉ vāḻkki iṭamviṭṭu iṭam alintu tirivatākaviruntatu. teṉṉintiyā ceḻippuṭiyatāyum ellāk kālaṅ kaḷilum āṭumāṭukaḷukku vēṇṭiya uṇavu kiṭikkak kūṭiyatākavum iruntamiyāl teṉṉintiya iṭiyarukku iṭamviṭṭu iṭam cella vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyirukkavilli. ākavē teṉṉintiya iṭiyariṉ vāḻkki ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruppatāka amintatu.
iṭiyar āṭu māṭukaḷi mēyccal nilattukku ōṭṭic ceṉ ṟārkaḷ. mantikaḷ mēyumpōtu avarkaḷ maraniḻalil taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ; poḻutupōkkiṉ poruṭṭu mūṅkiṟ kuḻalil iṉiya irākaṅkaḷip pāṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭu māṭu mēyppavarkaḷil oru piriviṉar kuṟumpar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ mayir nīṇṭu vaḷarum kuṟumpu āṭukaḷi vaḷarttaṉar. kuṟumpu āṭukaḷiṉ mayiriliruntu kampaḷi neyyappaṭṭatu.
kaṭalum kaṭalcārnta nilamum
itaṟku aṭutta paṭiyil kaṭaṟkari nilam makkaḷāl kuṭiyēṟap paṭṭatu. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ kariyi aṭutta kaṭalil mīṉ piṭittārkaḷ; piṉpu āḻamāṉa nīriṟ ceṉṟārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkariyil vāḻvōr paratavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ mīṉ piṭippatum marakkalañ ceyvatumāyiruntaṉa. mikapperiya marakkalaṅkaḷ kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. itaṉpiṉ tōlāl mūṭappaṭṭa kūṭikaḷ tōṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. neytal nilattil kiṭikkum mukkiya poruḷkaḷ uppum mīṉum. paratavar ivikaḷi maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷukkup paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytaṉar. paratavar ivvāṟu vaṇikarāyiṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu paṇṭaṅkaḷip poti māṭukaḷil ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. intiyap poruḷkaḷi āpirikkā, arāpiyā mutaliya mēṟku nāṭukaḷukkum, kiḻakkē malāyā, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkum ēṟṟic ceṉṟa kaṭalōṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkari nilaṅkaḷ cila payirc ceykikku ēṟṟavāyiruntaṉa. aṅku payiriṭappaṭṭatu.
vayalum vayalcārnta nilamum
maruta nilamakkaḷ nilatti uḻutu payiriṭṭārkaḷ. āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷ āṟṟu veḷḷatti vāykkāl vaḻiyāka vayalkaḷukkup pāycca aṟintiruntārkaḷ. ivvāṟu mika mika muṟkālattilēyē payirc ceyki teṉṉintiyāvil mika vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntatu. āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷukku appāl pañcu viḷiyum nilam iruntatu. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ pañciliruntu nūl nūṟkavum nūlāl āṭi neyyavum aṟintiruntārkaḷ.
ippoḻutu makkaḷ maraṅkaḷāl vīṭu kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. tēvikku atikamāṉa tāṉiyam vīṭukaḷil cēmittu vikkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷi luḷḷa uppu, mīṉ, veṇṇey, tayir, kal āyutaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavikkut tāṉiyam paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattil paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyamuṭiyāta paṇṭaṅkaḷi veḷiyē koṇṭu cellavēṇṭiya avaciyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu celvataṟku vaṇṭikaḷ ceyyak kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭakkūṭiya marañceṭi kaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu tirāviṭa nākarikam intiya nāṭṭilēyē tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. intiyāviṉ ayaliliruntu nākarikam intiyāvi aṭintateṉpataṟku ātāram ciṟitum kāṇappaṭavilli.1
pēccu
camiyalum, uṭiyum allāta iṉṉoṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭatu. atu pēccu. pēccu ici campantappaṭṭatu allatu ici campantappaṭāta tāka irukkalām. pāṭalukku muṉ pēccu iruntateṉap palar karutukiṉ ṟaṉar. ātiyil pakuti ici campantamum, pakuti ici campantamillātatumā yirunta pēcciṉ vēṟupāṭē pāṭalum vacaṉamum eṉa vēṟu palar karutukiṉ ṟaṉar. itaṉi naṉku aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyavilli. ilakkiya vaḷarcciyil uri naṭikku muṟpaṭṭatu pāṭal eṉat terikiṉṟatu. paṇ, pāṇ eṉpatu iciyiṉ pakutikkup peyar. paṇ eṉpatiliruntu pāṭu, pāṭṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pāṭutal tamiḻariṉ ātikālap poḻutu pōkkukaḷil oṉṟu. pāṇar toṭakkattil pāṭakarāyiruntaṉar. nākarikam vaḷarntapōtu araṇmaṉiyil aracāṅkap pāṭakar iruntaṉar. tamiḻar nākarikam vaḷarcci yuṟṟirunta kālattil pāṇar aracariṉ naṇparum avarukku ālōcaṉi kūṟuvōrumāyiruntaṉar.
camayam
maṉitaṉ āyutam ceyyum, uṇavic camikkum, uṭi aṇiyum, pēcum vilaṅkāka māttiram irukkavilli; camayamuṭiya vilaṅkākavum vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ virumpiyavikaḷip peṟavum, taṉṉi accuṟuttum tīmikaḷip pōkkavum valiya āṟṟali vēṇṭik koḷḷum vaḻikaḷiyum aṟintiruntāṉ. avvaḻikaḷ mantira vitti toṭarpāṉa kiriyikaḷ. avi viruntu, pāṭṭu, kūttu vaṭiviliruntaṉa. piṟkālattil viruntu pāṭṭu kūttu eṉpaṉa vevvēṟākap pirikkap paṭṭaṉa. iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa ovvoru nilattilum teyvaṅkaḷum kiriyi kaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyik kaṇṇāṭi pōl kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṟintu pōyiṉa. vāṇikam, āṭci, toḻil, āṭci muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. ilakkiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṉappāṭañ ceyyappaṭṭuvantaṉa. paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pala iṟantupōyiṉa eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyap poru ḷatikārattukku uri eḻutiya paḻiya āciriyarkaḷ tuṟikaḷ palavaṟṟukku ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭamuṭiyāmal iruntamiyē cāṉṟākum. ip poḻutu kiṭittuḷḷa paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻnta nilaṅkaḷukkēṟpat tōṉṟi vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa eṉpatit teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāṭalkaḷ
tamiḻnāṭu nila amippukkēṟpap pirikkappaṭṭirunta kālattil pāṭalkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ovvoru nilattilum annilattiṉ vāḻkkik kēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ākavē tiṇi eṉpatu kuṟitta nilattuk kuriya pāṭal vakiyik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. maṉitaṉiṉ kātal, pōr eṉpavi kaḷē pulavar pāṭutaṟku ēṟṟa karuttukkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē pāṭalkaḷ iru vakippaṭṭaṉa. akattiṇi, puṟattiṇi eṉpaṉa avaṟ ṟiṉ pirivukaḷ. akam eṉpatu kātaluṭiya iruvar māttiram uṇarntu aṉupavikkak kūṭiyatu; puṟam maṟṟavarōṭu kalantu paṅkupaṟṟat takkatu. aintu nilaṅkaḷukkumuriya kātaṟ ceyalkaḷ aintu eṉakkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟiñci nilattukkuriyavi kātalar oruvari oruvar kaṇṭu kātalittu uṭaṉ maṇattal, malik kāṭci mutaliyaṉa. āṭavarum makaḷirum taṉit taṉiyē etirppaṭṭu kātalippataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam maliyātalil kūṭalum kūṭal nimittamum kuṟiñci nilattukku uripporuḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
kātalaṉ kātaliyi curavaḻiyē peṇ vīṭṭār aṟiyātu koṇ ṭēkutal, kātalaṉ kātaliyi viṭṭup pirintu cellutal pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷip pālip pāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. pirintu ceṉṟa kātalaṉ varun tuṇiyum āṟṟiyiruppatākiya ceyalik kūṟuvatu mulli. tali vaṉip pirinta kātali avaṉ varavi niṉintu iraṅkiyiruttal neytal. maruta nilattil vitippu muṭintu aṟuppuk kālam variyil makkaḷ poḻutu pōkkutaṟku ēṟṟa oḻivu uṇṭu. akkālaṅkaḷil kātalaruk kiṭiyil ūṭal nērum. itu perumpālum talivaṉ parattiyaruṭaṉ naṭpuk koṇṭiruttalāl uṇṭākum.
tiṇikkuriya makkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiṟ cila ikkālattil cātip peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟavar, maṟavar, paratavar, iṭiyar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa avaṟṟuṭ cila.
tirāviṭanāṭu mika muṟkālattilēyē nākarikam peṟṟiruntatu
mika mika muṟkālattilēyē tirāviṭaruṭiyanākarikam mika uyar vaṭintiruntatu. nāṭṭiṉ ceḻippum vāṇikamum celvatti vaḷark kiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷiṉ kuḻappamillāta vāḻkkiyum celvam vaḷarvataṟku maṟṟoru kāraṇam. vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷ aṭikkaṭi aṉṉiyap paṭi eṭuppuk kaḷāl kalakkuṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kiyil vāḷip piṭittukkoṇṭu vīṭṭiyum uṟaviṉariyum kākkavēṇṭiyiruntatu. aṅku makkaḷukkum cottukkum pātukāppu irukkavilli. teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷukku ivvakit tolli irukkavilli. nāṭṭiṉ celvatti vaṟṟac ceyyum periyapōrkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil nikaḻavilli. teṉ kaṭalkaḷil kaṭaṟkoḷḷikkārarum kāṇappaṭavilli. tariyil amiti nilaviṟṟu. ak kālattil aṟiyappaṭṭirunta kaṭali aṭutta nāṭukaḷōṭu tamiḻar vāṇikam purintaṉar. pāpilōṉ, ekiptu, aciriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ intiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purintaṉa. nāṭu celvavaḷamaṭintatu. vaṭakkē vāḻnta makkaḷiviṭat teṟkē vāḻnta makkaḷ celvam paṭittirun taṉar. kaṭaṟ payaṇaṅkaḷ teṉmēṟku intiyāvukkum pāpilōṉukku miṭi yil oḻuṅkāka naṭipeṟṟatu. marakkalamōṭṭum mālumikaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷākaviruntaṉar. tantam, kuraṅku, mayil pōṉṟavikaḷiṉ tamiḻp peyarkaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉa. nilam ceḻippuṟṟiruntatu. kaṉikaḷil poṉ arikkappaṭṭatu. viram, muttu, pavaḷam mutaliyavum atikam kiṭittaṉa. tēkku, cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅ kaḷukku ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil kāṇappaṭum nāṭu, ūr eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻariṉ paḻiya paṇpāṭṭi uṇarttuvaṉa. nāṭu eṉṉum col naṭu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. palavaki marañceṭikaḷi naṭṭu uṇ ṭākkum iṭam nāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. ūr eṉpataṟku vayal cūḻnta iṭam eṉpatu poruḷ. ūr eṉpatu uḻu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. ūr eṉpatu uḻutali uṇarttum. muṟkālattil nāṭṭippaṟṟiyum ūrip paṟṟiyumuḷḷa tamiḻaruṭiya karuttu ituvākum. paḻiya cālatiyāviṉ talinakar 1ūr eṉappaṭṭatu. urōmariṉ nakarik kuṟikkum peyar ūrps. ivi ūr eṉṉum tamiḻccol campantamāṉavi. nāṭu eṉpataṟku etirc col kāṭu. kāṭu eṉpataṟkuk kaṭattaṟku ariyatu eṉpatu poruḷ. kāṭṭik keṭuttup payiriṭum nilam nāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu tirāviṭariṉ nākarikam it tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē paṭippaṭi vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷatu eṉa aṟintukoṇṭōm. iṉit tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ ciṟappu iyalpukaḷit taṉittaṉi eṭuttuk kūṟuvōm.
nākarika maṭinta tirāviṭar
toliviṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟutal
cūḻnilikkēṟpa makkaḷ evvāṟu nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟārkaḷ eṉpati viḷakkiyuḷḷōm. iṉṟu teṉṉintiya makkaḷ ilaṅki, malāyā, moricas, teṉṉāppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. mikap paḻaṅkālattilēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ āpirikkā mēṟkup pāracīkam, malāyā, kampōtiyā, cumattirā, yāvā, cīṉā mutaliya pala nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntārkaḷ. in nikaḻcciyi valiyuṟuttum cāṉṟukaḷ pala iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
“paṇṭi nāḷilē cīrum ciṟappum eytiyirunta miciram eṉṉum ekiptu nāṭṭiṉam, pāracīkak kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu aḻintupōṉa cumēru (Sumeria) nāṭṭilum, cōḻar kuṭiyēṟiṉamiyiṉālē. cōḻatēyam eṉṉum peyariṉi eytip piṟkālattilē moḻic citiviṉālē cālatēyā (Chaldea) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tolpatiyilum cōḻar kulattār kalattiṟ ceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟṟut tamatu āṇi celuttiya kirēttat (Crete) tīvilum ataṟkaṇittākiya yavaṉapurattilum (Greece) urōmar varuvataṟkumuṉ paḻiya ittāli (Italy) tēcattilum aipīriyā eṉappaṭṭa paḻiya speyiṉ (Spin) tēcattilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum tamiḻkkulattār vāḻntu nākarikam parappiṉārkaḷeṉa mēṟṟici aṟiñar ārāycciyāṟ kaṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar......cintunati tīrattilē pāṇṭiya maṉṉar āḷukiyilē mīṉāṭu eṉṉum peyarōṭu tikaḻntatum piṉṉāḷilē ‘iṟantōr mēṭu’ eṉṉum karuttuṭiya mukañcatari (Mohonjo Daro) eṉṉum peyareytiyatumākiya nāṭṭilē mituṉarāciyāṉatu yāḻ eṉṉum peyariṉāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu iṇiyāḻuruvattilē kuṟiyīṭu ceyyap paṭṭateṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar.
“maṇimalar eṉṉum kaṭṭurit tokutiyiṉuḷḷē paṇṭittamiḻar perumi eṉṉum uriyiṉi eḻutiya ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattu varalāṟṟuppakuti āciriyar vi.ār. irāmacantira tīṭcitaravarkaḷ, ‘teṉ ṉāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻ nākarikam urōmā purikkum, kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum paraviyatu. iṉṉum takkaṇa pīṭapūmiyic cērnta vēṭṭuvar1 (kirātar) eṉṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ yavaṉa tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟu taṅkaḷ araci nili niṟuttit taṅkaḷ peyariyum annāṭṭiṟkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. ituvē kirīṭ (Crete) tēcam. itaṉ nākarikattukkum tamiḻar nākarikattukkum oppumi mikutiyāyuḷḷatu.’ eṉa eḻutiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. villavar eṉṉum cērar peyari ‘hunters’ eṉa mēṉāṭṭār moḻipeyarkka atu tirumpavum tamiḻiṟku moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭu vēṭṭuvar eṉa amivatu iyalpu. ātaliṉālē takkiṇa pīṭapūmiyic cērnta vēṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ eṉat tīṭcitaravarkaḷ uriyilē kuṟikkappaṭṭōr villavarākiya cērar kulattiṉarē eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattilum patiṟṟup pattilum kāṇappaṭum cērar meykkīrtti ivvuṇmiyiṉi nili niṟuttukiṉṟatu. cēranāṭu kēraḷam eṉat tirivupaṭṭatupōlac cērar tīvu ‘krīt’ āyirukkalām. tamiḻnāṭṭuvēntar yavaṉarkaḷit tamatu kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷilē kāvalāḷarkaḷāka vittirunta ceytiyum, urōmāpuri vēntaṉākiya ākasṭas maṉṉaṉukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ tūtaṉuppiya ceyti yum, piṟavum ātāramākat tīṭcitaravarkaḷ, ‘mattiyatari nākarikameṉṟu ippōtu vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu teṉṉintiya nākarikam eṉṟu tuṇivāyc collalām’1 eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟukiṉṟār”2
ulakiṉ perum pakutiyil tirāviṭam vaḻaṅkiyataṟkuc cāṉṟu
mokañcatarō arappā mutaliya cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil kiṭit tuḷḷa muttirikaḷil akkālattu vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvaki eḻuttukkaḷ hitarāpāttu, tirunelvēli mutaliya viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ilaṅkiyilē kēkāliyi luḷḷa mali oṉṟilum ivvaki eḻuttup poṟikkappaṭṭiruppati heras pātiriyār kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷi mika otta eḻuttukaḷ teṉṉamerikkāvukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa īsrar tīvukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptu, mēṟku āciyā, ciṉṉa āciyā, kirēttā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya paḻiya eḻuttukaḷukkum mokañcatarō eḻuttu kaḷukkum oṉṟumi iruppatip paḻiya eḻuttu ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. ivveḻuttukkaḷi ellām naṉku ārāynta heras pātiri yār paḻiya cīṉam, cumēriyam, paḻiya ellam, paḻiya arāpi, acōkaṉ, pirāmi, miṉōvaṉ, paḻiya ekiptiyam, ilipiyaṉ, eṟṟūskaṉ eḻuttuk kaḷellām paḻiya tirāviṭa eḻuttiliruntu tōṉṟiyavi eṉak kūṟi yuḷḷār. mēṟku āciya, miṉōva, mokañcatarō, īsṭar tīvu eḻuttukaḷ ellām orē toṭakkattic cērntaṉaveṉṟum avi avaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷiṉ pōkkiṉpaṭi vevvēṟu vakiyil vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa eṉṟum carṭayāṉ mārcal, pērāciriyar lāṅkaṭaṉ ji.ār. haṉṭar pōṉṟa ārāycci vallār karutiṉārkaḷ. ikkoḷki nāḷ vītam valiyaṭikiṉṟatu. oṭṭuc coṟkaḷuṭiya moḻikaḷ perum pālum tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉiṉṟum piṟantaṉaveṉak kūṟalām.1
mokañcatarō akaḻ ārāycci aṟiyappaṭutaṟkup pala āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarēyē pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷ,
“catumaṟi āriyam varumuṉ cakamuḻutu niṉatāyiṉ
mutumoḻinī yaṉātiyeṉa moḻikuvatum viyappāmē”
eṉak kūṟiyatu mika viyakkattakkatu. tamiḻp paṟṟu mikkuṭiyavarāy viḷaṅkiya viruti civañāṉayōkikaḷ iṟṟikku muppattumūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ kūṟiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
“tirumalar maṇameṉa vorumiyiṉ malki
eṇṇil poḻiṟpayir paṇṇik kākkum
oppil perumi meypporu ḷaṉpu
pāṅki ṉilipeṟ ṟōṅkun tamiḻakat
tāṟaṟi vuṭimip pēṟuṟu makkaḷ
muṉṉart tōṉṟi maṉṉik keḻumi
imiḻiya lolicār tamiḻmoḻi pēci
maṇṇiṟ palaviṭa naṇṇik kuṭiyirun
tavvav viṭattuk kovviya vaṇṇam
kūṟṟu naṭiyuṭi vēṟṟumi yeyti
paṟpala viṉappeyar peṟṟup perukiṉar.
* * * * * * *
mutaliṭik kaḻaka muṉṉīr koḷḷap
paṭutali ṉintiyap paṭiyiṉ vaḷamvavi
ayaṉāṭ ṭavarī rāyira māṇṭāp
paṭiyeṭuttut tuṉ puṟuttiya paṭiyāl
nūlkaḷu mavaṟṟi ṉuṇṇiya vaḻakkum
aruki maṟintaṉa vātaliṉ”
intiyanāṭu muḻumiyilum vāḻnta makkaḷ tirāviṭar
ituvariyilum akaḻ ārāycciyiṉāl aṟiyappaṭṭa paḻiya nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ intiya nākarikamē mikap paḻami uṭiyatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3500 eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintuveḷi nākarika kālattil intiyā muḻumiyilum orē moḻiyi vaḻaṅkiya orē iṉamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. ammakkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉappaṭuvar. āriya makkaḷ ki.mu. iraṇṭāyirattil allatu ataṟkuppiṉ intiya nāṭṭi aṭintārkaḷ. akkālattil vaṭanāṭu muḻumiyilum tirāviṭa moḻi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu āriya makkaḷiṉ paḻiya pāṭalkaḷākiya vētaṅkaḷil tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ pala iruppatum piṟavumeṉa ārāycci yāḷar naṉku āyntu niṟuviyuḷḷāhkaḷ.1 āriyar varukikkup piṉpē intiya nāṭṭil vevvēṟu iṉa makkaṭ kalappum moḻikkalappum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ki.mu. iraṇṭāyirattil vaṭakkē imayattukkum teṟkē kumarikkum iṭippaṭṭa peru nilapparappu tirāviṭa nāṭāka viḷaṅkiṟ ṟeṉa, teḷitil aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
putiya makkaḷiṉ varukiyāl uṇṭāṉa māṟutalkaḷ
putiyamakkaḷukkum vaṭakkē vāḻntukoṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷukkumiṭiyil pōrkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. āriyaruṭiya vētapāṭal kaḷāl akkālattil tirāviṭar uyarnta nākarikam peṟṟuviḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.2 nākarikattil uyarnili aṭintirunta tirāviṭari velvatu āriyamakkaḷukku iyalātatākaviruntatu. nāḷaṭivil irucātiyārukkiṭiyil tirumaṇakkalappukaḷāl toṭarpukaḷ uṇ ṭāyiṉa.3 tirāviṭap peṇkaḷi āriya āṭavarum āriyap peṇkaḷit tirāviṭa āṭavarum maṇantaṉar. tirāviṭaruṭiya camūkam tāyāṭci muṟi yiṉatu. āriyaruṭiya camūkam tantiāṭci muṟiyiṉatu. tirāviṭap peṇkaḷ mūlam āriya āṭavarukkup piṟantavarkaḷ tanti āṭci muṟippaṭi āriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āriyappeṇkaḷ mūlam tirāviṭat tantiyārukkup piṟantavarkaḷ tirāviṭar tāyāṭci muṟippaṭi āriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivvakiyāṉa kalappup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka nikaḻntatu. vintiyamalikku vaṭakkē uḷḷa makkaḷ tammi āriyar eṉak kūṟik koḷvārāyiṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷiṭattil āriyak kulattiṉarukkuriya kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷ mikacciṟitē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.1
makkaḷiṭi kalappu uṇṭāṉapōtu moḻikaḷum koḷkikaḷum kalappavāyiṉa. tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ pala tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa mikappala coṟkaḷ iṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. accoṟkaḷukku mūlam aṟiyamuṭiyāmal irukkiṟatu. intō āriyamoḻikku iṉamuṭiyatāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkum āriyamoḻikaḷil iccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.2
putitāka vanta makkaḷiviṭa vaṭakkē vāḻntukoṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷ palar. makkaṭ kalappiṉāl cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāki moḻi māṟupaṭṭapōtum vāḻkki muṟiyil avarkaḷ tirāviṭa camūkattiṉar pōlavē kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. puttar kālamakkaḷ vāḻkkiyippaṟṟi ris ṭēviṭs eṉpār kūṟiyiruppatu itaṉi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.3
vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyamoḻi camakkirutamā?
vaṭanāṭṭilē makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi camakkirutameṉṟu palar niṉikkiṟārkaḷ. ikkaruttut tavaṟuṭiyatu. camakkirutam orupōtum pēccu moḻiyākavirukkavilli. atu kurumārukkuriya cātimoḻi yākavē iruntatu. intiyanāṭṭu moḻiyiṉ tiripukaḷākiya pirākirutam eṉappaṭṭa moḻikaḷē pēcappaṭṭuvantaṉa.4
āriya moḻiyic cērntaṉaveṉṟu karutappaṭum vaṭintiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippu tirāviṭa moḻi ilakkaṇatti ottuḷḷateṉṟum, oru moḻiyiṉ kuṭumpattoṭarpi aṟivatu ilak kaṇa amippiṉālallatu coṟkaḷik koṇṭu aṟital kūṭāteṉṟum, vaṭintiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tiripu eṉṟum pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār tuṇintuḷḷār.1
pāracīkar vaṭanāṭṭiṉ cilapakutikaḷi veṉṟu cila kālam āṭci naṭattiṉārkaḷ. piṉpu kirēkkar paṭieṭuppu nērntatu. piṉpu cittiyar, maṅkōliyar, avuṇar (Huns) mutaliya pala cātiyiṉar vaṭanāṭṭiṉmītu paṭi eṭuttuvantu nāṭṭil ciṟcila pakutikaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟi intiya makka ḷōṭu kalantārkaḷ. itaṉāl alakcāntar paṭi eṭuppuk kālam mutal mēlum makkaṭ kalappum moḻik kalappum intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭāyiṉa.
intiya makkaḷiṉ kula muṟiyāṉa ārāycci naṭattiya risli eṉpār iṉṟu intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum kulappiriviṉari kīḻvarumāṟu pirittuḷḷār; paṭattiṟ kāṇka.
tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar eṉṉum koḷki
tirāviṭar intiya nāṭṭukku ayaliliruntu vantārkaḷ. avar varukikkumuṉ nikurōvak kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷuḷḷa orucātiyiṉar aṅku vāḻntukoṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷ tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar (Pre-Dravidians) eṉappaṭu var eṉṟum, am makkaḷē iṉṟu malikaḷil vāḻvōreṉṟum oru cilar eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar. ik koḷ kikku ātāram oru ciṟitum kiṭik kavilli. tirāviṭa makkaḷ, vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa, aintu vēṟu vakit tiruttaṅkaḷuṭiyavarkaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭintārkaḷ eṉa muṉ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. avarkaḷil kuṟiñci pāli nilamakkaḷē maṟṟa makkaḷiṉ toṭarpiṉṟip paḻiya muṟiyil vāḻntu putiya cila paḻakkavaḻakkaṅ kaḷuṭiyarāy varukiṉṟaṉar. avar kaḷiṉ uṭal, niṟam, vaḷarcci mutaliyaṉa avarkaḷiṉ uṇavu, vāḻkki nili pōṉṟavi ētuvāka uṇṭāṉavi. tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ kūṟuvatu varumāṟu. “tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar (Pre -
