Dravidans), paḻiya tirāviṭar (Proto-Dravidans) eṉṉum koḷkikaḷ irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaṟpaṉi. paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarāvatu varalāṟṟukkārarāvatu teṉṉintiyāvil oṉṟukkuppiṉ oṉṟākat toṭarntu vanta paṇpāṭṭuk kālaṅkaḷil putiya makkaḷ pukuntu māṟutal kaḷik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ eṉṟu colvataṟkēṟṟa ātāram oṉṟum kāṭṭamuṭiyātu. paḻiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭukkuppiṉ putiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭum ataṟkuppiṉ irumpuk kālap paṇpāṭum muṟiyē oṉṟi oṉṟu toṭarntu vantaṉa eṉṟu koḷvataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷē kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻam poruḷārāycci ik koḷkikku vali aḷikkiṉṟatu. kāṭukaḷilum, malikaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷ kulamuṟiyākat teṉṉintiya makkaḷōṭu poruntātavarkaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷutal cālātu. putiya kaṟkālam mutal teṉṉintiyāvil aintu vakiyāṉa paṇpāṭukaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa veṉpatit teṉṉintiya makkaḷippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyvōr naṉku aṟivar. vēṭṭiyāṭiyum mīṉ piṭittum vāḻvōr paḻiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭic cērntavar. avarkaḷ toṭarpākak kāṭu kaḷilum kaṭaṟkarikaḷilum vāḻntamiyāl avarkaḷiṉ maṉappāṅkum vāḻkkip paḻakkaṅkaḷum taṉimuṟiyil vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. niṟattip paṟṟi nām ārāyavēṇṭiyatilli. itu cūḻaliṉ veppanili, ki yāḷum toḻil campantaṅkaḷāl uṇṭāvatu. payiriṭum toḻil paraviṉa tāl paḻiya poruḷātāra vaḻikaḷ muṟṟāka māṟutalaṭintaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷutal muṭiyātu. cila cūḻalkaḷil viṭappaṭṭōr paḻiya toḻil kaḷiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷayum paṟṟit tam vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉar. vēḷāḷar, kārāḷar, āyarvēṟu vakiyāṉa paṇpāṭukaḷi eytiṉar. pāli nila makkaḷ kuṟiñci makkaḷōṭu kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭil pāli eṉṟu taṉi nilam illi. ākavē kaṭaṟkarikaḷilum malikaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷit tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar eṉṟu kūṟutal cālātu”1
pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar paṇpāṭu eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
2“tamiḻ moḻikkuriya makkaḷippōlavē ammoḻiyum teṉṉintiyāvilēyē tōṉṟi yātum kuḻappamiṉṟi uyarnta ilakkiya vaḷarcci niliyi aṭintatu. kaṟkālam mutal tamiḻkkulam orē toṭakkattic cērntatāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ moḻiyip payiṉṟa ayal nāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷar cilar tamiḻ moḻiyi vaḻaṅkiya paḻiya makkaḷ mattiya āciyāviṉiṉṟu, vantārkaḷ eṉṟum, ataṟkuk kāraṇam paluccistāṉattil vaḻaṅkum pirākūy moḻiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭiya pala coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum tammaṉampōl kūṟiṉar. pirākūy moḻiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭiya coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭu tal, tamiḻ intiyamoḻiyaṉṟu eṉak kūṟuvataṟkup pōtumāṉa ātāra mākātu. iraṇṭu talimuṟikku muṟpaṭṭa ārāycciyāḷar ulakil vāḻnta paḻiya makkaṭ kulattiṉari ellām vēṟu eṅkō iruntu vanta varkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvatu iyalpāka iruntatu. avarkaḷukkut teṉṉintiyā vil kaṟkālap paṇpāṭu evvaḷavil paraviyiruntateṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ paḻiya aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻr teṉṉintiyāvil aṟiyamuṭiyāta kālam mutal vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷuḷḷaṉa veṉ patiyum avarkaḷ aṟiyātiruntārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭukku mattiya iṭam teṉṉintiyāvē. kaṭappā paḻiya mattiya kālap paḻaṅkaṟ kālap paṇpāṭṭukku mattiya iṭamākum. ekiptilē nīla āṟṟaṅ kariyi liruntu maṟintupōṉa paḻiya nākarikattiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cila iṉṟum takkāṇattil uḷḷaṉa. eliyaṭ cimit eṉpār kiḻakku āppirikka makka ḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉavum, iṉṟum takkāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavu mākiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ nampikkikaḷ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi iru makkaḷukkum toṭakkam oṉṟu eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tirāviṭar nākarikam
tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ mikap paḻiya nākarikattippaṟṟi mokañca tarō, arappā, caṅkutarō mutaliya aḻipāṭṭu mēṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷ veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. arappā mokañcatarō mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷil tirāviṭamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. akkālattil avarkaḷ ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiyarkaḷi otta allatu avarkaḷilum uyarnta nākarikamuṭiyavarkaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ. akkālattil irumpu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭavilli. nakaraṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vīṭukaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷukku māṭikaḷum māṭikaḷukkuc cellap paṭikkaṭṭukaḷumiruntaṉa. māṭikaḷiliruntu kaḻivu nīr kīḻē celva taṟkuc cūḷiyiṭṭa maṇkuḻāykaḷ porutti vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kūrivaḻiyāka vaḻiyum nīr pīlikaḷil viḻuntu piṉ kuḻāykaḷ vaḻiyē kīḻē ceṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭukkum kiṇaṟum kuḷikkumaṟiyum iruntaṉa. kaḻivunīr, kālvāykaḷ vaḻiyāka vītiyiluḷḷa periya kālvāyil viḻuntatu. kālvāykaḷ ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. taṇṇīr ceṉṟu viḻum iṭattil ceṅkaṟpatitta kuḻi iruntatu. pāramāṉa poruḷkaḷ kuḻiyil taṅkiniṉṟaṉa. cuttañ ceyvōr ak kuḻikaḷil taṅkiniṟkum aḻukkukaḷic cuttañ ceytaṉar. vīṭukaḷukku malakkūṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.
periya vītikaḷ 33-aṭi akalamuṭiyaṉa. ciṟiya vītikaḷ 18-aṭi akalamuṭiyaṉa. makkaḷ vāṟkōtumiyiyum, kōtumiyiyum viḷivittārkaḷ. āṭu, māṭu, erumi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ vīṭukaḷil vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉikaḷ paḻakki vēli ceyvikkappaṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ pañcu viḷivikkavum pañciliruntu nūl nūṟkavum nūliliruntu āṭi ceyyavum aṟintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷōṭum teṉṉintiyāvōṭum vāṇikam purintārkaḷ. oruvakic cuṇṇāmpuk kalliṟ cetukkappaṭṭa muttirikaḷum, nāṭṭiyamāṭum pāvaṉiyuḷḷa pāvikaḷ, cuvāttika aṭiyāḷamiṭṭa poruḷkaḷum, erumi kāṇṭāmirukam yāṉi māṭu pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅ kaḷum eḻuttukkaḷum veṭṭappaṭṭa muttirikaḷum, kal, poṉ, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷi mutaliyavikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷum ivipōṉṟa aḻakiya poruḷkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷ iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu makkaḷiviṭap palavakiyil uyarnta nākarika naṉṉili aṭintiruntārkaḷ eṉa ārāycciyāḷar naviṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ.1 cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3500 variyil.
piṟkālattil intiya nāṭṭi vantaṭinta āriya makkaḷum avar kaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉarum appaḻiya nākarika muṟikaḷ palavaṟṟik kik koṇṭaṉareṉṟum ataṟkuc cāṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ vētapāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉṟum ārāycci aṟiñar kūṟuvar. tirāviṭa makkaḷip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ vētaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāratam, irāmā yaṇam eṉṉum nūlkaḷil teṉṉāṭṭu aracarkaḷippaṟṟiyum teṉ ṉāṭṭippaṟṟiyum cila kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. annūlkaḷ kālat tukkuk kālam eḻutic cērkkappaṭṭaṉa vātaliṉ avikaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭu vaṉa ciṟanta varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷākamāṭṭā.
“māpāratattiyum irāmāyaṇattiyum nām ki.mu. 5ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiya nūlkaḷ eṉakkoḷḷalām. annūlkaḷiṉ ep pakuti ek kālattil ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṉum aiyappāṭum uṇṭākiṉṟatu. avi kaḷil kūṟappaṭum intiya nāṭṭiṉ pirivukaḷ ki.mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil uṟutiyāka amintiruntaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷalām. cātakak katikaḷ patiṉāṟu irācciyaṅkaḷiyum vēṟu cila nāṭukaḷiyum paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷi ellām intiya paṭamoṉṟil oḻuṅkupaṭa vittup pārttāl mikat teṟkē uḷḷa nāṭu kōtāvarikkut teṟkē pōkavilli. paḻiya putta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vintiyattukkuk kīḻē uḷḷa nāṭukaḷip paṟṟi aṟiyā.
pāṇiṉikkut teṉṉāṭṭip paṟṟit teriyātu avar teṟkē uḷḷaṉavākak kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ kaccā, avanti, kōcalam, karūcā, kaliṅkam eṉpaṉa. ivi vintattukku vaṭakkē uḷḷaṉa. pāṇiṉikku iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ viḷaṅkiya kārttiyāyaṉar teṉṉāṭṭip paṟṟi aṟintiruntār. patañcali kālattil teṉṉāṭu naṉṟāka aṟiyap paṭṭiruntatu. patañcaliyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 150. kārttiyāyaṉariṉ kālam, ki.mu. 350. paṇṭākkaratu koḷkiyiṉpaṭi pāṇiṉi ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiṉār.1 ak kālattil vaṭanāṭṭavarāl teṉṉāṭu ciṟitum aṟiyap paṭātiruntatu. mekastīṉas kālattil teṉṉintiyāvippaṟṟi vaṭa nāṭṭavar ciṟitu aṟintiruntārkaḷ. cāṇakkiyariṉ artta cāttirattil teṉṉāṭṭippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. acōkariṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷil cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar nāṭukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.2
tamiḻnāṭṭelli
ki.mu. 1000 variyil vintiyamalikku vaṭakē uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ āriyāvarttam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. vintiyattakkut teṟkē tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ki.mu. 1000 variyil epirēya moḻiyil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷ tamiḻākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭa velli tiruppati mali yākaviruntatu. akkālattil teluṅku taṉimoḻiyākap pirintuviṭṭatu. orukālattil tamiḻnāṭṭelli kiruṭṭiṇā āṟuvariyil irunta teṉac ciṟpanūliṟ kāṇappaṭuvatāka āciriyar u.vē. cāmināta aiya ravarkaḷ caṅkakālattamiḻum piṟkālat tamiḻum eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟip piṭṭuḷḷār. oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ teluṅku moḻikku illi. 1caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vēṅkaṭattukku vaṭakkē vaṭukar nāṭu iruntateṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
paḻam poruḷ ārāycci varalāṟṟu ārāyccikku evvakiyil tuṇipurikiṉṟatō avvāṟē paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ataṟkut tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa. caṅkanūlkaḷik koṇṭu iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tamiḻakattiṉ paṇpāṭu evvakiyiṉateṉa nām teḷḷitil aṟiyakkūṭum.
caṅkakālattil tamiḻnāṭu
2kiruṭṭiṇā āṟṟukkut teṟkē kaṉṉiyākumari variyilumuḷḷa nilapparappu mūṉṟu peruvēntarālum ēḻu kuṟunila maṉṉarālum āḷap paṭṭatu. ivarkaḷi aṉṟip pala ciṟṟaracarkaḷum iruntārkaḷ. kaṭaṟ kariyi aṭuttavum veḷiyākavuḷḷavum pakutikaḷē periya aracarāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. malikaḷum kāṭukaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉarukku uriyaṉavā yiruntaṉa. kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkari ōramākak kiruṭṭiṇā āṟutoṭakkam irāmanātapurappakutiyiluḷḷa toṇṭi variyiluḷḷa nāṭu cōḻar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. itaṉ mattiyil kāñci āḷukikkuṭpaṭṭa tirukkōyilūric cūḻnta malināṭu maliyamāṉukku uriyatāyiruntatu. cōḻa irācciyattukkut teṟkē pāṇṭiya nāṭu iruntatu. itu kaṭaṟkariyi liruntu kaṭaṟkari variyum parantu ikkāla maturi tirunelvēli tiruvi tāṅkūr mutaliya mākāṇaṅkaḷum kōyamuttūr kocci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum aṭaṅkiyatāyiruntatu. mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyil āyiṉ mali nāṭu iruntatu. tālami āyi ayoy (Aioi) eṉak kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷāṉ. tirunelvēliyiluḷḷa koṟkit tuṟi mukatti aṭuttu evvināṭu iruntatu. paḻaṉi maliyic cuṟṟip pēkaṉ nāṭu iruntatu. itaṟku vaṭakkē kaṭaṟkariyi aṭuttuc cēranāṭu iruntatu. itupālak kāṭṭuveḷikku ūṭāka cēlam kōyamuttūr variyum nīṇ ṭiruntatu. teṉ micūril toṭaṅki oṉṟiṉ pakkattē oṉṟāka iruṅkōvēḷiṉ arayam nāṭum, pāriyiṉ paṟampu nāṭum, atiyamāṉiṉ takaṭūrum (tarumapuri), ōriyiṉ kollimali yum iruntaṉa. mutal mūṉṟu nāṭu kaḷum micūr ellikkuḷ irun taṉa. ivvellikku appāl kiḻakkē kaṅkar nāṭum teṟkē koṅku nāṭum iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭ ṭiṉ mēṟkēyuḷḷa vaṭa ellip puṟam tuḷuva nāṭṭaracaṉākiya naṉṉaṉukkum, kiḻakkēyuḷḷa vaṭa ellippuṟam vēṅkaṭattup pullikkum, uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. ataṟku vaṭakkē āriya nāṭum (vaṭukarnāṭu), taṇṭāraṇiyamum iruntaṉa.
teṉṉāṭṭil paṭiyeṭuppukaḷ
muriya cantirakuptaṉ kālamvariyil teṉṉāṭṭil ayal nāṭṭup paṭi eṭuppukaḷ nēravilli. cantirakuptaṉuṭiya maraṇattukkup piṉ avaṉ makaṉ pintucāraṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉmītu paṭi eṭuttāṉ. itaṉmēl iṭiyiṭiyē vaṭanāṭṭavar paṭi eṭuppukaḷ nērntaṉa. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracar āriyariveṉṟu villum puliyum mīṉumākiya tattam koṭikaḷi mēruvil nāṭṭiyum ak kuṟikaḷi ammaliyil tīṭṭiyum uḷḷārkaḷ eṉak tamiḻp paḻam pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvaṉa āriyar tamiḻar pōrkaḷiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. cēra aracaruḷ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉum, pāṇṭiya aracaruḷ neṭuñceḻiyaṉum, cōḻa aracaruḷ karikālaṉum āriyari veṉṟatāka varalāṟu uṇṭu.
kaḷappirar
ki.pi. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaḷappirar eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar kiḷampit toṇṭi nāṭu oḻinta maṟṟi mūvēntar nāṭukaḷiyum aṭippaṭutti ēṟakkuṟiya irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ āṭci purintaṉar. kaḷappirar eṅkiruntu vantārkaḷ eṉpatum, piṟavum aṟiyamuṭiyāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṭuṅkōṉ kaḷappirari veṉṟu pāṇṭiya irācciyatti avariṭamiruntu mīṭṭāṉ.
pallavar
ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭumutal pallavar kāñciyit talinaka rākakkoṇṭu toṇṭināṭṭi āṇṭu vantaṉar. toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ maṇipallavattu nākakulappeṇṇukkum cōḻaṉukkum piṟantavaṉeṉṟu caṅkanūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṇipallava araca kuṭumpap peṇvaḻit tōṉṟiya iḷantiriyaṉiṉ cantatiyiṉar pallavar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷākalām. pallavam koṭiyik kuṟikkum. pallavar āṭci oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil maṟintupōyiṟṟu. pallavam eṉpataṉ maṟu peyarē toṇṭi eṉpatu es.kē. aiyaṅkār karuttu.
cāḷukkiyar
āntira nāṭṭiṉ mēṟkukkariyil vintayamalikkut teṟkilum cēranāṭṭu ellikku vaṭakkilum cāḷukkiyar eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar atikāramuṭiyavarāy ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkam āṭci purintaṉar. cāḷukkiyarukkum pallavarukkum pōrkaḷ naṭantaṉa. pulukēci eṉṉum cāḷukkiya vēntaṉ makēntiravaṉmaṉ eṉṉum pallava aracaṉōṭu porutu kiḻakkē kiruṭṭiṇāvukkum kōtāvarikkum iṭiyiluḷḷa veṅki nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi ataṉit taṉatu tampiyi āḷumpaṭi niyamittāṉ. atu kālattil kiḻakku cāḷukkiya nāṭu eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
ūr amippu
kōyiliccuṟṟi allatu aracaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyiccuṟṟiyē nakaraṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ovvoru kirāmattukkum kōyil mattiya iṭamāka viruntatu.1 makkaḷ kōyilkaḷiccuṟṟik kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. ovvoru toḻil ceyvōrum taṉittaṉi vītikaḷil vāḻntārkaḷ. kirāmattiṉ naṭuvil veḷiyiruntatu. atu maṉṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. aṅku niḻalmaram niṉṟatu. atic cuṟṟit tiṇṇiyiṭappaṭṭiruntatu. ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil makkaḷ aṅkē kūṭip poḻutupōkkiṉārkaḷ. aṅku ūrkkūṭṭaṅkaḷuṅ kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. niyāyattīrppum aṅku ceyyappaṭṭatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅ kaḷum aṅku naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ellāvakiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷukkum kiḷarcci kaḷukkum maṉṟam mattiya iṭamāyiruntatu. aṅku aruḷtaṟi niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. kirāmattil naṭakkavēṇṭiya ovvoru mukkiya nikaḻcciyip paṟṟiyum aṅku kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kūṭṭi ālōcikkappaṭṭaṉa. kirāmaṅkaḷ kirāmacapikaḷāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum avvak kirāmaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāl kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṟiyap paṭṭaṉa. ovvoru kōyilum ōr araṇmaṉiyiṉ amippip pōlak kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. aracaṉuṭiya māḷikiyum kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu.
nakaram
aracaṉuṭiya māḷiki allatu araṇmaṉiyic cuṟṟi matil iṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṟku aṭuttu araca kuṭumpattiṉarum aracaṉatu karumattalivarkaḷ vāḻum vītikaḷum irukkum. ivvītikaḷiccuṟṟi matiliṭappaṭṭirukkum. matilukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa vītikaḷil paṟpala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vāḻvārkaḷ. aṅku kaṭikaḷum kaṭitteruk kaḷum uṇṭu. pakivar ilakuvil aḻikkavum ēṟavum muṭiyāta matil nakaric cūḻntu iruntatu. matiliṉ vāyilkaḷukku periya maraṅkaḷāl irumpu cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭa katavukaḷ iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi paḻiya kōyilkaḷiliṭappaṭṭuḷḷa katavukaḷ pōṉṟavi. uḷḷē tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷāl avi tāḻiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭi vāyilil koṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. matiṟcuvarkaḷ vīrarniṉṟu pōrceytaṟkēṟṟa akalamuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. koti eṇṇeyi iṟippaṉavum, neruppi vīcuvaṉavum, kal eṟivaṉavum, ampu umiḻvaṉavum pōṉṟa poṟikaḷ pala matiṟcuvarkaḷil amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷimatilic cuṟṟip periya akaḻ iruntatu. itu āḻamuṭiyatākavum nīr niṟivuṭiyatākavum iruntatu. nīril periya mutalikaḷum mīṉkaḷum iruntaṉa. kōṭṭivāyililiruntu akaḻiyik kaṭakkak kūṭiyatākap palaki iṭappaṭṭiruntatu. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil palaki eṭukkappaṭṭatu. akaḻil veḷiyē muḷmaraṅkaḷ naṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kāvaṟkāṭu iruntatu. aṅku vēṭṭuvar vāḻntaṉar. ataṟku veḷiyē viḷi nilaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
ki.pi. 1225il intiyāvukku vantu tirumpiya1 cīṉap pirayāṇi oruvaṉ eḻutiyiruppatu koṇṭu akkālak kōṭṭi evvā ṟiruntateṉṟu. nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.
“iṅku (cōḻanāṭṭil) ēḻucuṟṟumatilkaḷuḷḷa nakaram uṇṭu. vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkunōkkiyuḷḷa veḷimatiliṉ nīḷam paṉṉiraṇṭu li (LI) kiḻakkiliruntu mēṟkē cellum matiliṉ nīḷam ēḻu ‘li’ ovvoru cuṟṟu matilukkumiṭiyil nūṟaṭi veḷiyuṇṭu. ēḻu matilkaḷil nāṉku ceṅkallāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭavi; iraṇṭu kaḷimaṇṇāl eṭukkappaṭṭavi; mattiyiluḷḷatu marattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. aṅku paḻamaraṅkaḷum pū maraṅkaḷum naṭappaṭṭa cōli uṇṭu. mutal iraṇṭu cuvarkaḷukku iṭiyiluḷḷa veḷiyil makkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ uṇṭu. avi akaḻiyāṟ cūḻappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟām nāṉkām cuvarkaḷuk kiṭiyiluḷḷa veḷiyil aracāṅka karumakārarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷuṇṭu. aintāvatu matilukkuḷ aracaṉuṭiya kumārar vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṟāvatu cuvarukkuḷ kōyilkaḷum pūcārikaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷum uṇṭu. ēḻāvatu cuvarukkuḷ nāṉūṟuvīṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya araṇmaṉi uṇṭu.”
muriyacantirakuptaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyippaṟṟic collap paṭum viparamum piṟavum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiluma utayaṇaṉ katiyilum kūṟappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi iṅku tarukiṉṟōm.
“aracaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉi periya pūñcōliyiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷatu. aṅku paḻakiya mayiṟ cēvalkaḷum aḻakiya kōḻi iṉaṅkaḷum ulāvit tirintaṉa. niḻal maraṅkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu piṉṉik kiṭantaṉa. aṅku nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ eppoḻutum pacciyāka irukkum. avi orupōtum ili utirttuvatilli. aṅku paṟavikaḷ pala vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. avi kiḷikaḷil kūṭukaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. aṅku pala kuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avikaḷil mikap periya paḻakiya mīṉkaḷ viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aracaṉuṭiya kumārarallāta piṟar ak kuḷaṅkaḷil mīṉpiṭikka aṉumatikkappaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.
aracaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyi alaṅkarikkum mulām pūcappaṭṭa tūṇkaḷil poṉkoṭikaḷ cuṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷmītu veḷḷi yāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṟavikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. araṇmaṉiyiccuṟṟi matilum akaḻum uḷḷaṉa. araṇmaṉiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil peṇkaḷ uṟiyum aṟikaḷ uṇṭu. aṅku kūṉum kuṟaḷum iruppar. peṇkaḷ uṟiyum aṟikaḷukku veḷiyē irācakumārarum irācakumārikaḷum uṟiyum aṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. avikaḷukku veḷiyē kolumaṇṭapa mum karumakārariṉ aṟikaḷum iruntaṉa. aracaṉukkut taṉi aṟikaḷ iruntaṉa.
araṇmaṉiyil pala irakaciya aṟikaḷum, curaṅka vaḻikaḷum, uḷḷē veḷiyuḷḷa tūṇkaḷum, maṟivāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum, kīḻē viḻuntu viṭakkūṭiya tariyum iruntaṉa.-Chandra Gupta Maurya and his time-p 194.-R K. Mookerji.
aracaṉum āṭciyum
aracaṉuṭiya atikāram ellā atikāraṅkaḷukkum mēlāka viruntatu. tantikkuppiṉ makaṉ eṉṟavāṟu aracurimi talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vantatu. aracaṉuṭiya kaṭami tuṭṭarit taṇṭippatum makkaḷiṉ amitiyik kāppatumākum. araṉuṭiya viruppamē nāṭṭiṉ caṭṭam; ataṉi maṟuttal evarukkum muṟiyākātu. pōril aracaṉē paṭiyi naṭattic ceṉṟāṉ. camātāṉa kālattil ellāt tīrppukaḷukkum avaṉē talivaṉāka viruntāṉ.
araṉ irukkum maṇṭapam mikavum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruntatu. avaṉic cūḻntu pala āṭamparaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. makkaḷ ataṉiyē virumpiṉārkaḷ.1 aracaṉ irukkumiṭam kolu, aracirukki allatu olakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. aracirukkivēḷi allāta maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷil aracaṉ makkaḷōṭu aḷavaḷāviṉāṉ. nakarattil naṭakkum viḻākkaḷilum avaṉ kalantukoḷvāṉ. avaṉuṭiya veṟṟikkākavum nīṇṭa vāḻviṟkākavum kōyilkaḷil tutikaḷ collappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ taṅkum māḷikikaḷ avaṉ nāṭṭiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa. nāṭu pōriṉṟi amitiyuṟṟirukkum kālaṅkaḷil aracaṉ perumakkaḷōṭu nāṭu muḻuvatiyum cuṟṟivantāṉ. āṭci avaṉ atikārattil iruntapōtum oḻuṅkum nītiyum nilaviṉa. aracaṉic cūḻntu pirapukkaḷ, perumakkaḷ, karuma viṉiñar mutaliyōr iruntaṉar. mukkiyamāṉa karumaṅkaḷic ceyvataṉ muṉ avaṉ uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷ eṉṉum kūṭṭat tārōṭu ālōcaṉi ceytāṉ. aṉupavamum kalviyum uḷḷavarkaḷē ālōcaṉiyāḷarāka avaṉāl teriyappaṭṭaṉar. kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aracirukika maṇṭapattil naṭantaṉa. araca kaṭṭaḷikaḷ nīṇṭa ālōcaṉikkup piṉṉarē veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭaḷikaḷ veḷiyāṉatum peruntaram allatu piramarāyaṉ eṉappaṭṭa amiccaṉ avikaḷit tirumantira ōlikkāraṉ eṉṉum mūloli kāppāṟṟum atikāriyāl pottakattil eḻutumpaṭi ceytāṉ. piṉpu avi paṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu viṭiyil atikārikaḷāl irācciyattiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkup pōkkap paṭṭaṉa. ak kaṭṭaḷikaḷ kirāmak kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkum aṟivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ik kūṭṭaṅ kaḷukku aracaṉuṭiya makkaḷ allatu uṟaviṉarē talivarākaviruntaṉar. ak kaṭṭaḷikaḷ mukkiyamuṭiyaṉavum niliyāṉavumāyiṉ avi kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉukkup potu amiccar, antaraṅka amiccar mutaliyōr iruntaṉar. araṇmaṉiyil talimip peruntaṉam (karuvūlam) iruntatu. aṅkiruntu pōr vīrar, karumakārar mutaliyōr campaḷattukkum araṇmaṉic celavukkum paṇam eṭukkappaṭṭatu. mākāṇaṅkaḷil ciṟuntaṉaṅkaḷ1 iruntaṉa. ivi perumpālum kōyilkaḷiliruntaṉa. ivikkup poruḷ perumpālum iṟiyili nilaṅkaḷālum, naṉkoṭi kaḷālum vantatu. ipporuḷil kōyiṟ celavu pōka mīti makkaḷ nalattiṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
aracāṅkattil matippum oḻukkamum vāynta oṟṟar iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ mākāṇat talivarkaḷiṉip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi aṟintu aracaṉukkut terivittārkaḷ. aracaṉ māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu iraviṟceṉṟu makkaḷiṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷi aṟintāṉ. oṟṟar tuṟavikaḷ, vaṇikar pōṉṟu vēṭantāṅki ayal nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu paki aracariṉ maṟivāṉa ceytikaḷi aṟintu vantaṉar. aracaṉit talivaṉākakkoṇṭu āṭci ceyyum muṟiyi āriya makkaḷ tirāviṭariṭamiruntu aṟintārkaḷ eṉpataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1avarkaḷ tamatu moḻiyi yum tirāviṭariṉ utaviyip peṟṟut tiruttik koṇṭaṉar.2
maṇṭala āṭci
āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa nilam āḷukiyiṉ poruṭṭu 3maṇṭalaṅkaḷ allatu mākāṇaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. maṇṭalaṅkaḷ nāṭukaḷākavum vaḷanāṭukaḷākavum pirikkap paṭṭaṉa. ovvoru nāṭṭilum pala kirāma capikaḷ iruntaṉa. pala kirāmaṅkaḷ cērāta kirāmam taṉiyūr eṉappaṭṭatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ cērikaḷākavum kuṭumpukaḷā(vaṭṭaṅkaḷ)kavum pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cila kōṭṭaṅkaḷ aracaṉiṉ mēṟpārviyiṉkīḻ nāṭṭukkuriya talivaṉāl (kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ) āḷappaṭṭaṉa. maṟṟa maṇṭa laṅkaḷukku aracaṉ maṇṭalikari niyamittāṉ. avarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālam avarkaḷiṉ oḻukkattiyum aracaṉiṉ nalleṇṇattiyum poṟuttiruntatu. aracaṉ maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉ pōkkittāṉ ceyyum cuṟṟup payaṇaṅkaḷālum oṟṟarkaḷālum aṟintāṉ.
maṇṭalikaṉ varikaḷit taṇṭiṉāṉ; niyāyat tīrppuc ceytāṉ; nāṭṭil amitiyum niyāyamum nilavumpaṭiyum, āṭci toṭarpāṉa karumaṅkaḷi naṉku naṭipeṟumpaṭiyum ceytāṉ.
maṇṭalaṅkaḷ muḻumiyilum cuṟṟup payaṇañceytu makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu vittiruttal, kaṇakku kaḷip paricōtittal, capikaḷik kaṇkāṇittal mutaliyaṉa avaṉuṭiya kaṭamikaḷ. avaṉ kōyiṟ kaṇakku kaḷiyum paricōtittāṉ. kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷ aintu pēraṭaṅkiya kūṭṭattōṭu maṇṭalika rāl ārāyappaṭṭaṉa.
maṇṭalikarukku aṭuttapaṭiyi luḷḷavar nāṭṭut talivar. avar nāṭukaṇkāṭci allatu nāṭuvāki eṉappaṭṭār. nāṭṭut talivarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ āṭcimuṟiyik kaṇkāṇittārkaḷ.
kirāmacapi
ovvoru kirāmattiṉ āṭcikkum kirāmacapi poṟuppā yiruntatu. mākāṇa atikārikaḷ paṭit talivarōṭu mākāṇam muḻumi yum payaṇañceytu vicāraṇikaḷ ceytum kaṇakkukaḷi mēṟpārttum vantaṉar. kirāmacapikaḷ kōyilkaḷil kūṭiṉa. kirāma capi uṟuppiṉariṉ eṇ kirāmaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu iruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil ainnūṟu pēr variyil iruntaṉar.
aṅkattavarkaḷit teriyum vāyppukkākak kirāmam pala kuṭumpu (vaṭṭaṅkaḷ)kaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru kuṭumpum capikku aṅkattavar ākakkūṭiyavarkaḷiṉ peyar eḻutiya ōlinaṟukkuk kaṭṭoṉṟi aṉuppiṟṟu. kalviyum cottum iruppatu uṟuppiṉaṉāva taṟkut takutiyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. kālvēlikku atikam varikoṭukkum nilamuḷḷavaṉ allatu taṉatu conta nilattil vīṭukaṭṭi vāḻpavaṉ terivukkut takutiyuṭiyavaṉākak koḷḷappaṭṭāṉ. aṅkattavaṉākat teriyappaṭupavaṉ 35 vayatukkum 70 vayatukkum iṭippaṭṭavaṉāyirut tal vēṇṭum. avaṉ kaṭanta mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷil capi uṟuppiṉaṉāka iruntavaṉātal kūṭātu. kuṭumpukaḷukkuc cīṭṭukaḷ mūlam aṟivikkap paṭṭapiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. ataṟku ellāk kōyil nampikaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷil mutiyavaṉ capikkut talimi vakit tāṉ. ac cīṭṭukaḷ ellām oru veṟum pāṉikkuḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ avikaḷuḷ oṉṟi eṭuttāṉ. ap peyar vācikkappaṭṭatu. ap peyaruṭiyavaṉ kuṭumpiṉ aṅkattavaṉākat teriyappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu ovvoru kuṭumpiṉ aṅkattiṉarum teriyap paṭṭārkaḷ.
kuḷaṅkaḷik kaṇkāṇippōr
kuḷaṅkaḷ āṇṭutōṟum tiruttappaṭuvati mēṟpārppataṟku oru capi iruntatu. atu kuḷattil cēṟṟi appuṟappaṭuttutal, nīrp pāyccutal, payiriṭuvōrukkuc camamāka nīr aḷittal mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇkāṇittatu. vītikaḷi amittal avikaḷi akalamākkutal mutaliyavikaḷi mēṟpārkkum capiyum iruntatu.
kuṭumpuvāriyāka aṅkattavar teriyappaṭṭa piṉpu capi avar kaḷip palavēṟu vēlikaḷip pārkkumpaṭi terivuceytatu. ovvoru aṅkattavaṉum kaṭami pārkkum kālam 360 nāḷkaḷākum. ic capiyiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ ‘karṇattāṉ’ eṉṉum kaṇakkaṉāl eḻuti vikkappaṭṭaṉa. kōyil nīṅkalākak kirāmattiṉ nirvākam capiyiṉ poṟuppil iruntatu.
niyāyattīrppu
aracaṉ tāṉē nēril niyāyat tīrppuc ceytāṉ. uṭaṉ kūṭṭattār avaṉukku ñāyattīrppil utavipurintaṉar. ciṟiya vaḻakkukaḷ ovvoru cēriyiyum mēṟpārkkum capiyāl viḷaṅkit tīrppaḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. kolivaḻakkukaḷ kirāmacapiyāl vicāraṇi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; cila camayaṅkaḷil mākāṇat talivar vicāraṇi ceytār. mutaṉmiyāṉa vaḻakkukaḷ paṭittalivaṉāl (cēṉāpatiyāl) aivar aṭaṅkiya capipiṉ utaviyōṭu vicāraṇi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa vaḻakkukaḷē aracaṉuṭiya vicāraṇikku viṭappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ nāṭukaḷic cuṟṟip payaṇam ceyyum kālaṅkaḷil avi vicāraṇi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
tiruṭṭukkuṟṟattukkup paṇam aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu. at taṇṭam taruma ācaṉam eṉappaṭṭatu. oppantaṅkaḷimīṟiya kuṟṟaṅ kaḷukkup paṇam aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu; cila camayaṅkaḷil ciṟit taṇṭaṉiyum vitikkappaṭṭatu. kaṇṇukkukkaṇ kiyukkukki eṉpatu pōla uṟuppukaḷik kaḷintuviṭuvatu potuvāṉa caṭṭamāka viruntatu. koti eṇṇiyil kivittal pōṉṟa kuṟṟaccōtaṉi aṟiyappaṭāta taṉṟu. kolikkut taṇṭaṉi koliyākaviruntatu. vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceyyappaṭāta kolikku 16 pacu māṭukaḷ aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu. iṟantavariṉ uyir āṟutal aṭitaṟkuk kirāmak kōyilil eppoḻutum eriyumpaṭi neyviḷakku ēṟṟi vikkappaṭṭatu. irācatturōkattukkuc caṭṭappaṭi taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. irācatturōkañ ceytavariṉ cottukkaḷ viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. avvāṟu kiṭittaporuḷ kōyilkaḷuk kum aṟaniliyaṅkaḷukkum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. nakarak kāvalar (Police) talivaṉ taṇṭanāyakaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. nakarkkāvalar vītikaḷikkāval purintaṉar. avarkaḷ patikāval eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ.
paṭi
tamiḻ aracar oruvari maṟṟavar kīḻppaṭuttumpaṭi perumpālum pōr viḷittaṉar. tēr, yāṉi, kutiri, kālāḷ eṉap paṭi nālvakip paṭṭatu. yāṉi vīraṉ pulittōṟ caṭṭi aṇintu yāṉimītu iruntāṉ. avaṉic cūḻntu paṭikkalan tāṅkiya vīrar ceṉṟaṉar. tērkaḷ niṟamūṭṭiya tuṇikaḷil alaṅ karikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi pōrkkaḷat tukku niriyākac ceṉṟaṉa. pōril vil, vēl, īṭṭi, vāḷ, kavaṇ, mutaliya pala vaki āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṭikaḷ aṇi, uṇṭi, oṭṭu mutaliya pala aṇivakuppukaḷāka niṉṟu pōrceytaṉa. muṉ aṇi ākkam, koṭippaṭi, tār, tūci eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpaṭi kūḻi eṉappaṭṭatu.
nāṭu irāṇuva campantamāṉa pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. 1paṭittalivaṉ aracaṉōṭu nēril toṭarpu vittiruntāṉ. paṭi yiṉ perum pakuti kālāḷ. muṉṉē kutirip paṭiyum yāṉip paṭiyum ceṉṟaṉa. tērkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭavilli. arāpiyar kirēkkar mutaliya ayal nāṭṭiṉarum paṭiyil iruntārkaḷ. tūtuvari ava māṉappaṭuttuvatum kolvatum nītik kēṭākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ic caṭṭatti mīṟuvatāl pōrkaḷ mūṇṭaṉa.
vari
nilavari tāṉiya vakiyākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. perumpālum viḷivil āṟiloṉṟu variyākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷum tōppukaḷum paṇavakiyil variyi iṟuttaṉa. varik kaṇakku eḻutum ēṭu varip pottakam eṉappaṭṭatu. varitaṇṭum karumikaḷ (uttiyōkattar) iruntārkaḷ. ovvoru nāṭṭilum uḷḷa periya vītiyil cuṅkam taṇṭappaṭṭatu. cuṅkam koṭāviṭiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷi appuṟaṅ koṇṭu cellamuṭiyāmal iruntatu.
pōril kiṭitta koḷḷip poruḷ, aracāṅkattukkuriya nilaṅkaḷ, kāṭu, muttuk kuḷippup pōṉṟa vakiyilum aracaṉatu karuvūlattukkup poruḷ vantatu. ivikaḷallāmal kaṭi vari, necavu vari, cekku vari pōṉṟa varikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. kaṇṇālak kaṭam eṉṉum kaliyāṇa variyum iruntatu.1
nīrpāyccum vāyppu
aracar payirukku nīrppāyccum vacatikaḷiatikam ceytaṉar. karikāṟ cōḻaṉ kaṭṭiya kāviri aṇi kiṭṭiya kālam variyil iruntatu. āṟukaḷum, kiḷi āṟukaḷum nīrppāyccum vāyppiṉ poruṭṭu maṟittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. tiricciṉāppaḷḷiyil kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻapurat tiluḷḷa kuḷattiṉ aṇikkaṭṭup patiṉāṟu mil nīḷamuḷḷatu. pala veṭṭu vāykkālkaḷum veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. celvar vīti ōraṅkaḷil kiṇaṟukaḷ veṭṭic cumitāṅki amittārkaḷ. vītikaḷiṉ apāyamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil pōrvīrar niṟuttappaṭṭiruntaṉar. ākavē pōkku varattuc ceyvōr tiruṭar payamiṉṟic ceṉṟaṉar. kālvāy veṭṭutal ēri veṭṭutal pōṉṟavikaḷ makkaḷāl kaṭṭāyappaṭuttic ceyvikkap paṭṭaṉa. avvakivēli ūḻiyam, āḷmāñci eṉappaṭṭatu. āṟṟuk kālveṭṭa, ērikuḻiveṭṭa, cumi tūkka eṉṉum colvaḻakkukaḷ paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracarukkuppuriyum kaṭṭāya ūḻiyam ilaṅkiyil irācakāriyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
poḻutupōkku
maṟpōr, cilampam, tērōṭṭam, yāṉippōr, kutiri ōṭṭam, māṭṭup pōr, āṭṭup pōr, kōḻip pōr, kāṭi kavutārippōr eṉpaṉa makkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa poḻutu pōkkukaḷākum. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil aṭikkappaṭṭu irukkavilli; avarkaḷ ammāṉi, pantu, pāṭṭu, āṭṭam, kummi, kuravi viḷiyāṭṭukaḷiṟ poḻutu pōkkiṉar; kiḷi, nākaṇavāy mutaliyavikaḷi vaḷarttaṉar.
pōkkuvarattu
muṟkālap pōkkuvarattu perumpālum kālnaṭi, vaṇṭi, pallakku, taṇṭiki, kaḻuti, erutu, yāṉi, kutiri, tēr vakikaḷā yiruntaṉa. ōriṭattiliruntu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkup paṇṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭu celvataṟkup potimāṭukaḷum kaḻutikaḷum vaṇṭikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. aracarum perumakkaḷum tēr pallakku, yāṉi mutaliyavi kaḷil ceṉṟaṉar.
kaṭaliṟ celvataṟkup palavaki marakkalaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. marakkalaṅ kaḷiṉ muṉpuṟam, ciṅka mukam, yāṉi mukam, kutiri mukam pōṉṟu ceyyap paṭṭiruntatu. marakkalaṅkaḷukkup pāy maraṅkaḷum pāyum iruntaṉa.
aḷavikaḷ
eṇṇey, ney, tayir pōṉṟaviyum tāṉiyaṅkaḷum aḷantu viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavi niṟuttuviṟkappaṭṭaṉa. tāṉiya aḷavi; 5 ceviṭu 1 āḻākku; 2 āḻākku 1 uḻakku; 2 uḻakku 1 uri; 2 uri 1 nāḻi; 8 nāḻi 1 kuṟuṇi; 2 kuṟuṇi 1 patakku; 4 kuṟuṇi allatu 2 patakku 1 tūṇi; 3 tūṇi allatu 12 kuṟuṇi 1 kalam.
paḻiya paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟāl perumpālum poruḷkaḷ paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. 2 paṭi aricikku 5 paṭi nelluk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. 5 paṭi nelluk kuttuvataṟku arippaṭi arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel 1/16 nāḻi neyyukkum, oru nāḻi tayir 10p pākku, 20 vāḻi ili allatu 1/16 nāḻi neykkum paṇṭamāṟṟuṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. nellum tayirum orē peṟupati uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa veṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. nāḷ vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvataṟku nel payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. paṇappuḻakkam iruntapōtum potuvāka nellē paṇattippōlap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
poṉ veḷḷi niṟi: 1 kaḻañcu 20 mañcāṭi; 1 mañcāṭi 2 kuṟuṇi; 1 mā 1/10 mañcāṭi; 1 kāṇi 1/40 mañcāṭi.
1 “akkālattē niṟuttal, mukattal mutaliya aḷavukaṭkum, poṉ irattiṉaṅkaḷ niṟuttaṟkum, poṉ māṟṟu nōkkutaṟkum aracāṅkattā rāl niyamikkappaṭṭa marakkāl mutaliyaṉa viruntaṉa. aracaṉ muttiriyiṭṭa marakkālukku irācirācaṉ kālattil irācakēcari eṉpatu peyar. itaṟkuc camāṉamāṉa tañcāvūrk kōyil marakkālukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. āṉāl nāṭutōṟum marakkāl peyarkaḷ māṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa. aracāṅka muttiriyiṭṭa eṭikkallukkuk kuṭiñikkal eṉṟu peyar. tañcāvūrk kōyilil itaṟkuc cama eṭiyuḷḷa kallukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. (ip peyarkaḷ civapirāṉ peyarāliṭappaṭṭaṉa) poṉmāṟṟukku vittirunta āṇikkut taṇṭavāṇiyeṉpatu peyar.”
tamiḻar tirumaṇam
paruvam aṭinta āṭavarum makaḷirum oruvari oruvar kātalittuc ciṟitukālam piṟar aṟiyātu cērntu oḻukuvārkaḷ eṉṟum piṉ āṭavaṉ taṉatu kātaliyit taṉatu ūrukku yārumaṟiyātu koṇṭu ceṉṟu aṅku maṇantukoḷvāṉ; allatu maṇamakaḷatu peṟṟōriṉ icivip peṟṟu avaḷ illattilēyē avaḷi maṇantukoḷvāṉ eṉṟum caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ip pāṭalkaḷ paḻiya marapip piṉpaṟṟic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi akkālat tirumaṇam avvāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tuṇital muṭiyātu.
ak kālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ maṇañ ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntāl avaṉ cila mutiyōrip peṇkēṭkumpaṭi peṇvīṭṭukku aṉuppu vāṉ. peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr peṇkoṭukka icintaṉarāyiṉ, kaṇi eṉap paṭṭa cōtiṭaṉāl kuṟikkappaṭṭa nallanāḷilē maṇamakaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattā ruṭaṉ maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭukkuvantu peṇṇi maṇantu taṉatu vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭupōvāṉ. tirumaṇattukku muṉ peṇṇukku viliyāka maṇamakaṉ pariyam eṉap poruḷ vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. ivvāṟu koṭukkappaṭum poruḷ cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḷarppukkūli eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. peṇkaḷi vilikoṭuttu vāṅki maṇattalākiya vaḻakkattiṉ niḻalē pariyam eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. tirāviṭa makkaḷuḷ cila kūṭṭattiṉar peṇkaḷi vili koṭuttu vāṅki maṇantukoḷḷum vaḻakkam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil purōkitariṉ utaviyiṉṟiyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. ak kālat tirumaṇaccaṭaṅku evvāṟirunta teṉpatic caṅka nūlkaḷik koṇṭu aṟitalkūṭum. akanāṉūṟṟil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷati iṅku tarukiṉṟōm.
“cōṟu paruppiṭṭuc camikkappaṭṭatu. palarkūṭi uṇṭapiṉpum cōṟu kuvintukiṭantatu. niriyākak kālkaḷi naṭṭup pantariṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tariyilveṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭiruntatu. viḷakkukaḷ koḷutti vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pūraṇi nāḷiṉ viṭiyaṟkāliyil tīyakōḷkaḷ akaṉṟa nalla nēramāka viruntatu. taliyilē kuṭattiyum kiyilē maṭpāttirattiyum vittukkoṇṭu peṇkaḷ vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kiyil vittirunta pāttiratti oruvar kiyiliruntu oruvarkikku māṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu mutiya peṇkaḷ periya āravārañceytaṉar. piṉpu āṇmakavi yīṉṟavarkaḷum, vayiṟṟil aḻakutēmal uṭiyavarkaḷum aḻakiya nakikaḷ aṇintavarkaḷumākiya peṇkaḷ muṉṉē vantārkaḷ. maṇamakaḷiṉ taliyil nīri ūṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu nīril kiṭanta pūvum nellum kūntalil kiṭantu viḷaṅkiṉa. appoḻutu avarkaḷ “nī kaṟpilvaḻuvātu kaṇavaṉukku iṉiyaḷākaviruntu neṭuṅkālam vāḻvā yāka” eṉa vāḻttiṉāhkaḷ. aṉṟu mālikkālam vantatu. peṇkaḷ vantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. “nī vīṭṭukkup peruntaliviyākuka” eṉṟu vāḻttiṉārkaḷ. cuṟṟattavar avaḷ kiyip piṭittu maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ.” (akam. 86)
“nalla ariciyic camitta neykalanta cōṟṟic cuṟṟattavar mutiyavarkaḷukkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. naṟcakuṉam kāṭṭum paṟavikaḷ teḷinta vāṉattē paṟantaṉa. tiṅkaḷ urōkiṇiyōṭu cērukiṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta nalla nēram vantatu. perukkic cuttañceyta vīṭu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. ellōrum kaṭavuḷit toḻutārkaḷ. periya mēḷamum maṇamuracum olittatu. maṇaviṉiyik kāṇa virumpiya peṇkaḷ virintuvantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. aṟukaṅkiḻaṅki veḷḷiya nūlāṟkaṭṭi vākip pūviṉ mēl vikkappaṭṭatum, maṇal parappappaṭṭatumākiya pantaliṉkīḻ tamar avaḷi maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ. maṇaviṉiyik kaṇṭa peṇkaḷ tamatu vīṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.” (akam. 136)
alaṅkāram
tamiḻ makkaḷ tamatu uṭampiyum tām payaṉpaṭuttiya ovvoru poruḷiyum alaṅ karittārkaḷ. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ muṟṟaṅkaḷil aḻakiya kōlaṅkaḷiṭṭaṉar. vīṭu kaḷit tāṅkum tūṇkaḷ, ki maraṅkaḷ, nilikaḷ, katavukaḷ, vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟaṅkaḷ tāṉum aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷil cuvarkaḷilum matilkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷilum aḻakiya uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruttali nām kāṇalām. intiyac ciṟpikaḷ kaṟkaḷi marattippōlap payaṉpaṭutti atil pala kaṇkavarum vēlippāṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. vīṭṭil payaṉ paṭuttum kaṭṭil, mukkāli, nāṟkāli, maṇi, āyutaṅkaḷiṉpiṭi, nīr aruntum pāttiraṅkaḷum aḻakiya vēlipāṭukaḷuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷilum, amarāvati, acantā, ellōrā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ alaṅkāra viruppattit terivikkiṉṟaṉa.1
peṇkaḷ tamatu kūntalip palavakiyāka vāri muṭintārkaḷ. kūntalmītu palavakip pūkkaḷic ceruki vēyntārkaḷ. cuṟāmīṉ, valampuriccaṅku, piṟi, irupakkamum yāṉi kuṭattiliruntu nīr koṭṭu vatum naṭuvē ilakkumi vīṟṟiruppatumākiya vaṭivuṭiya cītēviyār pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintārkaḷ. koṇṭiyil palavakip pūk kaḷiyum māliyiyum cūṭiṉārkaḷ; cantaṉam kuṅkumam mutaliyavi kaḷip pūci uṭali aḻakupaṭuttiṉārkaḷ; uṭampil mañcaḷpūcik kuḷit tārkaḷ. mārpu, tōḷkaḷil toyyil eṉṉum oruvaki kuḻampip pūci ataṉmēl karumpu pūṅkoṭi mutaliyavikaḷi eḻuti aḻaku ceytārkaḷ. kātil tōṭu allatu kuḻi aṇintārkaḷ. vaṟiya makkaḷ kātil ōliyic curuḷākkic cerukiṉārkaḷ. muttumāli, pavaḷamāli, caṅkili, kuṟaṅkuceṟi, kavāṉaṇi, paṭṭiki, cataṅki, kiṇkiṇi, cilampu, kaḻal, vaḷi, mōtiram, mēkali eṉpaṉa avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṟ cila. tiruppātam, tirumuṭi, puḷḷit toṅkal, koṟṟakkuṭi, poṟpū, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, kāṟi, aṭikkāṟi, aṉṉam, kiḷi, cōṉakacciṭukku, makuṭam, vāḷi, vaṭukavāḷi, cūṭakam, kaṇmalar, cuṭṭi, tāli, tālimaṇivaṭam, kaṇṭa ñāṇ, poṭṭu, patakkam, ēkāvali, kālvaṭam, kamalam, ceṭi, vākuvalayam, tuṭar eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa.
ici
2ovvoru nilattilum ovvoru ici vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kuṟiñci nilamakkaḷ kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. mullinila makkaḷ mullip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. atu iṉṟu mattiyamāvati eṉappaṭum. marutanila makkaḷ marutap paṇpāṭiṉāhkaḷ. atu kētāram eṉappaṭum. neytalukkuriya paṇ iṉṟu puṉṉākavarāḷi eṉappaṭum. palavaki mēḷaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. vevvēṟu ōcikaḷip piṟappikkum paṟikaḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅ kaḷil koṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. māṭukaḷik koḷḷiyiṭum pōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu ēṟukōṭpaṟi, veṟiyāṭumpōtu aṟiyappaṭu vatu murukiyam. maṇak kālattil cāṟṟappaṭuvatu maṇamuḻavu. tēriḻukkum pōtu muḻakkappaṭuvatu tērōṭṭuppaṟi. nellaṟukkum pōtu taṭṭappaṭuvatu nellarikkiṇi. aracaṉ veḷikkiḷampumpōtu olikkappaṭuvatu puṟappāṭṭuppaṟi. mīṉ piṭittaṟkuc cellumpōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu mīṉ kōṭpaṟi. cūṟi āṭumpōtu aṭikkappaṭuvatu cūṟikōṭṭi.
pōrkkālattil yāṉi, kutiri, kālāṭpaṭi kaḷukku uṟcākam mūṭṭum poruṭṭup palavaki vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi iyam, icik karuvi, vāttiyam, vācciyam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avi tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, narampukkaruvi, miṭaṟṟuk karuvi, tuḷikkaruvi eṉa nālvakippaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻa rukkuṭiya ēḻicikaḷ kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, kural, uḷi, viḷari, tāram. mūṅkil, koṉṟippaḻat tiṉ kōtu mutaliyavikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa kuḻal kaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ekkāḷa vakikaḷuḷ tāri, kāḷam, kākāḷam, ammiyam, ciṉṉam mutaliyaviyum, kompuvakiyil kōṭu, irali, vayir mutaliyavum iruntaṉa. narampuk karuviyil ciṟappuṭiyatu yāḻ 1avikaḷukku ēḻu, irupattoṉṟu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa narampukaḷ iruntaṉa.
paṟi, muracu, pēriki, ākuḷi, ellari, calliki, kiṇi mutaliyaṉa mēḷavakikaḷuṭcila. iṉṉum ilaṅkittīvil vaḻaṅkum mēḷavaki yil eḻupatukku mēluṇṭu. tāri uṭukki mutaliyaṉa pāṇar kiyil koṇṭutirintu aṭittuppāṭum ciṟiyamēḷaṅkaḷ.
tōṟkaruvikaḷ
“pērikki paṭaka miṭakki yuṭukki
cīrmiku mattaḷañ calliki karaṭiki
timili kuṭamuḻāt takki, kaṇappaṟi
tamarukan taṇṇumi tāvi ṟaṭāri
yantari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷiya
monti muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
nicāḷan tuṭumi ciṟupaṟi yaṭakka
māci ṟakuṇiccam viralēṟu pākan
tokka vupāṅkan tuṭiperum paṟiyeṉa
mikka nūlōr viritturit taṉarē”
uṭi
parutti intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭākum ceṭi, maṟṟiya nāṭṭumakkaḷ āṭi illāmal iruntapōtu allatu tōli uṭiyākak koṇ ṭirunta kālattil intiya makkaḷ pañci nūlākkavum, nūlāl āṭi neyyavum āṭikkup palavakic cāyaṅkaḷ ūṭṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu paḻiya cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. intiya nāṭṭu veppanilikkēṟpa makkaḷ aṟpa uṭiyiyē aṇintaṉar. āṭavar muḻaṅkālvariyum uṭi uṭuttu ariyil kaccukkaṭṭiyirun taṉar. mikappaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ taliyiccuṟṟi nāṭākkaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. piṉpu nāṭā pāki āka māṟiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ ariyil māttiram uṭi aṇintaṉar. peṇkaḷiṟcilar mārpik kaccukkaṭṭi maṟittaṉar. perum pālum peṇkaḷ arikkumēl uṭi aṇiyavilli. itaṉi cāṉci, amarāvati, piṟkālac ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷi lum kāṇappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu nām naṉku aṟiyalām. iṟavukki aṇiyum vaḻakkam akkālattil araṇmaṉikaḷilirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl koṇṭuvarap paṭṭirukkalāmeṉac cilar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil kirēkkap peṇkaḷ intiya nāṭṭukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭamiyi srāpō (Strabo) pōṉṟa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.1
2 “parutti intiya nāṭṭukku uriya ceṭi. maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōlkaḷi uṭukkumpōtu allatu uṭiyiṉṟiyirukkumpōtu tamiḻ makkaḷ pañciliruntu nūlnūṟṟu nūlāl aḻakiya āṭikaḷic ceytu uṭuttārkaḷ.3 taṟkālak kuṭiyiruppukaḷil nūlnūṟkum katirkaḷ kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. mokañ catarō kālattilēyē makkaḷ pañciliruntu āṭi neyyavum, ataṟkuc cāyamūṭṭavum, aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyavum aṟintiruntār kaḷeṉpatu aṅkukiṭitta maṇpāvikaḷik koṇṭu naṉku aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ āṭi neyya aṟi vataṉmuṉ marappaṭṭikaḷi yum taḻikaḷiyum uṭuttār kaḷ. pañcu uṭikkumuṉ maravuri tōṉṟiya tātaliṉ atu tuṟavikaḷukkum tūya uṭi yākap piṟkāla makkaḷāl karutappaṭṭatu. āṭiyik kuṟikka aimpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru peyarum akkālattil vaḻaṅkiya vev vēṟu vaki āṭiyik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pālāvipōṉṟa āṭi, iḻi ceṉṟa iṭam aṟiyamuṭi yātu nuṇṇiya nūlāl neyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mulli nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ āṭṭu mayiri liruntu kampaḷi ney tārkaḷ. āṭikaḷ veḷuttuk kañciyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṭavar veḷuttuk kañciyiṭṭa maṭiyik kulittu ariyiṟkaṭṭi ataṉ mēl oru āṭiyi varintu kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭavar caṭṭi tarikkavilli caṭṭi tarittal ūḻiyam purivōrukkuriyatāka viruntatu. iṟavukki aṇital iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ intiya aracariṉ meykāppāḷarā yirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl pukuttappaṭṭatākalām. paḻiya ōviyaṅ kaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum iṟavukki kāṇappaṭavilli. peṇkaḷ tamatu taṉaṅkaḷi aciyavoṭṭāmal kaccukkaṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. āṭavar talippāki aṇintārkaḷ. uttariyam tarittārkaḷ. kōvaṇam kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ.1
payircceyki
tamiḻ makkaḷ payirc ceykiyiyē mutaṉmiyāṉa toḻilākak koṇṭaṉar. “uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvār” eṉa vaḷḷuvarum uḻaviṉ ciṟappi mutaṉmiyākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya kamparum ēreḻupatil vēḷāṇ ciṟappi naṉkeṭuttiyampiyuḷḷār. payiriṭum nilaṅkaḷ naṉcey, puṉcey eṉa iruvakippaṭum. nel viḷiyum vayal naṉcey eṉavum maṟṟiya tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷiyum nilam puṉcey eṉavum paṭṭaṉa. payiriṭappaṭāta nilam taricu eṉappaṭṭatu. vēḷ eṉpatu nilattik kuṟikkum peyar. nilattit tiruttip payiriṭuvōr vēḷ āḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āḷar eṉpataṟku āḷpavar eṉṟu poruḷ. veḷḷatti ēri kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vittu veḷḷatti āṇṭami yiṉ vēḷāḷar veḷḷāḷar eṉavum paṭuvar. nilaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vēḷāḷar vēḷ allatu vēḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kuṟunila maṉṉarākavum iruntaṉar. vēḷāḷar koṭiyāṟ pukaḻpeṟṟōr. itaṉāl vēḷāṇmi eṉṉum collukkuk koṭi, upakāram eṉṉum poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. “iruntōmpi ilvāḻvatellām viruntōmpi-vēḷāṇmi ceytal poruṭṭu” eṉṉum kuṟaḷil vēḷāṇmi upakāram eṉṉum poruḷ tarutal kāṇka. cēra cōḻa pāṇaṭiya aracar vēḷāṇ marapil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē yāvar.
cāti
tamiḻ nāṭṭil nilam aintu vakiyiṉatāka viruntateṉa muṉṉō riṭattiṟ kūṟiṉōm. ovvoru nilamakkaḷum vevvēṟu peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oru nilattil vāḻum makkaḷ iṉṉoru nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷukkut tāḻntavareṉṟō uyarntavareṉṟō koḷḷum koḷki ak kālattil uṇṭākavilli. celvam, vaṟumi, oḻukkam, oḻukkak kēṭu eṉpavi pōṉṟa taṉmikaḷāl uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. uyarkuṭi eṉpatu pāvam paḻikaḷukku añcum kuṭi eṉpatu poruḷ. vaḷḷuvar tamatu nūlil aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭum kuṭip piṟappu ivviyalpiṉatātali aṟiya māṭṭātār atu vaṭanāṭṭār varuṇattit taḻuviyateṉa maruṇṭu kūṟuvar. “piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā ceytoḻil vēṟṟumiyāl” eṉa vaḷḷuvar piṟappiṉāl cātiyuṇṭeṉpāri maṟuttami kāṇka.
makkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē tiruttamaṭintu payirc ceyki vaḷarcci yaṭintatu. perumpālum nakar maruta nilattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulakilē paḻiya nākarikac ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nakaraṅkaḷ ellām vēḷāṇmi ceḻittōṅkiya āṟṟōraṅkaḷilēyē viḷaṅkiṉa. vēḷāṇmiyāl ceḻippuṟṟa nāṭukaḷil vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum uṇaviṉ poruṭṭum paṟpala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vantu taṅkuvā rāyiṉar. tantiyiṉ toḻili makaṉ ceytal eṉpatupōlak kālvaḻi kāl vaḻiyāka ovvoru kuṭumpamum perukiyapōtu ovvoru kuṭumpa mum taṉittaṉic cātiyākap perukiyatu. nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum cātippeyarkaḷ toḻilkaḷiyē kuṟittal kāṇka. taṉittaṉik kūṭṭa mākap perukiya makkaḷ tattamakkuḷḷēyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷ ḷum vaḻakkam nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu varutalālum cātik kaṭṭappāṭu vali aṭintatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil antaṇar aracar vaṇikar veḷḷāḷar eṉṉum nāṉku perum piriviṉar, iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷallāta kittoḻilum ēvaṟṟoḻilum ceyyum makkaḷum iruntaṉar. muṉ kūṟiya nālvarum uyarntōreṉṟum, piṉ kūṟiya iruvakiyiṉarum tāḻntōreṉṟum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar.
antaṇa reṉpōr tuṟavikaḷ. kōyiṟ pūci ceyyum makkaḷiyum kuṟikka ippeyar piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. āṭci purivōr aracar eṉavum, vāṇikam purivōr vaṇikar eṉavum, vēḷāṇmi ceyvōr vēḷāḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. ippirivukaḷ perumpālum nakaraṅkaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
iccātip pirivukaḷ maruta nilattilēyē perumpālum tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. vaṭanāṭṭavar toṭakkattil niṟampaṟṟip pirittirunta varuṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻaratu toḻil paṟṟiya cātikaḷum vēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa. varuṇam eṉpataṟku niṟam eṉpatu poruḷ. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ varuṇat tukkup piṟappē kāraṇam. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ pirāmaṇa, cattiriya, viciya, cūttira eṉṉum nāṟ pirivukaḷē teṉṉāṭṭu antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum pirivukaḷ eṉap piṟkālattavar mayaṅkiṉamiyāl pala taṭumāṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
antaṇar, pārppār, pirāmaṇar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ paṟṟiya mayakkam
antaṇar eṉpōr tuṟavikaḷāvar eṉpatu caṅka nūlkaḷālum, tirukkuṟaḷālum piṟacāṉṟukaḷālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kōyiṟ pūciceyvōr kōyilkaḷi mēṟpārttal paṟṟip pārppār eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. toṭakkattil iṉṉavartāṉ pārppā rāyirukkalām eṉṉum niyati irukkavilli. kōyilkaḷi mēṟ pārkkum kuṭumpattiṉar peruki oru pirivākap pirintaṉar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuṭ paṇi ceytamipaṟṟi upacāra vaḻakkil antaṇar eṉpaṭuvārāyiṉar. pārppār tuṟavikaḷallar. pirāmaṇar tamiḻ nāṭṭavarallar. ivarkaḷ vaṭa nāṭukaḷiliruntu teṉṉāṭukaḷilvantu kuṭiyēṟiṉōr. tillimūvā yiravar, 48,000var, 8,000var, 3,700var eṉa ikkūṭṭattiṉar paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pārppaṉak kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu kalantuḷ ḷārkaḷ mattiya kālat tamiḻnāṭṭu aracar vaṭamoḻikku matippuk koṭuttup pirāmaṇari ātarittamiyāl pārppaṉa vakuppiṉarum tammip pirāmaṇar eṉak kūṟuvarāyiṉar. ituvē antaṇar pārppār pirāmaṇar eṉpōriṉ varalāṟākum.
vāṇikam
“iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ intiyā tūrakkiḻakku, tūramēṟku nāṭukaḷōṭu mutaṉmiyāṉa kaṭal vāṇikam naṭattiṉatu eṉṟukāṭṭuvataṟkuḷḷa nalla cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kampōtiyā, yāvā, cumattirā, pōrṇiyō, yappāṉ, teṉcīṉā, malāyā, arāpiyā, pāracīkat tiluḷḷa mutaṉmiyāṉa paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ, āpirikkāviṉ kiḻakkukkari eṉpavikaḷil intiyar vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ innāṭukaḷōṭu māttiramaṉṟu. akkālattil aṟiyappaṭṭirunta ulakam muḻuvatōṭum, urōma irācciyaṅkaḷōṭum vāṇikam naṭatti ṉārkaḷ”1
“iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷōṭu oḻuṅkupaṭṭa muṟiyil vāṇikam naṭattiṉārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.”2 cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ vāṇikattukkum kittoḻilukkum mattiya iṭaṅkaḷākaviruntaṉa. uḷnāṭṭuppōkkuvarattukku iraṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷuḷḷa vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. avvaṇṭikaḷ iṉṟu cintuvil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṇṭikaḷip pōṉṟavi. iṉṟu cintu āṟṟil ōṭu kiṉṟa ōṭaṅkaḷum akkāla ōṭaṅkaḷi ottaṉavē. kaṭaṟ kariyi aṭutta nāṭukaḷiliruntu uṇavup poruḷkaḷip peṟum muṟiyil māttiram vāṇikam naṭipeṟavilli. ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu ulōkap poruḷkaḷum piṟavum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. paluccistāṉattilum irācaputtāṉattilumiruntu ulōkaṅkaḷum, teṉṉintiyāviliruntu caṅkum, ullāca vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷi liruntum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. pāracīkattilum āpkāṉistāṉattilum iruntu niṟakkaṟkaḷum, cīṉāvilum parmāvilum iruntu nīlakkaṟkaḷum peṟap paṭṭaṉa. muttiri veṭṭum kaṟkaḷ, cuṟṟivarakkumiḻi vitta pāṉikaḷ, pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyum kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa intiyap poruḷkaḷ ki.mu. 3500 variyil pāpilōṉukkuc ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.1
“ekiptukkum intiyāvukkum iṭiyil muṟkālattil pōkkuvarattu naṭipeṟṟateṉat terikiṟatu. alakcāntiriyāvil cantitta paṭitta intiyarkaḷ mūlam tāṉ pala ceytikaḷik kēṭṭu aṟintukoṇṭatākat tālami kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. intiyāviliruntu camayayāttirikarkaḷ ciriyāvi luḷḷa herāpolicukkuc (Hierapolis) ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu lūciyaṉ (Lucian) kūṟiyuḷḷār. kiṟittu piṟappataṟkumuṉ intiyar apicīṉiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purintārkaḷ eṉpatu kiṟittuva vētaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiyar arāpiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil avarkaḷ cīṉak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅki ṉārkaḷ. ki.mu. 350 il varalāṟu eḻutiya parōcas (Berossus) pāpilōṉil aṉṉiyar kūṭṭamākak kuṭiyēṟiyiruntaṉar eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ki.mu. 63 il piṟanta srāpō (Strabo) eṉpār urōma nāṭṭut taṅkam intiyāvukku atikam celvatippaṟṟik kavalippaṭṭār. piḷiṉi kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: intiyāvukkup payaṇañ ceypavarkaḷ ocelis (Ocelis) eṉṉum tuṟimukattil iṟaṅkuvatu nallatu. kippālus eṉṉum paruvakkāṟṟu vīcumāṉāl nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil muciṟit tuṟimukatti aṭiyalām. itaṉ cūḻalkaḷil kaṭaṟ koḷḷikkārar irukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ātalāl atu iṟaṅkuvataṟkut takunta iṭamaṉṟu. nāṉ iti eḻutukiṉṟa kālattil aṅku āḷkiṉṟa aracaṉiṉ peyar celiputturus. vēṟu oru vāyppāṉa tuṟimukam nelciṇṭi pakkāri (Nelcyndi Bacare) it tuṟimukam tolivilē uḷḷa maturiyiliruntu āṭcipuriyum pāṇṭiyaṉukkuriyatu. puḷuṟṟā eṉpavar “ākastaciṉ parivāraṅkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa putta caṉṉiyāci. avar nāṭṭukkuḷḷa vaḻakkappaṭi neruppil viḻuntu iṟantupōṉār. avaruṭiya camātik kaṭṭiṭam iṅku kiṭakkiṟatu” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
“kaṭal vāṇikam peritum tirāviṭar vacam iruntatu. atil āriyar ciṟupaṅku paṟṟiṉārkaḷ. arāpiyā, āpirikkā, cīṉak karikaḷil kuṭiyēṟiya intiyariṟ perumpālōr tirāviṭarātal vēṇṭum. vaṭanāṭṭu vāṇikat tiviṭat teṉṉāṭṭu vāṇikam mutaṉmiyuṭiya teṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa viliyuyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vantaṉa eṉṟum vaṭanāṭu tōl, kampaḷi eṉpavikaḷi aḷittaṉaveṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, caṅku mutaliyaṉa teṟkiliruntu vantaṉaveṉṟum kuṭaliyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tāmpiraparaṇi āṟu, ilaṅki, pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉpaṉa avarāl kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
“intiyāviliruntē nalla yāṉikaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷukkum koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. tantam, kuraṅku, mayil mutaliyavi intiya nāṭṭukkuriyavi. ivikaḷik kuṟikka epirēya (Hebrew) moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ epirēyam alla! tirāviṭam. intiyāviliruntu oru mayil ekiptukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. atu yūpitar kaṭavuḷukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉukku mayil aṉuppappaṭṭatippaṟṟip pavēru cātakam kūṟukiṉṟatu. innūl ki.mu. 400 variyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. kirēkka moḻiyilvaḻaṅkum oricā eṉpatu arici eṉṉum tirāviṭattiṉa tiripu. kirāmpum cātikkāyum malāya tīvukaḷil muṟkālattil kiṭippaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avi ki.pi. 180il alakcāntiriyāvukkuk koṇṭupōkap paṭṭaṉa. ākavē ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil intiya malāya vāṇikam naṭipeṟṟirukkalām. apicīṉiyāviṉ talinakarākiya akcamil (Auxam) naṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oru kallil eḻuttup poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkal ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil nāṭṭappaṭṭatu. avveḻuttukkaḷ intiyāvik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptil talinakarākiya mempis aḻipāṭukaḷil intiya āṭavar makaḷirik kāṭṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, aṅku intiyar kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum piḷiṇṭērs peṟṟi (Flinders Petrie) eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. puḷuṟṟā kālattil vāḻntavarākiya ṭiyō kiṟistōtam (Dio Chrysostom) alakcāntiriyāvil naṭamāṭiya pala nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē intiyarit tāṉ pārttatākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.”1
“kōṭikālac cūriya ayaṉa kālattil āṇṭutōṟum nūṟṟi yirupatu marakkalakaṅkaṉ mōyis harmus (Mois Harmos) eṉṉum ceṅ kaṭaṟ kariyiluḷḷa ekiptiya tuṟimukattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭaṉa. paruvak kāṟṟiṉ utaviyip peṟṟu avi nāṟpatu nāḷkaḷil intiyat tuṟi mukaṅkaḷi allatu ilaṅkit tīvi aṭintaṉa. marakkalaṅkaḷ vili uyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tirumpi vantaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ ceṅkaṭaṟkariyi liruntu nīla natikku oṭṭakaṅkaḷilēṟṟik koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. avi nīla āṟṟu vaḻiyāka alak cāntiriyāvi aṭintu piṉ urōma nāṭṭut talinakaric cērntaṉa.” ivvāṟu urōmaṉ varalāṟṟālariyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. iñci, miḷaku, kaṟuvā pōṉṟavikaḷukku mēṟku nāṭukaḷil mikat tēviyiruntatu. vayiram, muttu, paṭṭu eṉpa vaṟṟiyum avarkaḷ evvaḷavu poruḷ koṭuttum vāṅka āyattamāka viruntārkaḷ. kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu ceṉṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukku nūṟu maṭaṅku ilāpattukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl kavarappaṭṭu mikap palar kiḻakkuk kāṭukaḷōṭu vāṇikattil īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi cēra nāṭum pāṇṭiya nāṭum aḷittaṉa. kumari, koṟki, kāyal pāmpaṉ eṉṉum tuṟimukaṅkaḷ atika varuvāyaḷittaṉa. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ ārampa kālak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷiṉ vāṇikam urōmar vacam iruntatu. vayiraṅkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyil muttu vili ēṟap peṟṟatāyiruntatu. urōmap peṇkaḷ paṭṭu āṭikaḷi uṭukka virum piṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉ eṭikkup paṭṭi niṟuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ irattiṉaṅkaḷ iḻittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvi ṉiṉṟum urōmukku aṉuppap paṭṭaṉa. kōyamuttūrp pakutiyiluḷḷa pāṭiyūril kiṭikkum kōmētakatti urōma vaṇikar atika vili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. intiyap poruḷkaḷ urōmil nūṟu maṭaṅku vilikku viṟkappaṭṭateṉṟum āṇṭil eṭṭu ilaṭcam taṅka nāṇa yatti intiyā vāṇika mūlam iḻuttu viṭukiṉṟateṉṟum avvāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
camayakkoḷkikaḷ
āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ ulakam, uyir, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷi ārāyum uyarnta camayakkoḷki uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. mokañcatarō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟkiṭitta muttirik kaṟkaḷil palavaki vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṭiyē ālamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkattil vīṟṟirukkum kōlamuṭiya vaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaṭivam, kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷukkut talivaṉ (pacupati) eṉṉum karutti viḷakkukiṉṟateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. akkālattilēyē makkaḷ yōkattiṉ taṉmikaḷiyum ataṉāl peṟum āṟṟalkaḷiyum naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ. paḻiya tamiḻariṉ camayakkaruttukkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē putta ciṉa matakkoḷkikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. puttar aracamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkat tiruntār. ciṉak kaṭavuḷ acōkamara niḻalil iruppavar eṉṉum koḷkikaḷ mokañcatarō makkaḷ koṇṭirunta karuttip piṉ paṟṟiyavē. kaṭavuḷ yōkattiliruppatākak kāṭṭum vaṭivam kaṭavuḷāy varutaṟkum yōkappayiṟci avaciyam eṉpati akkāla makkaḷ koṇ ṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpati uṇarttutal kūṭum. putta, ciṉa mataṅkaḷ paḻiya tirāviṭa matakkoḷkikaḷit taḻuvi eḻuntaṉa veṉpati varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
paḻiyakālattil camayakkaruttukkaḷ ēṭṭil eḻuti vikkappaṭātu āciriya māṇākkamuṟiyil collik koṭukkappaṭṭuvantaṉa. ivvāṟu talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vanta camaya uṇmikaḷ maṟikaḷ eṉap paṭṭaṉa. upaniṭataṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭa camayanūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭum karuttukkaḷ tirāviṭariṉ koḷkikaḷē. akkālattil pirāmaṇar aracavakuppiṉariṉ pātaṅkaḷiliruntu upaniṭata uṇmikaḷip payiṉṟārkaḷ. upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇarāl aṟiyappaṭātavi. ikkaruttukkaḷē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayakkaruttukaḷākum. ak koḷkikaḷ ippoḻutu civa cittāntak koḷkikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. civacittāntak koḷkikaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉ toṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkivarum uyarnta karuttukkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar pāp kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻaruṭiya matam ellāmakkaḷukkum potuvāṉatu. ituvē vaḷḷuvaratum, tolkāppiyaratum matamākum. tirukkuṟaḷil viṉip payaṉ, maṟupiṟappu, uyiriṉ aḻiviṉmi, pāva puṇṇiyam, iṟivaṉ, iruviṉikaḷiyum paṟṟāmal iṟivaṉ niṟkum taṉmi, tavam, tavatti ṉāl peṟum āṟṟal pōṉṟavi kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mantira vitatikaḷi ṉālō kaṭavuḷukkuk kāṇikkikaḷ koṭuppatiṉālō uyirkaḷ uyar nili aṭiya māṭṭā veṉpatē aṟivuṭiyōr matamākum.
toṭakkattil camayam accam, utaviyaṟṟa taṉmi pōṉṟa ētuk kaḷāl tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu; piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟitākap paṇpāṭaṭintu uyar nili aṭintatu. ikkoḷkikaḷi muḷḷuḷḷa tāḻi marattil pūtta naṟuma™muḷḷa pūvukku oppiṭalām. iṉṟiya makkaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭu pūvip pōṟṟātu tāḻiyiṉ muḷḷuḷḷa ilikaḷip pōṟṟutal pōṉ ṟiruppatē.
uṇavu
makkaḷ tām vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa vevvēṟu vaki uṇa viṉi uṇṭuvantārkaḷ. tāvaram ūṉ eṉṉum iruvaki uṇavukaḷum perumpālum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ tāvaravakiyē uṇaviṉ perum pakutiyākavum ūṉ kaṟivakipōṉṟu ciṟitaḷavākavum iruntaṉa. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl vēḷāṉ vakuppiṉar pārppaṉar eṉṉum iruvakuppiṉarallāta maṟṟiyōrellām ūṉ uṇavu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. ūṉ uṇavu koḷḷutal ciṟantataṉṟu eṉṉum koḷki nilaviyiruntateṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷāl naṉku terikiṉṟatu.
kaṟivakikaḷukku miḷaku payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. miḷakukkuk kaṟi eṉpatu maṟṟoru peyar. muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ miḷakāyippaṟṟi aṟiyār. airōppiyar mūlam miḷakāy intiyāvi aṭintatu. miḷakāyc ceṭi teṉṉamerikkāvukkuriyatu. itaṉkāy miḷakupōl kāramāyiruttal paṟṟi miḷakāy (miḷaku+kāy) eṉappaṭṭatu. airōppiyar intiyāviṉiṉṟum miḷaki vāṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ. kirēkkarum urōmarum itaṉi atikam virumpiṉamiyāl itaṟku ‘yavaṉappiriyā’ eṉṉum iṉṉoru peyarum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. yavaṉar eṉpatu kirēkkariyum urōmariyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyar. miḷakik kuṟikkak kirēkkar pippili eṉṉum peyari vaḻaṅkiṉar. pippili eṉpatu tippili eṉpataṉ tiripu. pippili eṉṉum kirēkka colliṉ tiripē peppar eṉṉum āṅkilaccol. muṟkālattil miḷaku airōppiya makkaḷiṉ cokucāṉa uṇavup poruḷāka viruntatu. mattiya kālattil atu avarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poru ḷāka viruntatu. mutalil veṉīciya vaṇikarum, piṉpu pōrttukkēyarum, ollāntarum miḷaki mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil ollāntar miḷakiṉ viliyi irumaṭi ākkiṉamiyāl āṅkilarāl 1599il ‘kiḻakkintiya vāṇikak kampaṉi’ toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl intiyā vil āṅkilar āṭci tōṉṟi vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu.
nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷil cōṟu, ciṟṟuṇṭi vakikaḷik kuṟikkap paṟpala peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷ ellām vevvēṟu vakiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa uṇavuvakikaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. “aṟucuvi uṇṭi yamarntu illāḷūṭṭa” eṉṉum nālaṭiyār aṭiyiṉāl celvar aṟu cuviyuḷḷa uṇavukaḷi uṇṭārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.
kaṟivakikaḷukkut tāḷitam ceyyappaṭṭatu. tēṉ, kāy, piñcu, paḻam, kiḻaṅku, taḷir, ili mutaliyaṉa uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil matuvakikaḷik kuṟikka aṟupatu peyarkaḷ variyil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl akkālamakkaḷ matuvi atikam uṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. nel, tēṉ, paḻavakikaḷ, pūvakikaḷiliruntu matuvakikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avi cāṭikaḷil ūṟṟik kāramēṟumpaṭi nilattil putittu vikkappaṭṭaṉa. “tēṭkaṭuppaṉṉa nāṭpaṭutēṟal” eṉak kaḷḷiṉ kāram kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akkālak kaḷ vakikaḷ ikkālac cārāya vaki pōṉṟu vāliyiliṭṭu vaṭikkappaṭṭaviyalla; ikkālapīr (beer) eṉṉum matuppōṉṟavi.
ayalnāṭṭup payaṇakkārar intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa cila kuṟippukaḷ:
1iṟciṅ eṉṉum cīṉayāttirikaṉ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: viruntu koṭuppōr puttakurumāri vaṇaṅki avarkaḷi vīṭṭukku aḻik kiṟārkaḷ. viruntil perumpālum ceppup pāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi cāmpaliṭṭu miṉukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kuruvum ovvoru maṇiyil iruppār. oruvarōṭu oruvar muṭṭātapaṭi maṇikaḷ eṭṭa iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ orumuṟikkumēl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatilli. orumuṟi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuḻiyil vīcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭpāttiraṅkaḷi orupōtuma vittiruttal kūṭātu. picci iṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ivvāṟu vīcappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuvintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. cīṉātēcattil ceyyappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa uyarnta maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭapiṉ cuttañceytu vikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. viruntiṉar cāppiṭum iṭam cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭiruk kum. vāyilil periya pāṉikaḷil taṇṇīr vikkappaṭṭirukkum. kuru mār vantatum avarkaḷ tamatu mēl aṅkiyiṉ kaṭṭukaḷi aviḻp pārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamatu kikaḷip payaṟu allatu maṇṇittēyttuk kaḻuvuvārkaḷ. kurumār tamatu kuṇṭikiyiluḷḷa nīrip payaṉpaṭuttuvar; allatu viruntukku aḻittavar taṇṇīri ūṟṟa ataṉip payaṉ paṭuttuvar. piṉpu avarkaḷ maṇikaḷil amarvar. avarkaḷukku muṉṉāl uṇṇum taṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭum. uṇpataṉmuṉ kaṭavuḷit tutippatu vaḻakkamaṉṟu. viruntukku aḻittavar uṇavip parimāṟuvār. parimāṟumpōtu peruviraṟ parumiyuḷḷa iñcittuṇṭum orukaraṇṭi uppum koṭukkappaṭum. uppuk koṭuppavaṉ talimik kuruviṉmuṉ kikaṭṭi muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvāṉ; appoḻutu avar ellārukkum uṇavic carivarap parimāṟu eṉṟu colvār. ellōruk kum uṇavu paṭittapiṉ uṇṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu kāttirukka vēṇṭiya tilli.
mārkkōpōlō kūṟiyiruppatu: malapāril ellā āṭavarum makaḷirum nāḷil irumuṟi kuḷikkiṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu ceyyātavar tāḻntavar eṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ valatu kiyāl uṇkiṉṟaṉar. ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyāvatu avarkaḷ uṇavi iṭakkiyāl toṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ iṭakkiyi acuttamāṉa tēvikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoruvaṉum nīr parukuvataṟkut taṉimiyāṉa ēṉam vittirukkiṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉattiliruntu iṉṉoruvaṉ nīr uṇṇamāṭṭāṉ. nīr uṇṇumpōtu avarkaḷ ēṉatti vāyil muṭṭaviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ; vāykkuc ciṟitu tūrattil piṭip pārkaḷ. evaṉāvatu ēṉatti vāyil vittalkūṭātu; nīr kuṭippataṟku ataṉi aṉṉiyaṉiṭam koṭukkavum kūṭātu, aṉṉiyaṉiṭam ēṉam illāviṭiṉ avarkaḷ nīri avaṉ kiyil ūṟṟuvārkaḷ. avaṉ kiyi ēṉamākak payaṉpaṭuttuvāṉ.
1makkaḷ mikavum nuṭpamākac camiyal ceykiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nūṟuvakiyākac camiyal ceykiṟārkaḷ. tiṉamum camiyal vevvēṟu vakiyākavuḷḷatu.
muṭivuri
tirāviṭa makkaḷē intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātimakkaḷ. immakkaḷ iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ciṟanta nākarikavaḷam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. tirāviṭar nākarikap paṇpāṭukaḷ, camayakkaruttukkaḷ, kalikaḷ eṉpaṉa putitāka intiyanāṭṭi vantaṭinta makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷōṭu kalantu tōṟṟuvitta putiya cantatiyiṉarāl ciṟcila vēṟupāṭu kaḷuṭaṉ kikkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu avikaḷē, intiyakkali, intiya matakkoḷkikaḷ, intiya nākarikam eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.1 intiya nākarikattukku māttiramaṉṟu paḻiya ulaka nāka rikattukkē tirāviṭa aṭippaṭi uṇṭu eṉpati ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷ kaḷuṭaṉ icciṟiya nūlil viḷakkiyuḷḷōm.

tirāviṭar nākarikam
muṉṉuri
tirāviṭar nākarikam, kālam kuṟippiṭṭu elli kāṇamuṭiyāta paḻamiyuṭiyatu. iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē, iṉṟiyiṉum pārkka nākarikattāl uyarnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ cintu veḷiyil vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ mēṟku āciyāvilē yūpirātas, tikiras āṟṟōraṅkaḷil ak kālam vāḻnta cumēriya makkaḷōṭu vāṇikam naṭattiṉārkaḷ. intiya nāṭṭiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa muttirikaḷ pala, cumēriyāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cintuveḷi makkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭamakkaḷiṉ nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ micūril kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.1
iṟṟikku aiyāyiram allatu āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyē tirāviṭamakkaḷ ōviyam, ciṟpam, kaṭṭaṭaamippu, vāṇikam, kittoḻil, kappaṟ payaṇam, ilakkiyam, ici, kūttu, pōr, nakar amippup pōṉṟa paṟpala tuṟikaḷil mikat tēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. ivi pōṉṟa varalāṟukaḷip potumakkaḷum, paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavarum payilvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu. iṅkilāntippaṟṟi eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷi nūṟṟukkaṇakkiṟ kāṇkiṉṟōm. avikaḷi yellām namatu nāṭṭu māṇavar āvalōṭu kaṟkiṉṟaṉar. tirāviṭanāṭṭu varalāṟu, tirāviṭar nākarikam eṉpavikaḷip paṟṟip potu makkaḷum māṇavarum kaṟṟu aṟivataṟku ettaṉi nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa? itaṉi niṉikkuntōṟum tirāviṭaṉ evaṉēṉum nāṇam aṭiyāmal iruppāṉ eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. iv vakik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē ic ciṟiyanūl pala pakutikaḷāka veḷivarukiṉṟa teṉka.
ceṉṉi,
25-8-1947. na.ci. kantiyā.
tirāviṭar nākarikam
tōṟṟuvāy
teṉṉintiyāvilum ilaṅkiyilumuḷḷa pāṟikaḷē ulakiṟ paḻimiyuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum, avi oru pōtum nīruḷ mūḻkiyirukka villi yeṉṟum nila nūlār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. intiya nāṭṭil mika mikap paḻamitoṭṭē makkaḷ vāḻntu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ paḻiya kaṟkālam mutal ulōka kālam vari toṭarpākap payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum intiyāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ākavē intiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikam, ātikālam mutal intiyāvilēyē tōṉṟip paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarcci aṭintuḷḷateṉa, ārāycciyāḷar orutaliyāka navilkiṉṟaṉar. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl makkaṭ paṭippu intiya nāṭṭilēyē tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintatākalām eṉa mēlnāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ kūṟuvārāyiṉar. intiya nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta nākarikamē mēṟku āciyā, ekiptu, ciṉṉa āciyā, kirēttā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkum ceṉṟatu. ik karuttiṉi ārāycciyāḷar palar pukaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. ulaka nākarikat tōṟṟattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa cīrum tiruttamum peṟṟirunta makkaḷ tirāviṭarāvar. ivarkaḷ imayamali mutal kaṉṉiyākumari variyil vāḻntārkaḷ. iv vellikaḷukku uṭpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vaḻaṅkiya moḻi oṉṟē.1 ivvāṟu paṇṭu toṭṭē nākarika vaḷam paṭit tirunta nam muṉṉōrākiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ paḻiya varalāṟukaḷi naṉku aṟintukoḷvatu namatu kaṭaṉākum. intiya nāṭṭil āriya makkaḷ vantu kuṭiyēṟiya piṉpu cila māṟutalkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. āriya makka ḷiṭiyē pirāmaṇa matam eṉṉum putiya matamoṉṟu tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. pirāmaṇa matattiṉar cilar, teṉṉāṭu pōntu, tamiḻ aracarkaḷiṭiyē tamatu matattip parappalāyiṉar. itu, iṉṟu intiya makkaḷiṭiyē airōppiya kiṟittuva pātirimār tamatu matattip parappivaruvatu pōṉṟa tākum. teṉṉāṭu pōnta pirāmaṇarōṭu tirumaṇattiṉāl kalappuṟṟa tamiḻnāṭṭup pārppaṉar, pirāmaṇa matattit taḻuvalāyiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ tammi āriyareṉavum, tamatutāymoḻi āriyam eṉavum, āriyam teyva moḻi yeṉavum kūṟip pala māṟāṭṭaṅkaḷit teṉ ṉāṭṭil uṇṭupaṇṇalāyiṉar. itaṉāl teṉṉāṭṭil āriya tirāviṭap pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ paḻimitoṭṭē naṭantu vantaṉa. itaṉit tirumūlar kālam mutal iṉṟu variyum nām kāṇalākum. āriyam tirāviṭam eṉṉum iraṇṭil āriyak kaṭciyic cērnta pirāmaṇarē perumpālum tirāviṭar varalāṟukaḷi eḻutalāyiṉar. āriyar tirāviṭar varalāṟukaḷ eṉpaṉa moḻi ārāycci, makkaḷ iṉa ārāycci eṉpavi aḷavil niṉṟuviṭātu araciyal campantamum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ākavē avarkaḷ pirāmaṇak kuḻuviṉarukkum camakkirutattiṉ perumikkum iḻukkāṉa ceytikaḷi veḷiyiṭa orupōtum virumpavilli. ataṉāl uṇmi yāṉa tirāviṭar varalāṟukaḷ veḷivantila. mēlnāṭṭu aṟiñariṉ iṭiviṭāta pēruḻippiṉāl tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟum, avarkaḷ nākarikattippaṟṟiya pala ariya ceytikaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. uṇmi yil intiya nākarikameṉpatu tirāviṭar nākarikamē. 1ivvuṇmikaḷi aṅki nellikkaṉipōla yāvarum eḷitil paṭittuṇarumāṟu ic ciṟiya nūl veḷivaruvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu.
makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil paṇpaṭutal
makkaḷ mika mikak kīḻniliyiliruntu paṭippaṭiyē muṉṉēṟṟa maṭintārkaḷ. ātiyil avarkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, mīṉ piṭittum kāṭu kaḷiṟ kiṭikkum kāykaṉi kiḻaṅku vakikaḷi uṇṭum vāḻntārkaḷ. ivvāṟu aṉṟu aṉṟāṭa uṇaviṉ poruṭṭu alintu tirinta makkaḷ vēṭar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ. in nilimiyil oru vakiyāṉa paṇpāṭum uṇṭākavilli. avarkaḷ mirukaṅkaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭuvataṟku ēṟṟa vil, ampu, eṟiīṭṭi, kavaṇ pōṉṟa cila āyutaṅkaḷic ceytu payaṉ paṭutta aṟintaṉar. itaṉpiṉpu mantikaḷi mēyttu avikaḷiṉ payaṉ kaḷik koṇṭu vāḻum nili uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ak kālattilum makkaḷ āṟutaliṉṟi iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum alintu tirintamiyil tiruttamuṇṭāka villi. aṭutta paṭiyil avarkaḷ payiriṭa aṟintārkaḷ. appoḻutu avarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruntu payiriṭavum, payir viḷiyum kālatti etirpārttu irukkavum, paruvakālatti aṟintu maṟupaṭiyum payiriṭuva taṟku ōriṭattil nilittirukkavum vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. innilimiyil maṉitaṉukku ōyvu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. payir viḷivippatāl aṭutta aṟuppukkālam variyil palar uṇṇap pōtumāṉa uṇavu kiṭittatu. ākavē, makkaḷ ak kālattil ōyntiruntu tiruttam eytiṉārkaḷ.
nākarikam
makkaḷ tiruttamaṭintapōtu nakarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. nakara vāḻkki yiṉ paṇpaṭṭa nili nākarikam eṉappaṭṭatu. nākarikam eṉpataṟku nakar campantamāṉatu eṉṟu poruḷ. āṅkilattil nākarikattik kuṟikka vaḻaṅ kum “civilicēṣaṉ” eṉṉum collum ip poruḷiṉatē. 1nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil uṇṭākum celvamum aṟivum nakariṭattē cērum. palanāṭṭu vaṇikar kūṭum avviṭattil putiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittal, kittoḻil mutaliyaṉa perukuvatāl uṇṭākum pala vāyppukkaḷ, cokucāṉa vāḻkki, poḻutupōkku, ataṟku oḻivu, nalla paṇṭaṅkaḷ, karuttukkaḷ eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ kalappu, ataṉāl tōṉṟumvāṇikattip paṟṟiya vivēkam, uṇṭupaṇṇum tiṟami mutaliyaṉa uṇṭākum. nakaraṅkaḷil kittoḻil ceyvataṟkuc cilar taṉiyē viṭappaṭuvar. nākarikam nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kuṭiciyil ārampittu nakaril vantu malarkiṉṟatu.
nāṭṭiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷil tōṉṟi vaḷarum ellāt tiruttaṅkaḷiṉ tokutikaḷum nakaril vantu paṇpaṭṭa niliyi aṭiyumpōtu avi nākarikam eṉṉum peyarip peṟum.
paḻantamiḻariṉ vāḻkki
vāḻkki makkaḷ vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa māṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṭal ōraṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ mīṉ piṭittal, uppu viḷivittal, avikaḷip piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟpatāl kiṭikkum ūtiyattik koṇṭu tamakku vēṇṭum piṟaporuḷkaḷip peṟutal pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷ purivatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟē kāṭu, mali, vayal, maṇal veḷikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷum tattam vāḻkkiyiṉ poruṭṭu vevvēṟu toḻilkaḷip puritalik kāṇkiṟōm.
ivvulakam kaṭal, kāṭu, vayal, mali, maṇalveḷi eṉa aivakip paṭum. ivvivaki iṭaṅkaḷum intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭu. ākavē, intiya nāṭṭil āṅkāṅku vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ tiruttam, vāḻkkimuṟi mutaliyaṉavum iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa māṟupaṭum.
pāli nila makkaḷ
mali, kāṭukaḷi aṭutta otukkukaḷil maṇalveḷikaḷ kāṇap paṭum. ivviṭaṅkaḷil veyyilukku ularntu paṭṭuppōkāta pāli maraṅkaḷ vaḷarum. ataṉāl maṇal veḷikaḷip pālinilameṉa muṉ ṉōr vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ manti vaḷarkkavō, payiriṭavō aṟiyā tār; villu, ampu mutaliyavikaḷiṉ payaṉi aṟintavar. ivarkaḷ vēṭṭi āṭutalāl kiṭikkum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyi uṇṭum, vaḻiccelvōrik koḷḷiyiṭṭum vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ koṟṟavi (kāḷi). ikkaṭavuḷ vīra teyvamākaviruntatu. itaṟku maṉita palikaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. pāli nila makkaḷ eyiṉar, vēṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. eyiṉar talivaṉ kāḷi allatu viṭali eṉappaṭṭāṉ. eyiṉar kuṭiyiruppu kuṟumpu eṉappaṭṭatu.
malivāṇar
mali iṭaṅkaḷ kuṟiñci eṉappaṭṭaṉa. malikaḷil kuṟiñci eṉṉum oruvaki maram vaḷarntatu. itu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷukku orumuṟi karumikoṇṭu kariya taḷirkaḷi īṉum eṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. mali iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntōr kuṟavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ veṟpaṉ allatu cilampaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppu ciṟukuṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, malikaḷiṟ kiṭikkum kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkuvakikaḷ, maliccāralkaḷil tāṉē viḷiyum malinel ākiya ivikaḷi uṇṭum vāḻntaṉar. avari, tuvari, cāmi mutaliyaṉavum maliccāralkaḷil viḷi vikkappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟacciṟumiyar pakaṟkālattē puṉaṅkaḷil paraṇkaḷmītu iruntu tiṉik katiri uṇṇavarum kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭiṉar. irākkālattil kuṟavar paraṇ mītiruntu puṉatti mēyavarum yāṉi, paṉṟi mutaliyavikaḷik kavaṇil kalvittu eṟintu ōṭṭiṉar; maliccāralkaḷil toṅkum tēṉ kūṭukaḷi aḻittut tēṉ eṭuttaṉar; tēṉi mūṅkiṟkuḻāykaḷil ūṟṟi vittaṉar. ivarkaḷ pālinila makkaḷiṉum pārkkat tiruttamuṭiyōr. ivarkaḷ payiriṭac ciṟitu aṟintiruntaṉar.
kuṟavariṉ kulateyvam murukakkaṭavuḷ. kuṟavar maṟiyaṟuttup pali tūvi murukakkaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ; narapali iṭavilli. murukapūci ceypavaṉ murukaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ivaṉ teyvamēṟi āṭum kūttu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. kiyiṭattē vēli vittiruntamiyiṉ ivaṉ vēlaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. teyvattāl kuṟi nērnta peṇkaḷiṉ teyvak kuṟiyip pōkkuvataṟku veṟiyāṭṭu eṭukkappaṭṭatu. tēṉ, tiṉimā, iṟicci mutalayavi vēṭariṉ uyarnta uṇavukaḷākum. ivarkaḷ maliyiṭattē kiṭikkum yāṉikkompu, pulittōl, iṟicci mutaliyavikaḷip piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟu, viṟṟu, avaṟṟiṉ viliyākat tamakku vēṇṭiya piṟa poruḷkaḷip peṟuvar.
mulli nila makkaḷ
kuṟuṅkāṭukaḷil makkaḷ cilar vāḻntārkaḷ. aṅku mullikkoṭi atikam vaḷarntamiyiṉ, atu mulli nilam eṉappaṭṭatu. mullinila makkaḷ āṭumāṭukaḷi vaḷarttārkaḷ. avi koṭukkum pāl, tayir, ney, vēṭṭiyāṭuvatāl kiṭikkum iṟicci, payiriṭṭu viḷivikkum varaku mutaliya tāṉiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷik koṇṭu vāḻntaṉar; pāl, tayir, ney mutaliyavikaḷi ayal iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟu viṟṟut tamakku vēṇṭiya piṟa uṇavupporuḷkaḷip peṟṟaṉar. āyar vāḻum kuṭiyiruppu, cēri eṉappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki vēṭaṉukkum uḻavaṉukkum iṭippaṭṭatātaliṉ, ivarkaḷ iṭiyar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ. mulli nilamakkaḷ tirumāli vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. tirumāliṉ kiyiliruppatu caṅku, cakkaram eṉpaṉa. ivi ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ. mālvaṇakkam ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ oruvaki. māl eṉpatil el eṉpatu toṉikkiṉṟatu. el eṉpatu āl āyiṟṟu. el ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikkum. kariyamāl eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmiyāyiṉ mā eṉpatu karumiyikkuṟikkiṉṟatu. piṟkālattil tirumāl vaṇakkam kaṇṇaṉ vaṇakkamāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. āriya makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa viṣṇukkaṭavuḷiṉ peyar viṇ eṉpataṉ vēṟupāṭu eṉac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mullinila makkaḷ tirumālukkup pāṟpoṅkaliṭṭuk kuravik kūttāṭi vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. kuravik kūttu eṉpatu peṇkaḷ eḻuvar kikōttu vaṭṭamāy niṉṟu āṭum kūttu.
kaṭaṟkari makkaḷ
kaṭaṟkariyil neytal eṉṉum oruvaki nīrcceṭi vaḷarum. ataṉāl kaṭaṟkariyi aṭutta iṭaṅkaḷ neytal eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷ paratavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. paratavar kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷil ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭittārkaḷ. ivarkaḷē piṟkālattil, mēṟku āciyā, cumattirā, jāvā, malāyā, kam pōtiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku marakkalaṅkaḷ ōṭṭicceṉṟa mālumikaḷāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ talivar cērppaṉ, pulampaṉ, tuṟiyaṉ mutaliya peyar kaḷip peṟṟaṉar. uppu viḷivippatum neytal nila makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkit toḻilākaviruntatu. uppu viṟpōr umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. umaṇar kaḻutikaḷilum vaṇṭikaḷilum uppik koṇṭuceṉṟu piṟa iṭaṅkaḷil viṟṟuttamakku vēṇṭiya vāḻkkip poruḷkaḷip peṟṟaṉar.
makkaḷ kaṭal kaṭantu piṟanāṭukaḷukkuc cellat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, piṟanāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu cella vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭaṟkarikaḷi aṭintaṉa. ayalnāṭṭup paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭaṟkarikaḷil vantu iṟaṅkiṉa. av viṭaṅkaḷil cuṅkam koḷḷum poruṭṭu, aracar kāvalāḷar niṟut tappaṭṭaṉar. cuṅkac cālikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. paṇṭacālikaḷ amikkappaṭ ṭaṉa. kaṭaṟkarikaḷukkuc cellum pātikaḷ tiṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikarum piṟanāṭṭu vaṇikarum paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṟ kūṭiṉar. itaṉāl muṉ kirāmaṅkaḷāyirunta paratavar kuṭiyiruppukkaḷ ciṟappu mikka iṭaṅkaḷāyiṉa. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ nakarkaḷāka māṟiṉa.
paratavar varuṇaṉ eṉṉum kula teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ik kaṭavuḷiyē intiya nāṭṭi aṭinta āriya makkaḷum vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. āriyariṉ varuṇaṉ acuraṉ eṉappaṭṭār. acurar eṉappaṭṭōr tirāviṭarē yāvar. ik karuttiṉi vēta ārāycciyāḷar nili niṟutti yuḷḷārkaḷ, paratavar tāḻiyiṉ kīḻ cuṟākkōṭu naṭṭup pūraṇik kālaṅkaḷil ciṟappākak kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
maruta nila makkaḷ
mantikaḷi mēykkum nilikkup piṉ makkaḷ āṟṟōraṅkaḷil taṅkiyiruntu āṟṟu veḷḷattik kālvāykaḷāl vayalkaḷukkup pāyccit tāṉiyaṅkaḷi viḷivittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu veḷḷatti āṇṭamiyiṉ ivarkaḷ 1veḷḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āṟṟōraṅkaḷal lāta camaveḷikaḷil taṅkiṉōr, maḻi nīri ēri, kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vittu ēṟṟam, kapili, iṭā, piḻā mutaliyavikaḷāl iṟittup payir viḷivittuk kāri āṇṭamiyiṉ kārāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nīr ōraṅkaḷil maruta maraṅkaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarntamiyiṉ, vayalum vayal cārnta iṭaṅkaḷum marutam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vēḷāḷar talivaṉ ūraṉ allatu kiḻavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
ivarkaḷiṉ kula teyvam vēntaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. nalla aracaṉ āṭciyil maḻi peyyum eṉpatu iṉṟum potumakkaḷāl nampappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil maḻi peyvippōrākiya (Rain makers) mantira vittik kārarāṉa aracarē nāṭukaḷi āṇṭuvantārkaḷ eṉpatu paḻiya makkaḷ varalāṟṟāl aṟiya varukiṉṟatu. av vaḻipāṭṭiṉ niḻalē vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭākalām. aḵtaṉṟi, ‘kaṭavuḷ taṉ cārpil makkaḷi āḷvataṟku viṭutta avar putalvaṉ aracaṉ’ eṉṉum nampikkiyum makkaḷiṭiyē orukāl ōṅkiyiruntatu. piṟkāla makkaḷ iṟivaṉ allatu aracaṉi vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ.
muṟ kālattil atika uṇavu vittiruppavaṉē celvaṉāka matikkappaṭṭāṉ. uḻavaṉiṭattil atika celvam iruntatu. uṇavup poruḷkaḷi vēṇṭi makkaḷ maruta nilattukku vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ uḻavaṉiṭamiruntu tāṉiyaṅkaḷip peṟṟārkaḷ. ataṟku viliyākat tattam nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭikkum arum paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṭuttārkaḷ. paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu varuvataṟkum, koṇṭu celvataṟkum tiruntiya pātikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. kāṇiyāḷarākiya uḻavar, tamatu tāṉiyaṅ kaḷic cērttuvippataṟkum, cevvaṉē vāḻvataṟkum aḻakiya vīṭu kaḷi amittārkaḷ. payir ceyvataṟkuk kalappi, koḻu, nukam, katti, pāri, arivāḷ pōṉṟa palavaki āyutaṅkaḷ tēvippaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ ullācamāka vāḻvataṟkum poḻutu pōkkuvataṟkum virumpiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ makaḷir aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇiya virumpiṉārkaḷ. ivikaḷi utavakkūṭiya paḻakkamuṭiya makkaḷ avviṭattē taṅki ṉārkaḷ. kittoḻilkaḷ perukiṉa. avarkaḷ ceyyum vēlikaḷukkuk kūliyāka uḻavar tāṉiyaṅkaḷi aḷittaṉar. ivvāṟu maruta nilam, makkaṭ puḻakkamuḷḷa iṭamāka māṟiṟṟu. celvam eṅku uṇṭō, aṅku ataṉik koḷḷi koḷḷap pakivar varuvar. pakivari etirttup pōr ceyvataṟkup pōr vīrarum avarkaḷit talimi tāṅki naṭattu vataṟku aṟivum āṟṟalum uṭal valiyumuṭiya oruvaṉ vēṇṭum. av vakiyiṉaṉ oruvaṉ terintu eṭukkappaṭṭāṉ. pakivar, kuṭiyiruppuk kuḷ virivil nuḻiyātapaṭi kākka ataṉic cuṟṟi matil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu āṭciyum nakarum marutanilattē tōṉṟalāyiṉa.
āṭci
makkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷākac cērntu vāḻumpōtum, cottu uṇṭā yirukkum pōtum āṭci toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉ vēṭaṉāyirunta pōtu cottu irukkavilli. makkaḷ iṭiyarāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiya kālattilēyē avarkaḷiṭam celvam tiraḷat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. māṭu, āṭu eṉpaṉa muṟkāla maṉitaṉiṉ celvaṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. iṭiyar pul veḷikaḷil taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ. maṟṟac celvaṅkaḷ pōlallāmal, āṭu māṭukaḷ, kaṉṟukuṭṭikaḷ īṉṟu perukum. itaṉāl mantikaḷ periya aḷaviṉavākum. iṭiyaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ mantikaḷip pirittuk koḷḷutal kūṭātu. mēyccal nilattic ciṟu tuṇṭukaḷākap pirittāl atu mantikaḷ mēyvataṟkup payaṉaṟṟatākum. ākavē kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērntu vāḻum muṟi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kuṭumpat talivaṉ atikāramuṭiya vaṉāṉāṉ. kuṭumpat talivar palarukku, atika celvamum atika ati kāramumuṭiya oruvaṉ aracaṉāṉāṉ. ivvāṟu āṭci muṟi iṭiya ruḷḷēyē mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟi vaḷarcci aṭivatāyiṟṟu. aracaṉik kuṟikkat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum paḻiya col kō allatu kōṉ, itu iṭi yaṉi uṇarttum. itaṉāl āṭci, makkaḷ iṭiyaṉāyirukkumpōtu toṭaṅkiṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.
āṭciyil iruvaki
camātāṉa kāla āṭci, col āṭci; pōrkkāla āṭci, vāḷ āṭci; camātāṉa kāla āṭci, pōrk kālattil utavātu. ākavē, makkaḷ pōrk kālaṅkaḷil camātāṉa kāla āṭcit talivaṉi nīkkiviṭṭu, aṟivum āṇmiyumuḷḷa oruvaṉit talivaṉākat terinteṭup pārkaḷ. avaṉē paṭiyi naṭattic celvāṉ. avaṉ coṉṉatē caṭṭam. “iṭik” cātiyaruk kiṭiyil kuṭumpat talivaṉiṉ atikārattiviṭa āṭci yirukkavilli. pōrk kālattil avarkaḷ tamakkuḷ tuṇivum vīramumuḷḷa oruvaṉit talivaṉākat terinteṭuttu avaṉukkup paṇintu naṭantārkaḷ. pōr muṭintavuṭaṉ avaṉ taṉatu conta muyaṟciyip pārkkumpaṭi viṭappaṭṭāṉ. kuṭumpat talivaṉ, paṭit talivaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭu karuttukkaḷum kalantu camūkaṅkaḷukkuḷ aracanili vaḷarcci yaṭintatu. ivvaki nili maruta nilattilētāṉ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aṅku tāṉ kōṭṭikaḷōṭu kūṭiya nakaraṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. paḻiya nākarikam paṭitta nāṭukaḷ ellām āṟṟōraṅkaḷilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
aracaṉ
tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē aracaṉ āṭci mikap paṇṭik kālattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. aracaṉik kuṟikkum pala paḻan tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kōṉ, ēntal, vēntaṉ, maṉṉaṉ, kuricil, iṟivaṉ, vaḷḷal, aṇṇal mutaliyaṉa avaṟṟuṭ cila. āṭci eṉpatu āḷ eṉṉum viṉi aṭiyākap piṟantatu. āḷ eṉṉuñ col toṭakkattil āḷi uṇarttiṟṟu. āṭci eṉpatu iṭiyaṉ taṉatu mantikaḷip pakikaḷi ṉiṉṟuṅ kāttu uṇavu koṭuttuk kāppatu pōṉṟatu eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. ēntal eṉpatu, makkaḷi āpattiṉiṉṟum kāppavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil ēntu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantirukkalām.
aracaṉ vāḻum vīṭu, kōṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉiṉ atikāram uyarntapōtum oḻuṅkupaṭṭa pōrmuṟi uṇṭāṉa pōtum aracaṉiṉ vīṭu kōṭṭiyāka māṟiṟṟu. kōṭṭi valiya matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭiruntatu; ākavē aracaṉiṉ vīṭu araṇmaṉi eṉappaṭṭatu. araṇ eṉpatu toṭakkattil aḻaku, pātukāppu eṉpavikaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu; piṉpu kōṭṭiyiṉ cuvarik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. kōṭṭiyic cuṟṟi akaḻ iruntatu. akaḻik kuṟikkap pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭu, ōṭi, kēṇi, parikam, purici, pāmpuri mutaliyaṉa akaḻi uṇarttum peyarkaḷuṭ cila. itaṉāl tamiḻar akaḻip paṟṟi naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. kōṭṭi vāyil kōṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. matilukkup piṉṉē carivāka eḻuntu mēlē mēṭiyāka muṭivaṭiyum pakuti kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. kōṭṭiyiṉ mītu maṟintu niṉṟu ampu eyyumiṭam ñāyil eṉappaṭṭatu. araṇmaṉi yil pala aṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. aṟi eṉpatu aṟu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. paṇṭaṅkaḷi iṭṭu vikkum aṟi koṭṭaṟi allatu koṭṭaṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉ ciṟappuk kālaṅkaḷil peru makkaḷ cūḻa viruttal kolu viruttal allatu vīṟṟiruttal eṉappaṭṭatu. koṭi, tukil,tōki, katali, pū, taḻi mutaliyavakiḷāl koluccāvaṭi alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruntatu. aracaṉ ciṟappuk kālaṅkaḷil muṭi aṇintāṉ. avaṉ mukatti maḻit tiruntāṉ. koluvirukkumpōtu avaṉ kiyil kaṅkaṇam, viralil kaṇiyāḻi, kaḻuttil muttu māli irattiṉamālikaḷ, kālil kaḻal pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintiruntāṉ. ciṅkācaṉam, kaṭṭil eṉap paṭṭatu. aracaṉ ciṅkācaṉattil cāyntukoṇṭu irukkum aṇi, ariyaṇi eṉappaṭṭatu. avaṉ etirē aṭiyār, ūḻiyar, aracaṉiṉ pukaḻ kūṟum akavar, avaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷi veḷippaṭuttum vaḷḷuvar, pukaḻpāṭum pāṇar pulavar, vaḷḷal eṉappaṭṭa perumakkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. āṭkaḷ eṉappaṭṭa potu makkaḷ mariyātikkuriya tolivil niṉṟu viṇṇappitta vaḻakkukaḷik kēṭṭu aracaṉ tīrppu aḷittāṉ.
1tamiḻiṉ toṉmi
tamiḻum tamiḻaruṭiyaṉavum ākiya paḻiya varalāṟukaḷ iruḷil maṟintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. āṅkilam, cermaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷip pōlat tamiḻi ārāyntu kūṟa muṭiyātu. āṅkilattiṉ vaḷarcciyi ārāyntu piṉ nōkkic cellac cella nām oru niliyi aṭi vōm. aṅku atu āṅkilamākat tōṉṟātu. aṅku atu cākcaṉ, kotik eṉṉum moḻikaḷiṉ kalappākat tōṉṟum. tamiḻ, kaṭavuḷiṉ vāyil niṉṟu piṟantateṉṉum paḻaṅkati yuṇṭu. itu tamiḻiṉ aḷavu kaṭanta paḻimiyi uṇartta eḻuntatu ākalām.
paṇṭutoṭṭē iraṇṭu moḻikaḷ intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa. iv viru moḻikaḷip paṟṟi ilakkaṇa nūlār kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭa moḻi, teṉ moḻi eṉpaṉa muṟiyē camakkirutam, tamiḻ eṉpavikaḷik kuṟikkum. ivi am moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkiya iṭaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru kālat til teṉmoḻi intiyā muḻumiyilum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ moḻiyi vintiya malikkut teṟkē ōṭṭit tāṉ vaṭa nāṭṭil iṭam peṟuvataṟku vaṭa moḻi tiṇaṟik koṇṭirun tatu. iṉṟu intiya nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum muṉ piṉ nūṟu moḻikaḷ, iraṇṭi loru moḻikaḷiṉ citivukaḷ eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliyaṉa tamiḻiṉ kiḷikaḷākum. teluṅkum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum tamiḻiṉ toṭarpillāta moḻikaḷ eṉac cila ārāycci yāḷar kaṟpaṉi ceykiṉṟārkaḷ. itu, vaḷarnta piḷḷikaḷ tamatu tāy tantiyariṉ uṟavi maṟantiruppatu pōṉṟatu. camakkirutac citivukaḷi yum camakkirutac coṟkaḷiyum im moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu eṭuttuviṭṭāl, mīntiruppaṉa iṉṟu vaḻakkiṟantaṉavō, paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉavōvākiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷē yākum. vittuvāṉkaḷ cilar tamiḻ, camakkirutattiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉak kūṟit, tūya taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ uṟpattikaḷukku vaṭamoḻi mūlaṅkāṇa muyalvar. tamiḻ eṉṉum colliṉ aṭiyik kāṇamuyaṉṟa tamiḻ moḻinūl eḻutiya oruvar ataṟkuc camakkiruta ‘tamas’ (cūṭu) eṉṉum col aṭiyik kūṟiṉār. tamiḻ eppaṭi intiyāvukku vantatu? atu ākāyakaṅkiyip pōla aticayamākat tōṉṟavilli.
tamiḻar, āriyariṉum pārkka ainnūṟu1 āṇṭukaḷāvatu muṟpaṭṭavar kaḷāvar. avarkaḷiṉ ceḻippāṉa kālam ki.mu. 500 mutal ki.pi. 500 vari. tolkāppiyar, cākariṭis allatu puttar kālattavar ākalām. tol kāppiyar paḻiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. itaṉāl akkālattil pala ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa eṉṟu nām aṟiyalām. vāyppāṭamākac collappaṭṭu vanta irukku vēta pāṭalkaḷ ki.mu. 1500 mutal ki.mu. 1000 variyil ceyyappaṭṭavi. ikkālattil āriyar cintu natip paḷḷattākkilum kaṅki natip paḷḷat tākkilum paraviyiruntaṉar ātal vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattil āriyac cārpu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl ak kālattil āriyak koḷkikaḷ cila teṉṉāṭṭukku vantu viṭṭaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu.
āriyar varumpōtu tamiḻar intiyā muḻumiyum paravi vāḻn tārkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivu. avarkaḷ uyarnta nākarikap paṇpu aṭintirun taṉar. avarkaḷ airōppiyar,piṉīciyar, cīṉar, jappāṉiyar mutaliyavarkaḷōṭu toṭarpuvittiruntaṉar. cālamaṉ aracaṉ (ki.mu. 1000) tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntu mayilkaḷip peṟṟāṉ. epirēyattil kāṇappaṭum toki eṉpatu, tōki eṉṉum tūya tamiḻccolliṉ tiripu. ak kālattil epirēyaruk kum tamiḻarukku miṭiyil neruṅkiya vāṇikat toṭarpu iruntu vantatu.
tirāviṭar nākarikattiṉ virivu
paḻiya tirāviṭar eṉpōr tamiḻar, teluṅkar, maliyāḷar, kaṉṉaṭar mutaliya teṉṉintiya makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrāvar. ātiyil ivarkaḷ intiya nāṭu muḻumiyilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. ippoḻutu avarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āriya makkaḷ avarkaḷit tittiyar?, tācar, nākar eṉṉum peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. kaṉṉa parampari varalāṟukaḷum paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, paṭṭiyaṅ kaḷum āriyar varukikku muṉ cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya irācciyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi nili peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu terivikkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ pōrmuṟi, kaṭṭaṭa amippu, araciyal amippup pōṉṟa, kalikaḷil tali ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ulaka varalāṟṟilum intiya varalāṟṟilum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ teṉṉintiya makkaḷāvar eṉṟum, ik karuttiṉi viḷakkuvataṟkuc ciṟanta ārāycciyāḷar evarum muṉ varavilli eṉṟum iriccārṭ eṉpār kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
tirāviṭa nākarikamē intiya pāpilōṉiya ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭi
cumēriya makkaḷ, tirāviṭariṉ oru kiḷiyiṉar eṉa ṭākṭar hāl kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ṭākṭar hāliṉ karuttu uṇmiyāka irukkumāyiṉ, nākarikam mutaṉmutal intiya nāṭṭil toṭaṅki, maṟunāṭukaḷukkup paraviṟṟeṉṟu ṭākṭar es.kē. caṭṭarji kūṟiyuḷḷār. car. jāṉ mārṣal eṉpār, cumēriya nākarikam pāpilōṉil putitākat tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, cumēriya nākarikattukku aṭippaṭi intiya nākarikamātal kūṭumeṉṟum intiya, nākarikattiliruntē pāpilōṉiya, acīriya, mēṟku āciya nākarikaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟum pukaṉṟuḷḷār. moḻi ārāycci valla ṭākṭar caṭṭarji kirēttiya, iliciya, cumēriya, ellam, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, uṟpattiyil toṭarpuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, ciṉṉa āciyā, aiciyaṉ tīvukaḷ, pāpilōṉ mutaliyaṉa orē nākarikat toṭarpuṭiya nāṭukaḷeṉṟum viḷakkamākac cāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
ippoḻutu arappā mokañcatarō mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷāl intiyariṉ nākarikam cumēriya nākarikatti mika ottuḷḷateṉat terikiṉṟatu. ṭākṭar pāṉarjiyum maṟṟiya ārāycciyāḷarum, arappā mokañcatarō nākarikam āriya ruṭiyataṉṟu eṉat tuṇintuḷḷārkaḷ. paluccistāṉattil tirāviṭa moḻikku iṉamākiya pirākūy kāṇappaṭuvatāl, arappā mokañcatarō nākarikattukkuriyavarkaḷ paḻiya tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉa uṟutippaṭukiṉṟatu.
car. jāṉ ivāṉs eṉpār, teṉ intiyāvē makkaḷukkup piṟappiṭa mākalām eṉṟum, iṅku niṉṟē vaṭakkilum mattiya tari nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kuṟippiṭuvārāyiṉar. 1rīṭ eṉpār maṉitaṉuṭiya nākarika vaḷarccikku atika utavi ceytatu intiyā eṉṟum, intiya vāṇikamē uḷnāṭṭuk kittoḻilkaḷukkum, ayal nāṭṭu ēṟṟumati iṟakkumatikkum kāraṇamā yiruntateṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mēṟku āciyā, kiḻakku āpirikkā, cīṉā, jappāṉ malāyt tīvukaḷōṭu paṇṭu vāṇikam purintavar tirāviṭarē
muṟkālam kaṭaṟ payaṇam ceytu vantōr, tirāviṭarāvar. teṉ ṉintiya makkaḷ kaṭaṟkariyi aṭutta nāṭukaḷil vāḻntārkaḷ. ataṉāl avarkaḷukkuk kaṭaliṟ cellum paḻakkam eḷitil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tōṇi, ōṭam, kappal mutaliyaṉa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ. intiyāvukkum mēṟku āciyāvukkum paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟa kappalkaḷ perumpālum intiya nāṭṭukku uriyaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭaṟ pōkkuvarattu tirāviṭarvacam iruntateṉṟum riccārṭ pukaṉṟuḷḷār. intumāk kaṭalil kappalkaḷi ōṭṭic ceṉṟavarkaḷ avarkaḷē. kari ōraṅkaḷ avarkaḷukkuk kaṭaṟ payaṇañ ceyyum paḻakkam aḷittatu. āpirikkā, arāpiyā, malāyā, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku intiyap paṇṭaṅkaḷi ēṟṟic ceṉṟa mālumikaḷ avarkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ.
tirāviṭar jāvā, cumattirā kampōtiyā mutaliyavikaḷil kuṭiyēṟutal
avarkaḷ mēṟkē mecapetēmiyā mutal kiḻakkē intumāk kaṭaṟṟīvukaḷ vari kuṟiyēṟiyiruntami, tirāviṭamakkaḷiṉ paranta kaṭaṟ payaṇattippaṟṟi veḷiyiṭuvatākum. intiya nāṭṭil vētamoḻi iṭam peṟuvataṉmuṉ, tirāviṭa makkaḷ paḻiya cāltiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purin taṉar. ṭākṭar cēys (Dr. Sayce) eṉpār, intiyāvukkum pāpilōṉukku miṭiyil ki.mu. 3000 variyil vāṇikam naṭattirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, akkālattil aikkiya pāpilōṉiyāvil āṭci purinta ūr pākas eṉṉum aracaṉ āṭci purintāṉeṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. teṉṉintiya makkaḷ intiyak kaṭaṟṟīvukaḷ, jāvā, cumattirā, pōrṇiyō mutaliya nāṭu kaḷukkup payaṇañceytu avviṭaṅkaḷit tamatu kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭuka ḷākkiṉar; aṅkuniṉṟum avarkaḷ etirppuṟattēyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu cīyam, kampōtiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ ṟaṅkiṉar.
