ki.mu. 2,300il cumēriyariṉ nākarikam paḻamiyaṭintuḷḷatu. ak kālattil cumēriyap pulavarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum taṅkaḷ paḻiya varalāṟu kaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭuttivikka muyaṉṟuvantaṉar. ulakap paṭippippaṟṟiya varalāṟu, paḻiya aracaṉ oruvaṉ ceyta pāvattukkākap periya veḷḷap perukku nērnta varalāṟu pōṉṟavikaḷippaṟṟip pulavarkaḷ pāṭi ṉārkaḷ. periya veḷḷap perukkip paṟṟiya varalāṟu pāpilōṉiya, epirēyaruḷ vaḻaṅkip piṉpu kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭuk katikaḷuḷ cērntuḷḷatu.
kurumārākiya cumēriya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ paḻamiyi mika atikappaṭuttik kūṟiṉar. ulakap paṭippiliruntu periya veḷḷap perukku vari uḷḷa 432,000 āṇṭukaḷvari aracar palar āṭci purintaṉar eṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kurumār tamatu nākarikattiṉ paḻamiyic ciṟitu atikappaṭuttik kūṟiyirukkalām. nippūr nakar aḻipāṭu nilamaṭṭattiliruntu aṟupattāṟu aṭikaḷiṉ kīḻ uḷḷatu. ataṉ kīḻ aṟupattāṟu aṭikaḷukku akkāṭṭiṉ cārkaṉ aracaṉiṉ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nippūriṉ paḻami ki.mu. 5,262 variyiltāṉ. kiṣ nakaril ki.mu. 4,500lum, ūr nakaril ki.mu. 3,500lum pala araca paramparikaḷiluḷḷa aracar āṭcipurintārkaḷ ivviru nakaraṅkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālaṅkaḷilum cemittiyar1, cemittiyarallātār eṉṉum aracarkaḷukkiṭiyil pōṭṭi yiruntuvantatu. mutalām cārkaṉ, hamurapi mutaliyōr cemittiyarāvar.
kōyiṟ kurumār ki.mu. 3,000mutal kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil eḻutivitta kuṟippukaḷ ūril kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi ilakāṣ, uruk mutaliya nakaraṅ kaḷi āṇṭa aracarkaḷiṉ muṭicūṭṭu, veṟṟi, maraṇam mutaliyavikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avi kuṟippiṭukiṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ mika ēṟṟaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ilakāṣ nakari āṇṭa urukkakiṉā2 cila cīrt tiruttaṅkaḷic ceytāṉ. kurumār celvariṭamum vaṟiyavariṭamum iruntu poruḷi uṟiñcuvatit taṭukkap pala caṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. talimik kuru kāṭukaḷil maraṅkaḷi veṭṭutal kūṭātu; aṅkuk kiṭik kum paḻaṅkaḷukku vari taṇṭutalum kūṭātu; piṇaṅkaḷip putippataṟku uḷḷa vari aintil oṉṟākak kuṟikkappaṭutal vēṇṭum. kaṭavuḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākak koṭukkum varuvāy, āṭumāṭu mutaliyavikaḷik kurumār paṅkiṭutal ākātu eṉak kaḷimaṇ ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭa oru paṭṭiyam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kurumārukku māṟākac caṭṭañ ceyta urukkakiṉāvi ulūkal kākici eṉṉum aracaṉ vīḻtti, nakarik koḷḷiyiṭṭāṉ; kōvilkaḷ aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa; makkaḷ vītikaḷiṟ kollappaṭṭaṉar. kōvilkaḷiṉ kaṭavuṭ cilikaḷ eṭuttuc cellap paṭṭaṉa. ilakāṣ nakariṉ periya, tāyk kaṭavuṭ cili eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatik kuṟittu varuntit tiṉkiratāmu eṉṉum pulavar oruvar pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mutalām cārkaṉ
cumēriyariṉ ilakāṣ nakarukku irunūṟu mil tūrattil cārkaṉ eṉṉum cemittiya aracaṉ akēṭ1 eṉṉum nakariyamittu akkāṭ2 eṉṉum irācciyattit tōṟṟuvittāṉ. cūcāvil kiṭitta kalveṭṭu oṉṟil cārkaṉuṭiya uruvam veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil avaṉ nīṇṭa tāṭiyōṭum atikāramum cerukkum niṟinta tōṟṟattōṭum kāṭci yaḷikkiṉṟāṉ. cārkaṉ aracamarapiṉaṉallaṉ. avaṉuṭiya tāy kōvilil tēvatāci. avaḷ yārum aṟiyātu ivaṉip peṟṟuk kūṭiyil vittup puṟkaḷāl maṟittu āṟṟil viṭṭāḷ. aracaṉuṭiya vēliyāḷ oruvaṉ ak kuḻantiyik kaṇṭeṭuttāṉ. vaḷarntapōtu cārkaṉ aracaṉiṉ kiṇṇam tāṅkupavaṉāka vēli pārttāṉ. avaṉ aracaṉiṉ tayaviṉāl atikāramuṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. iṟutiyil avaṉ kalakam ceytu aracaṉi vīḻttic cimmācaṉattil amarntāṉ. avaṉ taṉṉi ulakattukku aracaṉ eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭāṉ; pala paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷip piṭittāṉ; nakaraṅkaḷik koḷḷiyaṭittāṉ. avaṉāl kollappaṭṭavarkaḷil oruvaṉ ilakāṣ nakari veṉṟu cūṟiyāṭiya ulūkalkākici eṉpavaṉāvaṉ.
ip periyaaracaṉ kiḻakku mēṟku teṟku vaṭakku eṉṉum nāṉku ticikaḷilum paṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟāṉ; ellatti veṉṟāṉ; veṟṟiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamākat taṉatu vāḷip pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭalil kaḻuviṉāṉ. mēṟku āciyāvik kaṭantu mattiyatarik kaṭali aṭintāṉ. avaṉ aimpattintu āṇṭukaḷ āṭci purintāṉ. ak kālattil palavakip paḻaṅkatikaḷ tōṉṟi avaṉik kaṭavuḷākac ceytuviṭṭaṉa. avaṉuṭiya maraṇakālattil avaṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭirunta nāṭukaḷ kalakam ceytaṉa.
avaṉukkuppiṉ avaṉuṭiya mūṉṟu kumārar oruvarukkuppiṉ oruvarākac cimmācaṉam ēṟiṉar.
ki.mu. irupattāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil ilakāṣ maṟupaṭiyum celvac ceḻippuṟṟatu. ak kālattil kutēyā3 eṉṉum aracaṉ āṭci purintāṉ. ivaṉ kōvilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ; paḻiya ilakkiyak kalvikku ātaravaḷittāṉ. valiyavarkaḷi aṭakki meliyarkaḷukku irakkam kāṭṭiṉāṉ.
cālatiyariṉ4 ūr nakaram ki.mu. 3,500mutal ki.mu. 700 variyil uyarniliyil iruntatu. ūreṉkūr aracaṉ mēṟku āciyāvit taṉatu āṇikku uṭpaṭuttiṉāṉ. yūpirātas āṟuvaḻiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa vāṇikattāl ūr celvam aṭintatu. nakarkaḷ kōvilkaḷāl aḻaku peṟṟaṉa. ūreṉkūr1 taṉatu āṇikkuṭpaṭṭa lārcā, uruk, nippūr mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya makaṉ tuṅki aimpatteṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ āṭci purintāṉ. virivil ic ciṟappukaḷ maṅkiṉa. pōrp pōkkuṭiya ellam makkaḷ kiḻakkiliruntum, amōriyar2 mēṟkilirun tum vantu ūr nakaric cūṟiyāṭiṉar. camātāṉattik kulittu aracaṉi yum ciṟippiṭittaṉar. amōriyarum ellattiṉarum cumēriyāvi 2,000 āṇṭukaḷ variyil āṭci purintārkaḷ. piṉpu vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum hamurapi eṉṉum pāpilōṉiyaṉ veḷit tōṉṟiṉāṉ; avaṉ uruk, aiciṉ mutaliya nakarkaḷip piṭittāṉ; ellammītu paṭi eṭuttu an nāṭṭaracaṉic ciṟippaṭuttiṉāṉ; amōriya acīriya nāṭukaḷil taṉatu āṇiyi nāṭṭiṉāṉ. pāracīkar talieṭukkum variyil cemittiyarē tikiras yūpirātas āṟukaḷukkiṭippaṭṭa nāṭukaḷi āṇṭārkaḷ. cumēriyarip paṟṟi vēṟu yātum aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. cumēriya nākarikam vaḷarntu yūpirātas, tikiras āṟukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē mēlnōkkic ceṉṟu, pāpilōṉ, acīriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum paravuvatāyiṟṟu.
poruḷātāram
payirc ceyki
cumēriyāviṉ cīrtiruttam mārikkālattil āṟukaḷ veḷḷammuṭṭi vaḻivatāl uṇṭāṉatu. cumēriyar veḷḷattik kālvāykaḷiṟ celutti vayalkaḷukkup pāycca aṟintārkaḷ. ki.mu. 50ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē avarkaḷ ivvāṟu puriya aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. vayalkaḷil kōtumi, vāṟkōtumi mutaliya tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷintaṉa; pērīntum kāykaṉikaḷ utavum palavakic ceṭikaḷum ceḻittu vaḷarntaṉa. kalappiyil erutukaḷip pūṭṭi uḻavum avarkaḷ aṟintiruntārkaḷ.
kittoḻil
cumēriyar cempiyum taṅkattiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iv viraṇṭu ulōkaṅkaḷiyum kalantu veṇkalatti uṇṭākkavum avarkaḷ aṟin tiruntārkaḷ. irumpiṉālum āyutaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷ pala tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭavi. tāṉiyat tāḷkaḷik koyyum arivāḷ pōṉṟa cila karuvikaḷ kaḷimaṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ūci, tuḷikkum karuvi pōṉṟavi cila tantattiṉālum irumpiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. araciṉar kaṇkāṇippiṉ kīḻ necavut toḻil atikam naṭipeṟṟatu. vīṭukaḷ nāṇal taṇṭukaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. niṉṟu cuḻalum kataviṉ tāḷ kuḻinta kalliṉmītu vikkappaṭṭatu. tari kaḷimaṇ iṭṭu aṭittu maṭṭam ceyyappaṭṭatu. nāṇalkaḷiṉ talippu kaḷi vaḷittuk kaṭṭuvatāl kūrivil vaḷiviṉatāyiruntatu. cila camayaṅ kaḷil kuṟukkē maraṅkaḷ iṭṭut taṭṭiyāka amikkappaṭṭa kūrimītu kaḷimaṇ parappappaṭṭatu. cuvarkaḷ vikkōlum kaḷimaṇṇum kalanta kalaviyāl pūcappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷil āṭu, māṭu, paṉṟi mutaliyavi vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. kuṭikkum nīr kiṇaṟukaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu.
vāṇipam
paṇṭaṅkaḷ nīrvaḻiyākac ceṉṟaṉa. cumēriyāvil kal kiṭippatu aritu. ākavē avi piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kaṭalvaḻiyē āṟṟuk kaḻimukam variyum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa; piṉpu kālvāykaḷ vaḻiyāka nakarukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvukkum cumēriyāvukkum vāṇipat toṭarpu iruntu vantatu. intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭavi pōṉṟa muttirikaḷ pala cumēriyāviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. cumēriyar nāṇayattip payaṉpaṭuttavilli. vāṇipam paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟiyil naṭipeṟṟatu, poṉṉum veḷḷiyum kuṟikkappaṭṭa viliyuṭiyaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avi paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vilikkup patil koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. cumēriyāvil vāṇipat toṭarpāṉa pala kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. avikaḷik koṇṭu cumēriyar naṭattiya vāṇipattip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ pala aṟiyakkūṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cumēriyariṉ camātikaḷuḷ poṉ, veḷḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷ, ēṉaṅkaḷ, āyutaṅkaḷ. payiriṭum karuvikaḷ pōlvaṉa kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
makkaḷiṉ pirivukaḷ
celvarum vaṟiyarum pala piriviṉarāyiruntaṉar. aṭimi vaḻakku valimi peṟṟiruntatu. celvarukkum vaṟiyarukkumiṭiyil oru vakuppiṉar vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷ ciṟiya vāṇipam ceyvōr, maruttuvar, kurumār eṉpōrāvar. maruttuvam uyarvaṭintiruntatu. ovvoru nōykkum taṉittaṉi maruntu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. maruntu camayattōṭu campantap paṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu. nōykaḷ āvikaḷiṉ kōḷāṟiṉāl uṇṭākiṉṟa teṉṟum, avikaḷi ōṭṭiṉālaṉṟi nōyi akaṟṟamuṭiyāteṉṟum avarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ mātattic cantira aḷaviyākak kaṇit taṉar. ovvoru nakarattiṉarum mātaṅkaḷukku vevvēṟu peyarkaḷ iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
āṭci
ovvoru nakarilum taṉittaṉi aracaṉ iruntu āṇṭāṉ. avaṉ patēci eṉappaṭṭāṉ. itaṟkup pūcāri aracaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. āḷukiyiṉ ovvoru pakutiyum camayattāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭ ṭiruntatu. ki.mu. 2,800lēyē vāṇipam palamaṭintiruntatu. appoḻutu ciṟṟaracarkaḷukkellām talivaṉākiya pēraracaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. avaṉ eppoḻutum accattuṭaṉ vāḻa nērntatu. enta nērattilum avaṉi iṉṉoruvaṉ vīḻtti avaṉ iṭatti aṭital kūṭum. aracaṉ vīṟṟirukkum maṇṭapattukku iru vācalkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi oruvaṉ māttiram cellakkūṭiya oṭukka muṭiyaṉa. aracaṉik kāṇpataṟku oruvaṉ māttiram oru kālattil aṉumatikkappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉ uḷḷē celvataṉ muṉ avaṉiṭattil āyutaṅkaḷ iruk kiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu vāyil kāvalaṉ cōtaṉi ceyvāṉ. aracaṉ vaḻipaṭum kōvilum maṟivil iruntatu.
pōrkkālaṅkaḷil aracaṉ vil, ampu, īṭṭi pōṉṟa āyutan tāṅkiya vīrarkaḷiṉ muṉṉē tērmītu ceṉṟāṉ. vāṇipap pātikaḷ, vāṇipap paṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉpavi kāraṇamākap perumpālum pōrkaḷ nikaḻn taṉa. meṉittās eṉṉum akkēṭiya aracaṉ tāṉ ellattiṉ mītu paṭi eṭuppatu aṅkuḷḷa veḷḷic curaṅkaṅkaḷiyum, cilikaḷ ceyyum kaṟkaḷiyum kippaṟṟu vataṟkeṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. pōril tōṟṟavarkaḷ aṭimikaḷāka viṟkappaṭṭaṉar; viṟpatu atika ūtiyam aḷiyāteṉak kaṇṭāl avarkaḷ pōrkkaḷattilēyē kollappaṭṭārkaḷ. pōril piṭipaṭṭavarkaḷil pattiloru pakutiyiṉar kōvilkaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉar.
camūka oḻuṅku kāṇiyāḷaṉ āṭcimuṟiyāl kākkap paṭṭu vantatu. pōrkaḷil veṟṟi uṇṭāṉapōtu, nilaṅkaḷ tiṟamiyuṭiya pirapukkaḷukkup pirittu aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. an nilaṅkaḷukku vari tavirkkappaṭṭatu. ivvaki atikārikaḷ nāṭṭil oḻuṅkip pātukāttārkaḷ; aracaṉukkup pōr vīrari aḷittārkaḷ. varikaḷ mūlam araca varuvāy cērkkappaṭṭuk kaḷañciyaṅkaḷil vittup pātukākkappaṭṭatu.
aracar, kāṇiyāḷar, atikārikaḷ āḷukiyippaṟṟi pala caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ivikaḷē hamurapiyiṉ1 caṭṭaṅkaḷukku aṭip paṭiyāṉavi. cemittiya caṭṭam peṇṇiṉ kūṭā oḻukkattukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitittatu; āṉāl cumēriya caṭṭam kaṇavaṉi iṉṉoru maṉiviyik koḷḷumpaṭi aṉumatittatuṭaṉ, muntiyavaḷip pintiya vaḷukkuk kīḻāka vittirukkumpaṭi vitittatu.
vāṇipam, tirumaṇam, kaṭaṉ koṭuttal, kaṭaṉ vāṅkutal, koḷvaṉa, viṟpaṉa pōṉṟavikaḷukkellām caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kōyilkaḷil vaḻakkukaḷ kēṭṭut tīrppu aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. niyāyat tīrppāḷar kurumārāka viruntaṉar. ovvoru vaḻakkum mutalil naṭuvar kiyil viṭappaṭṭatu. niyāyan tīrkkum iṭattukkuc celvataṉmuṉ vaḻakkukku iṭamiṉṟit tīrttuvippatu avarkaḷ kaṭamiyākum.
camayam
ūreṉkūr eṉṉum aracaṉ taṉatu caṭṭaṅkaḷic camāṣ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷ aruḷic ceytār eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. cumēriyah vil kaṭavuḷar palarākap perukiṉar. ov voru nakarattukkum ovvoru talimit teyvam iruntatu. makkaḷiṉ ovvoru vakic ceyalukkum naṉmi puriyum taṉit taṉit teyvaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. cumēriyar tōṉṟiya kālam mutal ñāyiṟu vaṇakkamum vaḷarcciyaṭintu vantatu. camāṣ eṉṉum ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ irāk kālattil vaṭa kaṭalil tuyiṉṟār; avar veḷiyē varumpaṭi viṭiyaṟ kālam katavit tiṟantuviṭṭatu. ceṅkuttāṉa vāṉattiṉmītu taṉatu tēric celuttik koṇṭu nerupput taṇalpōl avar mēlē ceṉṟār. ellil eṉṉum kaṭavuḷukkum avar maṉiviyākiya niṉlillukkum nippūril kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. uruk nakaril iṉṉi eṉṉum tarik kaṭavuḷākiya kaṉṉi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. cemittiyarum akkēṭiyarum ik kaṭavuḷi istār eṉa aḻittaṉar. kiṣ, ilakāṣ eṉṉum nakarkaḷil tolarōcā eṉṉum tāykkaṭavuḷ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. vāṉattil āvikaḷ niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar; tēvarkaḷ (angels) makkaḷip pātukākkiṉṟaṉar; picācukaḷ tēvarit turatti makkaḷip piṭittu varuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.
kōyilkaḷil pala teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avikaḷukku uṇavum kāṇikkikaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. māṭu, āṭu, puṟā, kōḻi, tārā, mīṉ, pērīntiṉ paḻam, attippaḻam, veḷḷarikkāy, veṇṇey, eṇṇey, palakāram mutaliyaṉa teyvaṅkaḷukku viruppamuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kaḷimaṇ paṭṭayamoṉṟil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvakik kāṇikkikaḷit teyvaṅkaḷ peṟuvatāl cumēriya kōviṟ kurumār atika celvatti īṭṭi ataṉāl atikāram uṭiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. perumpālāṉa karumaṅkaḷil avarkaḷē āṭci purivōrāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ evvaḷavukkuk kōyiṟ pūcārikaḷāka viruntārkaḷ, evvaḷavukku āṭci puripavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. urukkakiṉā eṉṉum aracaṉ kurumār celvariṭamum vaṟiyariṭamum paṇattik kavarvatik kaṇṭittu avarkaḷiṉ atikārattik kulittāṉ. avaṉ maraṇamāṉapōtu kurumār paḻiya nilimiyi eytiṉar.
cumēriyariṉ camātikaḷil uṇavum āyutaṅkaḷum vikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. itaṉāl avarkaḷ maraṇattukkup piṉṉum uyir aḻiyā tiruppati nampiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.
vāḻkki
kurumār kalviyōṭu paḻaṅkatikaḷiyum ciṟuvarkaḷukkup paṭippit tārkaḷ. paḻaṅkatikaḷ avarkaḷatu āṭcikku valimi aḷittaṉa. ovvoru kōyililum paḷḷikkūṭam naṭattappaṭṭatu. paḻiya kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil kūṭṭal, kaḻittal, perukkal, vakuttal, catura aḷavi, kaṉa aḷavi vāypāṭukaḷum kōṇalaḷavip paṟṟiya payiṟcikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kōyilkaḷil tēvatācikaḷ iruntārkaḷ. tirumaṇattippaṟṟiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. tantiyālaḷikkappaṭṭa cītaṉapporuḷ peṇṇiṉ atikārattukkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. kaṇavaṉip pōla maṉivikkum piḷḷika ḷiṭattil cama urimi uṇṭu. kaṇavaṉiṉ toṭarpiṉṟit taṉatu conta aṭimikaḷi vittirukkavō, avarkaḷi viṟṟuviṭavō avaḷukku atikāra muṇṭu. peṇkaḷ araci pōṉṟa nili variyil uyarntu nakari āḷavum muṭiyum. kūṭā oḻukkattukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu.
vaṟiya peṇkaḷiṉ vāḻkki cemmiyuṟavilli. celva makaḷir uyarnta ullāca vāḻkki naṭattiṉar. avarkaḷ mēṉiyil alaṅkārap poruḷkaḷiyum palavaki aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. camātikaḷil ivvakip poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
eḻuttukaḷ
cumēriya eḻuttukaḷ mika viyappaḷippaṉa avarkaḷiṉ eḻut teḻutum muṟi mikap paṇpaṭṭiruntatu. ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ, camayap pāṭalkaḷ, vāṇipat toṭarpāṉa eḻuttukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 36ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē ivarkaḷ eḻuta aṟintiruntārkaḷ. ki.mu. 32ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻututaṟkuk kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil āppu vaṭivamāṉa muṉiyuṭiya kucciyiṉāl eḻuttukaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakiyāṉa poruḷkaḷik koṇṭē aracāṅka āvaṇaṅkaḷ, koḷvaṉa, viṟpaṉa, uṭaṉpaṭikki āvaṇaṅkaḷ pōlvaṉa eḻutik kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. eḻutiya piṉpu kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ veyyilil allatu neruppiliṭṭu ularttappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, avar kaḷiṉ āvaṇaṅkaḷ, nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa nilikkak kūṭiyaṉa vāyiruntaṉa. cumēriya eḻuttukaḷ āppu vaṭivamāyiruntamiyiṉ avi āp peḻuttukaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
cumēriya eḻuttukaḷ valamiruntu iṭam vācikkappaṭṭaṉa: pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ iṭamiruntu valam eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. āp peḻuttukaḷ cumēriya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ōviya eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ curukku eḻuttukaḷākum. ivveḻuttukaḷ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka virintu eḻutappaṭum pōtu ōviya vaṭivaṅkaḷi iḻantu kīṟṟu vaṭivaṅ kaḷi aṭintaṉa.
eḻuttu nili ilakkiya niliyi aṭiyap pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷākum. neṭuṅkālam vāṇipattukku eḻuttu tuṇipurintatu. oppantaṅ kaḷ paṟṟuc cīṭṭu, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vili pōlvaṉa eḻutivikkappaṭṭaṉa. mantira vitti muṟikaḷ kiriyi muṟikaḷ, camayat toṭarpāṉa paḻaṅ katikaḷ, tutikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ mutaliyavi maṟintu pōkāmalum māṟupaṭā malum irukkumpaṭi avi eḻutivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu. 2,700lēyē cumēriyāvil periya nūl niliyaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. tellō eṉṉum paḻiya nakaril oṉṟaṉmītu oṉṟāka aṭukki oḻuṅkupaṭutti vikkappaṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkup periya nūlniliyam oṉṟu iruntatu.
ki.mu. 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cumēriya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ paḻiya varalāṟukaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭutti eḻutiṉārkaḷ. nikaḻkāla varalāṟukaḷiyum eḻutivittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūlkaḷ kiṭikkavilli. āṉāl avikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ pāpilōṉiya aracariṉ varalāṟukaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭṭaṭam
cumēriyāvil eḻuttukaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭintami pōlavē kaṭṭaṭak kaliyum vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. cumēriyar nāṇal taṇṭukaḷic caturamākavō, nīṇṭa caturamākavō allatu vaṭṭamākavō naṭṭārkaḷ. avikaḷiṉ talippukaḷi vaḷittu vilvaṭivu allatu ari vaṭṭavaṭivu uṇṭākum paṭi kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. nippūril vil vaṭivil amikkappaṭṭa kālvāy oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālam ki.mu. 3,500āka irukkalām. vil vaṭivuṭiya katavukaḷ ūr aḻipāṭukaḷil potuvākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi ki.mu. 2,000 aḷavilamikkappaṭṭaṉavākalām. aṅkuk kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭum vilkaḷ (arches) iṉṟiya viṟkaḷēyākum.
celvar tiṭarkaḷil araṇmaṉi pōṉṟa māḷikikaḷi amittu vāḻntārkaḷ. cila tiṭarkaḷ nila maṭṭattiliruntu nāṟpatu aṭikkumēl uḷḷaṉa. ciṟappāka amikkappaṭṭa pātiyālaṉṟi vīṭṭukku ēṟic cella muṭiyāmal iruntatu. ivvāṟu cumēriyac celvaṉ ovvoruvaṉuṭiya vīṭum kōṭṭi pōliruntatu. cuvariṉ veḷippuṟam civappu niṟamuṭiya tāyiruntatu. caturam, vaḷivu, mukkōṇam pōṉṟa cūḷiyiliṭṭa kaḷimaṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷāl atu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruntatu. uṭcuvar cāntiṭṭu maṭṭañ ceytu, cāyam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. vīṭṭiṉ naṭuvil muṟṟamum, muṟṟattiṉ nāṟpuṟattilum vīṭum iruntaṉa. celvarkaḷ māttiram vīṭukaḷukkuc cāḷaraṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. taṇṇīr kiṇaṟṟiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu. nakariṉ aḻukkunīr veḷiyē cellak kālvāykaḷ iruntaṉa. vīṭṭil taḷavāṭaṅkaḷ atikam irukkavilli. cila paṭukkikaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷum tantamum aḻutti alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa. kikaḷ vittu āṟakkūṭiya nāṟ kālikaḷiṉ1 pātaṅkaḷ ciṅkattiṉ pātaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
kōyilkaḷ amippataṟkuk kaṟkaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa. avi cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāyum, kampaḷi yiṉ mēl viliyuyarnta ulōkaṅkaḷip patittuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāyum uḷḷa vēlippāṭukaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa. ūril uḷḷa naṉṉār ālayattil ciṟitu nīlaniṟam koṭukkappaṭṭaṉavāyum, miṉukkam koṭukkap paṭṭaṉavāyum uḷḷa ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. cuvariṉ uṭpuṟam calavik kal, kalnār, niṟakkaṟkaḷ, poṉ mutaliyavi aḻuttiya marac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl maṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. nakarattiṉ mutaṉmiyāṉa kōyil, mēṭṭiṉmītu kaṭṭappaṭavilli; kōyilkaḷmītu mūṉṟu, nāṉku allatu ēḻu māṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. am māṭikaḷi aṭivataṟku veḷippuṟamākac caṅkup puripōṟ cuḻaṉṟu cellum paṭikkaṭṭuṭiya pāti iruntatu. mika uyaramāṉa māṭiyil nakarattiṉ periya teyvam vīṟṟirukkum. paṭi eṭuppuk kālaṅkaḷil kōyil araṇākavum payaṉpaṭṭatu.
kōyilkaḷ vīrar, kaṭavuḷar pōṉṟa ciṟpaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarik kappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tel el upēyiṭ2 aḻipāṭṭil cempi ṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa iṭapam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. itu cumēriyariṉ paḻiya ciṉṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ūr aḻipāṭṭil capāṭ araciyiṉ kallaṟiyil veḷḷiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pacuviṉ tali kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu.
cumēriyar taṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷik koṇṭu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceytārkaḷ. poṟkollar poṉṉil aḻakiya vēlikaḷ ceytaṉar. ūr aḻipāṭṭil poṉṉiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pala ēṉaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. avikaḷiṉ kālam ki.mu. 4000 āka irukkalām. aṅkuk kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa uruḷi vaṭivāṉa muttirikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. ivikaḷic cumēriya makkaḷ tamatu kiyeḻuttukaḷip poṟippataṟkup payaṉpaṭut tiṉar. cumēriyariṉ nākarikamē ekiptiṟkup paraviyateṉṟum ekiptiya nākarikattukku aṭippaṭi cumēriya nākarikamē yeṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ekiptu
āppirikkāvilē nīla āṟu eṉṉum periya nati oṉṟu uṇṭu. itu pālivaṉattuk kūṭākac ceṉṟu mattiyatarik kaṭaluḷ viḻukiṉṟatu. itaṉ iru ōraṅkaḷilum ēṟakkuṟiyat taṉittaṉi paṉṉiraṇṭu mil akalam variyil uḷḷa kīlamāṉa tari payirpacci yuṭiyatāyc ceḻippaṭin tuḷḷatu. ap pakuti ekiptu eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṟṟiṉ mukattuvāratti aṭutta pakuti kīḻ ekiptu eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉa. iv vāṟṟōraṅkaḷil mikap paḻaṅkālam mutal makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷippaṟṟiya varalāṟu aṟiyappaṭavilli.
ki.mu. 50ām nūṟṟāṇṭil paṇṭu eṉappaṭṭa oru nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟum putiya makkaṭ kūṭṭat tiṉar cilar vantu ivvāṟṟōraṅ kaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar. paṇṭunāṭu ceṅkaṭalukkuk kiḻakkēyuḷḷa ikkāla erittiriyā, comāli lantu eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷ eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. paṇṭu eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭa nāṭu mali yāḷak karikaḷ eṉa ikkāla ārāycci yāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.1
paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantu nīla āṟṟuk kaḻimukat tīvil2 kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ vaṭakki ṉiṉṟum teṟku nōkkip paravi ṉārkaḷ. paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vanta makkaḷukkut talivar ōras3. avariṉ aṭiyāḷam kaḻuku. paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷiṭiyē āṭci muṟiyi nāṭṭiyavar ivar ākalām. ōraciṉ talimiyil vanta makkaḷ mēl ekiptik kippaṟṟa evvaḷavu kālam ceṉṟateṉat teriyavilli. vaṭakkilum teṟkilum āṭcikaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa.
vaṭakkut teṟku irācciyaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa pala ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷiṉ iṇippukkaḷēyākum. ciṟiya nāṭukaḷi āṇṭa ciṟṟaracarkaḷip paṟṟiyō avarkaḷ kālattil naṭanta nikaḻccikaḷip paṟṟiyō yātum teriyavilli. avarkaḷ kālattil kiyāḷappaṭṭa kivēlip poruḷkaḷ kaṟkālat tavi. avi viyakkattakka aḻakuṭiyaṉa. ak kālattil ulōka vaki kaḷil cempu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ōraciṉ talimiyil vantavarkaḷiṉ veṟṟikkuk kāraṇam ulōka muṉikaḷ iṟukkappaṭṭa āyutaṅkaḷ eṉap piṟkālak kaṟpaṉik katikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
aintāvatu araca parampariyiṉar peyarkaḷ muṟippaṭutti eḻuti vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu eḻutuvataṟku muṟpaṭṭa cila kuṟippukaḷ iruntaṉa vākalām. aracar peyarkaḷit tavira vēṟu nikaḻccikaḷ eviyum kuṟikkappaṭavilli, teṟkē mutalil āṇṭa aracaṉippaṟṟic ciṟitu aṟikiṉṟōm. ivaṉi ki.mu. 34ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēl ekiptiyum kīḻ ekiptiyum iṇittāṉ. ivaṉ ōraciṉ marapil tōṉṟiyavaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ekiptiyak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷilum ñāpakac cilikaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ vācikkappaṭuvataṉ muṉpu epirēyariṉ camaya nūlkaḷik koṇṭum, eratōtacu1 eḻutiyavikaḷik koṇṭum ekiptiya varalāṟukaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. mutal ekiptiya parampariyit tōṟṟuvittavar mēṉs2 eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. vaṭanāṭu teṉṉāṭṭukku uṭpaṭṭirukkavilli; āṉāl atu teṟkē uḷḷa aracaṉukku aṭaṅkiyiruntatu. teṉṉāṭṭu aracaṉ taṉatu marapu vaṭanāṭṭu araca parampariyic cērntatu eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ iru nāṭukaḷukkum talivaṉ āṉāṉ.
ivaṉ allatu ivaṉukkuppiṉ vanta aracaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṟkum teṉṉāṭṭiṟkum mattiyil apiṭos3 eṉṉum nakari amittāṉ. itu mūṉṟām araca paramparik kāla nikaḻcci. āṟāvatu araca paramparik kālat til atu mempis nakari otta ciṟappaṭintiruntatu. mempis nakar eṉcip-mērpēpā4 eṉṉum aracaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭateṉac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
mutal iru araca parampariyiṉarum ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya 400 āṇṭukaḷ āṭcipurintaṉar. iraṇṭāvatu araca parampariyiṉar kālattil mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ yātum kāṇappaṭavilli. teṉṉāṭṭu aracaṉiṉ veṟṟiyōṭu iraṇṭāvatu parampari muṭivaṭikiṉṟatu. veṟṟiyāḷaṉākiya kācikem taṉatu peyarik kācikemūy eṉa māṟṟikkoṇṭu aracurimik kuriya peṇṇi maṇantu mūṉṟāvatu araca parampariyit tōṟṟuvit tāṉ. ivaṉ mutaṟ parōvā1 eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
kāci kemūyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2,980. ivaṉiliruntu valiya pala aracar tōṉṟi āṭcipurintārkaḷ. ivaṉuṭiya uruvaccili ōras vaṇakkat tiṉariṉ mutaṉmi iṭamākiya hirakōṉ pōlis2 eṉṉum kaḻuku nakarattiluḷḷa camātiyil kāṇappaṭṭatu. kāci kemūyiṉ camāti oṭuṅkic cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷip pōl oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟākak kaṟpāḷaṅkaḷi vittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ituvē mutal piramiṭ3 camāti eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉpiṉ ciṉeveru4 eṉṉum nāṉkāvatu parampariyil vanta aracaṉ periya piramiṭ camātiyik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ciṉaveruvukkup putalvaṉ illi. avaṉukkup piṉ kupuvā5 āṭcipurintāṉ. ivaṉ nālāvatu parampariyi nāṭṭiyavaṉāvaṉ. ivaṉ evvāṟu āṭcikku vantāṉ eṉṟu teriya villi; orupōtu tirumaṇa iṇippiṉāl vantirukkalām. ivaṉuṭiya makaṉ kavṟi6. kavṟiyiṉ makaṉ meṉkūrā. ivarkaḷ kicē7 eṉṉum nakarattil periya piramiṭ camātiyiyum periya maṉita mukac ciṅkattiyum amittārkaḷ eṉpativiṭa vēṟu yātum teriyavilli. ivarkaḷic ciyaps, micīriṉas eṉa eratōtacu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
kicēyil amikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷa periya piramiṭ camātiyiṉ uyaram 484 aṭi. itu 13½ ‘ēkkar’ tariyi mūṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. itik kaṭṭuvataṟku ovvoṉṟum iraṇ ṭari “ṭaṉ” pāramuḷḷa 2,300,000 cuṇṇāmpuk kaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila kaṟkaḷ 150 ṭaṉ pāramuḷḷaṉa. itaṉik kaṭṭi muṭippataṟku 100,000 aṭimikaḷ irupatu āṇṭukaḷ uḻittārkaḷ.
iraṇṭāvatu piramiṭ camātikku muṉṉāl veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa maṉitamukac ciṅkattiṉ uyaram 75aṭi. nīḷam 150 aṭi. ataṉ mukattiṉ kuṟukku aḷavu 18 aṭi 8 aṅkulam. ataṉ mūkkiṉ nīḷam 5 aṭi 7 aṅkulam.
payircceyki
ippoḻutu nil natik kaḻimukattil kāṇappaṭum turuttikaḷ muṉpu kuṭākkaṭalāka viruntaṉa. ippōtu it turuttikaḷil aṟupatu ilaṭcam makkaḷ paruttiyip payiriṭṭu ērāḷamāṉa pañci veḷināṭukaḷukku aṉuppukiṟārkaḷ.
nīla āṟu āṇṭil orumuṟi perukkeṭukkiṉṟatu. ap perukku muṉpiṉ nūṟu nāṭkaḷukku nilikkum; āṟṟunīr ivvāṟu aṇikaṭantu perukuvatāl pālinilam ceḻippaṭikiṉṟatu. aṅku nākarikam mikamika muṟkālattil tōṉṟiyiruntataṟkuk kāraṇam ituvē. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka ekiptiya makkaḷ āṟu perukum kālaṅkaḷi āvaluṭaṉ etirpārttiruppatu vaḻakkam. iṉṟum keyrō vītikaḷil, piracittam ceyvōr āṟṟup perukkiṉ niliyippaṟṟit tiṉam kāliyil aṟivippārkaḷ. veḷḷapperukkik kaṭṭuppaṭuttāviṭṭāl atu vayalkaḷi aḻittuviṭum eṉa makkaḷ aṟintiruntārkaḷ. varalāṟu toṭaṅ kāta kālattilēyē makkaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu kuṟukkum neṭukkumākak kaṭantu cellumpaṭi pala akaḻikaḷi veṭṭiṉārkaḷ. mēṟkoṇṭu perukum nīr akaḻikaḷil taṅkiṟṟu. āṟṟup perukkuk kuṟintapōtu nīriṟi kūṭi kaḷāl iṟittu vayalkaḷukkup pāyccappaṭṭatu. kōtumi nīla āṟṟi aṭutta nilaṅkaḷil tāṉāka viḷiva tilli. ākavē, mikap paḻiya kālattilēyē ekiptiyar paruva kālaṅkaḷil viḷiyum tāṉiyaṅkaḷip payiriṭa aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.
ekiptiya nāṭu payirc ceykikku mika vāyppuṭiyatāyiruntatu. atika vēli puriyāmalē tāṉiyam viḷivikkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟiya nāṭṭavar nilattik kalappiyāl uḻutum, maṇveṭṭiyāl kottiyum atikam uḻittut tāṉiyatti viḷivittaṉar. nīlanati perukiyapōtu vayalkaḷil nīr perukiṟṟu. veḷḷam vaṟṟiyapōtu ovvoruvaṉum taṉatu vayali vitittāṉ. tāṉiyaṅkaḷ maṇṇil putiyumpaṭi uḻakkap paṉṟikaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu aṟuppuk kālatti avarkaḷ etirpārt tiruntārkaḷ.
maraṅkaḷil ēṟip paḻaṅkaḷ paṟikkumpaṭi kuraṅkukaḷ paḻakkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. nīla āṟu peruki vayalkaḷil nīr pāyntapōtu āyirakkaṇak kāṉa mīṉkaḷ kuḷaṅkaḷil taṅkiṉa. mīṉ piṭikkap payaṉpaṭuttiya valikaḷi makkaḷ irāk kālaṅkaḷil kocu valikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. āṟṟu nīr pāyccuvatāl kuṭikaḷ ilāpam aṭiyavilli. ellā nilaṅkaḷum aracaṉukkuriyaṉa. avikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttuvōr aracaṉukku varuvāyil pattiloṉṟi variyākak koṭuttaṉar. ciṟṟaracar pōṉṟa pirapukkaḷukkum celvarukkum contamāṉa periya nilaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ivvaki nilam oṉṟil patiṉiyāyiram pacukkaḷ niṉṟaṉa. itikkoṇṭu ovvoru nilattiṉ parappum evvaḷaveṉṟu nām uyttu aṟiyalām. tāṉiyavaki, iṟicci, mīṉ eṉpaṉa mukkiya uṇavukaḷā yiruntaṉa. celvar uṇavukku mēl muntirikic cāṟṟik kuṭittārkaḷ. potumakkaḷ kōtumiyil vaṭitta matuvip parukiṉārkaḷ.
kuṭikaḷiṉ vāḻkki varuntattakkatāka iruntatu. kālvāy veṭṭutal, vītikaḷ amittal, araciṉar nilaṅkaḷit tiruttutal, piramiṭ camātikaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟkup periya kaṟkaḷi iḻuttal, kōyil, araṇmaṉi amittal pōṉṟavikaḷukku makkaḷ kūliyiṉṟi vēli ceytal kaṭṭāyamāka iruntatu. vayal vēli ceyyum kūliyāṭkaḷ tamatu vaṟumiyil tiruptiyaṭintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭ perum pakutiyiṉar aṭimikaḷāvar. ivarkaḷuṭ palar pōrkaḷil piṭittu aṭimikaḷākkap paṭṭavar; cilar kaṭaṉukkāka aṭimikaḷākkap paṭṭavar. cila camayaṅkaḷil aṭimikaḷip piṭippataṟkut toliviṭaṅkaḷukku āyutam tāṅkiya kūṭṭaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa peṇkaḷum ciṟuvarkaḷum nalla vilikku viṟkappaṭṭārkaḷ.
kittoḻilkaḷ
kuṭikaḷ vēli ceytapōtu celvam mikuntatu. kittoḻilum vāṇipamum purivōrukku uṇavupporuḷkaḷ otukkivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptil ulōkam kiṭippatilli. ākavē avarkaḷ ataṉi arāpiyāvilum niyūpiyāvilum iruntu peṟṟaṉar.
ekiptiya aracar pōriṟ ciṟipiṭikkap paṭṭavarkaḷiyum, ciṟit taṇṭaṉi peṟṟavarkaḷiyum ciṟiccālikaḷil kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka aṭittu vittaṉar. ivarkaḷ taṉimiyākavum, cila camayaṅkaḷil kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭum poṉ curaṅkaṅkaḷil vēli ceyyumpaṭi pōkkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ purinta kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkākap paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭu ivvāṟu ceyyappaṭṭatu. paḻiya araca paramparik kālattilēyē ekiptiyar cempit takarattōṭu kalantu veṇkalam ceyya aṟintiruntaṉar. vāḷ, talikkavacam, kēṭayam mutaliyavum, veṇkala vaṇṭic cakkaraṅkaḷ, uruḷikaḷ, miṇṭikaḷ, kampikaḷ, āppukaḷ, caṅkup puriāṇikaḷ, kaṟkaḷi aṟukkum arivāḷkaḷ pōṉṟavi veṇkalattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. maravēli ceyvatil avarkaḷ tiṟami aṭintiruntaṉar. marakkalam, vaṇṭi, nāṟkāli, kaṭṭil, cavappeṭṭi pōṉṟa aḻakiya poruḷkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōliliruntu āṭi, ampaṟāt tūṇi, kēṭakam, maṇi pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. camātic cuvar oṉṟil oruvaṉ tōl pataṉiṭum kāṭci eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tōl pataṉiṭupavaṉiṉ kiyil vit tiruppatupōṉṟa kattiyi iṉṟum ekiptil tōl vēli ceyvōr payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
pipiras eṉṉum nāṇaliliruntu kayiṟukaḷ, pāykaḷ, ceruppu, kaṭutāci mutaliyavi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. poruḷkaḷukkuc cāyam pūcutal, marapporuḷukkup pacikkūḻ (vārṉiṣ) pūcutal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aḻakiya caṇal āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ neyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷip paṭṭō caṇalō eṉṟu aṟivataṟkup pūtakkaṇṇāṭi mūlam pārkkavēṇṭi yirukkiṉṟatu. muṟkāla ekiptiyariṉ ki necavut tuṇikaḷ ikkāla iyantira necavut tuṇikaḷ pōṉṟavi. nīrāvip poṟikaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭuvataṉ muṉ vāḻnta camīpakāla makkaḷ paḻiya ekiptiyarilum pārkka evvaki yilāvatu atika tiṟamiyuṭiyavarkaḷallar.
vēli purivōril, oru pakutiyiṉar aṭimi kaḷum, maṟṟoru pakutiyiṉar aṭimikaḷallā tōrumāyiruntaṉar. iṉṟu intiyāvil kāṇap paṭutalpōla ovvoru toḻil ceyvōrum ovvoru cātiyiṉarāyiruntaṉar. piḷḷikaḷ tanti ceyta toḻiliyē ceytārkaḷ. periya pōrkaḷil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ periya nilaṅkaḷil vēli ceyvataṟkum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amippataṟkum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. mūṉṟām irāmcē eṉṉum aracaṉ taṉatu āṭciyil 113,000 aṭimikaḷik kōvil kaḷukkuk koṭuttāṉ. kittoḻil puriyum aṭimi kaḷallāta toḻilāḷar kaṇkāṇi oruvaṉiṉkīḻk kūṭṭāka vēli ceytārkaḷ. kūṭṭāka vēli ceyva tāl kiṭikkum ūtiyam taṉittaṉi ovvoru varukkum pakuntu aḷikkappaṭṭatu. vēliyāṭkaḷ vēlikkuc cellātu iṭiyiṭiyē kūṭṭāka niṉṟuviṭuvatu potunikaḻcci.
ekiptiyariṉ kaṭṭaṭamamikkum muṟi kirēkkar urōmar aṟintiruntavikaḷilum mēlāṉatu. mūṉṟām ceṉucaṟeṟ eṉṉum aracaṉ āṟṟu nīrit taṭuttuk kuḷamākkuvataṟku 27 mil nīḷamuḷḷa peruñcuvar oṉṟi eṭuttāṉ. itaṉāl 25,000 ēkkar nilam nīrpāyccip payir ceyvataṟku ēṟṟatākkappaṭṭatu. nīla āṟṟiyum ceṅkaṭaliyum iṇikkum vāykkālkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. āyiram ‘ṭaṉ’ pārattukku mēṟpaṭṭa kaṟṟūṇkaḷ neṭuntolivukku eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭaṉa. periya kaṟpāḷaṅkaḷ koḻuppup pūciya kuṟṟikaḷ mītu vittu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa aṭimikaḷāl iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭaṉa. poṟikaḷ arumi yāka iruntaṉa. āṭkaḷiṉ vēli malivāka iruntatu. camātic cuvar oṉṟil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa ōviyattil iraṇṭu periya kaṟṟūṇkaḷ ēṟṟap paṭṭa marakkalatti, eṇṇūṟupēr taṇṭu valittu ōṭṭum irupattēḻu marakkalaṅkaḷ iḻuttuc cellum kāṭci kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. nūṟu aṭi nīḷamum aimpatuaṭi akalamumuḷḷa marakkalaṅkaḷ nīlanatiyiliruntu ceṅ kaṭalukkuc ceṉṟu mattiyatarik kaṭali aṭintaṉa. nilattil cumikaḷ makkaḷāl koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil kaḻutikaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tālamikaḷ kālamvariyil oṭṭakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭa villi. vaṟiya makkaḷ naṭantu allatu oṭṭakattiṉ mītu ceṉṟārkaḷ; celvar aṭimikaḷāl cumakkum cāyvu nāṟkāliyil iruntu ceṉṟārkaḷ. piṟkālaṅkaḷil tērkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
ekiptil oḻuṅkāṉa añcal pōkkuvarattu naṭipeṟṟatu. pōkku varattu mikak kaṭumiyāka iruntatu. naṉṟāyamikkappaṭāta cila vītikaḷē iruntaṉa. vāṇika muṟi mikap paḻam pōkkāka iruntatu. ayalnāṭṭu vāṇikam ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarntatu. vaḻikaḷil amikkappaṭṭirunta cuṅkac cāvaṭikaḷil cuṅkam peṟappaṭṭatu. ekiptu ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu mūlap poruḷkaḷi vāṅkik kittoḻil paṇṭaṅkaḷi veḷināṭukaḷukkup pōkkiṟṟu. ciriyā, kirēttā, cipiras mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ vaṇikar ekiptiyac cantikaḷil kūṭiṉar. piṉīciya marakkalaṅkaḷ nīla āṟṟu vaḻiyākat tīps nakari aṭintaṉa.
nāṇayap puḻakkam vaḷarcci aṭiya villi. aracāṅka vēliyāḷariṉ campaḷaṅkaḷ, tāṉiyam, uroṭṭi, matu pōṉṟa vakikaḷil koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya aracariṉ karuvūlattil paṇam irukkavilli. atu vayalkaḷilum, kaṭikaḷilum kiṭikkum āyirakkaṇak kāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ paṇṭakacāliyāka iruntatu. mūṉṟām tutmōs1 eṉpa vaṉukkuppiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vili, mōtiraṅkaḷ allatu poṉ tuṇṭukaḷ vakiyil koṭukkappaṭṭatu. vilikaḷiṉ aḷavukku ivikaḷ niṟuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl aracāṅkattārāl vili kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa nāṇayaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkavilli. kaṭaṉ koṭuttu vāṅkum muṟi atikam vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntatu. itaṟku āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutum eḻuttāḷar palar iruntaṉar. ekiptiya eḻuttāḷaṉ, taṉ kiyil vittiruppativiṭac ciṟappākak kātil oru eḻutukōli vittukkoṇṭum aṭṭaṇik kāl kaṭṭikkoṇṭum iruppāṉ.
aracāṅkam
eḻuttāḷariyuṭiya niliyaṅkaḷ mūlam ekiptiya aracarum pirapukkaḷum nāṭṭil oḻuṅkiyum niyāyattiyum kāppāṟṟi vantārkaḷ. paḻiya eḻuttup poṟitta paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil avvaki eḻuttāḷar makkaḷ tokiyik kaṇakku eṭuttal, varumāṉa vari toṭarpāṉa kaṇakkukaḷic cōtittal mutaliyavikaḷic ceytārkaḷ eṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nīla āṟṟupperukki aḷappataṟku eḻuttāḷar cilar iruntaṉar; ataṉik koṇṭu avvāṇṭiṉ tāṉiya viḷiviyum, aracāṅkattiṉ varuṅkāla varuvāyiyum kaṇakkuc ceytu varuvāyk kēṟpa aracāṅkap pirivu kaḷukku muṉṉamē celavu otukkappaṭṭatu. eḻuttāḷar cilar vāṇikatti mēṟpārttārkaḷ. varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkattilēyē oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭa ivvakip poruḷātārat tiṭṭam uṇṭāyiruntatu.
koṭuttal, vāṅkutal, nilam, kuṟṟam toṭar pāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ mika vaḷarcci yaṭintiruntaṉa. aintāvatu araca paramparik kālattil taṉippaṭṭa cotturimi paṟṟiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ mikac cikkalāka iruntaṉa. namatu kālattip pōla ellōrukkum niyāyam orēvakiyāka iruntatu. niyāyātipati, vaḻakkāḷi etirikaḷiṉ vākkumūlaṅkaḷi eḻuti oppiṭṭup pārttut tīrppuc ceytāṉ. poyc cattiyañ ceytavaṉukku maraṇataṇṭaṉi vitikkap paṭṭatu. ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷil ciṟiya niyāyat tīrppu iṭaṅkaḷum, mempis, tīps, heliyō polis mutaliyaviṭaṅkaḷil periya niyāyat tīrppu iṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṭattiliruntu uṇmi peṟumporuṭṭu vētaṉi koṭukkappaṭṭatu. kampiṉāl aṭittal potuvāṉa taṇṭaṉiyāka iruntatu. mūkku, kātu, ki, nā mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ veṭṭavumpaṭṭaṉa. curaṅkaṅkaḷil vēli ceyyumpaṭi pōkkutal, tirukik kollutal, taliyik koytal, tūṇil kaṭṭi neruppiṉāl koḷuttutal pōṉṟa taṇṭaṉikaḷum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. catiyi arittut tiṉṉumpaṭi uṭal muḻuvatum oruvaki maruntip pūciviṭuvatu taṇṭaṉikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum koṭiyatākum. uyarnta vakuppiṉar palar, makkaḷ muṉṉiliyil kolittaṇṭaṉi aṭi yāmal tammittāmē koṉṟukoḷḷumpaṭi aṉumatikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. ikkālak kāvalar (pōlīs) pōṉṟavarkaḷ irukkavilli.
ekiptu, vaṉāntarattukkum kaṭalukkum iṭiyil iruntamiyāl ataṉik kāppataṟkup perumpaṭi vēṇṭiyirukkavilli. aracaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyil periya talivaṉ amiccaṉāka iruntāṉ. ivaṉ mutal mantiriyākavum karuvūlat talivaṉākavum kaṭami āṟṟiṉāṉ. aracaṉiṉ niyāyat tīrppē ellāt tīrppukaḷukkum muṭivāyiruntatu. nērakkūṭiya periya poruṭcelavik kaṭciyiṉar poruṭpaṭuttavilliyāyiṉ vaḻakku aracaṉ muṉ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. avaṉ nāṭṭic cuṟṟip payaṇañ ceytapōtu kāṇiyāḷarākiya pirapukkaḷ avaṉit tamatu ellikaḷil etirkoṇṭu kāṇikkikaḷ vaḻaṅki mariyāti ceytaṉar. iraṇṭām ameṉ arop eṉṉum aracaṉukkup pirapu oruvaṉ poṉṉālum, veḷḷiyālum ceyta vaṇṭikaḷiyum, tantattālum, karuṅkāli marattiṉālum ceyta pāvikaḷiyum; aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, pōr āyutaṅkaḷiyum, 680 kēṭakaṅkaḷiyum, 140 veṇkala īṭṭikaḷi yum, vili uyarnta ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyta palavaki ēṉaṅkaḷiyum koṭuttāṉ. iv viparam paḻiya paṭṭiyam oṉṟiṟ poṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. aracaṉukkut tuṇipuriyum perumakkaḷ capi oṉṟu iruntatu. aracaṉ kurumāriṉ tuṇiyip peṟṟut, tāṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟal eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. iṟantapiṉ avaṉ kaṭavuḷāka māṟukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum nampikki iruntu vantatu.
aracaṉ kaṭavuḷ pōṉṟa tōṟṟappolivōṭiruntāṉ. avaṉic cūḻntu meykāppāḷar niṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷil cilar paṭittalivar; cilar aracaṉukku āṭikaṭṭi alaṅkarippavar; palar uyarnta patavikaḷi luḷḷōrāvar. avaṉuṭiya mēṉiyi alaṅkarikkum poruḷkaḷi mēṟpārppataṟku irupatu karumakārar iruntārkaḷ. nāvitaṉ avaṉuṭiya mukatti maḻittu mayirik kattarittāṉ. taliyi alaṅkarippavarkaḷ avaṉi nīrāṭṭit taliyil muṭiyi vittārkaḷ; cilar avaṉuṭiya nakaṅkaḷi miṉukkiṉārkaḷ; vācaṉi ūṭṭuvōr avaṉ uṭalukku naṟumaṇam ūṭṭiṉar; kaṇṇimikku mi tīṭṭiṉar; itaḻkaḷukkum kaṉṉaṅkaḷukkum civappu niṟam pūciṉar.
oḻukkam
aracaṉ perumpālum taṉ cakōtariyi maṇantāṉ. makkaḷiṭiyum ivvakit tirumaṇaṅkaḷ paraviṉa. kiṟittuvukkuppiṉ iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil ekiptiya makkaḷil mūṉṟil iraṇṭu paṅkiṉar ivvaki vaḻakkam uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. avaṉ maṉiviyarallāta peṇkaḷum aracaṉuṭiya antappurattil iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pōrk kālaṅkaḷil pakivar nāṭukaḷiliruntu piṭikkappaṭṭavarkaḷum, pirapukkaḷiṉ kumārikaḷum, ciṟṟaracarāl koṭukkappaṭṭavarkaḷumāvar. mūṉṟām ameṉhorep eṉpavaṉukku nahariṉā nāṭṭu aracaṉ taṉatu mūtta kumāriyiyum munnūṟu kaṉṉip peṇkaḷiyum koṭuttāṉ. pirapukkaḷiṉ nilimikkēṟpa avarkaḷiṭiyēyum ivvaki āṭamparaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
naṭuttaramāṉa varuvāyuṭiya makkaḷ orē peṇṇi maṇantaṉar. ik kāla āṭavar makaḷir oḻukkaṅkaḷ pōlavē akkāla ekiptiya āṭavar makaḷiriṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷ uyarnta paṇpuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. muṟkālattil tirumaṇa nīkkaṅkaḷ mika arumiyāka iruntaṉa. pintiya araca paramparik kālattil tirumaṇa nīkkaṅkaḷ potu nikaḻcciyāka iruntu vantaṉa. oruvaṉ maṉiviyit tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytāl avaṉ kuṭumpac cottil niyāyamāṉa oru pakutiyip peṇṇukkuk koṭukkavēṇṭi yiruntatu. iṉṟu pala nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu pōlavē peṇkaḷiṉ nili uyarvaṭin tiruntatu. muṟkālattilāvatu ikkālattilāvatu eccātiyā rāvatu ekiptiyarip pōlap peṇkaḷukku uyarnili aḷikkavilli. ekiptiya ōviyaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ vītikaḷilē taṉiyē pōykkoṇṭum vantu koṇṭum iruppatākavum, potu iṭaṅkaḷil uṇavu uṇpatākavum, kit toḻil, vāṇikaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭiruppatākavum kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷi vīṭṭil kaṭṭuppaṭutti vikkum kirēkkar ivarkaḷiṉ viṭutaliyik kaṇṭu āccariyamaṭintaṉar. ekiptiyak kaṇavar tam maṉiviyarukkuk kīḻppaṭivatākak kaliyāṇa uṭaṉpaṭikkiyil kiyeḻut tiṭṭārkaḷ. tāyāṭci muṟiyiṉāl ekiptiyap peṇkaḷiṉ nili ciṟitu ciṟitāka uyarvaṭintatu. peṇ, vīṭṭukkut taliviyāyiruntāḷ. cot turimiyum peṇ vaḻiyākavē iruntuvantatu. tirumaṇap pativu kālattiṟ kaṇavaṉ taṉatu cottiyum varuṅkālat tēṭṭattiyum peṇṇukkē eḻuti vittāṉ. tāymūlam peṇṇic cērum cottukku ācip paṭṭē ekiptiyar cakōtariyi maṇantārkaḷ. tāymūlam kuṭumpatti aṭiyum cotti allatu tāyatti ayalavaṉ āṇṭaṉupavippati avarkaḷ virumpavilli. tālamikaḷ eṉṉum piṟkāla ekiptiya aracar kālattil kirēkkar vaḻakkaṅkaḷ paraviyiruntaṉa. ataṉāl tirumaṇa nīkkattip paṟṟiya urimi kaṇavaṉukku māttiram aḷikkappaṭṭatu. im māṟṟam uyar kuṭumpaṅkaḷil māttiram kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. potumakkaḷ tāyāṭci muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiṉārkaḷ. kōvilkaḷil tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar. ekiptiyar tamatu paḻiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyē nīṇṭakālam piṉpaṟṟi vantaṉar. pōrvīrar tām koṉṟa pakivariṉ valakkikaḷi veṭṭivantu eḻuttāḷaṉiṭattil kāṭṭip pativu ceytārkaḷ. makkaḷ cokkaṭṭāṉ pōṉṟa palavaki viḷiyāṭṭukaḷi viḷiyāṭiṉar. ikkāla viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟa kōli, pantu, pamparam pōṉṟavikaḷic ciṟuvar vittu viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. maṟpōr, kuttuccaṇṭi, māṭṭuc caṇṭi mutaliyavi makkaḷiṉ ciṟanta poḻutu pōkkukaḷāka iruntaṉa.
viruntuk kālaṅkaḷil viruntāḷikaḷukku ēvalar naṟumaṇam pūciṉārkaḷ; pūmālikaḷ iṭṭārkaḷ; matu vakikaḷ koṭuttārkaḷ. ekiptiya ōviyaṅkaḷil āṇkaḷ civappu niṟamākavum, peṇkaḷ mañcaḷ niṟamākavum eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. peṇkaḷ ikkāla muṟiyil kūntalik kaṭṭiyāka veṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. āṭavar poy mayiri aṇivataṟku vāyppākat taliyiyum maḻittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. aracaṉ periya poy mayir aṇintāṉ; avaṉuṭiya mukattukkuc civappuppoṭi pūcappaṭṭatu; itaḻkaḷukkuc civappuc cāyam ūṭṭappaṭṭatu; nakattukkuk kaṟuppuccāyam pūcappaṭṭatu; talimayirukku ney taṭavappaṭṭatu; kaṇṇukku mi tīṭṭappaṭṭatu. celvap peṇkaḷi aṭakkañ ceyta camāti aṟikaḷuḷ iruvakic civappuc cuṇṇaṅkaḷiyum eḻuvaki naṟumaṇat tilaṅkaḷi yum vittārkaḷ. iviyallāta vēṟu mēṉi miṉukkukkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi, mayir maḻikkum katti, mayir curuṭṭi, koṇṭi ūci, cīppu, cuṇṇam vikkum cimiḻkaḷ, maram, takaram, kalnār allatu veṇkalattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭukaḷ, karaṇṭikaḷ eṉpavi. āṭikaḷukkum mēṉikkum palavaki maṇapporuḷkaḷ ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷ, kariya pōḷam, piṟa maṇapporuḷkaḷ eṉpavi kaḷāl naṟumpuki ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
ekiptil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāṟ ciṟuvarum paṉṉiraṇṭu patimūṉṟu vayatukaḷ variyil nirvāṇamākac ceṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ; kaḻuttu māliyum kātaṇiyum aṇintaṉar. peṇ kuḻanti kaḷiṉ ariyil maṇikaḷ kōtta vaṭam aṇiyap paṭṭatu. potumakkaḷum vēliyāḷkaḷum ariyil oru uṭi māttiram uṭuttiruntaṉar. paḻiya ekiptil āṭavarum makaḷirum arikkumēl nirvāṇamākac ceṉṟaṉar. uṭi, muḻaṅkāl variyum maṟiyumpaṭi ariyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṟukiya veṇ caṇalāṭi yākum. nāṇam paḻakkattāl uṇṭāvatēyaṉṟi iyaṟkiyāka uṇṭāvataṉṟu. mutal araca paramparik kālak kurumārum ariyil āṭiyi uṭuttiṉar. celvam ēṟa ēṟa uṭiyum atikappaṭṭatu. vaṇṭi celuttuvōrum parivāraṅkaḷum āṭamparamāṉa uṭi aṇintukoṇṭu tamatu talivarkaḷiṉ tērkaḷ celvataṟku vaḻiyi āyattañ ceyyumpaṭi vītikaḷil ōṭic ceṉṟārkaḷ. celvac ceḻippuṇṭāṉa kālattup peṇkaḷ iṟukkamāka uṭukkum uṭikkup patil taḷarnta aṅkiyi aṇintārkaḷ. uṭi yil pala vakiyāṉa tiyal vēlippāṭukaḷ, curukkukaḷ, pāmpukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷ vittu alaṅkāraṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
irupālārum aṇikalaṉkaḷi virumpiṉar. kaḻuttu, mārpu, maṇikkaṭṭu, puyam, keṇṭikkāl mutaliyavikaḷil aṇikaḷ aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru eḻutuvōṉum allatu vaṇikaṉum veḷḷi allatu poṉṉiṉāṟ ceyta muttiriyi vittiruntāṉ; ovvoru āṭavaṉum mōtiramaṇintiruntāṉ. ovvoru peṇṇum alaṅ karikkappaṭṭa caṅkili aṇintiruntārkaḷ. patiṉeṭṭāvatu araca paramparik kālattil āṇ peṇ ciṟiyavar periyavar eṉṟa pētamiṉṟi ellōrum kātil tuḷiyiṭṭuk kātaṇi aṇintārkaḷ. irupālārum vili uyarnta kaṟkaḷ kōtta mālikaḷiyum piṟa nakikaḷiyum aṇintārkaḷ. akkāla ekiptiyap peṇ, mēṉi miṉukkut toṭarpāka ikkālap peṇṇiṭam kaṟṟukkoḷḷavēṇṭiyatu yātum illi.
kalvi
iṉṟiya urōmāṉiyak kattōlikkak kōyilkaḷiyoppap paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷ kōyilōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kurumār celvariṉ piḷḷikaḷukkuk kalvi kaṟpittārkaḷ. paḻiya paḷḷikkūṭa aḻipāṭu oṉṟil ak kālattil eṇṇap payaṉpaṭuttiya cippikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āciriyariṉ vēli aracāṅkattukku eḻuttu vēli utavum eḻuttāḷari uṇṭākkuvatē. māṇākkarukku eḻucci uṇṭākum poruṭṭu āciriyar kalviyippaṟṟi ūkkam eḻuppakkūṭiya kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutiṉār. “ulakil oruvaṉukkuk kīḻ aṭaṅki vāḻāta toḻilāḷi illi; paṭittavaṉ taṉṉittāṉē āḷ kiṉṟāṉ” eṉa oru pipiras ēṭṭil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
paḻaṅkāla māṇavar payaṉpaṭuttiya payiṟci ēṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ōrattil viṭappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻutappaṭāta pakutiyil āciriyar tiruttam ceytirukkiṉṟār. mukkiyak kalvi vācakam eḻututal. atu cūḷi yiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭu allatu cuṇṇāmpukkal taṭṭukaḷil eḻutappaṭṭatu. kalvi vāṇika campantamāka iruntatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷil māṇavar tam kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku mutukil aṭikkappaṭṭārkaḷ.
aracāṅkak karuvūlattōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭirunta uyarnta paḷḷiyil kōyil toṭarpāka māṇavarukku uyarnta kalvi aḷikkappaṭṭatu araciṉar kalviccāliyil iḷam eḻuttāḷarkaḷukku āṭcimuṟi pōtikkappaṭṭatu. kalvi muṭintatum avarkaḷ karumat talivarkaḷiṉ1 kīḻ cilakālam paḻakkam peṟṟaṉar. ivvāṟu kalvi muṟi ekiptilum pāpilōṉilum vaḷarcci yaṭintatu.
uyartarak kalvi payilum māṇavar kaṭutāciyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ak kālak kaṭutācikaḷ pipiras eṉṉum nāṇalip piḷantu kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka vittu aḻuttappaṭṭavi. aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ eḻutappaṭṭa pipiras kiyeḻuttuppaṭikaḷ iṉṟum appaṭiyē iruk kiṉṟaṉa. it tāḷkaḷ oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāka iṭappuṟamāka oṭṭappaṭṭuc curuṭṭivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakip puttakaṅkaḷ cila nāṟpatu aṭi nīḷam variyiluḷḷaṉa. pukiyāl uṇṭākum kari, marappiciṉ mutaliyavikaḷit taṇṇīril karittu maci ceyyappaṭṭatu. eḻutum kucci nāṇal taṇṭiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. atu niṟamacikaḷ pūcum tūriki1 pōṉṟatu. ivarkaḷiṉ moḻi āciya nāṭṭiliruntu vantateṉat terikiṉṟatu. mikap paḻiya eḻuttukaḷ ōviya vaṭivuṭiyaṉa. ōviya eḻuttukaḷ eṉpaṉa poruḷkaḷi uṇartta ap poruḷkaḷiṉ vaṭivāka eḻutappaṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ. vīṭṭi uṇartta nīṇṭa catura meḻuti nīḷamāṉa oru pakkattil oru veḷiviṭap paṭṭatu. paṭa vaṭiviliruntu eṇṇaṅkaḷik kuṟikkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ uṇ ṭāyiṉa. ik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ av vaṭivuṭiya poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkavilli. cila karuttukaḷik kuṟittaṉa. ciṅkattiṉ muṉpuṟam āḷum taṉmiyik kuṟittatu. kuḷavi aracat taṉmiyik kuṟittatu. vāṟpētti2 āyirattik kuṟittatu. nallatu pollātatu pōṉṟa maṉattiṉāl māttiram aṟiyappaṭum taṉmikaḷik kuṟippataṟkup pēccu vaḻakkil cila olikkuṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. iv volikkuṟikaḷ uṇarttuvaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷuṭiya cila poruḷkaḷumiruntaṉa. ākavē, maṉattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭum taṉmikaḷi viḷakkuvataṟkum ap parupporuḷkaḷ eḻutik kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. novēr eṉpatu nallatu eṉpati uṇarttum. kuḻalukkum novēr eṉṟu peyar. ākavē nallatu eṉpati uṇarttak kuḻal eḻutik kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
kōyiṟ kurumār ōviya eḻuttukaḷiliruntu oruvakic curuk keḻuttik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. itu kirēkka moḻiyil herāṭik (Heratic) eṉappaṭṭatu. ivveḻuttip potu makkaḷ vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum, āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutum poruṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itilum curukkamāṉa iṉṉoru vaki eḻuttum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. itu ṭemoṭik (Demotic) eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉi ellāp potumakkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
ilakkiyam
ekiptiyariṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ‘herāṭṭik’ eḻuttil eḻuti vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nālāvatu araca paramparik kālattu eḻuttāḷaṉ oruvaṉiṉ kallaṟi oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. atil aṅku aṭakkañ ceyyappaṭṭavaṉ puttaka niliyattiṉ eḻuttāḷaṉ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu; apputtaka ālayam paḻiya nūl niliyamō aracāṅka mūla āvaṇa niliyamō eṉṟu aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. camāti nūlkaḷ eṉap paṭṭa paḻiya nūlkaḷē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi aintām āṟām araca parampariyiṉariṉ camātic cuvarkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ki.mu. 2,000 mutal ekiptil pala nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. nūlkaḷ pipiras tāḷil eḻutap paṭṭavi. avi curuṭṭic cāṭikaḷil vittut taṭṭukaḷil variciyāka vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ cāṭikaḷil eḻuti oṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvakic cāṭi oṉṟiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa nūl oṉṟil ciṉpāṭ eṉṉum kaṭalōṭiyiṉ kati kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ekiptiyariṉ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil katikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ camayat toṭarpuṭiyaṉa. piṟkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kātal, camayam mutaliyaṉa campantap paṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptiyar iciyippaṟṟi naṉku aṟin tiruntārkaḷ; pāṭṭil iciyum uṇarcciyum ciṟantavi eṉa avarkaḷāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ivikaḷ amintapōtu avikaḷiṉ veḷittōṟṟam kavaṉikkappaṭavilli. cila camayaṅkaḷil pāṭalkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷiṉ mutaṟ coṟkaḷ orēvakiyāka iruntaṉa; cila pāṭalkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷ orē vakiyāka iruntaṉa; āṉāl coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa.
ekiptil varalāṟu mikap paḻaṅkālam mutal eḻutappaṭṭuvantatu. ekiptiya araca paramparikku muṟpaṭṭa aracarum varalāṟukaḷi eḻuti vantaṉar. paṭi eṭuppuk kālaṅkaḷil varalāṟu eḻutuvōr aracaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aracariṉ veṟṟiyip pukaḻntu eḻutiṉārkaḷ. varalāṟu eḻutuvatu aḻakuk kalipōl karutappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. 2,500il ekiptiya aracariṉ peyarkaḷ eḻuti vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ella nāṭu kaḷilumpōla ekiptilum moḻi māṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. neṭuṅkālam paḻiya moḻiyilēyē nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuvantaṉa. māṇavar am moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇattiyum nikaṇṭiyum kaṟṟu avikaḷi moḻipeyarttārkaḷ. ki.mu. 14vatu nūṟṟāṇṭil pulavarkaḷ puraṭciceytu nūlkaḷi makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum moḻiyil eḻuta ārampittārkaḷ. piṉṉum oru muṟi eḻuttu naṭiyum pēccu naṭiyum vēṟu vēṟāka māṟupaṭṭaṉa. ak kālattil paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pēccu naṭiyil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. iv vaḻak kāṟum māṟṟaṅkaḷum kirēkka arāpiya urōmaṉ makkaḷiṭiyum nikaḻntaṉa.
kaṇitamum viññāṉamum
ekiptil kalvi kaṟṟavarkaḷ peritum kurumārāvar. ivarkaḷ vāḻkki kontaḷippiṉṟi iruntatu. avarkaḷukkuk kōyilkaḷil ellā vāyppukaḷum iruntaṉa. ellā vakiyāṉa mūṭa nampikkika ḷiṭiyē avarkaḷē viññāṉattit tōṟṟuvittārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkatikaḷil tot1 eṉṉum aṟivuk kaṭavuḷ ekipti 3,000 āṇṭu āṇṭār eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar āṭcikkālattil muṉpiṉ ki.mu. 18,000 variyil viññāṉam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭateṉa avarkaḷ nampukiṟārkaḷ. ik kaṭavuḷ viññāṉattippaṟṟi irupattiyāyiram pakutikaḷaṭaṅkiya nūl eḻutivittār.
ekiptiya varalāṟṟu ārampa kālattil kaṇakku atika vaḷarcci yaṭintiruntatu. piramiṭ camāti kaṭṭuvataṟku aḷavippaṟṟiya kaṇakku atikam tēvippaṭṭatu. ekiptiyar nil āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil taṅkiruntami yāl avarkaḷ avvāṟṟiṉ vaṟṟup perukkukaḷi aḷantu kuṟippiṭṭu vantārkaḷ. aḷavukārarum eḻuttāḷarum eppoḻutum āṟṟiṉāl nīr pāyccappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷi aḷantu kaṇakkuc ceytārkaḷ. itaṉāl nila aḷavi toṭarpāṉa kalvi vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. ik kalvi ekiptilēyē uṇṭāṉateṉpati ellāp paḻiya makkaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭārkaḷ.
vāṉa ārāycciyāḷar kōyilkaḷiliruntu vāṉa cōtikaḷi nōk kiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pūmi taṭṭiyāṉa nīṇṭa catura vaṭivuṭiyateṉṟum ataṉ nāṉku mūlikaḷilum malikaḷiruntu vāṉattit tāṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum karutiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kirakaṇaṅkaḷik kuṟittuvikkavilli.
kurumār tām aṟintirunta vāṉa cāttirak kalviyi maṟṟavarkaḷ aṟiyātapaṭi maṟittuk kāppāṟṟi vantaṉar. avarkaḷ naṭcattiraṅkaḷiyum kirakaṅkaḷiyum vēṟupaṭutti aṟintaṉar; kirakaṅkaḷum, naṭcattiraṅkaḷum makkaḷukku nērum naṉmi tīmikaḷukkuk kāraṇamāyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷ.
avarkaḷ āṇṭi mūṉṟu paruva kālaṅkaḷākap pirittaṉar. oṉṟu nīlanatiyiṉ perukkuvaṟṟuṅ kālam; maṟṟatu payiriṭuṅ kālam; mūṉṟāvatu aṟuppukkālam. avarkaḷiṉ ovvoru mātamum muppatu nāḷkaḷ koṇṭatu. paṉṉiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ aintu nāḷkaḷ cērkkap paṭṭaṉa. ōr āṇṭu 365¼ nāḷkaḷākak koṇṭu nālu āṇṭukaḷukku oru muṟi oru nāḷic cērttu 366 nāḷ ōr āṇṭu eṉak kaṇakkiṭum muṟi yūliyar cīcar kālattil (ki.mu. 46) uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
ekiptiyar, uṭalit tilamiṭṭup pakkuvañ ceytu vantamiyāl ivarkaḷ maṉita uṭalippaṟṟi atikam ārāycci ceyyavilli. irattak kuḻāykaḷ, kāṟṟiyum nīriyum cila nīrpporuḷkaḷiyum koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, irutayamum kuṭalum maṉattiṉ uṟiviṭameṉṟum nampiṉārkaḷ. ekiptiya maruttuvar neṟṟi piṭari ki kāl eṉpavikaḷit toṭṭup pārttu irutayam toḻiṟpaṭuvati aṟintārkaḷ.
ekiptilē maruttuvam kurumāriṭattil ārampittatu. avarkaḷiṉ nampikkikaḷ mantira vittiyil tōyntiruntaṉa. maruntuk kuḷiki kaḷilum pārkkat tāyattukaḷē nōyip pōkkuvaṉa eṉṟum, nōykaḷ pēykaḷ piṭippatāl uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum nampiṉārkaḷ. nōykaḷ mantira vittiyāl pōkkappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru maruttuvaṉum ovvoru nōykku maruttuvam ceytāṉ. ovvoru nōykkum payaṉpaṭuttum maruntukaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. oru pipiras ēṭṭil pāmpukkaṭi mutal piḷḷiyip peṟṟa peṇṇukku uṇṭākum curam variyilumuḷḷa nōykaḷukku eḻunūṟu maruntukaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cuttattiṉāl nōyip pōkkalām eṉa ekiptiyar nampiṉārkaḷ. kuḷittu muḻuki uṭampic cuttamāka vittiruttal, viratamiruttal, vāyālum vayiṟṟālum pōka maruntu uṭkoḷḷutal pōṉṟa muṟikaḷi avarkaḷ kiyāṇṭārkaḷ. ekiptiyar tiṅkaḷil mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷ aṭuttu vayiṟṟāl pōkumpaṭi maruntu uṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, vānti eṭukkum maruntiyum uṭkoṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, malavācal mūlam kuḻāy vittu malatti eṭukkum muṟiyik kiyāṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum eratōtacu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kaṭṭaṭam, ōviyam, ici mutaliyaṉa
paḻiya kalikaḷuḷ kaṭṭaṭak kali mukkiyamuṭiyatu. atu camātik kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷi alaṅkarippatilum vīṭukaḷiṉ veḷippuṟaṅkaḷi alaṅkarip patilum toṭaṅki vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. vīṭukaḷ perumpālum kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. mara vēlippāṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. vīṭṭic cuṟṟi muṟṟamum muṟṟattic cuṟṟic cuvarum iruntaṉa. veḷi muṟṟattiliruntu kūrikkup paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. kuṭiyiruppavarkaḷ nāṟpuṟaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa aṟikaḷukku ap paṭikaḷāl iṟaṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ. celvarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷōṭu tōṭṭaṅkaḷ iṇintiruntaṉa. nakaraṅkaḷil potumakkaḷ taṅkum cōlikaḷ iruntaṉa. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil niṟamūṭṭiya pāykaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. nilattukku muraṭāṉa tuṇi virikkappaṭṭiruntatu. makkaḷ ataṉmītu iruntārkaḷ. paḻiya ekiptiyar āṟu aṅkulam uyaramuḷḷa maṇimītu kuntiyiruntu uṇavu uṇṭārkaḷ. celvar metti iṭappaṭṭa nāṟkālikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
kaṭṭaṭam amikkum kal mika viliyuṭiyatāyiruntatu. aracarum kurumārum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷik kalliṉāl amittārkaḷ. pirapukkaḷ atikap paṇattiyum kaṭṭaṭam amittaṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷiyum kōvil kaḷukkuk koṭuttaṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu araca paramparik kālattil piramiṭ camāti kaṭṭum vaḻakkam niṉṟupōyiṟṟu. kunupkotapa (ki.mu.2,180) eṉṉum aracaṉ tūṇkaḷ palavaṟṟi variciyāka nāṭṭi vinōtamāṉa camāti oṉṟi amittāṉ. itip piṉpaṟṟi nil natiyiṉ mēṟkuc carivukaḷil āyiram vakiyāṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. varalāṟṟil ekiptiyarē mikapperiya kaṭṭaṭam amippōrāyiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ periya kaṟciṟpikaḷumāvar. cepriṉ (Chepren) aracaṉiṉ piramiṭ camātikku muṉṉāl kāṇappaṭum maṉitamukac ciṅkam ekiptiyariṉ ciṟpat tiṟamikkuc cāṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṟantavariṉ uruvac cilikaḷ perumpālum camātikkuḷ vippataṟkākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cilikaḷ ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ camaya campantam peṟṟiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ ulōkam, kal eṉpavi kaḷil kaṭavuḷar, aracar, aṭimikaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum, vilaṅkukaḷum, paṟavikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷiyum, viyakkat takkaṉavākac ceytaṉar. camātic cuvarkaḷil mika aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. marakkalaṅkaḷ, marañceṭikaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, viḷinilaṅkaḷ, malikaḷ, kaṭal uyirkaḷ pōlvaṉa avikaḷuṭ cila.
necavāḷar palavaki āṭikaḷi neytaṉar. vīṭukaḷil payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum palavaki marattaḷavāṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. nāṟkālikaḷ poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa. āparaṇac cimiḻ, maṇapporuḷkaḷi iṭṭu vikkum pēḻikaḷ pōṉṟa aḻakiya poruḷ kaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. veḷḷi, poṉ, veṇkalam, paḷiṅku mutaliyavi kaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ēṉaṅkaḷ mēcikaḷ mītu vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kaḻuttaṇi, muṭi, mōtiram, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi, caṅkili mutaliyavi veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliyavikaḷālum niṟakkaṟkaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭu vēli muṭintatum peṇkaḷ yāḻ, kuḻal, vīṇi mutaliyavikaḷi vācittaṉar. kōyilkaḷilum araṇ maṉikaḷilum pāṭṭu, kūttu mutaliyavi naṭipeṟṟaṉa. aracaṉ ivikaḷip pārttum kēṭṭum kaḷippataṟkup pāṭuvōriyum āṭuvōriyum āyattañ ceykiṉṟa varkaḷ palar iruntaṉar.
camayam
ekiptiyar camayatti ellāvaṟṟukkum mēlākak koṇṭārkaḷ. intiya nāṭṭiṟpōla ekiptilum teyvaṅkaḷ mikap palavāyiruntaṉa. ekiptiyariṉ periya kaṭavuḷ viṇ. viṇṇil iyaṅkum mīṉkaḷ valiya teyvaṅkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. hatar eṉṉum periya pacukkaṭavuḷ viṇṇiṉ mītu niṉṟatu. ataṉ pātaṅkaḷil tari kiṭantatu. ataṉ vayiṟṟil patiṉāyiram viṇmīṉkaḷ kiṭantu aḻaku ceytaṉa. nōms eṉappaṭṭa ciṟṟaracarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷum avikaḷippaṟṟiya paḻaṅkatikaḷum vevvēṟāka iruntaṉa. viṇ teyvam cupa eṉappaṭṭatu. atu tarimītu metuvākap paṭuttiruntatu. tari, nūyiṭ (Nuit) eṉṉum peṇ teyvam eṉappaṭṭatu. vāṉam, pūmi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ cērkkiyiṉāl ellāp poruḷkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. irāci kaḷum viṇmīṉkaḷum periya teyvaṅkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cāku eṉṉum kaṭavuḷ teyvaṅkaḷi oru nāḷil mūṉṟu muṟi viḻuṅkiṟṟu. ivveṭ eṉṉum periya teyvam oṉṟu cantiraṉi viḻuṅkiṟṟu. tēvarkaḷ kōpittamiyāl atu cantiraṉi veḷiyē kakkiṟṟu. kirakaṇam uṇṭāvati ekiptiyar ivvāṟu viḷakkiṉar. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum periya teyvaṅkaḷ. ñāyiṟu irā allatu rī eṉappaṭṭatu. atu ovvoru kāliyilum tōṉṟum; teyvat taṉmiyuṭiya tōṇiyil ceṉṟu ākāca vītiyik kaṭakkum; ovvoru māliyum mēṟkē maṟintu pōkum. ñāyiṟu āṇkaṉṟu vaṭivilum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. vāṉattuk kūṭākat tiṉam paṟantu cellum kaḻukākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kaḻuku ekiptiya camaya araca aṭiyāḷamākum. ekiptiyar pala ceṭikaḷit tūymi yuṭiyaṉavākak karutiṉar. vaṉāntarattil niḻal ceyyum pērīntu, pacuntari kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum nīrūṟṟu, iḷippāṟat takunta cōlikaḷ, maṇalil vaḷarum atti mutaliyaṉa paricutta muṭiyaṉa. ekiptiyar avikaḷukku veḷḷarikkāy, muntirikip paḻam, attippaḻam mutaliyavikaḷip paliyākak koṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. veṇkāyatti avarkaḷ teyvamākak koṇṭaṉar.
erutu, mutali, kaḻuku, pacu, vāttu, veḷḷāṭu, āṭṭukkaṭā, pūṉi, nāy, kōḻik kuñcu, tūkkaṇaṅkuruvi, nari, pāmpu mutaliyavi kōyilkaḷukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭaṉa. avi kōyilil alintu tiriyum. kōyilukkuriya vilaṅkukaḷ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. avi iṉṟu intiyak kōyilkaḷil alintu tiriyum kōyil māṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi.
āṭum erutum ociris eṉṉum teyvattiṉ piṟappukaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. icis ekiptiyariṉ tāykkaṭavuḷ. irā eṉṉum kaṭavuḷ teṟkē vāḻnta makkaḷāl amaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. ociris, icis, ōras eṉṉum teyvaṅkaḷ ekiptiyariṉ periya kaṭavuḷarāvar. irā, amaṉ, pitā eṉpōr ekiptiyariṉ mummūrttikaḷ. aracaṉum oru teyvamāka iruntāṉ. avaṉuṭiya kiyil kaḻukum, neṟṟiyil pāmpum iruntaṉa. aracaṉ kurumārukkut talivaṉ. periya viḻākkaḷil avaṉē ūrvalaṅkaḷiṉ muṉpu ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉik kaṭavuḷ eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉamiyāl avarkaḷ avaṉukkup paṇintu aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. avaṉ taṉatu valimiyip payaṉpaṭuttavēṇṭiya avaciyam uṇṭākavilli. kurumār aracaṉiṉ tuṇivarkaḷākavum, aracāṅka uḷavāḷarkaḷākavum iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ mantira vitti toṭarpāṉa pala kiriyikaḷi vakuttiruntārkaḷ. makkaḷ kaṭavuḷik kiṭṭuvataṟkut tām iṉṟiyamiyātavarkaḷ eṉak koṇṭaṉar. kuruviṉ makaṉ kuruvāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭam irukka villi; āyiṉum kuruviṉ makaṉ kuruvāka vantamiyāl av vakuppup perukiṟṟu. aracariṉ ātaraviṉālum potu makkaḷiṉ paktiyiṉālum kurumār, celvamum atikāramum uṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. avarkaḷ kāṇiyāḷarākiya pirapukkaḷilum, araca kuṭumpattiṉarilum pārkka mēlāṉa niliyil iruntaṉar. teyvaṅkaḷukku iṭappaṭum palikaḷ, kāṇikki ivikaḷāl avarkaḷukku niṟiya uṇavu kiṭittatu. kōyil avarkaḷiṉ vicālamāṉa vīṭukaḷāka iruntatu. kiriyikaḷ purivatālum, kōyil nilaṅkaḷālum avarkaḷukku nirampiya varuvāy kiṭittatu. ivarkaḷukku ellā varikaḷum nīkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. pōril cēvittal, kaṭṭāya vēlikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ivarkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttavilli. maṟṟavarkaḷ poṟāmi koḷḷakkūṭiya atikāramum celvamum uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. kalvi ivarkaḷ vacam iruntatu. kalviyik kāppavarkaḷ ivarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ iḷiñarukkuk kalvi yaḷittārkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku atikāram uṇṭā vataṟku itu mukkiya kāraṇamākum.
ekiptiyar iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷip pakkuvañceytu putittu vittārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nampikkikaḷiliruntē iṟantavar uyirtteḻutal eṉṉum koḷki kiṟittuva matattiṟ paraviṟṟu. paru uṭalōṭu cērntu noyya uṭal oṉṟu irukkiṉṟateṉṟum, maraṇa kālattil, paṟavi kūṭṭi viṭṭup paṟantupōtal pōl atu uṭali viṭṭup paṟantu celkiṉṟa teṉṟum ivarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. an nuṇṇiya uṭal kā eṉappaṭṭatu. kā vil uyir taṅkiyiruntatu. kā vum uyirum iṟappatilli. uṭalip pātukāttu vittāl kā nīṇṭa kālam irukkum.
pāvam ciṟitu millāta iṟantavarkaḷ kā uṭalil ociris eṉṉum kaṭavuḷukku muṉ vantāl ellā makiḻccikaḷum uḷḷa ulakil ep poḻutum vāḻaviṭappaṭuvar. ocirici aṭivataṟku ōṭattil ēṟiccellu tal vēṇṭum. pāvamillāta āṭavariyum makaḷiriyum māttiram ōṭak kāraṉ ōṭattil ēṟṟuvāṉ. ociris eṉṉum teyvattukkumuṉ ceṉṟa vuṭaṉ at teyvam ovvoruvariyum vicāraṇi ceyyum; avarkaḷ coṉṉa uṇmiyi aṟivataṟku avarkaḷiṉ irutayatti oru taṭṭilum maṟṟa taṭṭil oru iṟakiyum vittu niṟukkum. intac cōtaṉiyil tavaṟiṉavarkaḷ eppoḻutum camāti aṟiyil taṅkumpaṭi tirumpa aṉuppappaṭuvārkaḷ. aṅku avarkaḷ paci tākam mutaliyavikaḷāl varuntikkoṇṭum mutalikaḷāl tuṉpam aṭintukoṇṭum taṅkuvārkaḷ. ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷiyik kāṇumpaṭi avarkaḷ veḷiyē varutal muṭiyātu.
ic cōtaṉiyil tēṟuvataṟkuk kurumār pala vaḻikaḷi aṟintirun tārkaḷ. camāti aṟiyil paci tākattukku vēṇṭiya uṇavu, nīr mutaliyavi kaḷiyum utavikku vēṇṭiya vēliyāḷkaḷiyum viṭṭuvippatu oṉṟu. maṟṟoṉṟu camāti aṟiyil mīṉ, paruntu, pāmpu, vaṇṭu mutaliya vikaḷip pōl ceyta uruvaṅkaḷi vittal. kurumārāl naṉku mantirikkappaṭṭāl ivi ellāp pakikaḷiyum, tīmikaḷiyum ōṭṭum. iṉṉorumuṟi maraṇap puttakattip putippatu. ip puttakattil pala mantiraṅkaḷum tutikaḷum eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi ociris teyvatti makiḻvikkattakkaṉa.
kurumār nītikaḷi makkaḷukkup pōtippataṟkup patilākat tāyattukaḷi viṟṟārkaḷ; mantira vittikaḷ campantamāṉa kiriyikaḷ purintārkaḷ. maraṇamaṭintavarukku vaḻiyil nērum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa iṭiyūṟukaḷi maraṇapputtakattāl vellalām eṉa an nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. nērmiyāka vāḻtalilum pārkka mantiraṅkaḷi uccarippatāl atika naṉmi uṇṭākum eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.
tēvarkaḷil oruvar iṉṉoruvarukku etirāka mantiraṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ekiptiyariṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mantira vittikkārarippaṟṟi atikam kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ mantirattiṉāl kuḷaṅkaḷi vaṟṟac ceytārkaḷ; tuṇiyuṇṭa uṟuppukaḷi uriya iṭaṅkaḷil poruntumpaṭic ceytārkaḷ. ovvoru vīṭṭiṉ vāyililum tīya āvikaḷi ōṭṭumpaṭi oru kaṭavuḷ iruntatu. ovvoru mātamum ovvoru nāḷum yātum oru teyvattukku urittāy iruntaṉa. oruvaṉ piṟanta nāḷiyum nērattiyum koṇṭu vāḻnāḷil avaṉukku eṉṉa nērum eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭatu. iṟantavarkaḷi avarkaḷ cellum ulakil naṉmippaṭuttum mantiraṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa.
ikkantaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ
ki.mu. 1,380il ikkantaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ paṭṭattukku vantāṉ. ivaṉ nālām amkotep eṉavum aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ. paṭṭa meytiyatum ivaṉ kōyiṟ kurumārukku māṟākap puraṭci ceytāṉ. kōyilkaḷil āṭukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭuvatiyum, viyapicāraṅkaḷ naṭappati yum veṟuttāṉ. kurumār mantiravitti, tāyattukaḷ viṟṟal mutaliyavi kaḷik koṇṭu vāṇipam purital, veḷippāṭu kūṟutal mutaliyavi aracāṅka ūḻalkaḷ eṉṟum, kurumāriṉ vārttikaḷ makkaḷukkut tīmi viḷivippaṉa eṉṟum tuṇintāṉ; pala kaṭavuḷar vaṇakkam tīyatu eṉṟu aṟintāṉ; ataṉ eṉṉum orē kaṭavuḷ māttiram vaḻipaṭappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṭṭaḷi ceytāṉ. ataṉ kaṭavuḷ ellā nalla kuṇaṅ kaḷum, ellā āṟṟalum, eṅkum niṟintirukkum iyalpum uṭiyar eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭār. ataṉ vaḻipāṭṭuk kuriyaṉavallāta ellāk kōyil kaḷiyum mūṭumpaṭi aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ; tīps nakaram puṉitamuṭiya taṉṟu eṉakkoṇṭu akērāṉ eṉṉum nakari amittu atit talinaka rākak koṇṭāṉ. kuṭikaḷ kurumāriṉ kaṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭuc cēmamāka vāḻntārkaḷ. celvamum atikāramum mikunta kurumāri aracaṉ orē aṭiyil vīḻttiviṭṭāṉ. kurumār tiri maṟiviliruntu aracaṉukku māṟākac cati ālōcaṉikaḷ ceytaṉar.
tūtaṉ kāpaṉ
ikkantaṉ maraṇamāṉa iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷiṉpiṉ avaṉ makaḷiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa tūtaṉ kāpaṉ āṭciyi ēṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ kurumāriṉ naṇpaṉāyiruntāṉ. ippoḻutu kurumār muntiya aracaṉ ceytataṟku māṟākat tamatu koḷkikaḷi nāṭṭiṉar. ivaṉuṭiya camātiyil aḷavillāta viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ac camātiyil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷiṉ matippu 30,000 taṅka nāṇayam variyilākum. ivaṉuṭiya āṭcipaṟṟi yātum teriyavilli. ivaṉukkuppiṉ harma hāl āṭci purintāṉ; mutalām cēti eṉpavaṉ ivaṉukkuppiṉ aracaṉā ṉāṉ. karnāk eṉṉum iṭattil apicipel maliyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa iv varacaṉiṉ camāti camīpakālattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṉukkuppiṉ iraṇṭām irāmcē āṭci purintāṉ. avaṉ pālattīṉi veṟṟikoṇṭu aṅkuniṉṟum pala yūtari aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭuvantāṉ. pipiḷiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭilē yāttiri ākamattil collappaṭum ekiptiya aracaṉ ivaṉ eṉṟu karutappaṭupavaṉ. iṟakkumpōtu ivaṉukku nūṟu putalvarum aimpatu putalviyarum iruntaṉar. ivaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ mikap palarāyiruntamiyiṉ aṭutta nūṟu āṇṭukaḷil avarkaḷ taṉik kūṭṭattiṉarākap perukiyiruntārkaḷ.
aracaṉuṭiya atikārattilum pārkka iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉariṉ atikāram mēlāka iruntatu. ak kūṭṭattiṉar kurumārāvar. ellā nāṭu kaḷilum aracarukkum kurumārukkumiṭiyil atikārattiṉ poruṭṭup pōṭṭi yiruntu vantatu. ovvoru pōrilum kiṭikkum koḷḷip poruḷiṉ perum pakuti kurumārukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. mūṉṟām irāmcē kālattil avarkaḷiṭam 107,000 aṭimikaḷ iruntārkaḷ. itu ekiptiya caṉattokiyil muppatilorupakuti; 750,000 ēkkar nilam avarkaḷvacam iruntatu. itu ekiptil payiriṭum nilam muḻumiyilum ēḻil oru paṅku. avarkaḷiṭam 500,000 āṭumāṭukaḷiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ ekiptilum ciriyāvilumuḷḷa 169 nakaraṅkaḷiṉ varuvāyip peṟṟaṉar. ovvoru āṇṭum kurumārukku 32,000 kilōkirām poṉṉum, pattu ilaṭcam kilōkirām veḷḷiyum, 18,50,000 mūṭṭi tāṉiyaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. karuvūlattip pārttapōtu vēliyāḷkaḷukkuk kūli koṭukka yātum eñciyirukkavilli.
inniliyil aracar kurumāriṉ aṭimikaḷātal iyalpēyākum. irāmciṭ kālattil mutaṉmik kuru avaṉuṭiya ceṅkōlip piṭuṅkit tāṉē āṭcipurintāṉ. ak kālattil periya kōyilkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. mūṭak koḷkikaḷ ceḻippaṭintaṉa. ellā vakiyāṉa muṭivu ceyvataṟkum kurumār nimittam pārppatic ciṟappākak koṇṭaṉar. ayalnāṭṭavar ekiptiṉ mītu paṭi eṭukka āyattam ceyyum kālattil nāṭṭiṉ celvam muḻumiyum kurumārāl uṟiñcappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
ekiptiṉ vīḻcci
pāpilōṉ, acīriyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷ palamaṭintaṉa. ekiptōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭa nāṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟum ekipti veṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 954il mēṟku ellip pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ilipiyar ekiptiṉ mītu paṭi eṭuttārkaḷ. ki.mu. 722il ettiyōppiyar teṟkiṉiṉṟum vantu tammi muṉ aṭimip paṭuttiya ekiptiyarip paḻivāṅkiṉar. ki.mu. 674il acīriyar ekipti veṉṟu atit tamatu tiṟi nāṭākkiṉar. ki.pi. 525il pāracīkar ekipti veṟṟi koṇṭaṉar. ki.mu. 322il alakcāntar ekipti māciṭōṉ irācciyattiṉ pakutiyākkiṉār. ki.mu. 30il ekiptu, urōma irācciyattiṉ pakutiyāka māṟic carittirattiliruntu maṟintatu. ki.pi. 650il mukammatiyar ekipti veṉṟu keyrō nakari amittārkaḷ. ippoḻutu ekiptiyar arāpiyarōṭu kalantārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyum arapi moḻiyōṭu kalan tuḷḷatu. ekiptu, ippoḻutu mucilimkaḷuṭiya atikārattil irukkiṉṟatu.
pāpilōṉ
cumēriyāvum akkēṭiyāvum cērnta nāṭu pāpilōṉiyāvākum. yūpirātas tikiras āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil kiṭakkum aḻipāṭukaḷi iṉṟu kāṇpōrkaḷ avviṭam celvamum atikāramum niṟinta makkaḷiṉ talinakarāka iruntateṉa orupōtum niṉikkamāṭṭārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyā cālatiyā1 eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. pāpilōṉiṉ mutalaracaṉ hamurapi (ki.mu. 2,123 - ki.mu. 2,081). ivaṉ cemittiya kulattavaṉ. arāpiyāvil vāḻnta paḻiya makkaḷ cemittiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivaṉukkuc camāṣ eṉṉum ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ caṭṭaṅkaḷi aruḷicceyyum kāṭciyum, ac caṭṭaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa kaṟṟūṇ oṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ tuṇi koṇṭu akkālattil vaḻaṅkiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ, makkaḷ vāḻkki mutaliya pala ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ2 cemittiyar pōṉṟa tōṟṟamum, iruṇṭa mayirum, tāṭiyum uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālārum mayiri nīḷamāka vaḷaraviṭṭaṉar. iru pālārum pātam variyum maṟikkakkūṭiya veṇcaṇalāṭi uṭuttārkaḷ; uṭalukku naṟumaṇam pūciṉārkaḷ; peṇkaḷ iṭatu tōḷ veḷiyē teriyumpaṭi viṭṭārkaḷ. āṭavarkaḷ iru tōḷkaḷiyum maṟittuc caṭṭiyiṭṭārkaḷ. celvam vaḷarntapōtu āṭikaḷukkup palavaki niṟaṅkaḷ ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa; kōṭu, vaṭṭam, puḷḷipōṉṟa palavaki vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; makkaḷ kālukku aḻakiya cerupput tarittaṉar; āṭavar taliyil pāki vittārkaḷ; peṇkaḷ, kaḻuttu māli, kivaḷi mutaliya aṇikaḷ aṇintaṉar. āṭavar kiyil aḻakiya kittaṭikaḷik koṇṭu tirintaṉar. avikaḷiṉ talikaḷil aḻakiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avar kaḷiṉ arippaṭṭiyil aḻakāka veṭṭappaṭṭa muttirikaḷ toṅkiṉa. ivi avarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. kurumār tamatu tōṟṟatti maṟippataṟku ucciyil nīṇṭa muṉiyuṭiya toppiyi aṇintaṉar. nākarikatti uṇṭākkum atika celvam aḻivukku ētu vākum. celvattiṉāl kalikaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa; makkaḷ ullācamākavum āṭamparamākavum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl makkaḷiṉ pōrkkuṇam maḻuṅki viṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu valiyuṭiyavarkaḷum paciyuṭiyavarkaḷumākiya veḷiyē yuḷḷavarkaḷ paṭi eṭukkiṟārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiṉ kiḻakku elliyil kācirīcar (Kassites) eṉṉum makkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāpilōṉiyariṉ celvattik kaṇṭu poṟāmiyaṭintārkaḷ. hamurapi iṟantu eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷuḷ ivarkaḷ pala taṭavi pāpilōṉmītu paṭi eṭuttu nāṭṭic cūṟiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. iṟutiyil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷarāy aṅku amarntārkaḷ. aṟunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉ kācarīcarkaḷ veḷiyē turattappaṭṭārkaḷ. aṭutta nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷil āṭci kuḻappam aṭintiruntatu. appoḻutu acīriyar pāpilōṉi veṉṟu ataṉi niṉēvāviṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭuttiṉar. pāpilōṉiyar kalakam ceytapōtu ceṉṉāccerip eṉṉum acīriya aracaṉ pāpilōṉi muṟṟāka aḻittāṉ. mītiyar taliyeṭuttapōtu acīriyariṉ palam kuṟin tiruntatu. napōpolacār eṉṉum pāpilēṉiyaṉ mītiyariṉ tuṇiyip peṟṟup pāpilōṉi acīriyariṭamiruntu viṭuttāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ ivaṉ kumāraṉākiya nepucaṭṉēcar(Nebuchadnezar) āṭcipurintāṉ. ivaṉē paḻiya ēḻu viyappukaḷil oṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa toṅku tōṭ ṭatti(Hanging gardens) amittāṉ.
toḻilāḷar
pāpilōṉiṉ orupakuti kāṭāka iruntatu. neruṅki vaḷarnta puṟkaḷiṉiṭiyē pāmpukaḷ tirintaṉa. pāpilōṉiya aracar kāṭukaḷil ceṉṟu ciṅkaṅkaḷōṭu nēriṟ pōrceytu avikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiṉar. itu aracariṉ ciṟanta poḻutupōkkākak karutappaṭṭatu.
nilattiṉ perumpakuti vārakkārarālum aṭimikaḷālum payiriṭap paṭṭatu. cila pakuti nilattukkuriya kuṭikaḷāl payiriṭappaṭṭatu. putiya kaṟkālattiṟpōla nilam pāriyiṉālum maṇveṭṭiyiṉālum kiḷaṟap paṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kalappi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṟṟuveḷḷam vayaluṭ pāyātapaṭi ovvoru vayaliyum cuṟṟip periya varampukaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āṟu perukumpōtu veḷiyē vaḻintuōṭum veḷḷam kālvāykaḷ mūlam kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vikkappaṭṭatu; vēṇṭiya kālaṅkaḷil maṭikaḷit tiṟantu vayalkaḷukku nīr pāyccappaṭṭatu. ēṟṟaṅkaḷāl iṟittu nīr pāyccuvatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. nīr tēkki vikkappaṭṭa kuḷam oṉṟiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 140 mil.
vayalkaḷil palavakit tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷintaṉa; palavakip paḻa maraṅkaḷ ceḻittu ōṅkiṉa. muntiriki, ‘aliv’ mutaliyaṉa pāpilōṉi liruntu kirīs, urōm mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṉa. nāṭṭil cempu, īyam, irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷ aritteṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; maṇṇeṇ ṇiyum eṭukkappaṭṭatu. hamurapi kālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kal āyutaṅkaḷukkup patil veṇkala āyutaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ulōkaṅ kaḷi urukki vārkkum toḻil aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu. ivi veḷināṭu kaḷukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷ, vikkōl kalanta kaḷimaṇṇi ṉālum, kaḷimaṇṇil arintu kāyaviṭappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷiṉālum, ceṅkaṟkaḷālum eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
uḷnāṭṭup pōkkuvarattukkuk kaḻuti pūṭṭiya vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu. 2,100il eḻutiya paṭṭiyamoṉṟil kutiriyip paṟṟi mutaṉmutal collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kiḻakkuttēcak kaḻuti eṉappaṭṭatu. mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiṉ vaṇikar pāpilōṉ nakaril kūṭi ṉārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyar nāṇayaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttavilli; kōtumi, tāṉiyam, poṉtuṇṭu, veḷḷi mutaliyavi nāṇayaṅkaḷukkup patil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭaṉ vāṅkiyavaṉ kaṭaṉit tiruppik koṭukka muṭiyāviṭiṉ avaṉuṭiya aṭimikaḷi allatu piḷḷikaḷik kaṭaṉkāraṉ piṭittukkoḷḷap pāpilōṉiyac caṭṭam aṉumatittatu. āṉāl avaṉ avarkaḷi mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumēl vittiruttal kūṭātu. pōrkaḷil akappaṭṭa pakivar aṭimiyākkappaṭṭaṉar. piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kirāmaṅkaḷit tākki aṭimikaḷip piṭittu vāṇikam purivōrum iruntaṉar. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa vēlikaḷ aṭimikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. peṇ aṭimikaḷ tammi aṭimikaḷāka vittirukkum talivaṉiṉ tayavil taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ; talivaṉ mūlam piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟārkaḷ. aṭimikaḷ kaṭaṉukkāka īṭu vikkavum viṟkavum paṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ iruppatilum iṟantupōtal ilāpam eṉat talivaṉ niṉit tāl avaṉ avarkaḷik koṉṟuviṭalām. tappi ōṭiya aṭimiyi oruvarum vīṭṭil maṟittu vittiruttal kūṭātu. av vaṭimiyip piṭittuk koṭuppavarkaḷukku vekumati aḷikkappaṭṭatu. aṭimikaḷ allāta potumakkaḷippōla ivarkaḷum pōrukku allatu potu vēlikaḷukkut tiraṭṭappaṭṭārkaḷ. aṭimi oruvaṉ aṭimi yallāta peṇṇi maṇantāl avaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷ aṭimikaḷāka māṭṭārkaḷ. talivaṉ virumpiṉāl aṭimiyi viṭutali ceyyalām.
caṭṭam
aracaṉāl pāpilōṉ āḷappaṭṭatu. kāṇiyāḷarākiya pirapukkaḷ aracaṉuṭiya tuṇivarkaḷākaviruntaṉar. ap pirapukkaḷiṉ atikāram ciṟituciṟitāka maṟitalum vaṇikarākiya celvariṉ atikāram atikarittatu. ivarkaḷ aracaṉukkum potumakkaḷum iṭippaṭṭa niliyiluḷḷavar kaḷāvār. aracaṉukkup piṉ nāṭu avaṉ virumpiya avaṉ putalvarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉic cērntatu. āṭci aracaṉāl amikkappaṭṭa pirapukkaḷ capiyāl naṭattappaṭṭatu.
hamurapi aracaṉiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. teyvattiṉ peyarāl naṭattappaṭum cila cōtaṉikaḷāl cila kuṟṟaṅkaḷ muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ mantira vittikkāraṉeṉṟō, orutti paratti eṉṟō kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭāl avarkaḷ yūpirātas āṟṟil eṟiyap paṭṭārkaḷ. naṉṟāka nīntak kūṭiyavar cārpil kaṭavuḷ iruntār. mantira vittikkāraṉ nīril mūḻki iṟavāviṭiṉ kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiyavaṉiṉ cotti avaṉ peṟṟāṉ. parattik kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭavaḷ iṟantuviṭṭāl, avaḷ parattiyāvāḷ! piḻittāl kuṟṟamaṟṟavaḷāvāḷ. kurumār nītipati kaḷāvar. kōyilkaḷil nītimaṉṟaṅkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ iṉṉoru vaṉiṉ kaṇṇiyō palliyō ūṉap paṭuttiṉāl avaṉukku atē taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. oru vīṭu viḻuntu ataṉi vāṅkiyavaṉik koṉṟuviṭṭāl, ataṉik kaṭṭiṉavaṉ allatu kaṭṭuvittavaṉ maraṇa taṇṭaṉi peṟṟāṉ. ciṟumi oruttiyi oruvaṉ aṭittuk koṉṟuviṭṭāl avaṉuṭiya ciṟumi avvāṟu kollappaṭṭāḷ. kālattil taṇṭaṉi kaḷukkup patil paṇam naṭṭa īṭākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. tantiyi oruvaṉ aṭittāl avaṉuṭiya kikaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cattira vittiyaṅ kāraṇa māka nōyāḷi iṟantāl allatu avaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ paḻutu aṭin tāl maruttuvaṉiṉ kiviralkaḷ naṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟpaḻittal, peṇ kaḷik kaṭattic cellutal, koḷḷi, tiruṭu, vēṟu kaṇavaṉi maṇat taṟkuk kaṇavaṉip peṇ koli ceytal, peṇkuru matukkaṭikaḷil nuḻital, ōṭippōṉa aṭimiyi vīṭṭil vittiruttal, pōril payantu ōṭutal, vīṭṭup peṇ kavaṉakkuṟivāyiruttal allatu cikkaṉamillātu celavu ceytal pōṉṟa kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. hamurapiyiṉ caṭṭattil kottar, ceṅkal cūḷiyiṭuvōr, tiyaṟkārar, kaṟkottar, taccar ōṭakkāraṉ, iṭiyaṉ, kūliyāḷ mutaliyōriṉ kūlikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tantiyiṉ cottu piḷḷikaḷic camapaṅkāka aṭintatu. kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ peṇ taṉatu maraṇam variyil kuṭumpat taliviyāka iruntāḷ. kurumār eḻuttāḷarāka vēlikaḷ purintaṉar. oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉik kolikkuṟṟañ cāṭṭi ak kuṟṟatti uṟutip paṭuttāviṭṭāl kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiyavaṉ kolittaṇṭaṉi aṭintāṉ. aracaṉuṭiya nītipatikaḷ aṭaṅkiya vicāraṇi capi oṉṟu pāpilōṉ paṭṭiṉattil iruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉē mēl vicāraṇikaḷi naṭattiṉāṉ.
teyvaṅkaḷ
aracaṉuṭiya atikāram caṭṭaṅkaḷālum, atikārikaḷālum, kurumārālum kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu. aracaṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ cārpāḷaṉāka (Agent) iruntāṉ. kaṭavuḷ peyarāl varikaḷ vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. kurumārāl araca atikāram vaḻaṅkappaṭum variyil aracaṉi uṇmiyāṉa aracaṉāka makkaḷ karutavilli. ivvaki atikāram vaḻaṅkum potu kurumār mārṭuk eṉṉum kaṭavuḷi alaṅkarittu ataṉi ūrvalam ceytārkaḷ. appoḻutu aracaṉ kuruvippōl uṭi uṭuttappaṭṭāṉ. cumēriya paṟṟēci eṉṉum kōyiṟ kurumārākiya ciṟṟaracar mutal nepuccaṭṉēcar variyil nāṭu kurumār atikārattuk kuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. talimuṟitōṟum kōyilkaḷiṉ celvam vaḷarntatu. teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tayavip peṟum poruṭṭu aracar kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ; avikaḷukku vēṇṭiya aṭimikaḷiyum, taḷavāṭaṅḷiyum koṭuttārkaḷ; periya nilaṅkaḷi avikaḷukku māṉiyaṅkaḷāka viṭṭārkaḷ. pōrkaḷil ciṟipiṭitta aṭimikaḷiṉ mutaṟ pakuti kōyilukkuk koṭukkap paṭṭatu; koḷḷip poruḷkaḷum avvāṟu koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. pērīntiṉ paḻam, tāṉiyam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ, cila nilaṅkaḷiliruntu kōyilkaḷukkuk kiṭittaṉa. ivvāṟu kiṭikkat tavaṟiyapōtu nilaṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷāl mēṟpārkkap paṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu nilaṅkaḷ kurumāriṉ kikku māṟiṉa. celvarum vaṟiyarum ivvulaka vāḻvil tamatu cemmiyi virumpik kōvil kaḷukku atikap poruḷik koṭuttārkaḷ. kōyiṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷil poṉ, veḷḷi, vayiram, maṇikaḷ pōṉṟa viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷ kuvintaṉa. kurumār ic celvattit tām nērākap payaṉpaṭutta muṭiyā tiruntatu. ākavē avarkaḷ payirttoḻil, kittoḻil naṭattuvō rākavum, vaṭṭikkup paṇam koṭuttu vāṅkuvōrākavu miruntaṉar. avar kaḷiṭattil periya nilaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avikaḷil vēli ceyvataṟku aṭimikaḷum iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṭimikaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷiṭattil vēli ceyyumpaṭi viṭṭuk kūli peṟṟaṉar. kurumār periya vaṇikarāyu miruntaṉar; kōyiliṟ kiṭikkum kāṇikkip poruḷkaḷ kōyililuḷḷa kaṭikaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. palar tamatu uṭamikaḷi avarkaḷiṭam nampikki yiruntatāl koṭuttu vittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutuvōrākavum, vaḻakkukaḷi vicāraṇi ceytu tīrppaḷippōrākavum iruntaṉar.
cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉ kōyiṟ paṇattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ. āṉāl itu āpattāṉa ceyalāka viruntatu. kōyiṟ cottil oru turumputāṉum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟavarkaḷ mītu kurumār koṭiya cāpaṅkaḷip poḻintārkaḷ aracaṉukkup potumakkaḷiṭamuḷḷa atikārattilum mēlākak kurumārukku iruntatu. aracaṉ iṟantupōkiṉṟavaṉ. kaṭavuḷar eppoḻutum iruppavar. kurumār tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟavarkaḷallar. avarkaḷukku varikaḷumilli; pōril kalappatāl nērum tuṉpamum illi. vaṇikar nāṭṭic celvattāl ceḻippaṭiyac ceytārkaḷ. kurumār ataṉi anupavittārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil pala teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiṟittuvukkumuṉ oṉpa tām nūṟṟāṇṭil eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi aṅku 65,000 kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭiṉattilum, kirāmattilum pala teyvaṅkaḷ irun taṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ camāṣ eṉavum, tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ naṉṉar eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru vīṭṭilum vīṭṭut teyvam iruntatu. avi kaḷukkuk kāliyilum māliyilum paliyiṭappaṭṭatu. muṉpu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa mārṭuk ippoḻutu tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ āṉāṉ.
cuvarkkattil tēvarkaḷ māttiram vāḻntārkaḷ. iṟantavar aralu eṉṉum narakattil, kiyum kālum kaṭṭuṇṭu kuḷirāl naṭuṅkikkoṇṭum, paci tākaṅkaḷāl varuntikkoṇṭum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ putalvar camātiyil kālantōṟum uṇavi vittālaṉṟi ivarkaḷukku uṇavu kiṭippatilli. ivvulakil keṭṭa vāḻkki naṭattiyavarkaḷukku atika taṇṭaṉikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ uṭalil kuṭṭa nōyum piṟa nōykaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa.
piṇaṅkaḷ nila aṟikaḷil putikkappaṭṭaṉa; cila koḷuttap paṭṭaṉa. piṇaṅkaḷ tilamiṭṭup pakkuvañ ceyyappaṭavilli. kūlikku aḻuvōr avikaḷik kaḻuvi, vācaṉi pūci, uṭutti, kaṇṇukku mi eḻuti viralkaḷil mōtiramiṭṭu alaṅkarittārkaḷ. peṇ piṇaṅkaḷiṉ pakkattē vācaṉip puṭṭikaḷ, cīppu, mi tīṭṭuṅ kucci, kaṇṇukkut tīṭṭum mi mutaliyaṉa vikkappaṭṭaṉa. nalla muṟiyākap piṇaṅkaḷ putikkap paṭāviṭṭāl iṟantavar iruppavarkaḷukkut tuṉpam viḷivippar; uṇavukkāka alintu tirivar; nāṭṭil koḷḷi nōyi uṇṭākkuvar eṉa eṇṇiṉar. iv vaccaṅ kāraṇamākavē pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ kurumārukkum kaṭavuḷarukkum nalla uṇavukaḷi aḷittu vantārkaḷ.
kaṭavuḷukku uṇavum nīrum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; āṭṭukkuṭṭi pali yiṭappaṭṭatu. āṭṭukkuṭṭi maṉitaṉukkup patil iṭappaṭum pali. kurumār puriyum palavakik kiriyikaḷōṭu paliyum iṭappaṭṭatu. oḻuṅkupaṭṭa vāḻkkiyilum pārkka muṟiyāṉa kiriyiyē mēlāṉatu eṉak koḷ ḷappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉuṭiya kaṭami kōvilkaḷukku muṟiyākap palika ḷiṭṭuc cariyāṉa tutikaḷ pāṭuvatākum. oruvaṉ viḻuntu kiṭakkum etiriyiṉ kaṇkaḷip piṭuṅkiviṭalām; kikaḷi veṭṭi viṭalām; avaṉuṭiya iṟicciyi aṟuttu vaṟukkalām; avikaḷik kuṟittuk kaṭavuḷ veṟuppaṭiya māṭṭār. kurumār kaṭavuḷukku etiril cāmpirāṇip puki iṭṭārkaḷ; kaṭavuḷari aḻakiya uṭikaḷālum aṇikalaṉkaḷālum alaṅkarittārkaḷ; kurumār avarkaḷmītu pala tutikaḷip pāṭiṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil palar oṉṟucērntu niṉṟu pāṭiṉar.
pāpilōṉiya camaya nūlkaḷum tutikaḷum cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kattōlikkak kiṟittuvak kōyilkaḷil ilattiṉ moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatupōla, cumēriya moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tutikaḷ pūtaṅkaḷi ōṭṭiṉa. ivi paḻiya mantira vitti toṭarpāka eḻuntavi. pūtaṅkaḷiliruntu tammip pātukāttuk koḷvataṟkut tāyattukaḷ, mantiraṅkaḷ eḻutiya ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ kaṭavuḷariṉ vaṭivaṅ kaḷ mutaliyaṉa uṭampil aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa; ciṟiya kaṟkaḷ kayiṟṟil allatu caṅkiliyil kōttuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟuppu, veḷḷi, civappu mutaliya kayiṟukaḷ nōkkattukku ēṟṟavāṟu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paricutta māṉa tikiras, yūpirātas āṟukaḷiṉ nīrit teḷippatālum pūtaṅkaḷ vilakiṉa. acur pāṉippāliṉ nūlniliyattil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ ēṭu kaḷil, cōtiṭam, kaṉaviṉ palaṉ, cakuṉaṅkaḷiṉ palaṉ pōṉṟavikaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paliyiṭappaṭṭa vilaṅkiṉ īralip pārttup pāpilōṉiyak kurumār varuṅ kāriyam kūṟiṉārkaḷ. iv vaḻakkam kirēkkak kurumārālum kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. nimittam pārppavaṉāvatu kōviṟ kuruvāvatu cakuṉam pārttup palaṉ kūṟuvataṉ muṉ aracaṉ pōrukkuc cellamāṭṭāṉ.
ilakkiyam
pāpilōṉiya vāṇikam caṭṭa campantamāṉa oḻuṅku peṟṟiruntatu. mempis, tīps nakaraṅkaḷiliruntatu pōlap pāpilōṉil eḻuttāḷar palar iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ īramāṉa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil nuṉi mukkōṇamāka veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻutukōlāl eḻutiṉārkaḷ. eḻutap paṭṭatu tirumukamāyiṉ atu kaḷimaṇ uṟiyil iṭṭu eḻutiyavaṉiṉ muttiri mēlē poṟikkappaṭṭatu. kōyilkaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nūlniliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ cāṭikaḷil aṭukki oḻuṅkupaṭuttit taṭṭukkaḷil niriyāka vikkap paṭṭaṉa. pōrcippā nūl niliyattiliruntu nūlkaḷ paṭiyeṭuttu acur pāṉippāliṉ nūl niliyattil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. acīriyāvil kāṇap paṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷiṉ mūlam pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāḻkkiyip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.
nīṇṭa kālam pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vācikkappaṭāmal iruntaṉa. heṉṟi iraviliṉcaṉ eṉṉum āṅkilar paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷāka muyaṉṟu 1847il av veḻuttukaḷi vācittār. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ cumēriya akkēṭiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ cemittiya muṟiyāṉa vaḷarcciyāl tōṉṟiyavi. toṭakkattil nūlkaḷ cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kālattil am moḻi māṟutalaṭintatu. paḻiya puṉita moḻiyākiya cumēriyattiliruntu nūlkaḷip pāpilōṉiya moḻippaṭuttuvataṟku akarātikaḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. niṉēvā nūl niliyattil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ perumpālum cumēriya, pāpilōṉiya acīriya moḻi akarātikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vakarātikaḷ akkāṭ aracaṉākiya cārkaṉ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭavi.
cumēriyattilum pāpilōṉiyattilum eḻuttukaḷ colliṉ pakuti kaḷāka iruntaṉa. pāpilōṉiyattukkuc contamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷ illi; colliṉ pakutikaḷi uṇartta 300 aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ variyil iruntaṉa. ivvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ mūlam kaṇakku, camayam, vāṇikam pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷ kōyiṟ kurumārāl māṇavarukkup payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aḻipāṭu oṉṟil paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavar pārttu eḻutiya nītivākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷip paṟṟiya kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ, camayak kiriyikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi eḻutappaṭṭa ēṭukaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. aracāṅka varalāṟukaḷ, aracariṉ camayappaṟṟu, avarkaḷiṉ veṟṟikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. perōcas (ki.mu.280) eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya varalāṟṟāciriyar paṭippuk kālam mutal maṉitaṉuṭiya varalāṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāpilōṉiṉ mutal aracaṉ kaṭavuḷāl teriyap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉ periya veḷḷapperukku variyil 36,000 āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉeṉṟum ulakam toṭaṅki 691,200 āṇṭukaḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
oḻukkam
kōyilkaḷil tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar. maṇaṅkaḷ peṟṟōrāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. maṇamakaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantikku vekumati aḷit tāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tanti peṇṇukku av vekumatiyilum pārkka atika peṟumatiyuḷḷa cītaṉam koṭuttāṉ. pāpilōṉiyaṉ oruvaṉ oru peṇṇiyē maṇantāṉ. viyapicārak kuṟṟattōṭu campantappaṭṭa iru pālārum āṟṟil eṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oruvaṉ peṇṇiṉ cītaṉap paṇattit tiruppik koṭuttut tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytukoḷḷalām. kaṇavaṉ koṭumiyāka naṭattukiṉṟāṉ, vicuvāca millātirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉap peṇ attāṭcip paṭuttiṉāl avaḷ kaṇavaṉik tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām.
peṇkaḷ āṭavarippōla veḷiyē ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṭam cottu iruntatu. avarkaḷ ataṉit tām virumpiyavāṟu ceyya urimi peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ cilar vāṇikam purintaṉar; cilar eḻuttāḷarāka iruntaṉar. uyar kuṭumpaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil taṉiyiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟapōtu aṇṇakarum vēliyāḷkaḷum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. tāḻnta vakuppiṉar aṭimikaḷ pōl vāḻntaṉar. ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiya makkaḷi atika nākarikam aṭiyātavarkaḷ eṉak karutiṉar.
kalikaḷ
pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāṇikam, poḻutupōkku, kaṭavuḷ patti mutaliya vikaḷi veḷiyiṭum ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. miṉukkañ ceyyap paṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ, veṇkalam, irumpu, veḷḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nuṭpamāṉa tiyal vēlikaḷ, mirutuvāṉa tuṇikaḷ, aḻakiya cāyam ūṭṭiya uṭikaḷ, kampaḷaṅkaḷ, mējikaḷ, paṭukkikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟavi pāpilōṉiyariṉ nākarikatti viḷakku kiṉṟaṉa. palavaki aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teyva uruvaṅkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. icik karuvikaḷ pala payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kuḻal, yāḻ, vīṇi, mēḷam, kompu, nāṇaṟkuḻal, ekkāḷam, kittāḷam, mattaḷam eṉpaṉa avikaḷuṭ cila. kōyilkaḷilum, araṇmaṉikaḷilum, celvariṉ viruntukaḷilum pāṭakar taṉittum, palar oṉṟu kūṭiyum pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil ōviyam eḻututal taṉik kaliyāka vaḷaravilli. cuvarkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi ekiptiya camātic cuvar kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟavipōla niṟaṅkaḷāl eḻutappaṭavilli. ciṟpak kaliyum naṉṟāka vaḷarcciyaṭiyavilli. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ muka irēkikaḷ perumpālum orē vakiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōriṟ piṭikkap paṭṭavarkaḷik kāṭṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷ orē vakiyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāpilōṉiyak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ tarikku mēlē cila aṭikaḷē uyarn tirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōyilkaḷiyō araṇmaṉikaḷiyō kāṭṭum ōviyaṅ kaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum kiṭikkavilli. vīṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. celvar ceṅkallip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vīṭukaḷukkuc caṉṉalkaḷ irukkavilli. vīṭukaḷiṉ katavukaḷ vītiyi nōkki irukkavilli. uḷ muṟṟatti nōkkiyiruntaṉa. mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku māṭikaḷuṭiya vīṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. kōyil vīṭukaḷippōlavē caturamāka viruntatu. kōyiliṉ mēlē ēṟic celvataṟku caṅkup puripōl paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ amikkappaṭṭa vaḻi veḷiyē iruntatu. itu ik kaṭavuḷiṉ uyaramāṉa vīṭākum. vāṉa ārāyccit toṭarpākavum itu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉ ucciyiliruntu kurumār viṇmīṉkaḷi nōkkiṉārkaḷ. pōrcippā vilirunta ivvakik kōpuram ēḻu maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ovvoru māṭiyum ovvoru kirakattukku uriyatu. ovvoru māṭiyum vevvēṟu niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. kīḻē uḷḷa māṭi caṉikku uriyatu. itu kaṟuppu niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṟku mēluḷḷatu veḷḷikkuriyatu. atu veḷḷiyā yiruntatu. mūṉṟāvatu māṭi viyāḻaṉuk kuriyatu. im māṭi karuñcivappu niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭatu. putaṉukkuriya nālāvatu māṭi nīla niṟamuṭiyatu. aintāvatu pacci kalanta civappu niṟamuṭiyatu. itu cevvāykkuriyatu. āṟāvatu māṭi veḷḷi niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. itu tiṅkaḷukkuriyatu. ēḻāvatu māṭi poṉṉiṟamāṉatu. itu ñāyiṟṟukkuriyatu. ivvāṟu ovvoru māṭiyum vārattiṉ ovvōr nāḷik kuṟippatā yiruntatu.
ceṅkaṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ veḷippuṟaṅkaḷ miṉukkañceytu niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avikaḷil vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, marañceṭikaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷippakkam aḻuttañ ceyyappaṭṭatu aḻakukkāka māttiramaṉṟu, veyyil maḻikaḷāl avi paḻutaṭiyāmal iruppataṟkumākum.
viññāṉam
