kaṭaliṟ celvataṟkup palavaki marakkalaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. marakkalaṅ kaḷiṉ muṉpuṟam, ciṅka mukam, yāṉi mukam, kutiri mukam pōṉṟu ceyyap paṭṭiruntatu. marakkalaṅkaḷukkup pāy maraṅkaḷum pāyum iruntaṉa.
aḷavikaḷ
eṇṇey, ney, tayir pōṉṟaviyum tāṉiyaṅkaḷum aḷantu viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavi niṟuttuviṟkappaṭṭaṉa. tāṉiya aḷavi; 5 ceviṭu 1 āḻākku; 2 āḻākku 1 uḻakku; 2 uḻakku 1 uri; 2 uri 1 nāḻi; 8 nāḻi 1 kuṟuṇi; 2 kuṟuṇi 1 patakku; 4 kuṟuṇi allatu 2 patakku 1 tūṇi; 3 tūṇi allatu 12 kuṟuṇi 1 kalam.
paḻiya paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟāl perumpālum poruḷkaḷ paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. 2 paṭi aricikku 5 paṭi nelluk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. 5 paṭi nelluk kuttuvataṟku arippaṭi arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel 1/16 nāḻi neyyukkum, oru nāḻi tayir 10p pākku, 20 vāḻi ili allatu 1/16 nāḻi neykkum paṇṭamāṟṟuṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. nellum tayirum orē peṟupati uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa veṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. nāḷ vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvataṟku nel payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. paṇappuḻakkam iruntapōtum potuvāka nellē paṇattippōlap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
poṉ veḷḷi niṟi: 1 kaḻañcu 20 mañcāṭi; 1 mañcāṭi 2 kuṟuṇi; 1 mā 1/10 mañcāṭi; 1 kāṇi 1/40 mañcāṭi.
1 “akkālattē niṟuttal, mukattal mutaliya aḷavukaṭkum, poṉ irattiṉaṅkaḷ niṟuttaṟkum, poṉ māṟṟu nōkkutaṟkum aracāṅkattā rāl niyamikkappaṭṭa marakkāl mutaliyaṉa viruntaṉa. aracaṉ muttiriyiṭṭa marakkālukku irācirācaṉ kālattil irācakēcari eṉpatu peyar. itaṟkuc camāṉamāṉa tañcāvūrk kōyil marakkālukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. āṉāl nāṭutōṟum marakkāl peyarkaḷ māṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa. aracāṅka muttiriyiṭṭa eṭikkallukkuk kuṭiñikkal eṉṟu peyar. tañcāvūrk kōyilil itaṟkuc cama eṭiyuḷḷa kallukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. (ip peyarkaḷ civapirāṉ peyarāliṭappaṭṭaṉa) poṉmāṟṟukku vittirunta āṇikkut taṇṭavāṇiyeṉpatu peyar.”
tamiḻar tirumaṇam
paruvam aṭinta āṭavarum makaḷirum oruvari oruvar kātalittuc ciṟitukālam piṟar aṟiyātu cērntu oḻukuvārkaḷ eṉṟum piṉ āṭavaṉ taṉatu kātaliyit taṉatu ūrukku yārumaṟiyātu koṇṭu ceṉṟu aṅku maṇantukoḷvāṉ; allatu maṇamakaḷatu peṟṟōriṉ icivip peṟṟu avaḷ illattilēyē avaḷi maṇantukoḷvāṉ eṉṟum caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ip pāṭalkaḷ paḻiya marapip piṉpaṟṟic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi akkālat tirumaṇam avvāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tuṇital muṭiyātu.
ak kālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ maṇañ ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntāl avaṉ cila mutiyōrip peṇkēṭkumpaṭi peṇvīṭṭukku aṉuppu vāṉ. peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr peṇkoṭukka icintaṉarāyiṉ, kaṇi eṉap paṭṭa cōtiṭaṉāl kuṟikkappaṭṭa nallanāḷilē maṇamakaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattā ruṭaṉ maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭukkuvantu peṇṇi maṇantu taṉatu vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭupōvāṉ. tirumaṇattukku muṉ peṇṇukku viliyāka maṇamakaṉ pariyam eṉap poruḷ vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. ivvāṟu koṭukkappaṭum poruḷ cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḷarppukkūli eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. peṇkaḷi vilikoṭuttu vāṅki maṇattalākiya vaḻakkattiṉ niḻalē pariyam eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. tirāviṭa makkaḷuḷ cila kūṭṭattiṉar peṇkaḷi vili koṭuttu vāṅki maṇantukoḷḷum vaḻakkam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil purōkitariṉ utaviyiṉṟiyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. ak kālat tirumaṇaccaṭaṅku evvāṟirunta teṉpatic caṅka nūlkaḷik koṇṭu aṟitalkūṭum. akanāṉūṟṟil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷati iṅku tarukiṉṟōm.
“cōṟu paruppiṭṭuc camikkappaṭṭatu. palarkūṭi uṇṭapiṉpum cōṟu kuvintukiṭantatu. niriyākak kālkaḷi naṭṭup pantariṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tariyilveṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭiruntatu. viḷakkukaḷ koḷutti vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pūraṇi nāḷiṉ viṭiyaṟkāliyil tīyakōḷkaḷ akaṉṟa nalla nēramāka viruntatu. taliyilē kuṭattiyum kiyilē maṭpāttirattiyum vittukkoṇṭu peṇkaḷ vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kiyil vittirunta pāttiratti oruvar kiyiliruntu oruvarkikku māṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu mutiya peṇkaḷ periya āravārañceytaṉar. piṉpu āṇmakavi yīṉṟavarkaḷum, vayiṟṟil aḻakutēmal uṭiyavarkaḷum aḻakiya nakikaḷ aṇintavarkaḷumākiya peṇkaḷ muṉṉē vantārkaḷ. maṇamakaḷiṉ taliyil nīri ūṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu nīril kiṭanta pūvum nellum kūntalil kiṭantu viḷaṅkiṉa. appoḻutu avarkaḷ “nī kaṟpilvaḻuvātu kaṇavaṉukku iṉiyaḷākaviruntu neṭuṅkālam vāḻvā yāka” eṉa vāḻttiṉāhkaḷ. aṉṟu mālikkālam vantatu. peṇkaḷ vantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. “nī vīṭṭukkup peruntaliviyākuka” eṉṟu vāḻttiṉārkaḷ. cuṟṟattavar avaḷ kiyip piṭittu maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ.” (akam. 86)
“nalla ariciyic camitta neykalanta cōṟṟic cuṟṟattavar mutiyavarkaḷukkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. naṟcakuṉam kāṭṭum paṟavikaḷ teḷinta vāṉattē paṟantaṉa. tiṅkaḷ urōkiṇiyōṭu cērukiṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta nalla nēram vantatu. perukkic cuttañceyta vīṭu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. ellōrum kaṭavuḷit toḻutārkaḷ. periya mēḷamum maṇamuracum olittatu. maṇaviṉiyik kāṇa virumpiya peṇkaḷ virintuvantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. aṟukaṅkiḻaṅki veḷḷiya nūlāṟkaṭṭi vākip pūviṉ mēl vikkappaṭṭatum, maṇal parappappaṭṭatumākiya pantaliṉkīḻ tamar avaḷi maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ. maṇaviṉiyik kaṇṭa peṇkaḷ tamatu vīṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.” (akam. 136)
alaṅkāram
tamiḻ makkaḷ tamatu uṭampiyum tām payaṉpaṭuttiya ovvoru poruḷiyum alaṅ karittārkaḷ. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ muṟṟaṅkaḷil aḻakiya kōlaṅkaḷiṭṭaṉar. vīṭu kaḷit tāṅkum tūṇkaḷ, ki maraṅkaḷ, nilikaḷ, katavukaḷ, vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟaṅkaḷ tāṉum aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷil cuvarkaḷilum matilkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷilum aḻakiya uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruttali nām kāṇalām. intiyac ciṟpikaḷ kaṟkaḷi marattippōlap payaṉpaṭutti atil pala kaṇkavarum vēlippāṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. vīṭṭil payaṉ paṭuttum kaṭṭil, mukkāli, nāṟkāli, maṇi, āyutaṅkaḷiṉpiṭi, nīr aruntum pāttiraṅkaḷum aḻakiya vēlipāṭukaḷuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷilum, amarāvati, acantā, ellōrā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ alaṅkāra viruppattit terivikkiṉṟaṉa.1
peṇkaḷ tamatu kūntalip palavakiyāka vāri muṭintārkaḷ. kūntalmītu palavakip pūkkaḷic ceruki vēyntārkaḷ. cuṟāmīṉ, valampuriccaṅku, piṟi, irupakkamum yāṉi kuṭattiliruntu nīr koṭṭu vatum naṭuvē ilakkumi vīṟṟiruppatumākiya vaṭivuṭiya cītēviyār pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintārkaḷ. koṇṭiyil palavakip pūk kaḷiyum māliyiyum cūṭiṉārkaḷ; cantaṉam kuṅkumam mutaliyavi kaḷip pūci uṭali aḻakupaṭuttiṉārkaḷ; uṭampil mañcaḷpūcik kuḷit tārkaḷ. mārpu, tōḷkaḷil toyyil eṉṉum oruvaki kuḻampip pūci ataṉmēl karumpu pūṅkoṭi mutaliyavikaḷi eḻuti aḻaku ceytārkaḷ. kātil tōṭu allatu kuḻi aṇintārkaḷ. vaṟiya makkaḷ kātil ōliyic curuḷākkic cerukiṉārkaḷ. muttumāli, pavaḷamāli, caṅkili, kuṟaṅkuceṟi, kavāṉaṇi, paṭṭiki, cataṅki, kiṇkiṇi, cilampu, kaḻal, vaḷi, mōtiram, mēkali eṉpaṉa avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṟ cila. tiruppātam, tirumuṭi, puḷḷit toṅkal, koṟṟakkuṭi, poṟpū, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, kāṟi, aṭikkāṟi, aṉṉam, kiḷi, cōṉakacciṭukku, makuṭam, vāḷi, vaṭukavāḷi, cūṭakam, kaṇmalar, cuṭṭi, tāli, tālimaṇivaṭam, kaṇṭa ñāṇ, poṭṭu, patakkam, ēkāvali, kālvaṭam, kamalam, ceṭi, vākuvalayam, tuṭar eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa.
ici
2ovvoru nilattilum ovvoru ici vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kuṟiñci nilamakkaḷ kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. mullinila makkaḷ mullip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. atu iṉṟu mattiyamāvati eṉappaṭum. marutanila makkaḷ marutap paṇpāṭiṉāhkaḷ. atu kētāram eṉappaṭum. neytalukkuriya paṇ iṉṟu puṉṉākavarāḷi eṉappaṭum. palavaki mēḷaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. vevvēṟu ōcikaḷip piṟappikkum paṟikaḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅ kaḷil koṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. māṭukaḷik koḷḷiyiṭum pōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu ēṟukōṭpaṟi, veṟiyāṭumpōtu aṟiyappaṭu vatu murukiyam. maṇak kālattil cāṟṟappaṭuvatu maṇamuḻavu. tēriḻukkum pōtu muḻakkappaṭuvatu tērōṭṭuppaṟi. nellaṟukkum pōtu taṭṭappaṭuvatu nellarikkiṇi. aracaṉ veḷikkiḷampumpōtu olikkappaṭuvatu puṟappāṭṭuppaṟi. mīṉ piṭittaṟkuc cellumpōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu mīṉ kōṭpaṟi. cūṟi āṭumpōtu aṭikkappaṭuvatu cūṟikōṭṭi.
pōrkkālattil yāṉi, kutiri, kālāṭpaṭi kaḷukku uṟcākam mūṭṭum poruṭṭup palavaki vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi iyam, icik karuvi, vāttiyam, vācciyam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avi tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, narampukkaruvi, miṭaṟṟuk karuvi, tuḷikkaruvi eṉa nālvakippaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻa rukkuṭiya ēḻicikaḷ kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, kural, uḷi, viḷari, tāram. mūṅkil, koṉṟippaḻat tiṉ kōtu mutaliyavikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa kuḻal kaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ekkāḷa vakikaḷuḷ tāri, kāḷam, kākāḷam, ammiyam, ciṉṉam mutaliyaviyum, kompuvakiyil kōṭu, irali, vayir mutaliyavum iruntaṉa. narampuk karuviyil ciṟappuṭiyatu yāḻ 1avikaḷukku ēḻu, irupattoṉṟu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa narampukaḷ iruntaṉa.
paṟi, muracu, pēriki, ākuḷi, ellari, calliki, kiṇi mutaliyaṉa mēḷavakikaḷuṭcila. iṉṉum ilaṅkittīvil vaḻaṅkum mēḷavaki yil eḻupatukku mēluṇṭu. tāri uṭukki mutaliyaṉa pāṇar kiyil koṇṭutirintu aṭittuppāṭum ciṟiyamēḷaṅkaḷ.
tōṟkaruvikaḷ
“pērikki paṭaka miṭakki yuṭukki
cīrmiku mattaḷañ calliki karaṭiki
timili kuṭamuḻāt takki, kaṇappaṟi
tamarukan taṇṇumi tāvi ṟaṭāri
yantari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷiya
monti muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
nicāḷan tuṭumi ciṟupaṟi yaṭakka
māci ṟakuṇiccam viralēṟu pākan
tokka vupāṅkan tuṭiperum paṟiyeṉa
mikka nūlōr viritturit taṉarē”
uṭi
parutti intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭākum ceṭi, maṟṟiya nāṭṭumakkaḷ āṭi illāmal iruntapōtu allatu tōli uṭiyākak koṇ ṭirunta kālattil intiya makkaḷ pañci nūlākkavum, nūlāl āṭi neyyavum āṭikkup palavakic cāyaṅkaḷ ūṭṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu paḻiya cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. intiya nāṭṭu veppanilikkēṟpa makkaḷ aṟpa uṭiyiyē aṇintaṉar. āṭavar muḻaṅkālvariyum uṭi uṭuttu ariyil kaccukkaṭṭiyirun taṉar. mikappaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ taliyiccuṟṟi nāṭākkaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. piṉpu nāṭā pāki āka māṟiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ ariyil māttiram uṭi aṇintaṉar. peṇkaḷiṟcilar mārpik kaccukkaṭṭi maṟittaṉar. perum pālum peṇkaḷ arikkumēl uṭi aṇiyavilli. itaṉi cāṉci, amarāvati, piṟkālac ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷi lum kāṇappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu nām naṉku aṟiyalām. iṟavukki aṇiyum vaḻakkam akkālattil araṇmaṉikaḷilirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl koṇṭuvarap paṭṭirukkalāmeṉac cilar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil kirēkkap peṇkaḷ intiya nāṭṭukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭamiyi srāpō (Strabo) pōṉṟa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.1
2 “parutti intiya nāṭṭukku uriya ceṭi. maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōlkaḷi uṭukkumpōtu allatu uṭiyiṉṟiyirukkumpōtu tamiḻ makkaḷ pañciliruntu nūlnūṟṟu nūlāl aḻakiya āṭikaḷic ceytu uṭuttārkaḷ.3 taṟkālak kuṭiyiruppukaḷil nūlnūṟkum katirkaḷ kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. mokañ catarō kālattilēyē makkaḷ pañciliruntu āṭi neyyavum, ataṟkuc cāyamūṭṭavum, aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyavum aṟintiruntār kaḷeṉpatu aṅkukiṭitta maṇpāvikaḷik koṇṭu naṉku aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ āṭi neyya aṟi vataṉmuṉ marappaṭṭikaḷi yum taḻikaḷiyum uṭuttār kaḷ. pañcu uṭikkumuṉ maravuri tōṉṟiya tātaliṉ atu tuṟavikaḷukkum tūya uṭi yākap piṟkāla makkaḷāl karutappaṭṭatu. āṭiyik kuṟikka aimpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru peyarum akkālattil vaḻaṅkiya vev vēṟu vaki āṭiyik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pālāvipōṉṟa āṭi, iḻi ceṉṟa iṭam aṟiyamuṭi yātu nuṇṇiya nūlāl neyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mulli nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ āṭṭu mayiri liruntu kampaḷi ney tārkaḷ. āṭikaḷ veḷuttuk kañciyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṭavar veḷuttuk kañciyiṭṭa maṭiyik kulittu ariyiṟkaṭṭi ataṉ mēl oru āṭiyi varintu kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭavar caṭṭi tarikkavilli caṭṭi tarittal ūḻiyam purivōrukkuriyatāka viruntatu. iṟavukki aṇital iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ intiya aracariṉ meykāppāḷarā yirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl pukuttappaṭṭatākalām. paḻiya ōviyaṅ kaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum iṟavukki kāṇappaṭavilli. peṇkaḷ tamatu taṉaṅkaḷi aciyavoṭṭāmal kaccukkaṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. āṭavar talippāki aṇintārkaḷ. uttariyam tarittārkaḷ. kōvaṇam kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ.1
payircceyki
tamiḻ makkaḷ payirc ceykiyiyē mutaṉmiyāṉa toḻilākak koṇṭaṉar. “uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvār” eṉa vaḷḷuvarum uḻaviṉ ciṟappi mutaṉmiyākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya kamparum ēreḻupatil vēḷāṇ ciṟappi naṉkeṭuttiyampiyuḷḷār. payiriṭum nilaṅkaḷ naṉcey, puṉcey eṉa iruvakippaṭum. nel viḷiyum vayal naṉcey eṉavum maṟṟiya tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷiyum nilam puṉcey eṉavum paṭṭaṉa. payiriṭappaṭāta nilam taricu eṉappaṭṭatu. vēḷ eṉpatu nilattik kuṟikkum peyar. nilattit tiruttip payiriṭuvōr vēḷ āḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āḷar eṉpataṟku āḷpavar eṉṟu poruḷ. veḷḷatti ēri kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vittu veḷḷatti āṇṭami yiṉ vēḷāḷar veḷḷāḷar eṉavum paṭuvar. nilaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vēḷāḷar vēḷ allatu vēḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kuṟunila maṉṉarākavum iruntaṉar. vēḷāḷar koṭiyāṟ pukaḻpeṟṟōr. itaṉāl vēḷāṇmi eṉṉum collukkuk koṭi, upakāram eṉṉum poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. “iruntōmpi ilvāḻvatellām viruntōmpi-vēḷāṇmi ceytal poruṭṭu” eṉṉum kuṟaḷil vēḷāṇmi upakāram eṉṉum poruḷ tarutal kāṇka. cēra cōḻa pāṇaṭiya aracar vēḷāṇ marapil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē yāvar.
cāti
tamiḻ nāṭṭil nilam aintu vakiyiṉatāka viruntateṉa muṉṉō riṭattiṟ kūṟiṉōm. ovvoru nilamakkaḷum vevvēṟu peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oru nilattil vāḻum makkaḷ iṉṉoru nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷukkut tāḻntavareṉṟō uyarntavareṉṟō koḷḷum koḷki ak kālattil uṇṭākavilli. celvam, vaṟumi, oḻukkam, oḻukkak kēṭu eṉpavi pōṉṟa taṉmikaḷāl uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. uyarkuṭi eṉpatu pāvam paḻikaḷukku añcum kuṭi eṉpatu poruḷ. vaḷḷuvar tamatu nūlil aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭum kuṭip piṟappu ivviyalpiṉatātali aṟiya māṭṭātār atu vaṭanāṭṭār varuṇattit taḻuviyateṉa maruṇṭu kūṟuvar. “piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā ceytoḻil vēṟṟumiyāl” eṉa vaḷḷuvar piṟappiṉāl cātiyuṇṭeṉpāri maṟuttami kāṇka.
makkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē tiruttamaṭintu payirc ceyki vaḷarcci yaṭintatu. perumpālum nakar maruta nilattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulakilē paḻiya nākarikac ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nakaraṅkaḷ ellām vēḷāṇmi ceḻittōṅkiya āṟṟōraṅkaḷilēyē viḷaṅkiṉa. vēḷāṇmiyāl ceḻippuṟṟa nāṭukaḷil vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum uṇaviṉ poruṭṭum paṟpala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vantu taṅkuvā rāyiṉar. tantiyiṉ toḻili makaṉ ceytal eṉpatupōlak kālvaḻi kāl vaḻiyāka ovvoru kuṭumpamum perukiyapōtu ovvoru kuṭumpa mum taṉittaṉic cātiyākap perukiyatu. nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum cātippeyarkaḷ toḻilkaḷiyē kuṟittal kāṇka. taṉittaṉik kūṭṭa mākap perukiya makkaḷ tattamakkuḷḷēyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷ ḷum vaḻakkam nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu varutalālum cātik kaṭṭappāṭu vali aṭintatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil antaṇar aracar vaṇikar veḷḷāḷar eṉṉum nāṉku perum piriviṉar, iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷallāta kittoḻilum ēvaṟṟoḻilum ceyyum makkaḷum iruntaṉar. muṉ kūṟiya nālvarum uyarntōreṉṟum, piṉ kūṟiya iruvakiyiṉarum tāḻntōreṉṟum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar.
antaṇa reṉpōr tuṟavikaḷ. kōyiṟ pūci ceyyum makkaḷiyum kuṟikka ippeyar piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. āṭci purivōr aracar eṉavum, vāṇikam purivōr vaṇikar eṉavum, vēḷāṇmi ceyvōr vēḷāḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. ippirivukaḷ perumpālum nakaraṅkaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
iccātip pirivukaḷ maruta nilattilēyē perumpālum tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. vaṭanāṭṭavar toṭakkattil niṟampaṟṟip pirittirunta varuṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻaratu toḻil paṟṟiya cātikaḷum vēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa. varuṇam eṉpataṟku niṟam eṉpatu poruḷ. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ varuṇat tukkup piṟappē kāraṇam. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ pirāmaṇa, cattiriya, viciya, cūttira eṉṉum nāṟ pirivukaḷē teṉṉāṭṭu antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum pirivukaḷ eṉap piṟkālattavar mayaṅkiṉamiyāl pala taṭumāṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
antaṇar, pārppār, pirāmaṇar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ paṟṟiya mayakkam
antaṇar eṉpōr tuṟavikaḷāvar eṉpatu caṅka nūlkaḷālum, tirukkuṟaḷālum piṟacāṉṟukaḷālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kōyiṟ pūciceyvōr kōyilkaḷi mēṟpārttal paṟṟip pārppār eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. toṭakkattil iṉṉavartāṉ pārppā rāyirukkalām eṉṉum niyati irukkavilli. kōyilkaḷi mēṟ pārkkum kuṭumpattiṉar peruki oru pirivākap pirintaṉar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuṭ paṇi ceytamipaṟṟi upacāra vaḻakkil antaṇar eṉpaṭuvārāyiṉar. pārppār tuṟavikaḷallar. pirāmaṇar tamiḻ nāṭṭavarallar. ivarkaḷ vaṭa nāṭukaḷiliruntu teṉṉāṭukaḷilvantu kuṭiyēṟiṉōr. tillimūvā yiravar, 48,000var, 8,000var, 3,700var eṉa ikkūṭṭattiṉar paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pārppaṉak kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu kalantuḷ ḷārkaḷ mattiya kālat tamiḻnāṭṭu aracar vaṭamoḻikku matippuk koṭuttup pirāmaṇari ātarittamiyāl pārppaṉa vakuppiṉarum tammip pirāmaṇar eṉak kūṟuvarāyiṉar. ituvē antaṇar pārppār pirāmaṇar eṉpōriṉ varalāṟākum.
vāṇikam
“iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ intiyā tūrakkiḻakku, tūramēṟku nāṭukaḷōṭu mutaṉmiyāṉa kaṭal vāṇikam naṭattiṉatu eṉṟukāṭṭuvataṟkuḷḷa nalla cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kampōtiyā, yāvā, cumattirā, pōrṇiyō, yappāṉ, teṉcīṉā, malāyā, arāpiyā, pāracīkat tiluḷḷa mutaṉmiyāṉa paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ, āpirikkāviṉ kiḻakkukkari eṉpavikaḷil intiyar vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ innāṭukaḷōṭu māttiramaṉṟu. akkālattil aṟiyappaṭṭirunta ulakam muḻuvatōṭum, urōma irācciyaṅkaḷōṭum vāṇikam naṭatti ṉārkaḷ”1
“iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷōṭu oḻuṅkupaṭṭa muṟiyil vāṇikam naṭattiṉārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.”2 cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ vāṇikattukkum kittoḻilukkum mattiya iṭaṅkaḷākaviruntaṉa. uḷnāṭṭuppōkkuvarattukku iraṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷuḷḷa vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. avvaṇṭikaḷ iṉṟu cintuvil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṇṭikaḷip pōṉṟavi. iṉṟu cintu āṟṟil ōṭu kiṉṟa ōṭaṅkaḷum akkāla ōṭaṅkaḷi ottaṉavē. kaṭaṟ kariyi aṭutta nāṭukaḷiliruntu uṇavup poruḷkaḷip peṟum muṟiyil māttiram vāṇikam naṭipeṟavilli. ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu ulōkap poruḷkaḷum piṟavum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. paluccistāṉattilum irācaputtāṉattilumiruntu ulōkaṅkaḷum, teṉṉintiyāviliruntu caṅkum, ullāca vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷi liruntum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. pāracīkattilum āpkāṉistāṉattilum iruntu niṟakkaṟkaḷum, cīṉāvilum parmāvilum iruntu nīlakkaṟkaḷum peṟap paṭṭaṉa. muttiri veṭṭum kaṟkaḷ, cuṟṟivarakkumiḻi vitta pāṉikaḷ, pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyum kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa intiyap poruḷkaḷ ki.mu. 3500 variyil pāpilōṉukkuc ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.1
“ekiptukkum intiyāvukkum iṭiyil muṟkālattil pōkkuvarattu naṭipeṟṟateṉat terikiṟatu. alakcāntiriyāvil cantitta paṭitta intiyarkaḷ mūlam tāṉ pala ceytikaḷik kēṭṭu aṟintukoṇṭatākat tālami kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. intiyāviliruntu camayayāttirikarkaḷ ciriyāvi luḷḷa herāpolicukkuc (Hierapolis) ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu lūciyaṉ (Lucian) kūṟiyuḷḷār. kiṟittu piṟappataṟkumuṉ intiyar apicīṉiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purintārkaḷ eṉpatu kiṟittuva vētaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiyar arāpiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil avarkaḷ cīṉak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅki ṉārkaḷ. ki.mu. 350 il varalāṟu eḻutiya parōcas (Berossus) pāpilōṉil aṉṉiyar kūṭṭamākak kuṭiyēṟiyiruntaṉar eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ki.mu. 63 il piṟanta srāpō (Strabo) eṉpār urōma nāṭṭut taṅkam intiyāvukku atikam celvatippaṟṟik kavalippaṭṭār. piḷiṉi kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: intiyāvukkup payaṇañ ceypavarkaḷ ocelis (Ocelis) eṉṉum tuṟimukattil iṟaṅkuvatu nallatu. kippālus eṉṉum paruvakkāṟṟu vīcumāṉāl nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil muciṟit tuṟimukatti aṭiyalām. itaṉ cūḻalkaḷil kaṭaṟ koḷḷikkārar irukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ātalāl atu iṟaṅkuvataṟkut takunta iṭamaṉṟu. nāṉ iti eḻutukiṉṟa kālattil aṅku āḷkiṉṟa aracaṉiṉ peyar celiputturus. vēṟu oru vāyppāṉa tuṟimukam nelciṇṭi pakkāri (Nelcyndi Bacare) it tuṟimukam tolivilē uḷḷa maturiyiliruntu āṭcipuriyum pāṇṭiyaṉukkuriyatu. puḷuṟṟā eṉpavar “ākastaciṉ parivāraṅkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa putta caṉṉiyāci. avar nāṭṭukkuḷḷa vaḻakkappaṭi neruppil viḻuntu iṟantupōṉār. avaruṭiya camātik kaṭṭiṭam iṅku kiṭakkiṟatu” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
“kaṭal vāṇikam peritum tirāviṭar vacam iruntatu. atil āriyar ciṟupaṅku paṟṟiṉārkaḷ. arāpiyā, āpirikkā, cīṉak karikaḷil kuṭiyēṟiya intiyariṟ perumpālōr tirāviṭarātal vēṇṭum. vaṭanāṭṭu vāṇikat tiviṭat teṉṉāṭṭu vāṇikam mutaṉmiyuṭiya teṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa viliyuyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vantaṉa eṉṟum vaṭanāṭu tōl, kampaḷi eṉpavikaḷi aḷittaṉaveṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, caṅku mutaliyaṉa teṟkiliruntu vantaṉaveṉṟum kuṭaliyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tāmpiraparaṇi āṟu, ilaṅki, pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉpaṉa avarāl kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
“intiyāviliruntē nalla yāṉikaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷukkum koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. tantam, kuraṅku, mayil mutaliyavi intiya nāṭṭukkuriyavi. ivikaḷik kuṟikka epirēya (Hebrew) moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ epirēyam alla! tirāviṭam. intiyāviliruntu oru mayil ekiptukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. atu yūpitar kaṭavuḷukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉukku mayil aṉuppappaṭṭatippaṟṟip pavēru cātakam kūṟukiṉṟatu. innūl ki.mu. 400 variyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. kirēkka moḻiyilvaḻaṅkum oricā eṉpatu arici eṉṉum tirāviṭattiṉa tiripu. kirāmpum cātikkāyum malāya tīvukaḷil muṟkālattil kiṭippaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avi ki.pi. 180il alakcāntiriyāvukkuk koṇṭupōkap paṭṭaṉa. ākavē ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil intiya malāya vāṇikam naṭipeṟṟirukkalām. apicīṉiyāviṉ talinakarākiya akcamil (Auxam) naṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oru kallil eḻuttup poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkal ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil nāṭṭappaṭṭatu. avveḻuttukkaḷ intiyāvik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptil talinakarākiya mempis aḻipāṭukaḷil intiya āṭavar makaḷirik kāṭṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, aṅku intiyar kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum piḷiṇṭērs peṟṟi (Flinders Petrie) eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. puḷuṟṟā kālattil vāḻntavarākiya ṭiyō kiṟistōtam (Dio Chrysostom) alakcāntiriyāvil naṭamāṭiya pala nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē intiyarit tāṉ pārttatākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.”1
“kōṭikālac cūriya ayaṉa kālattil āṇṭutōṟum nūṟṟi yirupatu marakkalakaṅkaṉ mōyis harmus (Mois Harmos) eṉṉum ceṅ kaṭaṟ kariyiluḷḷa ekiptiya tuṟimukattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭaṉa. paruvak kāṟṟiṉ utaviyip peṟṟu avi nāṟpatu nāḷkaḷil intiyat tuṟi mukaṅkaḷi allatu ilaṅkit tīvi aṭintaṉa. marakkalaṅkaḷ vili uyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tirumpi vantaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ ceṅkaṭaṟkariyi liruntu nīla natikku oṭṭakaṅkaḷilēṟṟik koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. avi nīla āṟṟu vaḻiyāka alak cāntiriyāvi aṭintu piṉ urōma nāṭṭut talinakaric cērntaṉa.” ivvāṟu urōmaṉ varalāṟṟālariyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. iñci, miḷaku, kaṟuvā pōṉṟavikaḷukku mēṟku nāṭukaḷil mikat tēviyiruntatu. vayiram, muttu, paṭṭu eṉpa vaṟṟiyum avarkaḷ evvaḷavu poruḷ koṭuttum vāṅka āyattamāka viruntārkaḷ. kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu ceṉṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukku nūṟu maṭaṅku ilāpattukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl kavarappaṭṭu mikap palar kiḻakkuk kāṭukaḷōṭu vāṇikattil īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi cēra nāṭum pāṇṭiya nāṭum aḷittaṉa. kumari, koṟki, kāyal pāmpaṉ eṉṉum tuṟimukaṅkaḷ atika varuvāyaḷittaṉa. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ ārampa kālak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷiṉ vāṇikam urōmar vacam iruntatu. vayiraṅkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyil muttu vili ēṟap peṟṟatāyiruntatu. urōmap peṇkaḷ paṭṭu āṭikaḷi uṭukka virum piṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉ eṭikkup paṭṭi niṟuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ irattiṉaṅkaḷ iḻittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvi ṉiṉṟum urōmukku aṉuppap paṭṭaṉa. kōyamuttūrp pakutiyiluḷḷa pāṭiyūril kiṭikkum kōmētakatti urōma vaṇikar atika vili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. intiyap poruḷkaḷ urōmil nūṟu maṭaṅku vilikku viṟkappaṭṭateṉṟum āṇṭil eṭṭu ilaṭcam taṅka nāṇa yatti intiyā vāṇika mūlam iḻuttu viṭukiṉṟateṉṟum avvāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
camayakkoḷkikaḷ
āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ ulakam, uyir, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷi ārāyum uyarnta camayakkoḷki uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. mokañcatarō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟkiṭitta muttirik kaṟkaḷil palavaki vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṭiyē ālamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkattil vīṟṟirukkum kōlamuṭiya vaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaṭivam, kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷukkut talivaṉ (pacupati) eṉṉum karutti viḷakkukiṉṟateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. akkālattilēyē makkaḷ yōkattiṉ taṉmikaḷiyum ataṉāl peṟum āṟṟalkaḷiyum naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ. paḻiya tamiḻariṉ camayakkaruttukkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē putta ciṉa matakkoḷkikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. puttar aracamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkat tiruntār. ciṉak kaṭavuḷ acōkamara niḻalil iruppavar eṉṉum koḷkikaḷ mokañcatarō makkaḷ koṇṭirunta karuttip piṉ paṟṟiyavē. kaṭavuḷ yōkattiliruppatākak kāṭṭum vaṭivam kaṭavuḷāy varutaṟkum yōkappayiṟci avaciyam eṉpati akkāla makkaḷ koṇ ṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpati uṇarttutal kūṭum. putta, ciṉa mataṅkaḷ paḻiya tirāviṭa matakkoḷkikaḷit taḻuvi eḻuntaṉa veṉpati varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
paḻiyakālattil camayakkaruttukkaḷ ēṭṭil eḻuti vikkappaṭātu āciriya māṇākkamuṟiyil collik koṭukkappaṭṭuvantaṉa. ivvāṟu talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vanta camaya uṇmikaḷ maṟikaḷ eṉap paṭṭaṉa. upaniṭataṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭa camayanūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭum karuttukkaḷ tirāviṭariṉ koḷkikaḷē. akkālattil pirāmaṇar aracavakuppiṉariṉ pātaṅkaḷiliruntu upaniṭata uṇmikaḷip payiṉṟārkaḷ. upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇarāl aṟiyappaṭātavi. ikkaruttukkaḷē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayakkaruttukaḷākum. ak koḷkikaḷ ippoḻutu civa cittāntak koḷkikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. civacittāntak koḷkikaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉ toṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkivarum uyarnta karuttukkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar pāp kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻaruṭiya matam ellāmakkaḷukkum potuvāṉatu. ituvē vaḷḷuvaratum, tolkāppiyaratum matamākum. tirukkuṟaḷil viṉip payaṉ, maṟupiṟappu, uyiriṉ aḻiviṉmi, pāva puṇṇiyam, iṟivaṉ, iruviṉikaḷiyum paṟṟāmal iṟivaṉ niṟkum taṉmi, tavam, tavatti ṉāl peṟum āṟṟal pōṉṟavi kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mantira vitatikaḷi ṉālō kaṭavuḷukkuk kāṇikkikaḷ koṭuppatiṉālō uyirkaḷ uyar nili aṭiya māṭṭā veṉpatē aṟivuṭiyōr matamākum.
toṭakkattil camayam accam, utaviyaṟṟa taṉmi pōṉṟa ētuk kaḷāl tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu; piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟitākap paṇpāṭaṭintu uyar nili aṭintatu. ikkoḷkikaḷi muḷḷuḷḷa tāḻi marattil pūtta naṟuma™muḷḷa pūvukku oppiṭalām. iṉṟiya makkaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭu pūvip pōṟṟātu tāḻiyiṉ muḷḷuḷḷa ilikaḷip pōṟṟutal pōṉ ṟiruppatē.
uṇavu
makkaḷ tām vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa vevvēṟu vaki uṇa viṉi uṇṭuvantārkaḷ. tāvaram ūṉ eṉṉum iruvaki uṇavukaḷum perumpālum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ tāvaravakiyē uṇaviṉ perum pakutiyākavum ūṉ kaṟivakipōṉṟu ciṟitaḷavākavum iruntaṉa. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl vēḷāṉ vakuppiṉar pārppaṉar eṉṉum iruvakuppiṉarallāta maṟṟiyōrellām ūṉ uṇavu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. ūṉ uṇavu koḷḷutal ciṟantataṉṟu eṉṉum koḷki nilaviyiruntateṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷāl naṉku terikiṉṟatu.
kaṟivakikaḷukku miḷaku payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. miḷakukkuk kaṟi eṉpatu maṟṟoru peyar. muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ miḷakāyippaṟṟi aṟiyār. airōppiyar mūlam miḷakāy intiyāvi aṭintatu. miḷakāyc ceṭi teṉṉamerikkāvukkuriyatu. itaṉkāy miḷakupōl kāramāyiruttal paṟṟi miḷakāy (miḷaku+kāy) eṉappaṭṭatu. airōppiyar intiyāviṉiṉṟum miḷaki vāṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ. kirēkkarum urōmarum itaṉi atikam virumpiṉamiyāl itaṟku ‘yavaṉappiriyā’ eṉṉum iṉṉoru peyarum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. yavaṉar eṉpatu kirēkkariyum urōmariyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyar. miḷakik kuṟikkak kirēkkar pippili eṉṉum peyari vaḻaṅkiṉar. pippili eṉpatu tippili eṉpataṉ tiripu. pippili eṉṉum kirēkka colliṉ tiripē peppar eṉṉum āṅkilaccol. muṟkālattil miḷaku airōppiya makkaḷiṉ cokucāṉa uṇavup poruḷāka viruntatu. mattiya kālattil atu avarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poru ḷāka viruntatu. mutalil veṉīciya vaṇikarum, piṉpu pōrttukkēyarum, ollāntarum miḷaki mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil ollāntar miḷakiṉ viliyi irumaṭi ākkiṉamiyāl āṅkilarāl 1599il ‘kiḻakkintiya vāṇikak kampaṉi’ toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl intiyā vil āṅkilar āṭci tōṉṟi vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu.
nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷil cōṟu, ciṟṟuṇṭi vakikaḷik kuṟikkap paṟpala peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷ ellām vevvēṟu vakiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa uṇavuvakikaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. “aṟucuvi uṇṭi yamarntu illāḷūṭṭa” eṉṉum nālaṭiyār aṭiyiṉāl celvar aṟu cuviyuḷḷa uṇavukaḷi uṇṭārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.
kaṟivakikaḷukkut tāḷitam ceyyappaṭṭatu. tēṉ, kāy, piñcu, paḻam, kiḻaṅku, taḷir, ili mutaliyaṉa uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil matuvakikaḷik kuṟikka aṟupatu peyarkaḷ variyil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl akkālamakkaḷ matuvi atikam uṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. nel, tēṉ, paḻavakikaḷ, pūvakikaḷiliruntu matuvakikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avi cāṭikaḷil ūṟṟik kāramēṟumpaṭi nilattil putittu vikkappaṭṭaṉa. “tēṭkaṭuppaṉṉa nāṭpaṭutēṟal” eṉak kaḷḷiṉ kāram kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akkālak kaḷ vakikaḷ ikkālac cārāya vaki pōṉṟu vāliyiliṭṭu vaṭikkappaṭṭaviyalla; ikkālapīr (beer) eṉṉum matuppōṉṟavi.
ayalnāṭṭup payaṇakkārar intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa cila kuṟippukaḷ:
1iṟciṅ eṉṉum cīṉayāttirikaṉ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: viruntu koṭuppōr puttakurumāri vaṇaṅki avarkaḷi vīṭṭukku aḻik kiṟārkaḷ. viruntil perumpālum ceppup pāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi cāmpaliṭṭu miṉukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kuruvum ovvoru maṇiyil iruppār. oruvarōṭu oruvar muṭṭātapaṭi maṇikaḷ eṭṭa iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ orumuṟikkumēl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatilli. orumuṟi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuḻiyil vīcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭpāttiraṅkaḷi orupōtuma vittiruttal kūṭātu. picci iṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ivvāṟu vīcappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuvintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. cīṉātēcattil ceyyappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa uyarnta maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭapiṉ cuttañceytu vikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. viruntiṉar cāppiṭum iṭam cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭiruk kum. vāyilil periya pāṉikaḷil taṇṇīr vikkappaṭṭirukkum. kuru mār vantatum avarkaḷ tamatu mēl aṅkiyiṉ kaṭṭukaḷi aviḻp pārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamatu kikaḷip payaṟu allatu maṇṇittēyttuk kaḻuvuvārkaḷ. kurumār tamatu kuṇṭikiyiluḷḷa nīrip payaṉpaṭuttuvar; allatu viruntukku aḻittavar taṇṇīri ūṟṟa ataṉip payaṉ paṭuttuvar. piṉpu avarkaḷ maṇikaḷil amarvar. avarkaḷukku muṉṉāl uṇṇum taṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭum. uṇpataṉmuṉ kaṭavuḷit tutippatu vaḻakkamaṉṟu. viruntukku aḻittavar uṇavip parimāṟuvār. parimāṟumpōtu peruviraṟ parumiyuḷḷa iñcittuṇṭum orukaraṇṭi uppum koṭukkappaṭum. uppuk koṭuppavaṉ talimik kuruviṉmuṉ kikaṭṭi muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvāṉ; appoḻutu avar ellārukkum uṇavic carivarap parimāṟu eṉṟu colvār. ellōruk kum uṇavu paṭittapiṉ uṇṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu kāttirukka vēṇṭiya tilli.
mārkkōpōlō kūṟiyiruppatu: malapāril ellā āṭavarum makaḷirum nāḷil irumuṟi kuḷikkiṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu ceyyātavar tāḻntavar eṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ valatu kiyāl uṇkiṉṟaṉar. ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyāvatu avarkaḷ uṇavi iṭakkiyāl toṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ iṭakkiyi acuttamāṉa tēvikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoruvaṉum nīr parukuvataṟkut taṉimiyāṉa ēṉam vittirukkiṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉattiliruntu iṉṉoruvaṉ nīr uṇṇamāṭṭāṉ. nīr uṇṇumpōtu avarkaḷ ēṉatti vāyil muṭṭaviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ; vāykkuc ciṟitu tūrattil piṭip pārkaḷ. evaṉāvatu ēṉatti vāyil vittalkūṭātu; nīr kuṭippataṟku ataṉi aṉṉiyaṉiṭam koṭukkavum kūṭātu, aṉṉiyaṉiṭam ēṉam illāviṭiṉ avarkaḷ nīri avaṉ kiyil ūṟṟuvārkaḷ. avaṉ kiyi ēṉamākak payaṉpaṭuttuvāṉ.

1. We have a continuity of culture from Paleolithic to neolithic, from neolithic to megalithic and megalithic to iron age in South India-Origin and Spread of the Tamils-V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar. M.A.
1. I ask what is the objection to state that a branch of Dravidians from south went to North and North-West and settled there? Diffusion of Dravidians in Rajaputana and Central India in prehistoric times is seen from the dialects Villi and Santal prevalent today, bearing close affinity to Dravidian languages. Add to this, the Mohenjo daro script which is very probably Dravidian.-Ibid pp. 10, 11.
2. If we take into consideration all these circumstances and examine them critically one has to assume that the authors of these early cultures in the East Mediterranean were emigrants from South India speaking Dravidian dialects. The language migrated and with it the peoples who spoke that language. So my humble thesis is that civilization of the future was borne not on the shores of the Mediterranean but on the coasts of the Indian peninsula and on the banks of its mihghty rivers the Kaveri, Thambraparni, the Periyar and Amarovathi, not to speak of the Krishna, Godavari and Narbada-Ibid p.27.
1. The word Dravida is the name for the speakers of a group of South Indian languages - Tamil, Malayalam, Kanarese and Telugu. No stretch of imagination is required to believe that of them Tamil is the oldest dialect and in my opinion the parental dialect. Though a clim has been recently made for the ancientness of the Kannada tongue, still it is safe to assert that Malayalam, Kanarese and Telugu became cultivated languages only a thousand years ago, when the influence of Sanskrit language had reached its high water mark in the Peninsula. So the term Dravidian we can definitely say, originally stood for the Tamil language and its descendants. - Ibid p. 14.
1. kaḷvar eṉṉum peyar valiyi uṇarttum; kaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu - kaḷiṟu, (pōrk)kaḷam mutaliya coṟkaḷ kaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantavi. matuvik kuṟikkum kaḷ eṉpataṟku valiyit taruvatu eṉpatu poruḷ. pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār.
1. ....whose growth in South India is evidenced by the presences of the artifacts of the paleolithic, neolithic and early iron ages throughout the country, and is traceable in the earliest strata of Tamil language and literature, show us that the growth of civilization in the Tamil land is entirely due to geographical cuses operating in situ and not to historical events such as incursions of foreigners-Environments and Culture-P.T.S. Aiyengar.
1. The very name ur spells like a pukka Tamil name-Ibid.
1 Elsewhere I have suggested that the Kirata tribes of South India might have been the people who were responsible for the Cretan civilization and Crete derived its name probably after this tribe - Ibid p.49.
The term Elam in Mesopotamia is suggestive and shows that to be a name given possibly by the emigrants from Ceylon who settled there and were responsible for the growth of the Town. For is not Ceylon known as Ilam (īḻam) in literature and epigraphy? The people of Elam were non Semitic and closly related to Sumerian culture. Their language was agglutinative but did not belong to the group of Alarodian like the Sumer tongue. In the same way Caria adjoining Lycia was very probobly Cera after a settlement from Kerala-Ibid p. 39.
1. We therefore conclude that the so called Mediterranean race had its origin in peninsular India, which was a part of the original Dravidian home which was in the submerged continent and connected south India with Africa, when the Indo Gangetic basin had not probably been formed. So the Dravidian element is not to be found in Indian culture alone but is largely traceable in Cretan, Aegean, Sumerian, Baby-lonian, Egyptian, Polynesian and other cultures of ancient world-Ibid p. 29
2. yāḻnūl-pa. 26, 49, vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ.
1. The linguistic affinity between Somali language and Tamil is remarkable. A distinct contribution of the ancient Dravidian to world culture is the Dravidian tongue. The group of agglutinative dialects with a few exceptions look to the ancient Tamil language primarily as their parent. Can we say that the service of Dravida was to give the tongue to the tongueless? -Origin and spread of the Tamils P. 39
1. Dialects of the same family of languages were spoken throughout India, except in the Vindyan regions, in the Neolithic age; and that is what has been called Dravidian family-Stone age in India p. 43. P.T.S. Ayangar.
It will thus appear that the Dravidian speakers were the latest occupants of India before the Indo-Europeans arrived...It will thus appear that the civilization of the Indus valley was associated with speakers of the Dravidian languages...Lastly the Brahmi script of later Vedic civilizations is itself traced to Indus valley pictographs...Thus the non aryan of the Rlgveda may be in a sense taken to the non Aryan responsible for the Indus civilization-Hindu civilization-pp 38, 30. Radha Kumud Mukerji.
2. He had to subdue or assimilate the aboriginal element. But the overthrow of the black skin was no means an easy task for the Aryan; The non-aryan of the Rigveda was fully fortified in the stronghold of his own civilization which was meterially quite advanced. Remnants of this civilization are traced in the ruins of cities unearthed at Harappa and Mohenjo-Daro-Ibid p 69.
3. There was also inevitably at work a process of fusion between the Aryan and the non aryan by intermarriage or by alliance-Hindu civilization-Ibid.
1. The Aryan ethnical type which closely intermingled with the non-aryan population before the caste system was fully developed never constituted more than a small fraction even of the population of the north - A short history of India Havell p. 29.
But as Dravidian society was matriarchal such intermarriages, with or without consent always exerted powerful influence in the Aryanisation of India, for in the course of time all the highest Dravidian families, both in the north and south climed Aryan descent on-their mother‘s side and and adopted Aryan custiom and religion - Ibid p. 31.
2. As a rule however the Aryan in adopting a Dravidian word changed it considerably in order to suit it to their tongue and whenever such a word was imperfectly understood and negligenlty reproduced the change became still greater.-Kannada Eng. Dictionary. p XV-Rev E. Kettel.
3. Rhys Davids gives of an account of the life in the days of Buddha which probably holds true generally for the Dravidian communities-The children of the Sun.-p. 337. W.J. Perry M.A.
4. The Vedic language was a “caste language” a “scholastic dialect of a class” employing forms of different linguistic periods, an artificially archic dialect,
handed down from one generation to the other within the class of priestly singers - Macdonnel.
Sankrit came into prominance about five thousand years ago in northern India. It appeared from the beginning not as an Indian vernacular, but as the hand miden of the Arya cult, a literary language, used as the vehicle of the aspirations of the Rishis when they appealed to the gods to satisfy their longings in this world and the next. The Hindus have called it the language of the gods-deva bhasa-and this apparently means that there is no evidence of its employment as a vernaculur by common men in their ordinary secular life before it became the language of the mantras by means of which men spoke to the gods-Stone age in India p. 45-P.T.S. Iyengar.
1. The so called Gaudian dialects now spoken in Northern India from Punjab down to Aryavarta agree in grammatical structure with the so called Dravidian dialects of south India. The family relationships of languages can best be ascertined not so much by similarities of their vocables but by and examination of the essential structure of the languages, by their schemes of accidence of gender, number and cases of nouns and adjectives, of voices, mood number gender, tenses and other inflections of verbs, of their essential structure-such as the order of words in sentences and the methods of formation of idioms. A comparative study of modern North Indian and South Indian dialects reveals the fact that their fundamental grammatical structure is so verymuch the same that it is possible to translate from oneof these languages into any other by the simple process if the substitution of one word for another-a procedure absolutely impossible when translation from Sanskrit or English into any of the spoken dialects of ancient or modern India-Stone age in India. p. 44.

1. The excavations at Mohenjo Daro have revealed the state of things of a date long anterior to that of the Birth of Buddha. In the words of Dr. Hirananda Sastri, the late Epigraphist of the Government of India. “There is no known structure in the pre-historic Egypt or Mesopatamia or elsewhere to compare
with the well built commodious houses of the citizens of Mohenjo Doro. Their sanitary baths and the elaborate system of drinage show that even the ordinary town folk there enjoyed a degree of comfort and luxury unknown in other parts of the then civilized world”. That was some what about B.C. 5000. Bulletin No. 3 of Sri Rama Varma Research Institute.

1. Chu Ju - Kua.
1. cōyukuvā (Chu Ju Kua 1225 A.D.) eṉṉum cīṉap pirayāṇi ilaṅki aracaṉip paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatu piṉvarumāṟu: “aracaṉ pala niṟa āṭikaḷi uṭuttirukkiṟāṉ; civapput tōlāṟceytu poṉvāriṭṭa ceruppit tarittirukkiṟāṉ; veḷiyē cellumpōtu pallakkil allatu yāṉimītu celkiṉṟāṉ; viṭūriyam, nīlam civappu mutaliya maṇikaḷ patittucceyta nakikaḷip pūṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. kiḻakkilum mēṟkilum kolumaṇṭapaṅkaḷ uṇṭu, iraṇṭu maṇṭapaṅkaḷilum poṉ maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, avikaḷiṉ kompukaḷum aṭimaramum poṉ mayamāṉavi; pūkkaḷum ilikaḷum kāykaḷum viṭūriyam nīlam civappu eṉṉum maṇikaḷāl amikkappaṭṭavi. aracaṉ ulāvum tariyum alaṅkarikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ammaraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil poṉ ciṅkācaṉaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ kāliyil kiḻakku maṇṭapattilum mēṟku maṇṭapat tilum koluvirukkiṟāṉ. aracaṉ irukkumpōtu cōṭippukaḷ veḷiyē miṉṉum. poṉmaraṅkaḷum kaṇṇāṭi maṟippukkaḷum oṉṟiṉ niḻal oṉṟil paṭṭuc cūriya utayampōl viḷaṅkum.
mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi pirayāṇi maliyāḷa aracaṉippaṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
aracaṉ uṭampip pōrttirukkiṟāṉ; avaṉ kālil cerupput tarittirukkavilli. avaṉ taliyil talippāki tarittirukkiṟāṉ; ariyil āṭi uṭuttirukkiṟāṉ. avi iraṇṭum veṇṇiṟamuṭiyaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil avaṉ veḷḷic caṭṭi aṇikiṟāṉ. avikaḷukku nīṇṭu oṭuṅkiya kikaḷuṇṭu. veḷiyē cellumpōtu avaṉ yāṉimītu celkiṉṟāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉ poṉmuṭi aṇikiṟāṉ. atu muttiṉālum irattiṉaṅkaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avaṉuṭiya kiyil poṉnāṭāk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu; avaṉuṭiya kālil poṉcaṅkili kiṭakkiṟatu. avaṉuṭiya ciṉṉaṅkaḷil civapputtaṭiyil mayiliṟaku kaṭṭiyakoṭi oṉṟu. ati irupatu pēr niṉṟu kāvalpurikiṉṟaṉar. avaṉit tiṭakāttiramuḷḷa ainnūṟu aṉṉiyanāṭṭup peṇkaḷ kāval purikiṉṟaṉar. muṉṉē celpavarkaḷ. nāṭṭiyamāṭik koṇṭu vaḻiyik kāṭṭic celkiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭiyap peṇkaḷukku muṉṉāl aracaṉiṉ karumattalivar koṇṭu cellappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tuṇiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa ēṇipōṉṟa tūkkil iruttappaṭṭu poṉṉālum veḷḷiyiṉālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa kampukaḷ mūlam cumakkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.”
aracaṉuṭiya meykāppāḷar peṇkaḷāka viruntaṉar eṉa mekastīṉas kūṟiyuḷḷār. srāpō (Strabo) eṉpār, “aracaṉuṭiya meykāppāḷar peṇkaḷ. avaṉ vēṭṭikkuc cellumpōtu peṇkaḷ avaṉic cūḻntu celkiṉṟaṉar. cila peṇkaḷ tērilum, cilar kutiri mēlum, cilar yāṉimēlum āyutam tāṅkiyavarkaḷākac celkiṉṟaṉar” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. kuṭaliyar kūṟiyiruppatu koṇṭu kirēkka varalāṟṟukkārar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: aracaṉ paṭukkiyiviṭṭu eḻuntatum villut tāṅkiya peṇkaḷ avaṉi etirkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. peṇ aṭimikaḷē avaṉik muḻukkāṭṭa vēṇṭum, paṭukkiyi virikkavēṇṭum, avaṉatu uṭikaḷi veḷukkavēṇṭum, avaṉi pūmālikaḷāl alaṅkarikkavēṇṭum. kuṭaliyar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi araṇmaṉi yil mūṉṟu taraṅkaḷāṉa peṇ vēliyāṭkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil kaṇikiyar
eṉappaṭṭa kīḻttarattiṉar aracaṉukkuk kuṭi piṭippar. avaṉukkup pakkattē eccil umiḻum poṉ kuṉṭikiyip piṭittu niṟpar. ivarkaḷukku aṭutta uyartarattiṉar aracaṉ māḷikiyil irukkumpōtu pakkattē ālavaṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu niṉṟu avaṉic caṉattilum tērilum irukkumpōtu cēvippar. ivarkaḷil mūppaṭikiṉṟavarkaḷ aracaṉuṭiya kaḷañciyattil allatu aṭukkaḷiyil vēli ceyyumpaṭi viṭappaṭuvar.” (Chandra Gupta Maurya and his times pp. 97-8.)
1. Subtreasruies.
1. The Vedic theory is found in the Aitareya brahmana. It asserts that the Devas i.e. their worshippers, the Hindus, originally had no king. In their struggle aginst the Asuras-when the Devas found that they were repeatedly defeated that they came to the conclusion that it was becuse the Asura had a king to lead them, they were successful. Therefore they decided to try the same experiment and they agreed to elect a king. It has a historical reference. It would refer to the tribal stage of the Aryans in India and it would suggest that the institution of kingship was borrowed from the Dravidians-Hindu polity-p. 5. K.P. Jayaswal M.A.
2. Dravidian characteristics have been traced alike in Vedic and classical Sanskrit in the Prakrits or early popular dialects and in the modern vernaculars derived from them. The presence of the second series of dental letters the so called cerebrals in the language of the Rigveda and their absence from any other Indo- European language is ascribed to Dravidian influence-The quarterly Journal of the Mythic society Vol. XVI. Kalipada Mitre M.A.B.L.
3. irācciyam pala maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, maṇṭalaṅkaḷ kōṭṭaṅkaḷākavum, kōṭṭaṅkaḷ kūṟukaḷākavum, kūṟukaḷ nāṭukaḷ vaḷanāṭukaḷākavum vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. nāṭukaḷum vaḷanāṭukaḷum, pala nakaraṅkaḷum kirāmaṅkaḷumuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ periya nakaraṅkaḷ taṉiyūr eṉṟum maṟṟavi ūrkaḷ paṟṟukkaḷ, kuṟippaṟṟukkaḷ eṉṟum peyar peṟum, -cōḻavamica carittirac curukkam - pak. 50 - kōpinātarāv.

The sculpture of Sanchi and Amaravathi and the Ajanta Cave pintings and the sculptures of Orissa (Buvaneswar) prove that in its forms also Hindu jewelry has remined unaltered during the last two thousand years. The ornaments of Sanchi are of the same archic character as those still made in central India and the Central Provinces and by the aboriginal tribes of the Bengal and Bombay presidency while those of Amaravathi shows more elaboration and finish of Dravidian jewelry of Madras presidency. -Industrial arts of India-p. 188.

3. Our knowledge of the costumes of Mohenjo Daro people is scanty as naked figures preponderate. A shawl is worn by a male figure covering the left shoulder
and passed under the right arm. It is difficult to say what was worn under the shawl, but the heroes and deities wore a thin strip of cotton on their loins. Some very rare fiqurines are depicted wearing kilt or drawers. The hir was tied with a woven fillet. The woven sari terminating well above the knees always fastened with girdles and in one case with a kamarband is also seen. The narrow strip of cloth used as sari at Mohenjo Daro very much resembles the niva mentioned in Vedic literature. Vol. 1. p. 53-Dr. Motichandra M.A. Ph.D.
1. Cotton manufacture did not obtin a real footing in Europe until last century at a date before history. The art was carried from India to Assyria and Egypt; but it was not until the thirteenth century that the cotton plant was introduced into southern Europe where its wool was at first used to make paper. The manufacture of it into cloth in imitation of the fabrics of Egypt and India was first attempted by the Italian States in the thirteenth century-Industrial arts of India p. 241.

tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ vāḻvum toṇṭum (1893 - 1967)
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ toṉmiyi ataṉ taṉit taṉmiyi ulaka moḻikaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu virivāka āyvu ceytavar tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ. tamiḻ iṉattiṉ mēṉmiyi varalāṟṟu nōkkil āyvu ceytavarum avarē.
‘toṇṭu ceyvāy! tamiḻukkut
tuṟitōṟum tuṟitōṟum tuṭitteḻuntē’
eṉpār puraṭcik kaviñar pāratitācaṉār. puraṭcik kaviñariṉ kaṉavi niṉivākkum vakiyil, tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ tuṟitōṟum tuṟitōṟum ariya āyvu nūlkaḷi eḻutiyavar. tamatu nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka vīḻcciyuṟṟa tamiḻiṉatti eḻucci peṟac ceytavar.
na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻp paṇiyō maliyiṉum māṇap periyatu. āyiṉum avaruṭiya vāḻkkip pativukaḷāka namakkuk kiṭippaṉa tiṉiyaḷavē yākum. tamiḻarkaḷ anta māmaṉitariṉ tamiḻp paṇiyik kūrntu aṟintu takka vakiyil avaruṭiya vāḻkki varalāṟṟip pativu ceyyat tavaṟi viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟutāṉ tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṉṟu avaruṭiya vāḻkakik kuṟippukaḷāka namakkuk kiṭippaṉa mikac cilavākum. avi:-
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ īḻa nāṭṭil kantarōṭi eṉṉum ūril 1893 ām āṇṭil tiru naṉṉiyar ciṉṉattampi eṉpāriṉ putalvarāyp piṟantār. takka āciriyariṭam payiṉṟu kalviyil tērntār. piṉṉar kantarōṭi eṉṉum ūril uḷḷa oru paḷḷiyil āciriyarākap paṇi purintār. āciriyar paṇiyilirukkum pōtē takka perum pulavarkaḷit tēṭic ceṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ muṟiyākap payiṉṟu perum pulavarāy viḷaṅkiṉār. āṅkila moḻiyilum tērcci peṟṟa vallunarāyt tikaḻntār. piṉṉar malēciyā nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu ciṟitukālam pukivaṇṭi aluvalakattil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṉ tamiḻaṟivu āḻamum akalamum koṇṭatu. avaratu vāḻkki muḻumiyum tamiḻ āyvup paṇiyē perum paṇiyāka amintatu. avar peṟṟa āṅkila aṟiviṉ tuṇiyāl tamiḻ moḻi, tamiḻiṉam toṭarpāṉa mēli nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi yellām nuṇukik kaṟṟār viyakkattakka kalvik kaṭalāy viḷaṅkiṉār. aṟupatukku mēṟpaṭṭa āyvu nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār.
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya nūlkaḷip patippittu veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vēṭki koṇṭār. ilaṅkiyil ataṟkup pōtiya vacati illi. ātalāl tamiḻnāṭṭukku varuki purintār. ceṉṉiyil “oṟṟumi niliyam” eṉṉum patippakattiṉ urimiyāḷarāyt tikaḻntavar vīrapāku piḷḷi eṉpavar. avar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭār. piṉṉar muttamiḻ niliyam, āciriyar nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, tirunelvēli civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vāyilāka na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ veḷivaralāyiṉa.
na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṉ arumit tuṇiviyār irattiṉammā eṉappaṭupavar. ivarukkut tirunāvukkaracu eṉṟoru makaṉum maṅkiyarkkaraci eṉṟoru makaḷārum uṇṭu. tuṇiviyār pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kālamāṉār. tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mēṉmikkāka allum pakalum ayarātu pāṭupaṭṭa tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi tamatu eḻupattu nāṉkām vayatil 1967il ilaṅkiyil maṟintār. eṉiṉum tamiḻ vāḻum vari avaratu pukaḻ eṉṟeṉṟum nilittu niṟkum.
tamiḻaṟiñar kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻppaṇi matippu mikkatu; kālatti veṉṟu nilittuniṟka vallatu. tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻnāṭu, tamiḻppaṇpāṭu, tamiḻiṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟṟi eḻutiyatil avarukkup perum paṅkuṇṭu. avaruṭiya āyvup paṇiyic ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
akarātip paṇi
tamiḻ moḻiyil mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiya akarāti vīramā muṉivar eḻutiya caturakarātiyē yākum. piṉṉar palvēṟu akarātikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa, vaḷarntaṉa, veḷivantaṉa. akarāti vaḷarccip paṇiyil kantiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷukkum perum paṅkuṇṭu. avar paṭitta akarātikaḷ aintu. 1. centamiḻ akarāti, 2. tamiḻ ilakkiya akarāti, 3. tamiḻp pulavar akarāti, 4. tirukkuṟaḷ akarāti, 5. kālak kuṟippu akarāti eṉpaṉa avi.
na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ centamiḻ akarāti muṉṉuri yil “nām tamiḻt tāy moḻikkuc ceyyum paṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka in nūlic ceytu muṭittōm” eṉkiṟār. “nūloṉṟic ceytu tamiḻulakukku utava vēṇṭum eṉṉum āvalāl palavakiyil muyaṉṟu in nūlic ceytu muṭittōm” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻ ilakkiya akarātiyil akattiyar mutal virākkiya tīpam īṟāka āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻp pulavar akarātiyil pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻppulavarkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avar tokutta tirukkuṟaḷ coṟ poruḷ akarāti payaṉpāṭu mikkatu. kālakkuṟippu akarāti putumi yāṉatu. tamiḻ moḻiyil itu pōṉṟa akarāti ituvari veḷivanta tilli “innūl ōr aṟivuk kaḷañciyam” eṉkiṟār muṉivar. mā. irāca māṇikkaṉār.
ilakkiyap paṇi
pulavarkaḷukku maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭṭu vanta tamiḻ ilakkiyac celvatti eḷiya makkaḷum paṭittup payaṉ peṟumāṟu urinaṭiyil vaḻaṅkiya vaḷḷal kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ.
pattuppāṭṭu, akanāṉūṟu, kalittoki, patiṟṟuppattu, paripāṭal, kaliṅkattupparaṇi, puṟapporuḷ viḷakkam, viṟaliviṭutūtu pōṉṟa ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷi ellām iṉiya eḷiya naṭiyil urinaṭiyil tantavar kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ. tirukkuṟaḷukkum, nītineṟi viḷakkattiṟkum ariya uri varintavar.
tamiḻmoḻi - tamiḻiṉam
tamiḻmoḻi - tamiḻiṉam toṭarpākap patiṉintukkum mēṟpaṭṭa āyvu nūlkaḷi eḻutiyavar kantiyā piḷḷi. tamiḻakam, tamiḻ intiyā, tamiḻar carittiram, tamiḻaryār?, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻantamiḻar, namatumoḻi, namatunāṭu, tamiḻ ārāycci, tamiḻ viḷakkam, muccaṅkam, akattiyar, cintuveḷi nākarikam, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, tamiḻarcamayam etu? civaṉ, civa camaya varalāṟu, tamiḻp paḻamiyum putumiyum pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ariya āyavu nūlkaḷip paṭittavar. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa nūṟkaṭaluḷ mūḻki eṭutta ariya koṟki muttukkaḷ avaratu nūlkaḷ. ettaṉi āṇṭukālap pēruḻippu! niṉittāl malipput tōṉṟum. tamiḻiṉ - tamiḻiṉattiṉ toṉmiyit tamiḻartam paṇpāṭṭi - nākarikatti, tamiḻartam uyar vāḻviyali ulakaṟiyac ceyta pēraṟiñar kantiyā piḷḷi.
tirāviṭa nākarikamum - āriyattāl viḷinta kēṭum
toṉmi mikka tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ ciṟappukaḷi viḷakkum vakiyil pala nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār. avi:-
tirāviṭar nākarikam, tirāviṭam eṉṟāl eṉṉa? tirāviṭa intiyā, teṉṉintiyak kulaṅkaḷum kuṭikaḷum pōṉṟa ariya āyvu nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār. āriyarkaḷāl viḷinta kēṭukaḷ kuṟittum, āriyar tamiḻar kalappu, āriyattāl viḷinta kēṭu, purōkitar āṭci, irāmāyaṇam naṭanta katiyā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār.
piṟa
māṇavar tam aṟivi virivu ceyyum vakiyil eṭṭu nūlkaḷum, makaḷirukkup pallāṇṭuk kālamāka iḻikkappaṭṭu varum koṭumikaḷ kuṟittu mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum eḻutiṉār. mēlum moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷ cilavum maṉita iṉattōṟṟam kuṟitta nūlkaḷ cilavum eṉap pala nūlkaḷi eḻutiyuḷḷār. puraṭcik kaviñar kūṟiyavāṟu tuṟitōṟum, tuṟitōṟum eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷi eḻutit tamiḻukku vaḷam cērttavar. na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi. puṭcikkaviñar pāratitācaṉār, tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷip paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu pārāṭṭukiṟār.
“na.ci. kantiyā eṉum nallavaṉ, vallavaṉ tamiḻ tamiḻiṉa varalāṟaṉittiyum tolporuḷ āyviṉ toki vaki, virittu nilanūl, kaṭalnūl cāṉṟukaḷ niṟittum tamiḻ nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ muṭṭaṟut tiyampiyum ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cāṉṟukaḷ koṭuttum paḻakka vaḻakka oḻukkam kāṭṭiyum viyakam viyakka varalāṟu eḻutiṉāṉ. poy akaṉṟu meyk ki uyarntatu.”
vāḻka! na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷiyiṉ perum pukaḻ!
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟē tamiḻar kaḷāl maṟakkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. tamiḻp pakivarkaḷāl maṟikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. aṅṅaṉamirukka am māmaṉitariṉ āyvu nūlkaḷ maṭṭum eṅṅaṉam kiṭikkum? āḻkaṭaliliruntu muttukkaḷ eṭuppatu pōla, taṅkac curaṅkattiliruntu taṅkatti veṭṭi eṭuppatu pōla, perumuyaṟci eṭuttu na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷit tēṭiṉēṉ. nūlniliyaṅkaḷit tēṭittēṭi eṉ kālkaḷ alinta vaṇṇam iruntaṉa. ataṉ viḷivāka aimpatu nūlkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. perum putiyalit tēṭi eṭuttatu pōl perumakiḻvuṟṟēṉ. 
aṉpaṉ
kō. tēvarācaṉ
akam nutalutal
ulakil vāḻum māntar aṉivarkkum uḷḷārnta eṇṇa ōṭṭaṅkaḷ ali aliyāy eḻuntu palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷil veḷip paṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkak kālattut tamiḻ māntar tamatu eṇṇaṅkaḷi oḻuṅku paṭutti oru variyaṟikkuḷ kaṭṭuppaṭutti vāḻa muṟpaṭṭataṉ viḷivē nākarikattiṉ toṭakkam eṉalām.
ulakil tōṉṟi vāḻntu varum ellā uyirkkum iṉpam eṉpatu iyalpāka virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa oṉṟākavē uḷḷatu. anta iṉpattukakut taṭiyō iṭiyīṭō nēriṉ atip pōkkik koḷḷa muyalum muṟiyil maṉitak kulattukkut taṉip paṇpu ciṟappāka veḷippaṭṭiruppatic caṅka ilakkiyattiṉ vaḻi naṉku aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉa mūṉṟu perum pirivāka maṉita vāḻviṉ iyalpi vakuttu ilakkaṇam ceytālum, avaṉ uḷḷattil eḻum uṇarvi veḷippaṭutta moḻiyum collum ataṉ poruḷum oṉṟiyoṉṟu piṉṉip piṇintirukkiṉṟa pāṅku puṟattiṇic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭilum akattiṇic ceyyuḷkaḷilēyē mikuntiruppatik kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.
tolkāppiyar ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṟār. kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum tēṟṟēkāram poruḷ kuṟiyāta col illi eṉpatiyē uṇarttukiṟatu. iṟṟi nāḷil nammil cilar vañcaka eṇṇattuṭaṉum palar makkaḷiṉ cintaṉi ōṭṭattit tūṇṭi nalvaḻip paṭuttavum uḷḷoṉṟu vittup puṟamoṉṟu pēcuvatik kēṭkiṟōm.
erutunōy kākkikkut teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟum pōtum kutirikkuk kompu muḷittatu pōlattāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum pōtum (muyaṟkompē) ac colliṉ poruḷiyum ataṉāl nuṇṇuṇar vuṭiyār aṟiyum vēṟu poruḷiyum ac col uṇarttuvatāka aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. viṭukatikaḷ colli aṟivit tūṇṭic cintikka vippatum cilēṭiyākap pēci uṭporuḷi uṇarat tūṇṭuvatum iṉṟum naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟa aṟivārnta ceyalkaḷ.
iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻp piriviṉ muṭivum maṉita ṉiṉ uḷḷuṇarvi veḷippaṭutta muyaṉṟa muyaṟciyē. akam - puṟam eṉṟa pirivil uḷḷattu uṇarvittāṉ nukarntāṉ eṉpati evvāṟu veḷippaṭuttuvatu? evvāṟu aṟivatu? aṟamō maṟamō - uyir uṭal vēṟupāṭukaḷāl, moḻiyāl, iciyāl, aḻukiyāl, ciki eṉṉum nāṭakattāl allavō veḷippaṭutta muṭiyum. akattil eḻum kātal uṇarvi oruvaṉum oruttiyum nukarnta nukarcciyi ittakiyatu eṉṟu piṟarkku aṟivuṟutta iyalātu. akattāl maṭṭumē uḷ mukamāka nāṭi ārāyntu aṟintu koḷḷamuṭiyum eṉpatu uṇmi eṉṟālum. ivvuṇarvu maṉitak kulattiṟku ottiruppatāl cila pala kuṟippukaḷi maṭṭumē veḷippaṭuttiṉāl pōtum maṟṟavarum aṟintu itu ivvāṟu irukkum eṉṟu uṇarntu makiḻavum oruvarkku oruvar utavi ceyyavum ētuvāka irukkiṟatu.
eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷuḷ akanāṉūṟu, kuṟuntoki, naṟṟiṇi ākiya mūṉṟum nāṉūṟu nāṉūṟu pāṭalkaḷālāṉa toki nūlkaḷ. caṅkakkālac cāṉṟōriṉ ariya muyaṟciyāl ivvāṟu tokukkap paṭṭālum akap poruḷ tiṇik kaḷaṅkaḷ aintum ivaṟṟuḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl kuṟuntoki 4 mutal 8 aṭikaḷum, naṟṟiṇi 9 mutal 12 aṭikaḷum, akanāṉūṟu 13 mutal 21 aṭikaḷum koṇṭa aṭivariyaṟi kaḷiyuṭiyaṉa. ēṉō akanāṉūṟu nīṇṭa āciriyap pāvāṉ amintu neṭuntoki eṉap peyar peṟṟālum kaḷiṟṟi yāṉi niri, maṇimiṭip pavaḷam, nittilak kōvi eṉa mup pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. uyir eḻuttukaḷ 12 mey eḻuttukaḷ 18 eṉpatāl eḻuttukaḷ ovvoṉṟaṟkum pattup pattākak kaḷiṟṟu yāṉi niri 12 x 10 = 120 pāṭalkaḷākavum maṇimiṭi pavaḷam 18 x 10 = 180 pāṭalkaḷākavum pirittaṉarō eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. mataṅ koṇṭa yāṉi pōṉṟa aimpulaṉiyum oḻuṅku paṭuttak kaḷiṟṟuyāṉi niri eṉṟu uyir paṉṉiraṇṭi 120 ākap pakuttaṉarō! maṇipōṉṟa meyyāṉa uṭali niralpaṭa māliyākat toṭukkappaṭṭatāka eṇṇip patiṉeṭṭi 180 maṇimiṭi pavaḷamākat tokuttaṉarō! uḷḷamākiya kaṭaliṉ āḻattiliruntu cēkaritta nittilatti muḻumi peṟṟa māliyākat tarittu makiḻavō muḻuvatum nūṟi eḻunta veṟṟi yiṉpattik kuṟikkavō 100 nūṟu pāṭalkaḷi nittilak kōvi eṉat tokuttaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. inta muppāṉ piriviṟkuk kāraṇam muḻumiyākat tōṉṟavilli.
vakupaṭāmal oṟṟip paṭiyāy eñci niṟkum eṇkaḷiyuṭiya pāṭalkaḷ uripporuḷāl pālit tiṇip pāṭalkaḷākavum, iraṇṭum eṭṭum urip poruḷāl puṇarcciyi uṇarttum kuṟiñcit tiṇip pāṭalkaḷākavum, nāṉkil muṭiyum eṇṇuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ nāṉku uṟutip poruḷkaḷi eṇṇi urip poruḷāl āṟṟi irukkum mullit tiṇip pāṭalkaḷākavum, āṟāvatu eṇṇil muṭiyum pāṭalkaḷ toṭarntu cellum āṟupōla maṉam orunilip paṭāmal māṟi māṟi uṭal koḷḷavāyppāka amintu nīr vaḷam mikka marutattiṇip pāṭalākavum, muḻumi peṟṟa oṉṟōṭu cuḻiyic cērttatu pōṉṟa pattām eṇṇuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ yāṉ eṉ talivaṉōṭu cērntu eṉṟu muḻumi peṟuvōṉō eṉṟu iraṅki ēṅkum urip poruḷāl neytal tiṇip pāṭalākavum pakuttut tokuttirukkum pāṅku akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷil maṭṭumē kāṇappaṭum ciṟappākum. mulli, marutam, neytal ākiya mut tiṇip pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum camamāka nāṟpatu pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭu potuvāka amintuḷḷatu. puṇarcci urip poruḷi uṇarttum kuṟiñcip pāṭalkaḷ eṇpatāka amintaṉa; pirivi uṇarttum pālittiṇip pāṭalkaḷ irunūṟāka uḷḷatu vāḻvil iṉpam aṭiya tuṉpattil mikutiyum uḻala vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu eṉpati uṇarttavō eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
akattuṟip pāṭalkaḷ nāṭakap pāṅkil amintuḷḷaṉa. colla vanta karutti nili nāṭṭa uvami mūlamāka viḷakkuvatu mikavum eḷitu. peṇkaḷ nayamākap pēcik karutti valiyuṟuttic ceyal paṭuttuvatil vallavarkaḷ. akattuṟiyil tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṉṟu pēcum pēccukaḷ tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiṉ taṉittaṉmiyik kāṭṭuvaṉa. tōḻiyiṉ pēccil uḷḷuṟip poruḷum iṟiccip poruḷum veḷippaṭum pāṅku niṉintu niṉintu pōṟṟattakkaṉa.
uḷḷuṟi eṉpatu teyvam nīṅkalākak kūṟappaṭum. avvan nilattuk karup poruḷkaḷi nilikkaḷaṉākak koṇṭu uṇarappaṭum kuṟippup poruḷākum. karup poruḷ nikaḻcacikaḷ uvamam pōla amintu avaṟṟiṉ ottu muṭivatu pōlap peṟappaṭum oru karuttup poruḷākum.
iṟicci tāṉē poruṭ puṟattatuvē eṉpār tolkāppiyar. iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷumāruḷavē iṟicci tāṉē urip puṟattatuvē eṉṟum pāṭam. akattiṇik karupporuḷkaḷiṉ mūlam peṟappaṭum kuṟippup poruḷ iṟicci eṉṟu koḷḷalām. ak kuṟippup poruḷiliruntu vēṟu oru karuttup peṟappaṭumāyiṉ ak karuttē iṟicciyil piṟakkum poruḷ eṉṟu koḷḷalām. akanāṉūṟṟil mutal karupporuḷkaḷukkē ciṟappiṭam koṭuttup pēcappaṭukiṟatu. mika nuṭpamāṉa uḷḷuṟi uvamamum iṟiccip poruḷum āṅkāṅku kaṇṭu uṇarntu makiḻumāṟu amintuḷḷaṉa. āciriyar caṅka ilakkiyac ceyyuḷkaḷil peritum payiṟciyuṭiyavar eṉpati avaratu urinaṭiyāl kāṇamuṭikiṟatu. ceyyuḷ ilakkaṇam kaṭanta urinaṭip pāṭṭu eṉṟu collumaḷavukkut toṭarkaḷ amin tuḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa eccac coṟkaḷāl karuttit teḷivuṟuttum pāṅku ivvāciriyarkkē kivanta kaliyāka amintu nammi ellām viyakka vikkiṟatu.
caṅkak kālattil vaḻakkilirunta coṟkaḷi niṉivuṟuttum pāṅkil ariya coṟkaḷit tamatu urinaṭiyil kiyāṇṭu tamiḻip paḻam perumi kuṉṟāmal kāttiṭa ivaratu urinaṭi ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum eṉpati inta akanāṉūṟṟu urinaṭiyip payilvār uṇarvar eṉpatu uṟuti.
ariya nayam mikka centamiḻt toṭariyum āciriyar tamatu uriyil toṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār. meyyiṉ niḻal pōlat tiraṇṭa āyattōṭu viḷiyāṭi makiḻvēṉ eṉṟu 49 ām pāṭalil kuṟippiṭu kiṟār. mey - uṇmi avaravar niḻal avaravari viṭṭup piriyātu atu pōla taliviyi viṭṭup piriyāta tōḻiyarōṭu talivi viḷiyāṭi yati eṇṇi makiḻalām.
uppuviṟkum peṇ ‘nellukku veḷḷuppu’ eṉṟu kūvik ki vīci naṭakkiṟāḷ. paṇṭa māṟṟu muṟiyi itu namakku uṇarttukiṟatu.
nīṉiṟa vaṇṇaṉ kuṉiyā niṉṟa āyar peṇkaḷiṉ tukili eṭuttuk koṇṭu kuruntamarat tēṟiṉāṉākap pāṉiṟa vaṇṇaṉ iṭiyar kulap peṇkaḷiṉ māṉattik kākkak kurunta marak kiḷiyit tāḻttit taḻikaḷāl maṟittuk kāttāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum varalāṟṟip pālit tiṇic ceyyuḷ 59 il kūṟiyiruppatu eṇṇi makiḻattakkatu. peṇ yāṉi uṇpataṟku āṇ yāṉi yāmarattiṉ kiḷiyit vaḷittut tāḻttit taruvatik kaṇṭum, matanīr oḻukum kaṉṉattil moykkum vaṇṭukaḷi ōṭṭum taḻikaḷiṉ ceyaliyum oruṅku iṇittup pārttut talivaṉiṉ taṇṇaḷiyi eṇṇi āṟi irukkalām eṉṉum tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟi mika āḻamāka āciriyar viḷakkiya pāṅku pōṟṟutaṟku uriyatām.
aṉpaṉ
pulavar ta. āṟumukaṉ
nūlaṟimukavuri
tiru. na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi 1930-40kaḷil tamiḻ, tamiḻilakkiyam, tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻar varalāṟu, tirāviṭa varalāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuṭikaḷ pōṉṟa pala viṭayaṅkaḷ paṟṟi akkālattuc cātāraṇa tamiḻ vācakar niliyil peritum vācikkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār. paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷāṉa akanāṉūṟu, kalittoki, paripāṭal pōṉṟa vaṟṟiṉi urinaṭiyil eḻuti makkaḷiṭiyē caṅka ilakkiyam paṟṟiya uṇarviṉi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. tirukkuṟaḷukkāṉa oru collaṭivit tirukkuṟaḷ akarāti eṉṉum peyaril veḷiyiṭṭavar. ivi yāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka ulaka varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷic curukkamākak kālavaricippaṭuttit tantār. centamiḻ akarāti eṉṟavōr akara mutaliyiyum tokuttār.
iṉṟu piṉṉōkkip pārkkum poḻutu 1940-50kaḷil iḷiñar kaḷāka irunta pala tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷ tamiḻ paṟṟiya taṅkaḷ ārvatti vaḷarttuk koḷvataṟkāṉa vācippu viṭayaṅkaḷi aḷittavar. ivaruṭiya perumpālāṉa paṭippukkaḷ akkālattiṟ piracurikkap paṭṭa oṟṟumi eṉṉum itaḻiṉ aluvalakattālēyē veḷiyiṭap peṟṟaṉa. ivar veḷiyiṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala oṟṟumi itaḻil veḷivan tiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, ataṉi ippoḻutu niccayamāka eṉṉāṟ colla muṭiyavilli. oṟṟumi aluvalakam ivaṟṟip piracurittatu eṉpati aṟivēṉ. ēṉeṉil ilaṅkiyil tamiḻāciriyarāka irunta eṉatu takappaṉāriṭattu innūlkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavi iruntaṉa.
tiru na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷiyiṉ peyar tamiḻakattilē toṭarntu pōṟṟappaṭumaḷavukku īḻattil niṉikkappaṭuvatilli eṉṟa uṇmiyi mikunta maṉavaruttattuṭaṉ pativu ceyyavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. aṅku avar attuṇip pōṟṟappaṭātiruppataṟkuk kāraṇam ēṟattāḻa avarkaḷatu ellā nūlkaḷumē tamiḻakattilēyē veḷiyiṭap peṟṟaṉa.
in nūlkaḷiṉ piracurap piṉpulam paṟṟi etuvum teḷivākat teriyavilli. avaṟṟiṉ poruḷiyal amcaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya taravukaḷum ippoḻutu teriyātuḷḷaṉa.
īḻattut tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟṟiṟkūṭa ivaratu peyar mukkiyap paṭuttap peṟātu pōyuḷḷatu. mikunta ciramattiṉ piṉṉar avarip paṟṟik kiṭittuḷḷa takavalkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācar, na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ tamiḻp paṅkaḷippukkaḷ tamiḻakattil pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟami īḻattavarkkup perumi tarukiṉṟatu. ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ peyari nōkkumpoḻutu tamiḻ vācakarkaḷukku ulaka nilippaṭṭa, tamiḻnilip paṭṭa taravukaḷit tokuttut taruvatē ivaratu peruñcirattiyāka iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ippatippakattiṉ paṇiyiṉi ūkkuvikka vēṇṭiyatu tamiḻcārnta niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷiṉatum tamiḻp periyōrkaḷiṉatum kaṭamiyākum. uṇmiyil itaṉi oru aṟivuppacip piṇit tīrvākavē nāṉ kāṇkiṟēṉ. 
2/7, ṟāmskēṭ, aṉpuṭaṉ
58, 37āvatu oḻuṅki, kārttikēcu civattampi
veḷḷavatti, takicār ōyvunili pērāciriyar
koḻumpu - 6 yāḻppāṇap palkalik kaḻakam. 
karuvinūl tanta na.ci. kantiyā
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa mūlavarkaḷil yāḻppāṇam tanta pēraṟiñar na.ci. kantiyāvum oruvar. uṇmiyāṉa aṟiñarkaḷik kālaṅkaṭantu aṭiyāḷam kāṇpatum avartam paṭippukkaḷit tēṭip piṭittup purappatum tamiḻiṉattiṉ paḻakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟu.
tamiḻiṉ, tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟu toṭarpākat tamiḻil nūlkaḷ mikak kuṟivu. ōr āyiram nūlkaḷāvatu tamiḻiṉ tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟupaṟṟi veḷivaravēṇṭum. virivāka eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭiya tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟṟu varivu muyaṟcikku vaḻikāṭṭum karuvi nūlkaḷi varintiruppavartāṉ na.ci. kantiyā perumakaṉār.
ulakam muḻuvatum uṟṟuk kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya varalāṟṟiṟku uriyavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. avarkaḷē ulaka moḻikaḷi īṉṟa mūlamoḻikkuc contakkārarkaḷ. avarkaḷē ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ piṟappiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ulaka mutal nākarikattip paṭittavarkaḷ. inta uṇmikaḷit tamiḻarkaḷum aṟiyavilli ulakamum aṟiyavilli.
tamiḻar carittiram, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻar, tamiḻar yār, ulaka nākarikattil tamiḻarpaṅku, cintuveḷit tamiḻar, tamiḻ intiyā, tamiḻakam, maṟinta nākarikaṅkaḷ ākiyaṉa na.ci. kantiyāviṉ kuṟippiṭattakunta nūlkaḷākum.
ulaka mutal nākarikam eṉa iṉṟu ulakam nampikkoṇṭirukkak kūṭiya nīla āṟṟaṅkari nākarikam (Nile Civilisation) tamiḻ nākarikattiṉ atāvatu cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa nākarikamē eṉpati ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷāl viḷakkupavi mēliya nūlkaḷ.
moḻiniliyil tamiḻiṉ ulaka mutaṉmiyip pāvāṇar nilināṭṭiṉār eṉṟāl iṉa niliyil tamiḻiṉa ulaka mutaṉmiyi na.ci. kantiyā nilināṭṭiṉār eṉṟu urikkalām.
nīla āṟṟaṅkari nākarika muṭiviṉpiṉ naṇṇilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyil uruvāṉa piṟitoru vaḻinākarikamē kirēkka nākarikam. kirēkka nākarikat tiṉ uṭivil teṟippil piṟaku malarntaviyē iṉṟiya mēli nākarikam. ulakiṉ ellā nākarikaṅkaḷiyum tāṅkiniṟkum tāy nākarikamē tamiḻ nākarikam.
tamiḻ nākarikat toṉmiyic caṅka nūlkaḷukku appāl cintuveḷi akaḻvāyvum ulaka varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ulaka mutal nākarikam paṟṟit terivittirukkum karuttukkaḷum namakku mēlum viḷakkum pakutikaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkellām ceṉṟu nuṇmāṇ nuḻipula muyaṟciyāl tamiḻiṉa varalāṟu eḻutiyavartāṉ na.ci. kantiyā perumakaṉār.
na.ci. kantiyā perumakaṉār nūlkaḷit tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟṟik kaṟkum muyaṟciyil nāṉ īṭupaṭattoṭaṅkiyapōtu tēṭik kaṟṟēṉ. pacciyappaṉ kallūriyiṉ mēṉāḷ tamiḻp pērāciriyar ceṉṟa āṇṭu iyaṟkiyeytiya varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñar kō. nilavaḻakaṉār na.ci. kantiyā avarkaḷic ceṉṉiyil avar vāḻnta nāḷil cantittatu paṟṟiyum avariṉ paṉṉūṟpulami paṟṟiyum emmiṭam makiḻntu kūṟuvār. na.ci. kantiyā avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum avar vittiruntār. arukiya paḻiya nūlkaḷip pēṇuvāriṭattilum nūlakaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟilum maṭṭumē oṭuṅkikkiṭanta na.ci. kantiyā nūlkaḷi mīṇṭum accil veḷiyiṭuvār ilarē eṉṟu kavaliyuṟṟēṉ. amiḻtam patippakam ivariṉ nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmit tamiḻ neñcaṅkaḷ in nūlkaḷi uccimōntu vāriyaṇittup pukaḻntu kaṟṟup payaṉaṭivārkaḷ eṉpatil eṉakku nampikkiyuṇṭu.
pērā. ku. aracēntiraṉ
patippuri
vaḷam cērkkum paṇi
“kumarināṭṭiṉ tamiḻiṉap perumiyi nimirac ceytāṉ,” “cintiyum ceyalum centamiḻukku cērtta nūl oṉṟā iraṇṭā” eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭa tamiḻīḻa aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyāviṉ nūlkaḷi mīṇṭum maṟupatippuc ceytu tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḷam cērkkum paṇiyic ceyya muṉ vantuḷḷōm.
ipperumakaṉār eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṟupatukku mēṟpaṭṭavi yākum. innūlkaḷ ciṟitum peritumāka uḷḷavi. ivaṟṟip poruḷ vāriyākap pirittu irupatukku mēṟpaṭṭa nūl tiraṭṭukaḷākat tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk kaḷamākavum, taḷamākavum vaḷam cērkkum viramaṇi māli yākavum koṭuttuḷḷōm. moḻikkum iṉattiṟkum araṇāka amiyum ivvaṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷ etirkālat tamiḻ ulakiṟkup perum payaṉit taravallaṉa.
na.ci. kantiyā
ivar 1893il tamiḻīḻa maṇṇil navāliyūr eṉṉum ūril piṟantavar. piṟanta ūrilēyē paḷḷippaṭippi muṭittu avvūrilēyē āciriyap paṇiyāṟṟiyavar. piṉṉar malēciya maṇṇil cilakālam toṭarvaṇṭit tuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār. ivar tamiḻ īḻa maṇṇil piṟantiruntālum tamiḻakattil iruntutāṉ avar tamiḻ moḻikkum tamiḻ iṉattiṟkum perumi cērkkum nūlkaḷ pala eḻutiṉār eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
tamiḻukkut toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷil na.ci. kantiyā kuṟippiṭattakkavar. taṉṉi muṉṉilip paṭuttātu moḻiyiyum iṉattiyum muṉṉilippaṭuttiya perumikkuriyavar. ulaka moḻi kaḷuḷ tamiḻmoḻi toṉmimikkatu. ulakamoḻikaḷukku vērākavum cāṟākavum amintatu. tamiḻar camayamum, kaliyum paṇpāṭum, varalāṟum, tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭa aṟiviyalum ulakukku muṉṉōṭiyākat tikaḻvaṉa. ivaṟṟiyellām tam nuṇṇaṟivāl kaṇṭaṟintu pala nūlkaḷi yāttavar.
tamiḻiyam paṟṟiya āyvi ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ palaruḷar. ivarkaḷ aṉivarum kali, ilakkiyam, camayam, moḻi, varalāṟu, nākarikam, paṇpāṭu pōṉṟa pala tuṟikaḷil paṅkāṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, na.ci.ka. it tuṟikaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟip potu aṟivut tuṟiyilum pukuntu puttākkac ceytikaḷit tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk koṭuttavar. ivaruṭiya moḻipeyarppu paṭippāri īrkkak kūṭiyavi. paṭittaliṉ nōkkam paṟṟiyum palvēṟu poruḷ paṟṟiyum kūṟupavi. tamiḻ akarātit tuṟiyil ivar eḻutiya kālakkuṟippu akarāti tamiḻ ulakiṟkup putuvaravāy amintatu.
tamiḻ moḻikku ariya nūlkaḷit tantōr mikac cilarē. niṟitamiḻ aṟiñar maṟimali aṭikaḷum, tamiḻ maliyām tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum tamiḻ moḻikku ākkamum ūkkamum cērkkattakka nūlkaḷit tamiḻarkaḷukku vippāka eḻutic ceṉṟavarkaḷ. avar tam variciyil ivar tam nūlkaḷiṉ variciyum aṭaṅkum. tamiḻ āyvu varalāṟṟil ivariṉ paṅkaḷippu kuṟikkattakkatu. kaḻakak kālac ceyti kaḷip paṭittup poruḷ purintu koḷvatu palarukkuk kaṭiṉamāka iruntatu. ivaṟṟi eḷitil paṭittup poruḷ puriyum urinaṭip pōkki mutaṉmutalil kiyāṇṭavar. ivaratu moḻinaṭi, karuttukku mutaṉmi tarupavi. urinaṭi, eḷimiyum teḷivum uṭiyatu. ciṟuciṟu vākkiya amippil teḷinta nīrōṭi pōṉṟatu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ teṉṉakam peṟṟirunta nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷik kaṇṇukkuk kāṭciyākavum paṭippāriṉ karuttukku viruntākavum aḷittavar.
nūl tiraṭṭukaḷ nuvalum ceytikaḷ
1800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar kālam toṭṭu ivar vāḻnta kālam vari tamiḻariṉ varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷi nuṭpamāka ārāyn tuḷḷār. avar maṟiviṟkup piṟaku iṉṟu vari avaruṭiya āyviṟku mēlum ākkam tarum ceytikaḷ aṟivulakil tamiḻukkum, tamiḻarkaḷukkum valuvāy amintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻum civaneṟiyum ōṅkiyiruntami. - tāyāṭcik kālam mutaṉmi peṟṟiruntatu. moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟam, camaṟkirutam eppaṭit tōṉṟiyatu - āriyar yār - intiyāviṟku eppaṭi vantaṉar - tamiḻarōṭu evvāṟu kalappuṟṟaṉar - ātimakkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum, piṟappum - intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātimakkaḷ, tirāviṭa makkaḷ - ātimakkaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷil kuṭipeyarntatu - moḻikkum, camayattukkum uḷḷa uṟavu - camaṟkirutam camayamoḻi āṉataṟkāṉa āyvukaḷ - vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṭakkam - vaḻipāṭu eṅkellām iruntatu - paṇṭiya makkaḷ eḻutu vataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷ - mēli nāṭukaḷilum, ceṉṉi yilum irunta nūl niliyaṅkaḷ - āriyamoḻi intiya maṇṇil vērūṉṟiya varalāṟu - tamiḻukku nērnta kēṭukaḷ - tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟitta katikaḷ - vaḷḷuvac camayam - paṇṭiya nākarika nāṭukaḷ - annāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya nili - akattiyar paṟṟiya paḻaṅkatikaḷ - tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ - ulakanāṭukaḷil tamiḻar nākarikac cuvaṭukaḷ - tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi tamiḻ - tirāviṭariṉ piṟappiṭam - aivaki nilaṅkaḷ, pākupāṭukaḷ - paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ - cintu veḷi nākarika mēṉmi - puttariṉ piṟappu - avarip paṟṟiya katikaḷ - irāmakāti paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ - teṉṉavariṉ kulaṅkaḷ, kuṭikaḷ, vaṭavariṉ kulaṅkaḷ, .kuṭikaḷ - tamiḻiṉ paḻami, tamiḻaka elli amippu - paṇṭikkāla kalvi muṟi, urinaṭi - vētaṅkaḷ, vētaṅkaḷukkum ākamaṅkaḷukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ - nīrvaḻi, nilavaḻi vaṇikam ciṟantōṅkiya nili - malivaḻi, kaṭalvaḻi, nilavaḻi poruḷkaḷ nirampiya kālam - cuṟṟam taḻikka vāḻnta nili - tamiḻa vaṇikar, vēṟṟu nāṭṭu vaṇikaruṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭu mikkōṅkiyirunta kālam - vāṉanūl kaliyum, ciṟpak kaliyum, kaṭṭaṭak kaliyum , icikkaliyum mikkōṅkiyirunta kālam - āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa piṟamoḻi kalappaṟṟa tūya tamiḻ peruki yirunta kālam - ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷukkut tamiḻar nākarikam nāṟṟaṅkāl - ulakap paṇpāṭukaḷukkut tamiḻar paṇpāṭu toṭṭilāka amintami - akarātikaḷ - aṟivut tēṭalukkuriya ceytikaḷ - ivartam nūlkaḷiṉ uyirkkūṟukaḷāka aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
vāḻum moḻi tamiḻ
tamiḻ iḷiñarkaḷ tam muṉṉōriṉ perumiyi uṇara, etirkāla vāḻviṟku ēṇippaṭikaḷāy amivaṉa innūlkaḷ. ekiptiya moḻi, cumēriya moḻi, ilattīṉum, pāliyum kirēkkamum arapik moḻiyum vāḻntu ciṟanta kālattil tamiḻ moḻiyum vāḻntu ciṟantatu. paḻamperum moḻikaḷ pala māṇṭum cila kāppaka moḻikaḷākavum aṟiñarkaḷiṉ pārvi moḻiyākavum iruntu varum ik kālattil iṉṟaḷavum iḷami kuṉṟā vaḷam niṟinta moḻiyāka tamiḻ moḻi vāḻkiṟatu eṉṟu nām perumikoḷḷalām.
āṉāl, ittamiḻ moḻiyiṉ nili iṉṟu āṭcimoḻiyāka aluval moḻiyāka, icimoḻiyāka, kalvi moḻiyāka, aṟamaṉṟa moḻiyāka , vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāka, kuṭumpa moḻiyāka illāta iraṅkat takka niliyāka uḷḷatu. tāymoḻiyiṉ ciṟappip puṟantaḷḷi vēṟṟumoḻiyit tūkkip piṭikkum avala nili mikuntuḷḷatu. mukatti iḻanta māntaṉ uyirvāḻa muṭiyātu. eppaṭi uyiraṟṟavaṉō, avvāṟē moḻiyi iḻanta iṉam irunta iṭam teriyāmal aḻiyum eṉpatu ulaka varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmi. oru iṉatti aḻikka vēṇṭumeṉil moḻiyi aḻittāltāṉ iṉatti aḻikka muṭiyum. ulaka makkaḷellām tam kiyeḻuttit tam tam tāymoḻiyil pōṭuvatip perumiyākak koḷvar. āṉāl tamiḻ maṇṇiṉ niliyō? eṅkaṇum kāṇāta avalam niṟintatu. moḻiyiyum iṉattiyum uyarvākak karutiya iṉaṅkaḷ ulaka araṅkil uyarntu niṟkum uṉṉatattiyum tāḻvākak karutiya iṉaṅkaḷ ulaka araṅkil tāḻvuṟṟu iruppatiyum tamiḻarkaḷ iṉiyēṉum aṟivārkaḷā?
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭaṉ
iḷam talimuṟikkum māṇavarkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭattakka ivvariya nūlkaḷit tokuttu 23 tiraṭṭukaḷākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kāppukkum, tamiḻariṉ eḻuccikkum vittiṭum innūlkaḷ. tamiḻar yār, etirikaḷ yār eṉum ariya uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum nūlkaḷ. ivariṉ pēruḻippāl eḻutappaṭṭa in nūlkaḷ paḻiya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi mīṭṭeṭukkum nūlkaḷ. tamiḻar kaḷukkuḷḷa palavīṉatti uṇarvataṟkum palatti uyirppippataṟkum uriya nūlkaḷākum. in nūlkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu iḷantalimuṟikku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. ivaṟṟik kāppatum pōṟṟuvatum tamiḻarkaḷ kaṭaṉ. 
māṇṭupōṉa icurēl moḻiyiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum mīṭ ṭeṭutta icurēliyarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu namakkup pāṭamāka amintuḷḷatu. ulaka makkaḷukku nākarikam iṉṉateṉak kāṭṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. ulakil mutaṉmutalil uḻavut toḻiliyum kaṭal vāḻviyum vaḷartta varkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. mutaṉmutalil vīṭamippum, teruvamippum nakaramippum nāṭamippum kaṇṭavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. ulakil mutaṉmutalil moḻiyum kaliyum āṭcip pirivukaḷum, caṭṭaṅkaḷum piṟa kūṟukaḷum vakuttavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ.
eḻuccikku vittiṭa...
uṭampu nōkāmal ki nakattiṉ kaṇkaḷil aḻukkupaṭāmal tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷic cāḷaramākak koṇṭu vāḻum kūṭṭattāl tamiḻukkum tamiḻarkaḷukkum viḷinta cīraḻivi iṟṟit talimuṟi aṟital vēṇṭum. maṟumalarccikkum urimip pōrukkum uṉṉata eḻuccikkum antanta nāṭukaḷil iḷiñarkaḷ muṉṉeṭuttuc ceṉṟa varalāṟu nam kaṇmuṉṉē kāṭciyākat terikiṟatu. ayarlāntu, cermaṉi, turukki, cappāṉiṉ aṉṟiya niliyum, iṉṟiya niliyum - tamiḻ iḷiñarkaḷ paṭittāltāṉ namnāṭṭiṉ eḻuccikku vittiṭa muṭiyum eṉpati innūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka uṇara muṭikiṟatu.
in nūl tiraṭṭukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku eṉakkup peritum utaviyāka iruntavar ceṉṉi vāḻ pulavarum, vaṭaceṉṉi tamiḻ vaḷarccip pēraviyiṉ ceyalāḷar, niṟuvaṉarumāṉa pulavar kō. tēvarācaṉ, mu.ka.,ka.i., āvār. ivariṉ pērutaviyāl muḻumiyāka nūlkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttut tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk koṭiyākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. avarukku em naṉṟi. innūlkaḷip poruḷ vāriyāka pirittut tiraṭṭukaḷāka ākkiyuḷḷōm. ovvoru tiraṭṭiṟkum takka tamiḻc cāṉṟōriṉ aṟimuka uriyōṭu veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. ivarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟi eṉṟum. in nūlākkap paṇikku utaviya kō. araṅkarācaṉ, mēlaṭṭi ākkattiṟku utaviya pirēm, kaṇiṉi iyakkunarkaḷ caravaṇaṉ, kuppucāmi, kaliyaracaṉ, kaṭṭunar taṉacēkaraṉ, innūlkaḷ piḻiyiṉṟi veḷivara meypput tirutti utaviya pulavar cīṉivācaṉ, pulavar āṟumukam, celvarācaṉ ākiyōrukkum maṟṟum acciṭṭu utaviya ‘prāmṭ’ accakattār maṟṟum ‘veṅka ṭēcuvarā’ accakattārukkum em patippakam cārpil naṉṟiyit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
